7/-!
8# HRS.Energy.WeakReference — Lemmas 5.6 and 5.7 (weak nonnegative interaction)
9
10Main paper, Section 5.2 "A weak nonnegative interaction" (source lines 6144-6172 and 6271-6278):
11`q(x,y) = q(y,x) ≥ 0`, `q(x,y) ≤ A w(x-y)` with `w = g + c_*`, and `a := sup_x ∫ q(x,y) dμ(y) < a₀`
12(`N:eq:q-small`); `ℝ_{N,τ} = Q^q_{N,τβ}(μ)` for `0 ≤ τ ≤ 1`.
13
14Representation decisions (recorded in `docs/ISSUES.md` §H):
15* `ℝ_{N,τ} = (μ^{⊗N}).withDensity (W/K)` with `W(X) = exp(-τβN F_N^q(X, μ))` and `K = ∫⁻ W dμ^{⊗N}`; its
16  one-coordinate conditional laws are `HRS.External.siteKernel μ W i x` (the kernel does not depend on the
17  normalisation of `W`), the same objects Wu's inequality (`HRS.External.wu_resampling`) consumes.
18* `a < a₀` is a hypothesis on a number `a` bounding `∫⁻ q(x,·) dμ` for every `x` (no real `iSup`).
19* "a₀ chosen small depending on the fixed upper temperature and the density bounds; A finite and fixed":
20  `a₀` and the constants are chosen after `A`, the temperature interval and the density bounds, before `ρ`, `q`.
21* Lemma 5.6 is stated without the clause "these bounds also hold after one pin and for the cavity law"; that
22  clause is a separate statement (still to be written), and the coverage row stays partial until then.
23* `|log K^q_{N,β}(μ)| ≤ C N δ_N` (Lemma 5.7) is stated as two-sided `lintegral` bounds.
24-/
25

27open scoped ENNReal

35/-- Unnormalised Gibbs weight `exp(-β N F_N^q(X, μ))` of a pair kernel `q`. -/
36def pairWeight (q : Torus d → Torus d → ℝ) (N : ℕ) (β : ℝ) (μ : Measure (Torus d))
37    (X : Config d N) : ℝ≥0∞ :=
38  ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (-β * N * modulatedEnergy q X μ))
39
40/-- `K^q_{N,β}(μ) = ∫ exp(-β N F_N^q) dμ^{⊗N}`. -/
41def pairPartition (q : Torus d → Torus d → ℝ) (N : ℕ) (β : ℝ) (μ : Measure (Torus d)) : ℝ≥0∞ :=
42  ∫⁻ X, pairWeight q N β μ X ∂(Measure.pi fun _ => μ)
43
44/-- `Q^q_{N,β}(μ)`, the normalised Gibbs law of the pair kernel `q`. -/
45def pairGibbsLaw (q : Torus d → Torus d → ℝ) (N : ℕ) (β : ℝ) (μ : Measure (Torus d)) :
46    Measure (Config d N) :=
47  (Measure.pi fun _ => μ).withDensity fun X => pairWeight q N β μ X / pairPartition q N β μ
48
49/-! ### Route for Lemmas 5.6/5.7 (worker MAIN-02)
50
51Done here (Dobrushin layer, source lines 6175-6190): the conditional weight of `ℝ_{N,τ}` in coordinate `i`
52factors as `c(x) · v(z)` with `v(z) = exp(-(τβ/N) ∑_{l≠i} q(z,x_l) + τβ (q*μ)(z))`
53(`weight_update`, `siteKernel_eq`); `e^{-τβ a} ≤ ∫ v dμ`, `v ≤ e^{τβ a}` (Jensen via the tangent line) give the
54density bound `≤ e^{2τβ a} μ` (`siteKernel_le`); a change of one coordinate `j` changes `v` in `L¹(μ)` by at
55most `e^{τβ a}(τβ/N) 2a` (`1 - e^{-u} ≤ u`), hence `siteKernel_coeff` (`4 e^{2τβ a} τβ a / N`); Wu's
56inequality with constant `2` (`wu_clause`, via `wu_resampling_half` for `W/K`); and `K ≥ 1`
57(`one_le_partition`, Jensen with `E[N F^q] = -½ ∬q ≤ 0`).
58
59Done (worker `weakm`):
60
61* `weak_marginal_aux` (source 6206-6268), via `weak_marginal_strong` (sections WM_A-WM_J): cavity formula for the
62  marginal density, Laplace residual through the variance of the truncated sum, convolution `L²` estimate
63  (Riesz envelope, polar coordinates, level-set integrals), Schur contraction with explicit constants.
64* `weak_pressure_upper_aux` (source 6281-6340), sections WM_K-WM_Z.  Route: `Z(t) = ∫ e^{-t G} dμ^{⊗N}` with
65  `G = N F_N^q`, `K^q_{N,t}(μ) = Z(t)` and `log Z(t)' = -E_t[G]`, `(log Z)'' = Var_{ℝ_t}(G)`.  The variance bound
66  (`variance_G_le`, Wu's inequality applied twice with pinned marginals, `evariance_G_le`, `second_moment_gi`)
67  is `Var ≤ C (ψ₂(2NP/t) + ψ₂(N))` (`variance_at`; `ψ₂` = order of `∫ min(w,L)²`, regime wise `1`, `log(eL)`,
68  `L^{2-d/s}`) using the marginal `L²` bound for the field difference (`theta_abstract`) and the level-set
69  bounds `row_bounds_real`.  The ODE comparison lemma `ode_bound`/`logZ_upper` with the explicit antiderivatives
70  `exists_W` (integrable singularity `J_N(t)`) gives `log K ≤ β(½∬q + W(β)) ≤ C N δ_N`. -/

76/-- row integral `H x = ∫ q(x,y) dμ(y)` -/
77def rowInt (q : Torus d → Torus d → ℝ) (μ : Measure (Torus d)) (x : Torus d) : ℝ :=
78  ∫ y, q x y ∂μ
79
80/-- Hypotheses on the weak kernel. -/
81structure WeakKernel (μ : Measure (Torus d)) (a : ℝ) (q : Torus d → Torus d → ℝ) : Prop where
82  meas : Measurable (Function.uncurry q)
83  symm : ∀ x y, q x y = q y x
84  nonneg : ∀ x y, 0 ≤ q x y
85  row : ∀ x, ∫⁻ y, ENNReal.ofReal (q x y) ∂μ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal a
86
87section WK
88
89variable {μ : Measure (Torus d)} [IsProbabilityMeasure μ] {a : ℝ} {q : Torus d → Torus d → ℝ}
90
91lemma WeakKernel.measurable_left (h : WeakKernel μ a q) (x : Torus d) : Measurable (q x) :=
92  h.meas.comp (measurable_const.prodMk measurable_id)
93
94lemma WeakKernel.measurable_right (h : WeakKernel μ a q) (y : Torus d) :
95    Measurable (fun x => q x y) :=
96  h.meas.comp (measurable_id.prodMk measurable_const)
97
98lemma WeakKernel.integrable_row (h : WeakKernel μ a q) (x : Torus d) : Integrable (q x) μ := by
99  refine ⟨(h.measurable_left x).aestronglyMeasurable, ?_⟩
100  unfold HasFiniteIntegral
101  have : ∀ y, ‖q x y‖ₑ = ENNReal.ofReal (q x y) := fun y => by
102    rw [Real.enorm_eq_ofReal (h.nonneg x y)]
103  simp_rw [this]
104  exact lt_of_le_of_lt (h.row x) ENNReal.ofReal_lt_top
105
106lemma WeakKernel.integrable_col (h : WeakKernel μ a q) (y : Torus d) :
107    Integrable (fun x => q x y) μ := by
108  simpa [h.symm] using h.integrable_row y
109
110lemma WeakKernel.rowInt_nonneg (h : WeakKernel μ a q) (x : Torus d) : 0 ≤ rowInt q μ x :=
111  integral_nonneg fun y => h.nonneg x y
112
113lemma WeakKernel.rowInt_le (h : WeakKernel μ a q) (ha : 0 ≤ a) (x : Torus d) :
114    rowInt q μ x ≤ a := by
115  have h1 : ENNReal.ofReal (rowInt q μ x) = ∫⁻ y, ENNReal.ofReal (q x y) ∂μ :=
116    ofReal_integral_eq_lintegral_ofReal (h.integrable_row x)
117      (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => h.nonneg x y)
118  have := (h.row x)
119  rw [← h1] at this
120  exact (ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal_iff ha).1 this
121
122lemma WeakKernel.integral_col (h : WeakKernel μ a q) (y : Torus d) :
123    ∫ x, q x y ∂μ = rowInt q μ y := by
124  unfold rowInt; simp_rw [h.symm _ y]
125
126lemma WeakKernel.measurable_rowInt (h : WeakKernel μ a q) : Measurable (rowInt q μ) := by
127  have : StronglyMeasurable (Function.uncurry q) := h.meas.stronglyMeasurable
128  exact (this.integral_prod_right' (ν := μ)).measurable
129
130end WK
131
132lemma abs_exp_neg_sub_le {s t : ℝ} (hs : 0 ≤ s) (ht : 0 ≤ t) :
133    |Real.exp (-s) - Real.exp (-t)| ≤ |s - t| := by
134  have key : ∀ s t : ℝ, 0 ≤ s → s ≤ t → Real.exp (-s) - Real.exp (-t) ≤ t - s := by
135    intro s t hs hst
136    have h1 : Real.exp (-s) ≤ 1 := by rw [Real.exp_le_one_iff]; linarith
137    have h2 : -(t - s) + 1 ≤ Real.exp (-(t - s)) := Real.add_one_le_exp _
138    have h3 : Real.exp (-t) = Real.exp (-s) * Real.exp (-(t - s)) := by
139      rw [← Real.exp_add]; congr 1; ring
140    have h4 : 0 ≤ Real.exp (-s) := (Real.exp_pos _).le
141    have : Real.exp (-s) - Real.exp (-t) = Real.exp (-s) * (1 - Real.exp (-(t - s))) := by
142      rw [h3]; ring
143    rw [this]
144    nlinarith
145  rcases le_total s t with h | h
146  · have := key s t hs h
147    have h5 : Real.exp (-t) ≤ Real.exp (-s) := Real.exp_le_exp.2 (by linarith)
148    rw [abs_of_nonneg (by linarith), abs_of_nonpos (by linarith)]; linarith
149  · have := key t s ht h
150    have h5 : Real.exp (-s) ≤ Real.exp (-t) := Real.exp_le_exp.2 (by linarith)
151    rw [abs_of_nonpos (by linarith), abs_of_nonneg (by linarith)]; linarith

156/-- pair sum -/
157def pairSum (q : Torus d → Torus d → ℝ) (X : Config d N) : ℝ :=
158  ∑ k, ∑ l ∈ Finset.univ.erase k, q (X k) (X l)
159
160/-- part of the pair sum not involving coordinate `i` -/
161def pairRest (q : Torus d → Torus d → ℝ) (i : Fin N) (x : Config d N) : ℝ :=
162  ∑ k ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, ∑ l ∈ (Finset.univ.erase k).erase i, q (x k) (x l)
163
164lemma pairSum_update (q : Torus d → Torus d → ℝ) (hq : ∀ x y, q x y = q y x) (i : Fin N)
165    (x : Config d N) (z : Torus d) :
166    pairSum q (Function.update x i z)
167      = 2 * ∑ l ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, q z (x l) + pairRest q i x := by
168  unfold pairSum pairRest
169  rw [← Finset.add_sum_erase _ _ (Finset.mem_univ i)]
170  have h1 : ∑ l ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, q (Function.update x i z i) (Function.update x i z l)
171      = ∑ l ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, q z (x l) := by
172    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun l hl => ?_
173    rw [Function.update_self, Function.update_of_ne (Finset.ne_of_mem_erase hl)]
174  have h2 : ∀ k ∈ Finset.univ.erase i,
175      ∑ l ∈ Finset.univ.erase k, q (Function.update x i z k) (Function.update x i z l)
176        = q (x k) z + ∑ l ∈ (Finset.univ.erase k).erase i, q (x k) (x l) := by
177    intro k hk
178    have hki : k ≠ i := Finset.ne_of_mem_erase hk
179    have hi : i ∈ Finset.univ.erase k := Finset.mem_erase.2 ⟨hki.symm, Finset.mem_univ i⟩
180    rw [← Finset.add_sum_erase _ _ hi]
181    rw [Function.update_of_ne hki, Function.update_self]
182    congr 1
183    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun l hl => ?_
184    rw [Function.update_of_ne (Finset.ne_of_mem_erase hl)]
185  rw [h1, Finset.sum_congr rfl h2, Finset.sum_add_distrib]
186  have h3 : ∑ k ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, q (x k) z = ∑ l ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, q z (x l) :=
187    Finset.sum_congr rfl fun k _ => hq _ _
188  rw [h3]; ring
189
190
191/-- one-site factor -/
192def vfun (q : Torus d → Torus d → ℝ) (μ : Measure (Torus d)) (N : ℕ) (β' : ℝ) (i : Fin N)
193    (x : Config d N) (z : Torus d) : ℝ :=
194  Real.exp (-(β' / N) * ∑ l ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, q z (x l) + β' * rowInt q μ z)
195
196/-- the rest in the exponent -/
197def restExp (q : Torus d → Torus d → ℝ) (μ : Measure (Torus d)) (N : ℕ) (β' : ℝ) (i : Fin N)
198    (x : Config d N) : ℝ :=
199  -(β' / (2 * N)) * pairRest q i x + β' * ∑ k ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, rowInt q μ (x k)
200    - β' * N * (∫ x, ∫ y, q x y ∂μ ∂μ) / 2
201
202lemma exponent_eq (q : Torus d → Torus d → ℝ) (μ : Measure (Torus d)) (β' : ℝ) (X : Config d N)
203    (hN : 0 < N) :
204    -β' * N * modulatedEnergy q X μ
205      = -(β' / (2 * N)) * pairSum q X + β' * ∑ k, rowInt q μ (X k)
206          - β' * N * (∫ x, ∫ y, q x y ∂μ ∂μ) / 2 := by
207  unfold modulatedEnergy pairSum rowInt
208  have : (N : ℝ) ≠ 0 := by positivity
209  field_simp
210  ring
211
212lemma weight_update (q : Torus d → Torus d → ℝ) (hq : ∀ x y, q x y = q y x)
213    (μ : Measure (Torus d)) (β' : ℝ) (i : Fin N) (x : Config d N) (z : Torus d) (hN : 0 < N) :
214    pairWeight q N β' μ (Function.update x i z)
215      = ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (restExp q μ N β' i x)) * ENNReal.ofReal (vfun q μ N β' i x z) := by
216  unfold pairWeight vfun
217  rw [← ENNReal.ofReal_mul (Real.exp_pos _).le, ← Real.exp_add]
218  congr 2
219  rw [exponent_eq q μ β' _ hN, pairSum_update q hq]
220  rw [← Finset.add_sum_erase _ _ (Finset.mem_univ i)]
221  have : (N : ℝ) ≠ 0 := by positivity
222  simp only [restExp, Function.update_self]
223  have h : ∑ k ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, rowInt q μ (Function.update x i z k)
224      = ∑ k ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, rowInt q μ (x k) :=
225    Finset.sum_congr rfl fun k hk => by rw [Function.update_of_ne (Finset.ne_of_mem_erase hk)]
226  rw [h]
227  field_simp
228  ring

233section TVpart

235lemma integrable_of_bdd' {E : Type*} [MeasurableSpace E] (μ : Measure E) [IsProbabilityMeasure μ]
236    {f : E → ℝ} (hf : Measurable f) {M : ℝ}
237    (hb : ∀ z, |f z| ≤ M) : Integrable f μ :=
238  Integrable.of_bound hf.aestronglyMeasurable M (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun z => by
239    rw [Real.norm_eq_abs]; exact hb z)
240
241lemma real_withDensity_ofReal {E : Type*} [MeasurableSpace E] (μ : Measure E) {f : E → ℝ}
242    (h0 : ∀ z, 0 ≤ f z)
243    (hint : Integrable f μ) {S : Set E} (hS : MeasurableSet S) :
244    ((μ.withDensity (fun z => ENNReal.ofReal (f z))) S).toReal = ∫ z in S, f z ∂μ := by
245  rw [withDensity_apply _ hS, ← ofReal_integral_eq_lintegral_ofReal hint.restrict
246    (Filter.Eventually.of_forall h0)]
247  exact ENNReal.toReal_ofReal (integral_nonneg h0)
248
249lemma tv_normalized {E : Type*} [MeasurableSpace E] (μ : Measure E) [IsProbabilityMeasure μ]
250    {u u' : E → ℝ} (hu : Measurable u)
251    (hu' : Measurable u') (h0 : ∀ z, 0 ≤ u z) (h0' : ∀ z, 0 ≤ u' z) {M m : ℝ}
252    (hM : ∀ z, u z ≤ M) (hM' : ∀ z, u' z ≤ M) (hm : 0 < m) (hZ : m ≤ ∫ z, u z ∂μ)
253    (hZ' : m ≤ ∫ z, u' z ∂μ) (S : Set E) :
254    |(∫ z in S, u z / ∫ z, u z ∂μ ∂μ) - ∫ z in S, u' z / ∫ z, u' z ∂μ ∂μ|
255      ≤ 2 / m * ∫ z, |u z - u' z| ∂μ := by
256  set Z := ∫ z, u z ∂μ with hZdef
257  set Z' := ∫ z, u' z ∂μ with hZ'def
258  have hZp : 0 < Z := lt_of_lt_of_le hm hZ
259  have hZ'p : 0 < Z' := lt_of_lt_of_le hm hZ'
260  have iu : Integrable u μ := integrable_of_bdd' μ hu (M := M) fun z => by
261    rw [abs_of_nonneg (h0 z)]; exact hM z
262  have iu' : Integrable u' μ := integrable_of_bdd' μ hu' (M := M) fun z => by
263    rw [abs_of_nonneg (h0' z)]; exact hM' z
264  have idiff : Integrable (fun z => |u z - u' z|) μ := (iu.sub iu').abs
265  set D := ∫ z, |u z - u' z| ∂μ with hD
266  have hD0 : 0 ≤ D := integral_nonneg fun z => abs_nonneg _
267  have hZZ' : |Z' - Z| ≤ D := by
268    have : Z' - Z = ∫ z, (u' z - u z) ∂μ := (integral_sub iu' iu).symm
269    rw [this]
270    calc |∫ z, (u' z - u z) ∂μ| ≤ ∫ z, |u' z - u z| ∂μ := abs_integral_le_integral_abs
271      _ = D := by simp_rw [abs_sub_comm]; rfl
272  have hpt : ∀ z, |u z / Z - u' z / Z'| ≤ |u z - u' z| / Z + u' z * (|Z' - Z| / (Z * Z')) := by
273    intro z
274    have : u z / Z - u' z / Z' = (u z - u' z) / Z + u' z * ((Z' - Z) / (Z * Z')) := by
275      field_simp; ring
276    rw [this]
277    refine (abs_add_le _ _).trans ?_
278    rw [abs_div, abs_of_pos hZp, abs_mul, abs_of_nonneg (h0' z), abs_div, abs_of_pos (mul_pos hZp hZ'p)]
279  have ig : Integrable (fun z => |u z - u' z| / Z + u' z * (|Z' - Z| / (Z * Z'))) μ :=
280    (idiff.div_const Z).add (iu'.mul_const _)
281  have hint : ∫ z, (|u z - u' z| / Z + u' z * (|Z' - Z| / (Z * Z'))) ∂μ = D / Z + |Z' - Z| / Z := by
282    rw [integral_add (idiff.div_const Z) (iu'.mul_const _), integral_div, integral_mul_const]
283    congr 1
284    rw [← hZ'def]; field_simp
285  have e1 : (∫ z in S, u z / Z ∂μ) - ∫ z in S, u' z / Z' ∂μ = ∫ z in S, (u z / Z - u' z / Z') ∂μ :=
286    (integral_sub (iu.div_const Z).restrict (iu'.div_const Z').restrict).symm
287  rw [e1]
288  have step : |∫ z in S, (u z / Z - u' z / Z') ∂μ| ≤ ∫ z, (|u z - u' z| / Z + u' z * (|Z' - Z| / (Z * Z'))) ∂μ := by
289    calc |∫ z in S, (u z / Z - u' z / Z') ∂μ| ≤ ∫ z in S, |u z / Z - u' z / Z'| ∂μ :=
290          abs_integral_le_integral_abs
291      _ ≤ ∫ z in S, (|u z - u' z| / Z + u' z * (|Z' - Z| / (Z * Z'))) ∂μ :=
292          integral_mono ((iu.div_const Z).sub (iu'.div_const Z')).abs.restrict ig.restrict hpt
293      _ ≤ _ := setIntegral_le_integral ig (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun z =>
294            add_nonneg (div_nonneg (abs_nonneg _) hZp.le)
295              (mul_nonneg (h0' z) (div_nonneg (abs_nonneg _) (mul_pos hZp hZ'p).le)))
296  refine step.trans ?_
297  rw [hint]
298  have h1 : D / Z ≤ D / m := div_le_div_of_nonneg_left hD0 hm hZ
299  have h2 : |Z' - Z| / Z ≤ D / m := by
300    calc |Z' - Z| / Z ≤ D / Z := div_le_div_of_nonneg_right hZZ' hZp.le
301      _ ≤ D / m := h1
302  calc D / Z + |Z' - Z| / Z ≤ D / m + D / m := add_le_add h1 h2
303    _ = 2 / m * D := by ring
304
305end TVpart

311variable {μ : Measure (Torus d)} [IsProbabilityMeasure μ] {a : ℝ} {q : Torus d → Torus d → ℝ}

313lemma WeakKernel.measurable_vfun (h : WeakKernel μ a q) (N : ℕ) (β' : ℝ) (i : Fin N)
314    (x : Config d N) : Measurable (vfun q μ N β' i x) := by
315  unfold vfun
316  refine Real.measurable_exp.comp ?_
317  refine Measurable.add ?_ (h.measurable_rowInt.const_mul _)
318  exact (Finset.measurable_sum _ fun l _ => h.measurable_right (x l)).const_mul _
319
320lemma vfun_pos (N : ℕ) (β' : ℝ) (i : Fin N) (x : Config d N) (z : Torus d) :
321    0 < vfun q μ N β' i x z := Real.exp_pos _
322
323lemma WeakKernel.vfun_le (h : WeakKernel μ a q) (ha : 0 ≤ a) {β' : ℝ} (hβ : 0 ≤ β') (N : ℕ)
324    (i : Fin N) (x : Config d N) (z : Torus d) :
325    vfun q μ N β' i x z ≤ Real.exp (β' * a) := by
326  unfold vfun
327  refine Real.exp_le_exp.2 ?_
328  have h1 : 0 ≤ ∑ l ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, q z (x l) :=
329    Finset.sum_nonneg fun l _ => h.nonneg _ _
330  have h2 : 0 ≤ β' / N := div_nonneg hβ (Nat.cast_nonneg N)
331  have h3 : β' * rowInt q μ z ≤ β' * a := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (h.rowInt_le ha z) hβ
332  nlinarith
333
334lemma WeakKernel.integral_vfun_ge (h : WeakKernel μ a q) (ha : 0 ≤ a) {β' : ℝ} (hβ : 0 ≤ β')
335    {N : ℕ} (hN : 0 < N) (i : Fin N) (x : Config d N) :
336    Real.exp (-(β' * a)) ≤ ∫ z, vfun q μ N β' i x z ∂μ := by
337  set t : Torus d → ℝ := fun z =>
338    -(β' / N) * ∑ l ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, q z (x l) + β' * rowInt q μ z with ht
339  have hvt : ∀ z, vfun q μ N β' i x z = Real.exp (t z) := fun z => rfl
340  set c0 : ℝ := -(β' * a) with hc0
341  have ivf : Integrable (vfun q μ N β' i x) μ :=
342    integrable_of_bdd' μ (h.measurable_vfun N β' i x) (M := Real.exp (β' * a)) fun z => by
343      rw [abs_of_pos (vfun_pos N β' i x z)]; exact h.vfun_le ha hβ N i x z
344  have iH : Integrable (rowInt q μ) μ :=
345    integrable_of_bdd' μ h.measurable_rowInt (M := a) fun z => by
346      rw [abs_of_nonneg (h.rowInt_nonneg z)]; exact h.rowInt_le ha z
347  have it : Integrable t μ := by
348    refine Integrable.add ?_ (iH.const_mul _)
349    exact (integrable_finsetSum _ fun l _ => h.integrable_col (x l)).const_mul _
350  have hpt : ∀ z, Real.exp c0 * (1 + (t z - c0)) ≤ vfun q μ N β' i x z := by
351    intro z
352    have := Real.add_one_le_exp (t z - c0)
353    have h2 : Real.exp (t z) = Real.exp c0 * Real.exp (t z - c0) := by
354      rw [← Real.exp_add]; congr 1; ring
355    rw [hvt, h2]
356    have := Real.exp_pos c0
357    nlinarith
358  have hint : ∫ z, Real.exp c0 * (1 + (t z - c0)) ∂μ ≤ ∫ z, vfun q μ N β' i x z ∂μ :=
359    integral_mono ((integrable_const _).add (it.sub (integrable_const _)) |>.const_mul _) ivf hpt
360  refine le_trans ?_ hint
361  have e1 : ∫ z, Real.exp c0 * (1 + (t z - c0)) ∂μ = Real.exp c0 * (1 + (∫ z, t z ∂μ - c0)) := by
362    rw [integral_const_mul, integral_add (f := fun _ => (1:ℝ)) (g := fun z => t z - c0)
363      (integrable_const _) (it.sub (integrable_const _)),
364      integral_sub (f := t) (g := fun _ => c0) it (integrable_const _)]
365    simp
366  rw [e1]
367  have ht0 : c0 ≤ ∫ z, t z ∂μ := by
368    have e2 : ∫ z, t z ∂μ = -(β' / N) * ∑ l ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, rowInt q μ (x l)
369        + β' * ∫ z, rowInt q μ z ∂μ := by
370      rw [ht]
371      simp only
372      rw [integral_add ((integrable_finsetSum _ fun l _ => h.integrable_col (x l)).const_mul _)
373        (iH.const_mul _), integral_const_mul, integral_const_mul, integral_finsetSum _
374        fun l _ => h.integrable_col (x l)]
375      simp_rw [h.integral_col]
376    rw [e2]
377    have h1 : ∑ l ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, rowInt q μ (x l) ≤ N * a := by
378      calc ∑ l ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, rowInt q μ (x l)
379          ≤ ∑ l ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, a := Finset.sum_le_sum fun l _ => h.rowInt_le ha _
380        _ = ((Finset.univ.erase i).card : ℝ) * a := by simp
381        _ ≤ N * a := by
382          refine mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right ?_ ha
383          rw [Finset.card_erase_of_mem (Finset.mem_univ i)]
384          simp
385    have h2 : 0 ≤ ∫ z, rowInt q μ z ∂μ := integral_nonneg fun z => h.rowInt_nonneg z
386    have h3 : 0 ≤ β' / N := div_nonneg hβ (Nat.cast_nonneg N)
387    have h4 : -(β' / N) * ∑ l ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, rowInt q μ (x l) ≥ -(β' / N) * (N * a) := by
388      nlinarith
389    have h5 : -(β' / N) * (N * a) = -(β' * a) := by
390      have : (N : ℝ) ≠ 0 := by positivity
391      field_simp
392    have : 0 ≤ β' * ∫ z, rowInt q μ z ∂μ := mul_nonneg hβ h2
393    rw [hc0]; linarith
394  have := Real.exp_pos c0
395  nlinarith
396
397end Site

403variable {μ : Measure (Torus d)} [IsProbabilityMeasure μ] {a : ℝ} {q : Torus d → Torus d → ℝ}

405lemma WeakKernel.measurable_pairWeight (h : WeakKernel μ a q) (N : ℕ) (β' : ℝ) :
406    Measurable (pairWeight q N β' μ) := by
407  unfold pairWeight
408  refine ENNReal.measurable_ofReal.comp (Real.measurable_exp.comp ?_)
409  unfold modulatedEnergy
410  have h1 : Measurable (fun X : Config d N => ∑ i, ∑ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, q (X i) (X j)) :=
411    Finset.measurable_sum _ fun i _ => Finset.measurable_sum _ fun j _ => by
412      have := h.meas.comp (f := fun X : Config d N => (X i, X j))
413        ((measurable_pi_apply i).prodMk (measurable_pi_apply j))
414      exact this
415  have h2 : Measurable (fun X : Config d N => ∑ i, ∫ y, q (X i) y ∂μ) :=
416    Finset.measurable_sum _ fun i _ => h.measurable_rowInt.comp (measurable_pi_apply i)
417  exact (((h1.const_mul _).sub (h2.const_mul _)).add_const _).const_mul _
418
419lemma WeakKernel.siteKernel_eq (h : WeakKernel μ a q) (ha : 0 ≤ a) {β' : ℝ} (hβ : 0 ≤ β')
420    {N : ℕ} (hN : 0 < N) (i : Fin N) (x : Config d N) :
421    External.siteKernel μ (pairWeight q N β' μ) i x
422      = μ.withDensity (fun z => ENNReal.ofReal (vfun q μ N β' i x z / ∫ w, vfun q μ N β' i x w ∂μ)) := by
423  rw [External.siteKernel_apply (h.measurable_pairWeight N β')]
424  set V := ∫ w, vfun q μ N β' i x w ∂μ with hV
425  have hVp : 0 < V := lt_of_lt_of_le (Real.exp_pos _) (h.integral_vfun_ge ha hβ hN i x)
426  have ivf : Integrable (vfun q μ N β' i x) μ :=
427    integrable_of_bdd' μ (h.measurable_vfun N β' i x) (M := Real.exp (β' * a)) fun z => by
428      rw [abs_of_pos (vfun_pos N β' i x z)]; exact h.vfun_le ha hβ N i x z
429  have hnorm : External.normalizer μ (pairWeight q N β' μ) i x
430      = ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (restExp q μ N β' i x)) * ENNReal.ofReal V := by
431    unfold External.normalizer
432    simp_rw [weight_update q h.symm μ β' i x _ hN]
433    have hmv : Measurable fun z => ENNReal.ofReal (vfun q μ N β' i x z) :=
434      ENNReal.measurable_ofReal.comp (h.measurable_vfun N β' i x)
435    rw [lintegral_const_mul _ hmv,
436      ← ofReal_integral_eq_lintegral_ofReal ivf (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun z => (vfun_pos N β' i x z).le)]
437  rw [hnorm]
438  congr 1
439  funext z
440  rw [weight_update q h.symm μ β' i x z hN]
441  set c := ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (restExp q μ N β' i x)) with hc
442  have hc0 : c ≠ 0 := (ENNReal.ofReal_pos.2 (Real.exp_pos _)).ne'
443  have hct : c ≠ ⊤ := ENNReal.ofReal_ne_top
444  rw [ENNReal.mul_inv (Or.inl hc0) (Or.inl hct)]
445  rw [ENNReal.ofReal_div_of_pos hVp, div_eq_mul_inv]
446  calc c * ENNReal.ofReal (vfun q μ N β' i x z) * (c⁻¹ * (ENNReal.ofReal V)⁻¹)
447      = (c * c⁻¹) * (ENNReal.ofReal (vfun q μ N β' i x z) * (ENNReal.ofReal V)⁻¹) := by ring
448    _ = _ := by rw [ENNReal.mul_inv_cancel hc0 hct, one_mul]
449
450end Site2

456variable {μ : Measure (Torus d)} [IsProbabilityMeasure μ] {a : ℝ} {q : Torus d → Torus d → ℝ}
457
458lemma WeakKernel.siteKernel_le (h : WeakKernel μ a q) (ha : 0 ≤ a) {β' : ℝ} (hβ : 0 ≤ β')
459    {N : ℕ} (hN : 0 < N) (i : Fin N) (x : Config d N) {C : ℝ} (hC : Real.exp (2 * (β' * a)) ≤ C) :
460    External.siteKernel μ (pairWeight q N β' μ) i x ≤ ENNReal.ofReal C • μ := by
461  rw [h.siteKernel_eq ha hβ hN i x]
462  have hVp := lt_of_lt_of_le (Real.exp_pos _) (h.integral_vfun_ge ha hβ hN i x)
463  refine Measure.le_iff.2 fun S hS => ?_
464  rw [withDensity_apply _ hS, Measure.smul_apply, smul_eq_mul, ← setLIntegral_const]
465  refine lintegral_mono fun z => ?_
466  refine ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal ?_
467  rw [div_le_iff₀ (lt_of_lt_of_le (Real.exp_pos _) (h.integral_vfun_ge ha hβ hN i x))]
468  have h1 := h.vfun_le ha hβ N i x z
469  have h2 := h.integral_vfun_ge ha hβ hN i x
470  have h3 : Real.exp (2 * (β' * a)) * Real.exp (-(β' * a)) = Real.exp (β' * a) := by
471    rw [← Real.exp_add]; congr 1; ring
472  have h4 : Real.exp (2 * (β' * a)) * Real.exp (-(β' * a)) ≤ C * ∫ w, vfun q μ N β' i x w ∂μ := by
473    calc _ ≤ C * Real.exp (-(β' * a)) :=
474          mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hC (Real.exp_pos _).le
475      _ ≤ _ := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h2 ((Real.exp_pos _).le.trans hC)
476  linarith
477
478lemma WeakKernel.abs_vfun_sub_le (h : WeakKernel μ a q) (ha : 0 ≤ a) {β' : ℝ} (hβ : 0 ≤ β')
479    {N : ℕ} (i j : Fin N) (hij : i ≠ j) (x y : Config d N) (hxy : ∀ k, k ≠ j → x k = y k)
480    (z : Torus d) :
481    |vfun q μ N β' i x z - vfun q μ N β' i y z|
482      ≤ Real.exp (β' * a) * (β' / N * (q z (x j) + q z (y j))) := by
483  unfold vfun
484  set A : Config d N → ℝ := fun x => β' / N * ∑ l ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, q z (x l) with hA
485  have hA0 : ∀ x, 0 ≤ A x := fun x => mul_nonneg (div_nonneg hβ (Nat.cast_nonneg N))
486    (Finset.sum_nonneg fun l _ => h.nonneg _ _)
487  have hAx : ∀ x : Config d N, -(β' / N) * ∑ l ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, q z (x l)
488      + β' * rowInt q μ z = -A x + β' * rowInt q μ z := fun x => by simp only [hA]; ring
489  simp_rw [hAx]
490  have e : ∀ x : Config d N, Real.exp (-A x + β' * rowInt q μ z)
491      = Real.exp (-A x) * Real.exp (β' * rowInt q μ z) := fun x => by rw [← Real.exp_add]
492  simp_rw [e]
493  rw [← sub_mul, abs_mul, abs_of_pos (Real.exp_pos _)]
494  have hle : Real.exp (β' * rowInt q μ z) ≤ Real.exp (β' * a) :=
495    Real.exp_le_exp.2 (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (h.rowInt_le ha z) hβ)
496  have hdiff : |A x - A y| ≤ β' / N * (q z (x j) + q z (y j)) := by
497    have hj : j ∈ Finset.univ.erase i := Finset.mem_erase.2 ⟨hij.symm, Finset.mem_univ j⟩
498    have hs : ∑ l ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, q z (x l) - ∑ l ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, q z (y l)
499        = q z (x j) - q z (y j) := by
500      rw [← Finset.sum_sub_distrib]
501      rw [Finset.sum_eq_single j]
502      · intro l _ hl; rw [hxy l hl]; ring
503      · intro hj'; exact absurd hj hj'
504    have : A x - A y = β' / N * (q z (x j) - q z (y j)) := by
505      simp only [hA]; rw [← mul_sub, hs]
506    rw [this, abs_mul, abs_of_nonneg (div_nonneg hβ (Nat.cast_nonneg N))]
507    refine mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left ?_ (div_nonneg hβ (Nat.cast_nonneg N))
508    have := h.nonneg z (x j); have := h.nonneg z (y j)
509    rw [abs_le]; constructor <;> linarith
510  calc |Real.exp (-A x) - Real.exp (-A y)| * Real.exp (β' * rowInt q μ z)
511      ≤ |A x - A y| * Real.exp (β' * a) :=
512        mul_le_mul (abs_exp_neg_sub_le (hA0 x) (hA0 y)) hle (Real.exp_pos _).le (abs_nonneg _)
513    _ ≤ _ := by
514        rw [mul_comm]
515        exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hdiff (Real.exp_pos _).le
516
517lemma WeakKernel.siteKernel_coeff (h : WeakKernel μ a q) (ha : 0 ≤ a) {β' : ℝ} (hβ : 0 ≤ β')
518    {N : ℕ} (hN : 0 < N) (i j : Fin N) (hij : i ≠ j) (x y : Config d N)
519    (hxy : ∀ k, k ≠ j → x k = y k) (S : Set (Torus d)) (hS : MeasurableSet S) :
520    |(External.siteKernel μ (pairWeight q N β' μ) i x).real S
521        - (External.siteKernel μ (pairWeight q N β' μ) i y).real S|
522      ≤ 4 * Real.exp (2 * (β' * a)) * β' * a / N := by
523  have hbd : ∀ (x : Config d N) z, |vfun q μ N β' i x z| ≤ Real.exp (β' * a) := fun x z => by
524    rw [abs_of_pos (vfun_pos N β' i x z)]; exact h.vfun_le ha hβ N i x z
525  have ivf : ∀ x : Config d N, Integrable (vfun q μ N β' i x) μ := fun x =>
526    integrable_of_bdd' μ (h.measurable_vfun N β' i x) (hbd x)
527  have hVp : ∀ x : Config d N, 0 < ∫ w, vfun q μ N β' i x w ∂μ := fun x =>
528    lt_of_lt_of_le (Real.exp_pos _) (h.integral_vfun_ge ha hβ hN i x)
529  have hreal : ∀ x : Config d N,
530      (External.siteKernel μ (pairWeight q N β' μ) i x).real S
531        = ∫ z in S, vfun q μ N β' i x z / ∫ w, vfun q μ N β' i x w ∂μ ∂μ := by
532    intro x
533    rw [h.siteKernel_eq ha hβ hN i x, Measure.real]
534    exact real_withDensity_ofReal μ (fun z => div_nonneg (vfun_pos N β' i x z).le (hVp x).le)
535      ((ivf x).div_const _) hS
536  rw [hreal x, hreal y]
537  have htv := tv_normalized μ (h.measurable_vfun N β' i x) (h.measurable_vfun N β' i y)
538    (fun z => (vfun_pos N β' i x z).le) (fun z => (vfun_pos N β' i y z).le)
539    (fun z => h.vfun_le ha hβ N i x z) (fun z => h.vfun_le ha hβ N i y z) (Real.exp_pos (-(β' * a)))
540    (h.integral_vfun_ge ha hβ hN i x) (h.integral_vfun_ge ha hβ hN i y) S
541  refine htv.trans ?_
542  -- bound the L¹ distance
543  have iq : ∀ x : Config d N, Integrable (fun z => q z (x j)) μ := fun x => h.integrable_col (x j)
544  have hI : ∫ z, |vfun q μ N β' i x z - vfun q μ N β' i y z| ∂μ
545      ≤ Real.exp (β' * a) * (β' / N * (2 * a)) := by
546    calc ∫ z, |vfun q μ N β' i x z - vfun q μ N β' i y z| ∂μ
547        ≤ ∫ z, Real.exp (β' * a) * (β' / N * (q z (x j) + q z (y j))) ∂μ :=
548          integral_mono ((ivf x).sub (ivf y)).abs
549            (((iq x).add (iq y)).const_mul _ |>.const_mul _)
550            (fun z => h.abs_vfun_sub_le ha hβ i j hij x y hxy z)
551      _ = Real.exp (β' * a) * (β' / N * (rowInt q μ (x j) + rowInt q μ (y j))) := by
552          rw [integral_const_mul, integral_const_mul, integral_add (iq x) (iq y),
553            h.integral_col, h.integral_col]
554      _ ≤ _ := by
555          refine mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left ?_ (Real.exp_pos _).le
556          refine mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left ?_ (div_nonneg hβ (Nat.cast_nonneg N))
557          have := h.rowInt_le ha (x j); have := h.rowInt_le ha (y j); linarith
558  have hm : 2 / Real.exp (-(β' * a)) = 2 * Real.exp (β' * a) := by
559    rw [div_eq_mul_inv, ← Real.exp_neg, neg_neg]
560  rw [hm]
561  have h2 : (2 : ℝ) * Real.exp (β' * a) * (Real.exp (β' * a) * (β' / N * (2 * a)))
562      = 4 * Real.exp (2 * (β' * a)) * β' * a / N := by
563    have : Real.exp (2 * (β' * a)) = Real.exp (β' * a) * Real.exp (β' * a) := by
564      rw [← Real.exp_add]; congr 1; ring
565    rw [this]; ring
566  rw [← h2]
567  exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hI (by positivity)
568
569end Site3

575variable {μ : Measure (Torus d)} [IsProbabilityMeasure μ] {a : ℝ} {q : Torus d → Torus d → ℝ}

577lemma siteKernel_div_const {E : Type*} [MeasurableSpace E] {n : ℕ} (μ : Measure E)
578    [IsProbabilityMeasure μ] (W : (Fin n → E) → ℝ≥0∞) (K : ℝ≥0∞) (hK0 : K ≠ 0) (hKt : K ≠ ⊤)
579    (i : Fin n) :
580    External.siteKernel μ (fun X => W X / K) i = External.siteKernel μ W i := by
581  unfold External.siteKernel
582  congr 1
583  funext x z
584  have hKi0 : K⁻¹ ≠ 0 := ENNReal.inv_ne_zero.2 hKt
585  have hKit : K⁻¹ ≠ ⊤ := ENNReal.inv_ne_top.2 hK0
586  have hn : External.normalizer μ (fun X => W X / K) i x = External.normalizer μ W i x * K⁻¹ := by
587    unfold External.normalizer
588    simp_rw [div_eq_mul_inv]
589    exact lintegral_mul_const' _ _ hKit
590  rw [hn]
591  simp only [div_eq_mul_inv]
592  rw [ENNReal.mul_inv (Or.inr hKit) (Or.inr hKi0), inv_inv]
593  calc W (Function.update x i z) * K⁻¹ * ((External.normalizer μ W i x)⁻¹ * K)
594      = W (Function.update x i z) * (External.normalizer μ W i x)⁻¹ * (K⁻¹ * K) := by ring
595    _ = _ := by rw [ENNReal.inv_mul_cancel hK0 hKt, mul_one]
596
597lemma WeakKernel.pairWeight_le (h : WeakKernel μ a q) (ha : 0 ≤ a) {β' : ℝ} (hβ : 0 ≤ β')
598    {N : ℕ} (hN : 0 < N) (X : Config d N) :
599    pairWeight q N β' μ X ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (β' * N * a)) := by
600  unfold pairWeight
601  refine ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal (Real.exp_le_exp.2 ?_)
602  rw [exponent_eq q μ β' X hN]
603  have h1 : 0 ≤ pairSum q X := Finset.sum_nonneg fun k _ => Finset.sum_nonneg fun l _ => h.nonneg _ _
604  have h2 : ∑ k, rowInt q μ (X k) ≤ N * a := by
605    calc ∑ k, rowInt q μ (X k) ≤ ∑ _k : Fin N, a := Finset.sum_le_sum fun k _ => h.rowInt_le ha _
606      _ = N * a := by simp
607  have h3 : 0 ≤ ∫ x, ∫ y, q x y ∂μ ∂μ := integral_nonneg fun x => h.rowInt_nonneg x
608  have hN' : (0 : ℝ) < N := by exact_mod_cast hN
609  have h4 : 0 ≤ β' / (2 * N) * pairSum q X := mul_nonneg (by positivity) h1
610  have h5 : 0 ≤ β' * N * (∫ x, ∫ y, q x y ∂μ ∂μ) / 2 := by positivity
611  have h6 : β' * ∑ k, rowInt q μ (X k) ≤ β' * (N * a) := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h2 hβ
612  have e : β' * N * a = β' * (N * a) := by ring
613  rw [e]
614  have : -(β' / (2 * N)) * pairSum q X = -(β' / (2 * N) * pairSum q X) := by ring
615  rw [this]
616  linarith
617
618lemma WeakKernel.partition_pos_lt (h : WeakKernel μ a q) (ha : 0 ≤ a) {β' : ℝ} (hβ : 0 ≤ β')
619    {N : ℕ} (hN : 0 < N) :
620    pairPartition q N β' μ ≠ 0 ∧ pairPartition q N β' μ ≠ ⊤ := by
621  have hW := h.measurable_pairWeight N β'
622  constructor
623  · unfold pairPartition
624    intro h0
625    rw [lintegral_eq_zero_iff hW] at h0
626    have hne : ∀ᵐ X ∂(Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ), False := by
627      filter_upwards [h0] with X hX
628      have : (0 : ℝ≥0∞) < pairWeight q N β' μ X := ENNReal.ofReal_pos.2 (Real.exp_pos _)
629      exact this.ne' hX
630    have := ae_iff.mp hne
631    simp at this
632  · unfold pairPartition
633    refine ne_of_lt (lt_of_le_of_lt (lintegral_mono fun X => h.pairWeight_le ha hβ hN X) ?_)
634    simp
635
636lemma WeakKernel.wu_clause (h : WeakKernel μ a q) (ha : 0 ≤ a) {β' : ℝ} (hβ : 0 ≤ β')
637    {N : ℕ} (hN : 0 < N) (κ : ℝ) (hκ0 : 0 ≤ κ) (hκ : κ ≤ 1 / 2)
638    (hcoef : ∀ i j : Fin N, i ≠ j → ∀ x y : Config d N, (∀ k, k ≠ j → x k = y k) →
639      ∀ S : Set (Torus d), MeasurableSet S →
640        |(External.siteKernel μ (pairWeight q N β' μ) i x).real S
641          - (External.siteKernel μ (pairWeight q N β' μ) i y).real S| ≤ κ / N)
642    (F : Config d N → ℝ) (hF : Measurable F) :
643    ProbabilityTheory.evariance F (pairGibbsLaw q N β' μ) ≤
644      2 * ∑ i, ∫⁻ x, ProbabilityTheory.evariance (fun z => F (Function.update x i z))
645        (External.siteKernel μ (pairWeight q N β' μ) i x) ∂(pairGibbsLaw q N β' μ : Measure (Config d N)) := by
646  obtain ⟨hK0, hKt⟩ := h.partition_pos_lt ha hβ hN
647  set K := pairPartition q N β' μ with hK
648  set W := pairWeight q N β' μ with hWdef
649  have hW : Measurable W := h.measurable_pairWeight N β'
650  have hW' : Measurable (fun X => W X / K) := by
651    simpa [div_eq_mul_inv] using hW.mul_const K⁻¹
652  have hpos : ∀ X, 0 < W X / K := fun X =>
653    ENNReal.div_pos (ENNReal.ofReal_pos.2 (Real.exp_pos _)).ne' hKt
654  have hbd0 : ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (β' * N * a)) / K ≠ ⊤ :=
655    (ENNReal.div_lt_top ENNReal.ofReal_ne_top hK0).ne
656  have hbd : ∀ X, W X / K ≤ ((ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (β' * N * a)) / K).toNNReal : ℝ≥0∞) := by
657    intro X
658    rw [ENNReal.coe_toNNReal hbd0]
659    exact ENNReal.div_le_div_right (h.pairWeight_le ha hβ hN X) K
660  have hnorm : ∫⁻ X, W X / K ∂(Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ) = 1 := by
661    simp_rw [div_eq_mul_inv]
662    rw [lintegral_mul_const' _ _ (ENNReal.inv_ne_top.2 hK0)]
663    exact ENNReal.mul_inv_cancel hK0 hKt
664  have := External.wu_resampling_half μ (fun X => W X / K) hW' hpos _ hbd hnorm
665    (fun _ _ => κ / N) (fun _ _ => div_nonneg hκ0 (Nat.cast_nonneg N))
666    (fun i j hij x y hxy S hS => by
667      rw [siteKernel_div_const μ W K hK0 hKt i]
668      exact hcoef i j hij x y hxy S hS)
669    (fun i => (External.row_sum_le_of_uniform hN le_rfl hκ0 i).trans hκ) F hF
670  simp_rw [siteKernel_div_const μ W K hK0 hKt] at this
671  exact this
672
673end Wu
674
675section Lower
676
677set_option linter.unusedSectionVars false
678
679variable {μ : Measure (Torus d)} [IsProbabilityMeasure μ] {a : ℝ} {q : Torus d → Torus d → ℝ}
680
681lemma WeakKernel.integrable_prod (h : WeakKernel μ a q) :
682    Integrable (fun p : Torus d × Torus d => q p.1 p.2) (μ.prod μ) := by
683  refine ⟨h.meas.aestronglyMeasurable, ?_⟩
684  unfold HasFiniteIntegral
685  have : ∀ p : Torus d × Torus d, ‖q p.1 p.2‖ₑ = ENNReal.ofReal (q p.1 p.2) := fun p => by
686    rw [Real.enorm_eq_ofReal (h.nonneg _ _)]
687  simp_rw [this]
688  have hm : Measurable fun p : Torus d × Torus d => ENNReal.ofReal (q p.1 p.2) :=
689    ENNReal.measurable_ofReal.comp h.meas
690  rw [lintegral_prod (fun p : Torus d × Torus d => ENNReal.ofReal (q p.1 p.2)) hm.aemeasurable]
691  calc ∫⁻ x, ∫⁻ y, ENNReal.ofReal (q x y) ∂μ ∂μ ≤ ∫⁻ _x, ENNReal.ofReal a ∂μ :=
692        lintegral_mono fun x => h.row x
693    _ < ⊤ := by simp
694
695lemma WeakKernel.integrable_energy (h : WeakKernel μ a q) (N : ℕ) :
696    Integrable (fun X : Config d N => modulatedEnergy q X μ) (Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ) := by
697  have hI := h.integrable_prod
698  have hpair : ∀ i j : Fin N, i ≠ j →
699      Integrable (fun X : Config d N => q (X i) (X j)) (Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ) :=
700    fun i j hij => integrable_pair_comp μ hij (H := fun p => q p.1 p.2) hI
701  have hconv_int : Integrable (fun x => ∫ y, q x y ∂μ) μ := hI.integral_prod_left
702  have h1 : ∀ i : Fin N, Integrable (fun X : Config d N => ∫ y, q (X i) y ∂μ)
703      (Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ) := fun i =>
704    integrable_comp_eval (μ := fun _ : Fin N => μ) (i := i) hconv_int
705  have hT1 : Integrable (fun X : Config d N => ∑ i, ∑ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, q (X i) (X j))
706      (Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ) :=
707    integrable_finsetSum _ fun i _ => integrable_finsetSum _ fun j hj =>
708      hpair i j (Finset.ne_of_mem_erase hj).symm
709  have hT2 : Integrable (fun X : Config d N => ∑ i, ∫ y, q (X i) y ∂μ)
710      (Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ) := integrable_finsetSum _ fun i _ => h1 i
711  unfold modulatedEnergy
712  exact ((hT1.const_mul _).sub (hT2.const_mul _)).add (integrable_const _)
713
714lemma WeakKernel.integral_energy (h : WeakKernel μ a q) {N : ℕ} (hN : 1 ≤ N) :
715    ∫ X : Config d N, modulatedEnergy q X μ ∂(Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ) =
716      -(1 / (2 * N)) * ∫ p : Torus d × Torus d, q p.1 p.2 ∂(μ.prod μ) := by
717  have hI := h.integrable_prod
718  set G := ∫ p : Torus d × Torus d, q p.1 p.2 ∂(μ.prod μ) with hG
719  have hN' : (N : ℝ) ≠ 0 := by positivity
720  have hpair : ∀ i j : Fin N, i ≠ j →
721      Integrable (fun X : Config d N => q (X i) (X j)) (Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ) :=
722    fun i j hij => integrable_pair_comp μ hij (H := fun p => q p.1 p.2) hI
723  have hpair' : ∀ i j : Fin N, i ≠ j →
724      ∫ X : Config d N, q (X i) (X j) ∂(Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ) = G :=
725    fun i j hij => integral_pair_comp μ hij (H := fun p => q p.1 p.2) hI
726  have hconv_int : Integrable (fun x => ∫ y, q x y ∂μ) μ := hI.integral_prod_left
727  have hconv_mean : ∫ x, ∫ y, q x y ∂μ ∂μ = G := by
728    rw [hG, integral_prod _ hI]
729  have h1 : ∀ i : Fin N, Integrable (fun X : Config d N => ∫ y, q (X i) y ∂μ)
730      (Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ) := fun i =>
731    integrable_comp_eval (μ := fun _ : Fin N => μ) (i := i) hconv_int
732  have h1' : ∀ i : Fin N, ∫ X : Config d N, ∫ y, q (X i) y ∂μ ∂(Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ) = G :=
733    fun i => by
734      have := integral_comp_eval (μ := fun _ : Fin N => μ) (i := i) hconv_int.aestronglyMeasurable
735      exact this.trans hconv_mean
736  have hT1 : Integrable (fun X : Config d N => ∑ i, ∑ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, q (X i) (X j))
737      (Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ) :=
738    integrable_finsetSum _ fun i _ => integrable_finsetSum _ fun j hj =>
739      hpair i j (Finset.ne_of_mem_erase hj).symm
740  have hT2 : Integrable (fun X : Config d N => ∑ i, ∫ y, q (X i) y ∂μ)
741      (Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ) := integrable_finsetSum _ fun i _ => h1 i
742  have E1 : ∫ X : Config d N, ∑ i, ∑ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, q (X i) (X j)
743      ∂(Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ) = (N : ℝ) * ((N : ℝ) - 1) * G := by
744    rw [integral_finsetSum _ fun i _ => integrable_finsetSum _ fun j hj =>
745      hpair i j (Finset.ne_of_mem_erase hj).symm]
746    have : ∀ i : Fin N, ∫ X : Config d N, ∑ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, q (X i) (X j)
747        ∂(Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ) = ((N : ℝ) - 1) * G := by
748      intro i
749      rw [integral_finsetSum _ fun j hj => hpair i j (Finset.ne_of_mem_erase hj).symm]
750      rw [Finset.sum_congr rfl fun j hj => hpair' i j (Finset.ne_of_mem_erase hj).symm]
751      rw [Finset.sum_const, Finset.card_erase_of_mem (Finset.mem_univ i)]
752      simp only [Finset.card_univ, Fintype.card_fin, nsmul_eq_mul]
753      rw [Nat.cast_sub hN]; simp
754    simp_rw [this]
755    simp only [Finset.sum_const, Finset.card_univ, Fintype.card_fin, nsmul_eq_mul]
756    ring
757  have E2 : ∫ X : Config d N, ∑ i, ∫ y, q (X i) y ∂μ ∂(Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ) = N * G := by
758    rw [integral_finsetSum _ fun i _ => h1 i]
759    simp_rw [h1']
760    simp
761  unfold modulatedEnergy
762  rw [integral_add, integral_sub, integral_const_mul, integral_const_mul, E1, E2, integral_const]
763  · have hμ : (Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ).real Set.univ = 1 := by simp [Measure.real]
764    rw [hμ, one_smul]
765    have : ∫ x, ∫ y, (fun x y => q x y) x y ∂μ ∂μ = G := hconv_mean
766    rw [this]
767    field_simp
768    ring
769  all_goals first
770    | exact (hT1.const_mul _)
771    | exact (hT2.const_mul _)
772    | exact ((hT1.const_mul _).sub (hT2.const_mul _))
773    | exact integrable_const _
774
775lemma WeakKernel.one_le_partition (h : WeakKernel μ a q) (ha : 0 ≤ a) {β' : ℝ} (hβ : 0 ≤ β')
776    {N : ℕ} (hN : 0 < N) : 1 ≤ pairPartition q N β' μ := by
777  set t : Config d N → ℝ := fun X => -β' * N * modulatedEnergy q X μ with ht
778  have hN' : (0 : ℝ) < N := by exact_mod_cast hN
779  have it : Integrable t (Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ) :=
780    (h.integrable_energy N).const_mul _
781  have iet : Integrable (fun X => Real.exp (t X)) (Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ) := by
782    refine Integrable.of_bound (Real.continuous_exp.comp_aestronglyMeasurable it.1)
783      (Real.exp (β' * N * a)) (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun X => ?_)
784    rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_pos (Real.exp_pos _)]
785    refine Real.exp_le_exp.2 ?_
786    have := h.pairWeight_le ha hβ hN X
787    unfold pairWeight at this
788    exact (ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal_iff (Real.exp_pos _).le).1 this |> Real.exp_le_exp.1
789  have hK : pairPartition q N β' μ = ENNReal.ofReal (∫ X, Real.exp (t X) ∂(Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ)) := by
790    unfold pairPartition pairWeight
791    rw [ofReal_integral_eq_lintegral_ofReal iet (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun X => (Real.exp_pos _).le)]
792  rw [hK]
793  refine ENNReal.one_le_ofReal.2 ?_
794  have h1 : ∫ X, (1 + t X) ∂(Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ) ≤ ∫ X, Real.exp (t X) ∂(Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ) :=
795    integral_mono ((integrable_const _).add it) iet fun X => by
796      have := Real.add_one_le_exp (t X); linarith
797  refine le_trans ?_ h1
798  rw [integral_add (integrable_const _) it]
799  have e : ∫ X, t X ∂(Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ)
800      = -β' * N * (-(1 / (2 * N)) * ∫ p : Torus d × Torus d, q p.1 p.2 ∂(μ.prod μ)) := by
801    rw [ht]; simp only
802    rw [integral_const_mul, h.integral_energy hN]
803  have hG : 0 ≤ ∫ p : Torus d × Torus d, q p.1 p.2 ∂(μ.prod μ) :=
804    integral_nonneg fun p => h.nonneg _ _
805  have e2 : -β' * N * (-(1 / (2 * N)) * ∫ p : Torus d × Torus d, q p.1 p.2 ∂(μ.prod μ))
806      = β' / 2 * ∫ p : Torus d × Torus d, q p.1 p.2 ∂(μ.prod μ) := by
807    field_simp
808  have : 0 ≤ β' / 2 * ∫ p : Torus d × Torus d, q p.1 p.2 ∂(μ.prod μ) :=
809    mul_nonneg (by positivity) hG
810  rw [e, e2]
811  simp
812  linarith
813
814end Lower
815
816end WeakRef
817
818
819lemma deltaN_nonneg (s : ℝ) {N : ℕ} (hN : 1 ≤ N) : 0 ≤ deltaN d s N := by
820  have hN' : (1 : ℝ) ≤ N := by exact_mod_cast hN
821  unfold deltaN
822  split_ifs with h1 h2
823  · positivity
824  · have : 0 ≤ Real.log (Real.exp 1 * N) := by
825      apply Real.log_nonneg
826      have := Real.add_one_le_exp (1 : ℝ)
827      nlinarith [Real.exp_pos 1]
828    positivity
829  · positivity
830
831end HRS

841variable {E : Type*} [MeasurableSpace E] {μ : Measure E} [IsProbabilityMeasure μ]
842  {B : Type*} [Fintype B]

844/-- The law of coordinate `i` of the `(n+1)`-particle pinned ensemble has density `pinWeight`. -/
845theorem marginal_self_succ (V : WeakPotential μ) {lam : ℝ} (hlam : 0 ≤ lam) (a : B → E) {n : ℕ}
846    (i : Fin (n + 1)) :
847    (pinnedLaw V lam (n + 1) a).map (fun y => y i) = μ.withDensity (pinWeight V lam a n) := by
848  have hψ := isField_pinField V hlam a
849  have hψx : ∀ x, IsField μ (pinField V lam (pinsWith a x)) V.M
850      (V.M' + lam * Fintype.card (Option B) * V.γ) := fun x => isField_pinField V hlam _
851  ext A hA
852  rw [Measure.map_apply (measurable_pi_apply _) hA]
853  have hS : MeasurableSet ((fun y : Fin (n + 1) → E => y i) ⁻¹' A) :=
854    measurable_pi_apply _ hA
855  change (fieldLaw V (pinField V lam a) lam (n + 1)) _ = _
856  unfold fieldLaw
857  rw [withDensity_apply _ hS, ← lintegral_indicator hS, withDensity_apply _ hA]
858  set e := MeasurableEquiv.piFinSuccAbove (fun _ : Fin (n + 1) => E) i with he
859  have hmp := measurePreserving_piFinSuccAbove (fun _ : Fin (n + 1) => μ) i
860  have hsymm : ∀ (x : E) (y : Fin n → E), e.symm (x, y) = insertAt i x y := fun _ _ => rfl
861  set h : (Fin (n + 1) → E) → ℝ≥0∞ := fun y =>
862    ((fun y : Fin (n + 1) → E => y i) ⁻¹' A).indicator
863      (fieldDensity V (pinField V lam a) lam (n + 1)) y with hh
864  have hhm : Measurable h :=
865    (measurable_fieldDensity hψ.measurable V lam (n + 1)).indicator hS
866  have step1 : ∫⁻ y, h y ∂(Measure.pi fun _ : Fin (n + 1) => μ)
867      = ∫⁻ p, h (e.symm p) ∂(μ.prod (Measure.pi fun _ : Fin n => μ)) := by
868    have := hmp.lintegral_comp_emb e.measurableEmbedding (fun p => h (e.symm p))
869    rw [← this]
870    exact lintegral_congr fun y => by rw [e.symm_apply_apply]
871  rw [step1, lintegral_prod (fun p => h (e.symm p)) (hhm.comp e.symm.measurable).aemeasurable,
872    ← lintegral_indicator hA]
873  refine lintegral_congr fun x => ?_
874  simp only [hsymm]
875  by_cases hx : x ∈ A
876  · have h1 : ∀ y : Fin n → E, h (insertAt i x y)
877        = (ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (pinField V lam a x))
878            * (fieldNorm V (pinField V lam a) lam (n + 1))⁻¹)
879            * fieldWeight V (pinField V lam (pinsWith a x)) lam y := by
880      intro y
881      have hm : insertAt i x y ∈ (fun y : Fin (n + 1) → E => y i) ⁻¹' A := by simp [hx]
882      simp only [hh]
883      rw [Set.indicator_of_mem hm]
884      unfold fieldDensity
885      rw [fieldWeight_insertAt]; ring
886    simp_rw [h1]
887    rw [lintegral_const_mul _ (measurable_fieldWeight (hψx x).measurable V lam), Set.indicator_of_mem hx]
888    unfold pinWeight fieldNorm
889    ring
890  · have h1 : ∀ y : Fin n → E, h (insertAt i x y) = 0 := by
891      intro y
892      have hm : insertAt i x y ∉ (fun y : Fin (n + 1) → E => y i) ⁻¹' A := by simp [hx]
893      simp only [hh]
894      rw [Set.indicator_of_notMem hm]
895    simp_rw [h1]
896    simp [Set.indicator_of_notMem hx]
897
898
899/-- Cavity factor `G(x) = E_{cavity}[exp(-lam ∑_k q(x, y_k))]`. -/
900def cavG (V : WeakPotential μ) (lam : ℝ) (a : B → E) (n : ℕ) (x : E) : ℝ :=
901  ∫ y, Real.exp (-lam * ∑ k, V.q x (y k)) ∂(pinnedLaw V lam n a)
902
903lemma fieldWeight_pin_eq (V : WeakPotential μ) (lam : ℝ) (a : B → E) (x : E) {n : ℕ}
904    (y : Fin n → E) :
905    fieldWeight V (pinField V lam (pinsWith a x)) lam y
906      = fieldWeight V (pinField V lam a) lam y
907          * ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (-lam * ∑ k, V.q x (y k))) := by
908  unfold fieldWeight
909  rw [← ENNReal.ofReal_mul (Real.exp_pos _).le, ← Real.exp_add]
910  congr 2
911  simp_rw [pinField_pinsWith, Finset.sum_sub_distrib, ← Finset.mul_sum]
912  have h3 : ∑ k, V.q (y k) x = ∑ k, V.q x (y k) :=
913    Finset.sum_congr rfl fun k _ => V.q_symm _ _
914  rw [h3]; ring
915
916lemma measurable_sumq' (V : WeakPotential μ) {n : ℕ} (x : E) :
917    Measurable fun y : Fin n → E => ∑ k, V.q x (y k) := by
918  refine Finset.measurable_sum _ fun k _ => ?_
919  exact (V.measurable_q.comp (measurable_const.prodMk (measurable_pi_apply k)))
920
921lemma cavG_nonneg (V : WeakPotential μ) (lam : ℝ) (a : B → E) (n : ℕ) (x : E) :
922    0 ≤ cavG V lam a n x := integral_nonneg fun _ => (Real.exp_pos _).le
923
924lemma cavG_le_one (V : WeakPotential μ) {lam : ℝ} (hlam : 0 ≤ lam) (a : B → E) (n : ℕ) (x : E) :
925    cavG V lam a n x ≤ 1 := by
926  have := isProb_pinnedLaw V hlam n a
927  unfold cavG
928  calc ∫ y, Real.exp (-lam * ∑ k, V.q x (y k)) ∂(pinnedLaw V lam n a)
929      ≤ ∫ _y, (1 : ℝ) ∂(pinnedLaw V lam n a) := by
930        refine integral_mono_of_nonneg (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun _ => (Real.exp_pos _).le)
931          (integrable_const _) (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => ?_)
932        refine Real.exp_le_one_iff.2 ?_
933        have : 0 ≤ lam * ∑ k, V.q x (y k) :=
934          mul_nonneg hlam (Finset.sum_nonneg fun k _ => V.q_nonneg _ _)
935        linarith
936    _ = 1 := by simp
937
938lemma measurable_cavG (V : WeakPotential μ) {lam : ℝ} (hlam : 0 ≤ lam) (a : B → E) (n : ℕ) :
939    Measurable (cavG V lam a n) := by
940  have := isProb_pinnedLaw V hlam n a
941  unfold cavG
942  have hm : Measurable fun p : E × (Fin n → E) => Real.exp (-lam * ∑ k, V.q p.1 (p.2 k)) := by
943    refine Real.measurable_exp.comp ((measurable_const.mul ?_))
944    refine Finset.measurable_sum _ fun k _ => ?_
945    exact V.measurable_q.comp (measurable_fst.prodMk ((measurable_pi_apply k).comp measurable_snd))
946  exact (hm.stronglyMeasurable.integral_prod_right').measurable
947
948lemma fieldNorm_pin_eq (V : WeakPotential μ) {lam : ℝ} (hlam : 0 ≤ lam) (a : B → E) (x : E)
949    (n : ℕ) :
950    fieldNorm V (pinField V lam (pinsWith a x)) lam n
951      = fieldNorm V (pinField V lam a) lam n
952          * ENNReal.ofReal (cavG V lam a n x) := by
953  have hψ := isField_pinField V hlam a
954  unfold fieldNorm
955  simp_rw [fieldWeight_pin_eq]
956  have hmeas : Measurable fun y : Fin n → E => ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (-lam * ∑ k, V.q x (y k))) :=
957    ENNReal.measurable_ofReal.comp (Real.measurable_exp.comp
958      (measurable_const.mul (measurable_sumq' V x)))
959  have h1 : ∫⁻ y, fieldWeight V (pinField V lam a) lam y
960        * ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (-lam * ∑ k, V.q x (y k))) ∂(Measure.pi fun _ : Fin n => μ)
961      = fieldNorm V (pinField V lam a) lam n *
962        ∫⁻ y, ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (-lam * ∑ k, V.q x (y k))) ∂(pinnedLaw V lam n a) := by
963    change _ = _ * ∫⁻ y, _ ∂(fieldLaw V (pinField V lam a) lam n)
964    unfold fieldLaw
965    rw [lintegral_withDensity_eq_lintegral_mul _ (measurable_fieldDensity hψ.measurable V lam n) hmeas]
966    unfold fieldDensity
967    have hW := measurable_fieldWeight hψ.measurable V lam (n := n)
968    have e1 : ∀ y : Fin n → E, ((fun y : Fin n → E => fieldWeight V (pinField V lam a) lam y *
969          (fieldNorm V (pinField V lam a) lam n)⁻¹) * fun y : Fin n → E =>
970            ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (-lam * ∑ k, V.q x (y k)))) y
971        = (fieldNorm V (pinField V lam a) lam n)⁻¹ *
972          (fieldWeight V (pinField V lam a) lam y *
973            ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (-lam * ∑ k, V.q x (y k)))) := fun y => by
974      simp only [Pi.mul_apply]; ring
975    rw [lintegral_congr e1]
976    have hm2 : Measurable fun y : Fin n → E => fieldWeight V (pinField V lam a) lam y *
977        ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (-lam * ∑ k, V.q x (y k))) := hW.mul hmeas
978    rw [lintegral_const_mul _ hm2, ← mul_assoc,
979      ENNReal.mul_inv_cancel (fieldNorm_pos hψ.measurable V lam n).ne'
980        (fieldNorm_lt_top hψ hlam n).ne, one_mul]
981  rw [h1]
982  congr 1
983  have := isProb_pinnedLaw V hlam n a
984  unfold cavG
985  rw [ofReal_integral_eq_lintegral_ofReal]
986  · refine Integrable.of_bound ?_ 1 (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => ?_)
987    · exact (Real.measurable_exp.comp (measurable_const.mul (measurable_sumq' V x))).aestronglyMeasurable
988    · rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_pos (Real.exp_pos _)]
989      refine Real.exp_le_one_iff.2 ?_
990      have : 0 ≤ lam * ∑ k, V.q x (y k) :=
991        mul_nonneg hlam (Finset.sum_nonneg fun k _ => V.q_nonneg _ _)
992      linarith
993  · exact Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun _ => (Real.exp_pos _).le
994
995
996/-- Cavity formula: the marginal of coordinate `i` of the `(n+1)`-particle ensemble has `μ`-density
997`c · exp(ψ(x)) · G(x)`, with `ψ = pinField a` and `G = cavG` the cavity Laplace functional. -/
998theorem marginal_cavity (V : WeakPotential μ) {lam : ℝ} (hlam : 0 ≤ lam) (a : B → E) {n : ℕ}
999    (i : Fin (n + 1)) :
1000    ∃ c : ℝ, 0 < c ∧ (pinnedLaw V lam (n + 1) a).map (fun y => y i)
1001      = μ.withDensity (fun x => ENNReal.ofReal (c * (Real.exp (pinField V lam a x) * cavG V lam a n x))) := by
1002  have hψ := isField_pinField V hlam a
1003  have hn := fieldNorm_pos hψ.measurable V (lam := lam) (ψ := pinField V lam a) n
1004  have hn1 := fieldNorm_pos hψ.measurable V (lam := lam) (ψ := pinField V lam a) (n + 1)
1005  have hnt := fieldNorm_lt_top (V := V) hψ hlam n
1006  have hn1t := fieldNorm_lt_top (V := V) hψ hlam (n + 1)
1007  set c : ℝ≥0∞ := fieldNorm V (pinField V lam a) lam n * (fieldNorm V (pinField V lam a) lam (n + 1))⁻¹
1008    with hc
1009  have hc0 : c ≠ 0 := mul_ne_zero hn.ne' (ENNReal.inv_ne_zero.2 hn1t.ne)
1010  have hct : c ≠ ∞ := ENNReal.mul_ne_top hnt.ne (ENNReal.inv_ne_top.2 hn1.ne')
1011  refine ⟨c.toReal, ENNReal.toReal_pos hc0 hct, ?_⟩
1012  rw [marginal_self_succ V hlam a i]
1013  congr 1
1014  funext x
1015  unfold pinWeight
1016  rw [fieldNorm_pin_eq V hlam a x n, ENNReal.ofReal_mul ENNReal.toReal_nonneg,
1017    ENNReal.ofReal_toReal hct, ENNReal.ofReal_mul (Real.exp_pos _).le]
1018  simp only [hc]
1019  ring
1020

1022/-- Marginal density bound: every one-coordinate marginal of the pinned ensemble is `≤ C μ`,
1023`C = exp(M + M' + β γ)` (stationarity + `weak_siteKernel_le`). -/
1024theorem marginal_le (V : WeakPotential μ) {β : ℝ} (hβ : 0 ≤ β) {N n : ℕ} (hN0 : 0 < N)
1025    (hN : n + Fintype.card B ≤ N) (a : B → E) (i : Fin n) :
1026    (pinnedLaw V (β / N) n a).map (fun y => y i)
1027      ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (V.M + V.M' + β * V.γ)) • μ := by
1028  have hlam : 0 ≤ β / N := div_nonneg hβ (Nat.cast_nonneg N)
1029  have hψ := isField_pinField V hlam a
1030  have hP := isProb_pinnedLaw V hlam n a
1031  refine Measure.le_iff.2 fun A hA => ?_
1032  rw [Measure.map_apply (measurable_pi_apply _) hA]
1033  have hS : MeasurableSet ((fun y : Fin n → E => y i) ⁻¹' A) := measurable_pi_apply _ hA
1034  have hinv := invariant_density (μ := μ) (W := pinnedDensity V (β / N) n a)
1035    (measurable_fieldDensity hψ.measurable V _ n) (fieldDensity_pos hψ hlam n) (fieldDensity_le hψ hlam n) i
1036    (φ := ((fun y : Fin n → E => y i) ⁻¹' A).indicator 1) (measurable_one.indicator hS)
1037  rw [← pinnedLaw_eq] at hinv
1038  have h1 : ∀ x : Fin n → E, ∫⁻ z, ((fun y : Fin n → E => y i) ⁻¹' A).indicator (1 : (Fin n → E) → ℝ≥0∞)
1039      (update x i z) ∂(siteKernel μ (pinnedDensity V (β / N) n a) i x)
1040      = siteKernel μ (pinnedDensity V (β / N) n a) i x A := by
1041    intro x
1042    rw [← lintegral_indicator_one hA]
1043    refine lintegral_congr fun z => ?_
1044    by_cases hz : z ∈ A
1045    · simp [Set.indicator_apply, hz]
1046    · simp [Set.indicator_apply, hz]
1047  simp_rw [h1] at hinv
1048  calc (pinnedLaw V (β / N) n a) ((fun y : Fin n → E => y i) ⁻¹' A)
1049      = ∫⁻ x, ((fun y : Fin n → E => y i) ⁻¹' A).indicator (1 : (Fin n → E) → ℝ≥0∞) x
1050          ∂(pinnedLaw V (β / N) n a) := by rw [lintegral_indicator_one hS]
1051    _ = ∫⁻ x, siteKernel μ (pinnedDensity V (β / N) n a) i x A ∂(pinnedLaw V (β / N) n a) := hinv.symm
1052    _ ≤ ∫⁻ _x, (ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (V.M + V.M' + β * V.γ)) • μ) A ∂(pinnedLaw V (β / N) n a) :=
1053        lintegral_mono fun x => weak_siteKernel_le V hβ hN0 hN a i x A
1054    _ = _ := by rw [lintegral_const, measure_univ, mul_one]

1057/-- `e^{-x} ≤ e^{-m}(1-(x-m)) + (x-m)²/2` for `x, m ≥ 0`. -/
1058lemma exp_neg_le_quad {x m : ℝ} (hx : 0 ≤ x) (hm : 0 ≤ m) :
1059    Real.exp (-x) ≤ Real.exp (-m) * (1 - (x - m)) + (x - m) ^ 2 / 2 := by
1060  set φ : ℝ → ℝ := fun y => (y - m) ^ 2 / 2 - Real.exp (-y) + Real.exp (-m) * (1 - (y - m)) with hφ
1061  have hφ' : ∀ y, HasDerivAt φ ((y - m) + Real.exp (-y) - Real.exp (-m)) y := by
1062    intro y
1063    have h1 : HasDerivAt (fun y : ℝ => (y - m) ^ 2 / 2) (y - m) y := by
1064      have := ((hasDerivAt_id y).sub_const m).pow 2
1065      convert this.div_const 2 using 1
1066      all_goals (try simp)
1067      all_goals (try ring)
1068    have h2 : HasDerivAt (fun y : ℝ => Real.exp (-y)) (-Real.exp (-y)) y := by
1069      have := (hasDerivAt_id y).neg.exp
1070      simpa using this
1071    have h3 : HasDerivAt (fun y : ℝ => Real.exp (-m) * (1 - (y - m))) (-Real.exp (-m)) y := by
1072      have := (((hasDerivAt_id y).sub_const m).const_sub 1).const_mul (Real.exp (-m))
1073      simpa using this
1074    convert (h1.sub h2).add h3 using 1
1075    ring
1076  set ψ : ℝ → ℝ := fun y => (y - m) + Real.exp (-y) - Real.exp (-m) with hψ
1077  have hψ' : ∀ y, HasDerivAt ψ (1 - Real.exp (-y)) y := by
1078    intro y
1079    have h2 : HasDerivAt (fun y : ℝ => Real.exp (-y)) (-Real.exp (-y)) y := by
1080      have := (hasDerivAt_id y).neg.exp
1081      simpa using this
1082    convert (((hasDerivAt_id y).sub_const m).add h2).sub_const (Real.exp (-m)) using 1
1083    funext z; simp [hψ]
1084  have hψm : ψ m = 0 := by simp [hψ]
1085  have hψmono : MonotoneOn ψ (Set.Ici 0) := by
1086    refine monotoneOn_of_deriv_nonneg (convex_Ici 0) (fun y _ => (hψ' y).continuousAt.continuousWithinAt)
1087      (fun y _ => (hψ' y).differentiableAt.differentiableWithinAt) fun y hy => ?_
1088    rw [(hψ' y).deriv]
1089    have hy' : 0 < y := by simpa using hy
1090    have : Real.exp (-y) ≤ 1 := Real.exp_le_one_iff.2 (by linarith)
1091    linarith
1092  have hφm : φ m = 0 := by simp [hφ]
1093  rcases le_total m x with h | h
1094  · have hmono : MonotoneOn φ (Set.Ici m) := by
1095      refine monotoneOn_of_deriv_nonneg (convex_Ici m) (fun y _ => (hφ' y).continuousAt.continuousWithinAt)
1096        (fun y _ => (hφ' y).differentiableAt.differentiableWithinAt) fun y hy => ?_
1097      rw [(hφ' y).deriv]
1098      have hy' : m < y := by simpa using hy
1099      have := hψmono (show (0:ℝ) ∈ Set.Ici 0 from le_refl 0 |> fun _ => by simp) (show m ∈ Set.Ici 0 from hm) hm
1100      have h5 := hψmono (show m ∈ Set.Ici (0:ℝ) from hm) (show y ∈ Set.Ici (0:ℝ) from le_of_lt (lt_of_le_of_lt hm hy')) hy'.le
1101      simp only [hψm, hψ] at h5
1102      linarith
1103    have := hmono (Set.mem_Ici.2 le_rfl) (Set.mem_Ici.2 h) h
1104    have e1 : φ x = (x - m) ^ 2 / 2 - Real.exp (-x) + Real.exp (-m) * (1 - (x - m)) := rfl
1105    linarith
1106  · have hanti : AntitoneOn φ (Set.Icc 0 m) := by
1107      refine antitoneOn_of_deriv_nonpos (convex_Icc 0 m) (fun y _ => (hφ' y).continuousAt.continuousWithinAt)
1108        (fun y _ => (hφ' y).differentiableAt.differentiableWithinAt) fun y hy => ?_
1109      rw [(hφ' y).deriv]
1110      have hy' : 0 < y ∧ y < m := by simpa using hy
1111      have h5 := hψmono (show y ∈ Set.Ici (0:ℝ) from hy'.1.le) (show m ∈ Set.Ici (0:ℝ) from hm) hy'.2.le
1112      simp only [hψm, hψ] at h5
1113      linarith
1114    have := hanti (show x ∈ Set.Icc 0 m from ⟨hx, h⟩) (show m ∈ Set.Icc 0 m from ⟨hm, le_rfl⟩) h
1115    have e1 : φ x = (x - m) ^ 2 / 2 - Real.exp (-x) + Real.exp (-m) * (1 - (x - m)) := rfl
1116    linarith

1118lemma laplace_bounds {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] (P : Measure Ω) [IsProbabilityMeasure P]
1119    {X : Ω → ℝ} (hX : Measurable X) (h0 : ∀ ω, 0 ≤ X ω) {K : ℝ} (hK : ∀ ω, X ω ≤ K) :
1120    Real.exp (-∫ ω, X ω ∂P) ≤ ∫ ω, Real.exp (-X ω) ∂P ∧
1121      ∫ ω, Real.exp (-X ω) ∂P ≤ Real.exp (-∫ ω, X ω ∂P) + variance X P / 2 := by
1122  have hint : Integrable X P :=
1123    Integrable.of_bound hX.aestronglyMeasurable K (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun ω => by
1124      rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_nonneg (h0 ω)]; exact hK ω)
1125  have hint2 : Integrable (fun ω => Real.exp (-X ω)) P :=
1126    Integrable.of_bound (Real.measurable_exp.comp hX.neg).aestronglyMeasurable 1
1127      (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun ω => by
1128        rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_pos (Real.exp_pos _)]
1129        exact Real.exp_le_one_iff.2 (by linarith [h0 ω]))
1130  set m := ∫ ω, X ω ∂P with hm
1131  have hm0 : 0 ≤ m := integral_nonneg h0
1132  have hlin : ∫ ω, Real.exp (-m) * (1 - (X ω - m)) ∂P = Real.exp (-m) := by
1133    have h1 : Integrable (fun ω => X ω - m) P := hint.sub (integrable_const m)
1134    rw [integral_const_mul, integral_sub (f := fun _ => (1:ℝ)) (g := fun ω => X ω - m)
1135      (integrable_const _) h1, integral_sub (f := X) (g := fun _ => m) hint (integrable_const _)]
1136    simp [hm]
1137  have hI : Integrable (fun ω => Real.exp (-m) * (1 - (X ω - m))) P :=
1138    ((integrable_const (1:ℝ)).sub (hint.sub (integrable_const m))).const_mul _
1139  have hvar : variance X P = ∫ ω, (X ω - m) ^ 2 ∂P := variance_eq_integral hX.aemeasurable
1140  have hsq : Integrable (fun ω => (X ω - m) ^ 2) P := by
1141    refine Integrable.of_bound ((hX.sub_const m).pow_const 2).aestronglyMeasurable (K ^ 2 + m ^ 2 + 2 * |K| * m + 1)
1142      (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun ω => ?_)
1143    rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_nonneg (sq_nonneg _)]
1144    have h1 := h0 ω; have h2 := hK ω
1145    have : |X ω - m| ≤ |K| + m := by
1146      rw [abs_le]; constructor <;> nlinarith [le_abs_self K, neg_abs_le K]
1147    have h3 : (X ω - m) ^ 2 ≤ (|K| + m) ^ 2 := by
1148      rw [← sq_abs]; exact pow_le_pow_left₀ (abs_nonneg _) this 2
1149    nlinarith [sq_abs K, abs_nonneg K]
1150  constructor
1151  · calc Real.exp (-m) = ∫ ω, Real.exp (-m) * (1 - (X ω - m)) ∂P := hlin.symm
1152      _ ≤ ∫ ω, Real.exp (-X ω) ∂P := by
1153        refine integral_mono hI hint2 fun ω => ?_
1154        have := Real.add_one_le_exp (-(X ω - m))
1155        calc Real.exp (-m) * (1 - (X ω - m)) ≤ Real.exp (-m) * Real.exp (-(X ω - m)) :=
1156              mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (by linarith) (Real.exp_pos _).le
1157          _ = Real.exp (-X ω) := by rw [← Real.exp_add]; ring_nf
1158  · calc ∫ ω, Real.exp (-X ω) ∂P
1159        ≤ ∫ ω, (Real.exp (-m) * (1 - (X ω - m)) + (X ω - m) ^ 2 / 2) ∂P := by
1160          refine integral_mono hint2 ?_ fun ω => exp_neg_le_quad (h0 ω) hm0
1161          exact hI.add (hsq.div_const 2)
1162      _ = Real.exp (-m) + variance X P / 2 := by
1163          rw [integral_add hI (hsq.div_const 2), hlin, integral_div, hvar]
1164
1165lemma abs_exp_neg_sub_le' {s t : ℝ} (hs : 0 ≤ s) (ht : 0 ≤ t) :
1166    |Real.exp (-s) - Real.exp (-t)| ≤ |s - t| := by
1167  have key : ∀ s t : ℝ, 0 ≤ s → s ≤ t → Real.exp (-s) - Real.exp (-t) ≤ t - s := by
1168    intro s t hs hst
1169    have h1 : Real.exp (-s) ≤ 1 := by rw [Real.exp_le_one_iff]; linarith
1170    have h2 : -(t - s) + 1 ≤ Real.exp (-(t - s)) := Real.add_one_le_exp _
1171    have h3 : Real.exp (-t) = Real.exp (-s) * Real.exp (-(t - s)) := by
1172      rw [← Real.exp_add]; congr 1; ring
1173    have h4 : 0 ≤ Real.exp (-s) := (Real.exp_pos _).le
1174    have : Real.exp (-s) - Real.exp (-t) = Real.exp (-s) * (1 - Real.exp (-(t - s))) := by
1175      rw [h3]; ring
1176    rw [this]
1177    nlinarith
1178  rcases le_total s t with h | h
1179  · have := key s t hs h
1180    have h5 : Real.exp (-t) ≤ Real.exp (-s) := Real.exp_le_exp.2 (by linarith)
1181    rw [abs_of_nonneg (by linarith), abs_of_nonpos (by linarith)]; linarith
1182  · have := key t s ht h
1183    have h5 : Real.exp (-s) ≤ Real.exp (-t) := Real.exp_le_exp.2 (by linarith)
1184    rw [abs_of_nonpos (by linarith), abs_of_nonneg (by linarith)]; linarith
1185
1186/-- Laplace residual for a sum of nonnegative integrable summands with cut level `L`. -/
1187lemma laplace_sum {Ω ι : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] (P : Measure Ω) [IsProbabilityMeasure P]
1188    [Fintype ι] (u : ι → Ω → ℝ) (hm : ∀ k, Measurable (u k)) (h0 : ∀ k ω, 0 ≤ u k ω)
1189    (hi : ∀ k, Integrable (u k) P) {lam L : ℝ} (hlam : 0 ≤ lam) (hL : 0 ≤ L) :
1190    |∫ ω, Real.exp (-(lam * ∑ k, u k ω)) ∂P - Real.exp (-(lam * ∑ k, ∫ ω, u k ω ∂P))|
1191      ≤ 2 * (lam * ∑ k, ∫ ω, max (u k ω - L) 0 ∂P)
1192        + lam ^ 2 / 2 * variance (fun ω => ∑ k, min (u k ω) L) P := by
1193  set U : Ω → ℝ := fun ω => lam * ∑ k, u k ω with hU
1194  set X : Ω → ℝ := fun ω => lam * ∑ k, min (u k ω) L with hX
1195  set Y : Ω → ℝ := fun ω => lam * ∑ k, max (u k ω - L) 0 with hY
1196  have hUXY : ∀ ω, U ω = X ω + Y ω := fun ω => by
1197    simp only [hU, hX, hY, ← mul_add, ← Finset.sum_add_distrib]
1198    congr 1
1199    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun k _ => ?_
1200    rcases le_total (u k ω) L with h | h
1201    · simp [min_eq_left h, max_eq_right (by linarith : u k ω - L ≤ 0)]
1202    · simp [min_eq_right h, max_eq_left (by linarith : 0 ≤ u k ω - L)]
1203  have hmU : Measurable U := measurable_const.mul (Finset.measurable_sum _ fun k _ => hm k)
1204  have hmX : Measurable X :=
1205    measurable_const.mul (Finset.measurable_sum _ fun k _ => (hm k).min measurable_const)
1206  have hmY : Measurable Y :=
1207    measurable_const.mul (Finset.measurable_sum _ fun k _ => ((hm k).sub_const L).max measurable_const)
1208  have hU0 : ∀ ω, 0 ≤ U ω := fun ω =>
1209    mul_nonneg hlam (Finset.sum_nonneg fun k _ => h0 k ω)
1210  have hX0 : ∀ ω, 0 ≤ X ω := fun ω =>
1211    mul_nonneg hlam (Finset.sum_nonneg fun k _ => le_min (h0 k ω) hL)
1212  have hY0 : ∀ ω, 0 ≤ Y ω := fun ω =>
1213    mul_nonneg hlam (Finset.sum_nonneg fun k _ => le_max_right _ _)
1214  have hXK : ∀ ω, X ω ≤ lam * (Fintype.card ι * L) := fun ω => by
1215    refine mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left ?_ hlam
1216    calc ∑ k, min (u k ω) L ≤ ∑ _k : ι, L := Finset.sum_le_sum fun k _ => min_le_right _ _
1217      _ = _ := by simp
1218  have hiU : Integrable U P := by
1219    simpa [hU] using ((integrable_finset_sum _ fun k _ => hi k).const_mul lam)
1220  have hiX : Integrable X P :=
1221    Integrable.of_bound hmX.aestronglyMeasurable _ (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun ω => by
1222      rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_nonneg (hX0 ω)]; exact hXK ω)
1223  have hiY : Integrable Y P := by
1224    have : Y = fun ω => U ω - X ω := funext fun ω => by rw [hUXY ω]; ring
1225    rw [this]; exact hiU.sub hiX
1226  have hint : ∀ k, ∫ ω, u k ω ∂P = ∫ ω, u k ω ∂P := fun _ => rfl
1227  have hEU : ∫ ω, U ω ∂P = lam * ∑ k, ∫ ω, u k ω ∂P := by
1228    simp only [hU]
1229    rw [integral_const_mul, integral_finset_sum _ fun k _ => hi k]
1230  have hEUXY : ∫ ω, U ω ∂P = ∫ ω, X ω ∂P + ∫ ω, Y ω ∂P := by
1231    rw [← integral_add hiX hiY]
1232    exact integral_congr_ae (Filter.Eventually.of_forall hUXY)
1233  have hEY : ∫ ω, Y ω ∂P = lam * ∑ k, ∫ ω, max (u k ω - L) 0 ∂P := by
1234    simp only [hY]
1235    rw [integral_const_mul, integral_finset_sum]
1236    intro k _
1237    exact ((hi k).sub (integrable_const L)).pos_part
1238  have hEX0 : 0 ≤ ∫ ω, X ω ∂P := integral_nonneg hX0
1239  have hEY0 : 0 ≤ ∫ ω, Y ω ∂P := integral_nonneg hY0
1240  -- the three comparisons
1241  have c1 : |∫ ω, Real.exp (-U ω) ∂P - ∫ ω, Real.exp (-X ω) ∂P| ≤ ∫ ω, Y ω ∂P := by
1242    rw [← integral_sub]
1243    · refine (abs_integral_le_integral_abs).trans ?_
1244      refine integral_mono_of_nonneg (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun _ => abs_nonneg _) hiY
1245        (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun ω => ?_)
1246      refine (abs_exp_neg_sub_le' (hU0 ω) (hX0 ω)).trans ?_
1247      rw [hUXY ω, add_sub_cancel_left, abs_of_nonneg (hY0 ω)]
1248    · exact Integrable.of_bound (Real.measurable_exp.comp hmU.neg).aestronglyMeasurable 1
1249        (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun ω => by
1250          rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_pos (Real.exp_pos _)]
1251          exact Real.exp_le_one_iff.2 (by linarith [hU0 ω]))
1252    · exact Integrable.of_bound (Real.measurable_exp.comp hmX.neg).aestronglyMeasurable 1
1253        (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun ω => by
1254          rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_pos (Real.exp_pos _)]
1255          exact Real.exp_le_one_iff.2 (by linarith [hX0 ω]))
1256  obtain ⟨hl, hu⟩ := laplace_bounds P hmX hX0 hXK
1257  have c3 : |Real.exp (-∫ ω, X ω ∂P) - Real.exp (-∫ ω, U ω ∂P)| ≤ ∫ ω, Y ω ∂P := by
1258    refine (abs_exp_neg_sub_le' hEX0 (integral_nonneg hU0)).trans ?_
1259    rw [hEUXY, sub_add_cancel_left, abs_neg, abs_of_nonneg hEY0]
1260  have hvarX : variance X P = lam ^ 2 * variance (fun ω => ∑ k, min (u k ω) L) P := by
1261    simp only [hX]; exact variance_const_mul _ _ _
1262  rw [← hEU, ← hEY]
1263  have hvarX' : lam ^ 2 / 2 * variance (fun ω => ∑ k, min (u k ω) L) P = variance X P / 2 := by
1264    rw [hvarX]; ring
1265  rw [hvarX']
1266  have t1 := abs_sub_abs_le_abs_sub (∫ ω, Real.exp (-U ω) ∂P) (∫ ω, Real.exp (-X ω) ∂P)
1267  have := abs_add_three (∫ ω, Real.exp (-U ω) ∂P - ∫ ω, Real.exp (-X ω) ∂P)
1268    (∫ ω, Real.exp (-X ω) ∂P - Real.exp (-∫ ω, X ω ∂P))
1269    (Real.exp (-∫ ω, X ω ∂P) - Real.exp (-∫ ω, U ω ∂P))
1270  have e : ∫ ω, Real.exp (-U ω) ∂P - Real.exp (-∫ ω, U ω ∂P)
1271      = (∫ ω, Real.exp (-U ω) ∂P - ∫ ω, Real.exp (-X ω) ∂P)
1272        + (∫ ω, Real.exp (-X ω) ∂P - Real.exp (-∫ ω, X ω ∂P))
1273        + (Real.exp (-∫ ω, X ω ∂P) - Real.exp (-∫ ω, U ω ∂P)) := by ring
1274  rw [e]
1275  have h2 : |∫ ω, Real.exp (-X ω) ∂P - Real.exp (-∫ ω, X ω ∂P)| ≤ variance X P / 2 := by
1276    rw [abs_le]; constructor <;> linarith
1277  linarith
1278

1280lemma integral_le_of_le_smul {ν : Measure E} {D : ℝ} (hD : 0 ≤ D) (hν : ν ≤ ENNReal.ofReal D • μ)
1281    {g : E → ℝ} (hg0 : ∀ z, 0 ≤ g z) (hgi : Integrable g μ) :
1282    Integrable g ν ∧ ∫ z, g z ∂ν ≤ D * ∫ z, g z ∂μ := by
1283  have h1 : Integrable g (ENNReal.ofReal D • μ) := hgi.smul_measure ENNReal.ofReal_ne_top
1284  refine ⟨h1.mono_measure hν, ?_⟩
1285  calc ∫ z, g z ∂ν ≤ ∫ z, g z ∂(ENNReal.ofReal D • μ) :=
1286        integral_mono_measure hν (Filter.Eventually.of_forall hg0) h1
1287    _ = D * ∫ z, g z ∂μ := by
1288        rw [integral_smul_measure, ENNReal.toReal_ofReal hD, smul_eq_mul]

1290lemma marginal_symm (V : WeakPotential μ) {lam : ℝ} (hlam : 0 ≤ lam) (a : B → E) {n : ℕ} (i j : Fin n) :
1291    (pinnedLaw V lam n a).map (fun y => y i) = (pinnedLaw V lam n a).map (fun y => y j) := by
1292  rw [pinnedLaw_eq]
1293  exact map_eval_eq_of_symm μ _ (measurable_fieldDensity (isField_pinField (V := V) hlam a).measurable V lam n)
1294    (fun σ y => fieldDensity_comp_perm V _ _ σ y) i j
1295
1296
1297/-- Laplace residual of the cavity functional at a point `x` (source `N:eq:laplace-residual`):
1298`|G(x) - exp(-(β/N) n ∫ q(x,·) dν)| ≤ 2 lam n D ∫(q-L)_+ dμ + lam² n D ∫ min(q,L)² dμ`. -/
1299theorem cavG_residual (V : WeakPotential μ) {β : ℝ} (hβ : 0 ≤ β) {N n : ℕ} (hN0 : 0 < N)
1300    (hN : n + Fintype.card B ≤ N) (hn : 0 < n) (a : B → E) (hκ : weakKappa V β ≤ 1 / 2)
1301    (x : E) {L : ℝ} (hL : 0 ≤ L) :
1302    |cavG V (β / N) a n x
1303        - Real.exp (-((β / N) * (n * ∫ z, V.q x z
1304            ∂((pinnedLaw V (β / N) n a).map (fun y => y ⟨0, hn⟩)))))|
1305      ≤ 2 * ((β / N) * (n * (Real.exp (V.M + V.M' + β * V.γ) * ∫ z, max (V.q x z - L) 0 ∂μ)))
1306        + (β / N) ^ 2 / 2 * (2 * n * (Real.exp (V.M + V.M' + β * V.γ)
1307            * ∫ z, (min (V.q x z) L) ^ 2 ∂μ)) := by
1308  have hlam : 0 ≤ β / N := div_nonneg hβ (Nat.cast_nonneg N)
1309  have hP := isProb_pinnedLaw V hlam n a
1310  set P := pinnedLaw V (β / N) n a with hPdef
1311  set i₀ : Fin n := ⟨0, hn⟩ with hi₀
1312  set ν := P.map (fun y => y i₀) with hν
1313  set D := Real.exp (V.M + V.M' + β * V.γ) with hD
1314  have hD0 : 0 ≤ D := (Real.exp_pos _).le
1315  have hνP : ∀ k : Fin n, P.map (fun y => y k) = ν := fun k => marginal_symm V hlam a k i₀
1316  have hνle : ν ≤ ENNReal.ofReal D • μ := marginal_le V hβ hN0 hN a i₀
1317  have hq0 : ∀ z, 0 ≤ V.q x z := fun z => V.q_nonneg x z
1318  have hqm : Measurable (V.q x) := V.measurable_q.comp (measurable_const.prodMk measurable_id)
1319  have hqi : Integrable (V.q x) μ := V.integrable_q x
1320  have : IsProbabilityMeasure ν := by rw [hν]; infer_instance
1321  -- the summands
1322  have hmeas : ∀ k : Fin n, Measurable (fun y : Fin n → E => V.q x (y k)) :=
1323    fun k => hqm.comp (measurable_pi_apply k)
1324  have hint : ∀ k : Fin n, Integrable (fun y : Fin n → E => V.q x (y k)) P := by
1325    intro k
1326    have h1 : Integrable (V.q x) ν := (integral_le_of_le_smul hD0 hνle hq0 hqi).1
1327    rw [← hνP k] at h1
1328    exact (integrable_map_measure hqm.aestronglyMeasurable (measurable_pi_apply k).aemeasurable).1 h1
1329  have key := laplace_sum P (fun k y => V.q x (y k)) hmeas (fun k y => hq0 _) hint hlam hL
1330  have hmap : ∀ (g : E → ℝ), Measurable g → ∀ k : Fin n,
1331      ∫ y, g (y k) ∂P = ∫ z, g z ∂ν := by
1332    intro g hg k
1333    rw [← hνP k, integral_map (measurable_pi_apply k).aemeasurable hg.aestronglyMeasurable]
1334  -- (i) the functional
1335  have e1 : cavG V (β / N) a n x = ∫ y, Real.exp (-((β / N) * ∑ k, V.q x (y k))) ∂P := by
1336    simp [cavG, neg_mul, hPdef]
1337  -- (ii) mean
1338  have e2 : ∑ k : Fin n, ∫ y, V.q x (y k) ∂P = n * ∫ z, V.q x z ∂ν := by
1339    rw [Finset.sum_congr rfl fun k _ => hmap (V.q x) hqm k]
1340    simp
1341  -- (iii) tail
1342  have hg1m : Measurable (fun z => max (V.q x z - L) 0) := (hqm.sub_const L).max measurable_const
1343  have hg1i : Integrable (fun z => max (V.q x z - L) 0) μ := by
1344    refine Integrable.mono' hqi hg1m.aestronglyMeasurable (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun z => ?_)
1345    rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_nonneg (le_max_right _ _)]
1346    exact max_le (by linarith) (hq0 z)
1347  have e3 : ∑ k : Fin n, ∫ y, max (V.q x (y k) - L) 0 ∂P
1348      ≤ n * (D * ∫ z, max (V.q x z - L) 0 ∂μ) := by
1349    rw [Finset.sum_congr rfl fun k _ => hmap (fun z => max (V.q x z - L) 0) hg1m k]
1350    simp only [Finset.sum_const, Finset.card_univ, Fintype.card_fin, nsmul_eq_mul]
1351    exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (integral_le_of_le_smul hD0 hνle (fun z => le_max_right _ _) hg1i).2
1352      (Nat.cast_nonneg n)
1353  -- (iv) variance
1354  have hfm : Measurable (fun z => min (V.q x z) L) := hqm.min measurable_const
1355  have hfbd : ∀ z, |min (V.q x z) L| ≤ L := fun z => by
1356    rw [abs_of_nonneg (le_min (hq0 z) hL)]; exact min_le_right _ _
1357  have hf2 : MemLp (fun z => min (V.q x z) L) 2 ν :=
1358    MemLp.of_bound hfm.aestronglyMeasurable L (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun z => by
1359      rw [Real.norm_eq_abs]; exact hfbd z)
1360  have hvar := weak_linear_variance_real V hβ hN0 hN a hκ ν hνP (fun z => min (V.q x z) L) hfm hf2
1361  have hvar2 : variance (fun z => min (V.q x z) L) ν ≤ D * ∫ z, (min (V.q x z) L) ^ 2 ∂μ := by
1362    refine (variance_le_expectation_sq hfm.aestronglyMeasurable).trans ?_
1363    have hgi : Integrable (fun z => (min (V.q x z) L) ^ 2) μ :=
1364      Integrable.of_bound (hfm.pow_const 2).aestronglyMeasurable (L ^ 2) (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun z => by
1365        rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_nonneg (sq_nonneg _)]
1366        have := hfbd z
1367        have h0 : 0 ≤ min (V.q x z) L := le_min (hq0 z) hL
1368        nlinarith [min_le_right (V.q x z) L])
1369    have := (integral_le_of_le_smul hD0 hνle (fun z => sq_nonneg _) hgi).2
1370    simpa [Pi.pow_apply] using this
1371  rw [e1]
1372  rw [e2] at key
1373  have hvar' : variance (fun y : Fin n → E => ∑ k, min (V.q x (y k)) L) P
1374      ≤ 2 * n * (D * ∫ z, (min (V.q x z) L) ^ 2 ∂μ) := by
1375    refine hvar.trans ?_
1376    have h2n : (0 : ℝ) ≤ 2 * n := by positivity
1377    exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hvar2 h2n
1378  refine key.trans ?_
1379  have hl2 : 0 ≤ (β / N) ^ 2 / 2 := by positivity
1380  have := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hvar' hl2
1381  have h3 := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left e3 hlam
1382  linarith
1383
1384end HRS.WeakRef.Abs
1385
1386end WM_A

1397/-- Cauchy-Schwarz for a nonnegative weight: `(∫ w g)² ≤ (∫ w) (∫ w g²)`. -/
1398lemma cs_weight {w g : E → ℝ} (hw : Measurable w) (hw0 : ∀ z, 0 ≤ w z) (hwi : Integrable w μ)
1399    (hg : Measurable g) {C : ℝ} (hC : 0 ≤ C) (hgb : ∀ z, |g z| ≤ C) :
1400    (∫ z, w z * g z ∂μ) ^ 2 ≤ (∫ z, w z ∂μ) * ∫ z, w z * g z ^ 2 ∂μ := by
1401  have i1 : Integrable (fun z => w z * g z) μ := by
1402    refine Integrable.mono' (hwi.const_mul C) (hw.mul hg).aestronglyMeasurable
1403      (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun z => ?_)
1404    rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_mul, abs_of_nonneg (hw0 z), mul_comm]
1405    exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right (hgb z) (hw0 z)
1406  have i2 : Integrable (fun z => w z * g z ^ 2) μ := by
1407    refine Integrable.mono' (hwi.const_mul (C ^ 2)) (hw.mul (hg.pow_const 2)).aestronglyMeasurable
1408      (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun z => ?_)
1409    rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_mul, abs_of_nonneg (hw0 z), mul_comm, abs_of_nonneg (sq_nonneg _)]
1410    refine mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right ?_ (hw0 z)
1411    calc g z ^ 2 = |g z| ^ 2 := (sq_abs _).symm
1412      _ ≤ C ^ 2 := pow_le_pow_left₀ (abs_nonneg _) (hgb z) 2
1413  have h : ∀ t : ℝ, 0 ≤ (∫ z, w z ∂μ) * (t * t) + (-2 * ∫ z, w z * g z ∂μ) * t
1414      + ∫ z, w z * g z ^ 2 ∂μ := by
1415    intro t
1416    have : ∫ z, w z * (g z - t) ^ 2 ∂μ
1417        = (∫ z, w z ∂μ) * (t * t) + (-2 * ∫ z, w z * g z ∂μ) * t + ∫ z, w z * g z ^ 2 ∂μ := by
1418      have e : ∀ z, w z * (g z - t) ^ 2 = w z * g z ^ 2 + (-2 * t) * (w z * g z) + (t * t) * w z :=
1419        fun z => by ring
1420      simp_rw [e]
1421      have j1 : Integrable (fun z => (-2 * t) * (w z * g z)) μ := i1.const_mul _
1422      have j2 : Integrable (fun z => (t * t) * w z) μ := hwi.const_mul _
1423      have j3 : Integrable (fun z => w z * g z ^ 2 + (-2 * t) * (w z * g z)) μ := i2.add j1
1424      rw [integral_add j3 j2, integral_add i2 j1, integral_const_mul, integral_const_mul]
1425      ring
1426    rw [← this]
1427    exact integral_nonneg fun z => mul_nonneg (hw0 z) (sq_nonneg _)
1428  have := discrim_le_zero h
1429  unfold discrim at this
1430  nlinarith
1431

1433section Schur

1435variable {q : E → E → ℝ}
