1437lemma measurable_rowInt' (hqm : Measurable (Function.uncurry q)) {h : E → ℝ} (hh : Measurable h) :
1438    Measurable fun x => ∫ y, q x y * h y ∂μ := by
1439  have : Measurable fun p : E × E => q p.1 p.2 * h p.2 :=
1440    (hqm.comp measurable_id).mul (hh.comp measurable_snd)
1441  exact (this.stronglyMeasurable.integral_prod_right').measurable
1442
1443lemma integrable_q_prod (hqm : Measurable (Function.uncurry q)) (hq0 : ∀ x y, 0 ≤ q x y) {a : ℝ}
1444    (hrow : ∀ x, ∫ y, q x y ∂μ ≤ a) (hqi : ∀ x, Integrable (q x) μ) :
1445    Integrable (fun p : E × E => q p.1 p.2) (μ.prod μ) := by
1446  change Integrable (Function.uncurry q) (μ.prod μ)
1447  rw [integrable_prod_iff (hqm.aestronglyMeasurable)]
1448  refine ⟨Filter.Eventually.of_forall hqi, ?_⟩
1449  have h0 : ∀ x, ∫ y, ‖Function.uncurry q (x, y)‖ ∂μ = ∫ y, q x y ∂μ := fun x => by
1450    refine integral_congr_ae (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => ?_)
1451    simp [Function.uncurry, abs_of_nonneg (hq0 x y)]
1452  simp_rw [h0]
1453  have hm : Measurable fun x => ∫ y, q x y ∂μ := by
1454    simpa using measurable_rowInt' (μ := μ) hqm (h := fun _ => 1) measurable_const
1455  refine Integrable.mono' (integrable_const a) hm.aestronglyMeasurable
1456    (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun x => ?_)
1457  rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_nonneg (integral_nonneg fun y => hq0 x y)]
1458  exact hrow x
1459
1460/-- Schur test on `L²(μ)` for a symmetric nonnegative kernel with row sums `≤ a`. -/
1461lemma schur_sq (hqm : Measurable (Function.uncurry q)) (hq0 : ∀ x y, 0 ≤ q x y)
1462    (hqs : ∀ x y, q x y = q y x) {a : ℝ} (ha : 0 ≤ a) (hrow : ∀ x, ∫ y, q x y ∂μ ≤ a)
1463    (hqi : ∀ x, Integrable (q x) μ) {g : E → ℝ} (hg : Measurable g) {C : ℝ} (hC : 0 ≤ C)
1464    (hgb : ∀ z, |g z| ≤ C) :
1465    ∫ x, (∫ y, q x y * g y ∂μ) ^ 2 ∂μ ≤ a ^ 2 * ∫ y, g y ^ 2 ∂μ := by
1466  have hqx : ∀ x, Measurable (q x) := fun x => hqm.comp (measurable_const.prodMk measurable_id)
1467  have hJm : Measurable fun x => ∫ y, q x y * g y ^ 2 ∂μ :=
1468    measurable_rowInt' hqm (hg.pow_const 2)
1469  have hIm : Measurable fun x => ∫ y, q x y * g y ∂μ := measurable_rowInt' hqm hg
1470  have hpt : ∀ x, (∫ y, q x y * g y ∂μ) ^ 2 ≤ a * ∫ y, q x y * g y ^ 2 ∂μ := by
1471    intro x
1472    refine (cs_weight (hqx x) (hq0 x) (hqi x) hg hC hgb).trans ?_
1473    exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right (hrow x) (integral_nonneg fun y => mul_nonneg (hq0 x y) (sq_nonneg _))
1474  have hprod : Integrable (fun p : E × E => q p.1 p.2 * g p.2 ^ 2) (μ.prod μ) := by
1475    have hi := integrable_q_prod hqm hq0 hrow hqi
1476    refine Integrable.mono' (hi.const_mul (C ^ 2))
1477      ((hqm.comp measurable_id).mul ((hg.comp measurable_snd).pow_const 2)).aestronglyMeasurable
1478      (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun p => ?_)
1479    simp only [Real.norm_eq_abs, id]
1480    rw [abs_mul, abs_of_nonneg (hq0 _ _), abs_of_nonneg (sq_nonneg _), mul_comm]
1481    refine mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right ?_ (hq0 _ _)
1482    calc g p.2 ^ 2 = |g p.2| ^ 2 := (sq_abs _).symm
1483      _ ≤ C ^ 2 := pow_le_pow_left₀ (abs_nonneg _) (hgb _) 2
1484  have hJi : Integrable (fun x => ∫ y, q x y * g y ^ 2 ∂μ) μ := hprod.integral_prod_left
1485  have hIi : Integrable (fun x => (∫ y, q x y * g y ∂μ) ^ 2) μ := by
1486    refine Integrable.mono' (hJi.const_mul a) (hIm.pow_const 2).aestronglyMeasurable
1487      (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun x => ?_)
1488    rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_nonneg (sq_nonneg _)]
1489    exact hpt x
1490  calc ∫ x, (∫ y, q x y * g y ∂μ) ^ 2 ∂μ ≤ ∫ x, a * ∫ y, q x y * g y ^ 2 ∂μ ∂μ :=
1491        integral_mono hIi (hJi.const_mul a) hpt
1492    _ = a * ∫ y, ∫ x, q x y * g y ^ 2 ∂μ ∂μ := by
1493        rw [integral_const_mul, integral_integral_swap hprod]
1494    _ ≤ a * ∫ y, a * g y ^ 2 ∂μ := by
1495        refine mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left ?_ ha
1496        have hgi : Integrable (fun y => a * g y ^ 2) μ :=
1497          Integrable.of_bound ((hg.pow_const 2).const_mul a).aestronglyMeasurable (a * C ^ 2)
1498            (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => by
1499              rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_nonneg (mul_nonneg ha (sq_nonneg _))]
1500              refine mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left ?_ ha
1501              calc g y ^ 2 = |g y| ^ 2 := (sq_abs _).symm
1502                _ ≤ C ^ 2 := pow_le_pow_left₀ (abs_nonneg _) (hgb _) 2)
1503        refine integral_mono hprod.integral_prod_right hgi fun y => ?_
1504        have : ∫ x, q x y * g y ^ 2 ∂μ = (∫ x, q y x ∂μ) * g y ^ 2 := by
1505          rw [← integral_mul_const]
1506          exact integral_congr_ae (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun x => by simp only [hqs x y])
1507        rw [this]
1508        calc (∫ x, q y x ∂μ) * g y ^ 2 ≤ a * g y ^ 2 :=
1509              mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right (hrow y) (sq_nonneg _)
1510          _ = a * g y ^ 2 := rfl
1511    _ = a ^ 2 * ∫ y, g y ^ 2 ∂μ := by rw [integral_const_mul]; ring
1512
1513end Schur

1518lemma sq_le_two_add (a b c : ℝ) : (a - b) ^ 2 ≤ 2 * (a - c) ^ 2 + 2 * (c - b) ^ 2 := by
1519  nlinarith [sq_nonneg (a - c - (c - b))]

1521lemma sq_le_three_add (a b c d : ℝ) :
1522    (a - d) ^ 2 ≤ 3 * ((a - b) ^ 2 + (b - c) ^ 2 + (c - d) ^ 2) := by
1523  nlinarith [sq_nonneg (a - b - (b - c)), sq_nonneg (a - b - (c - d)), sq_nonneg (b - c - (c - d))]
1524
1525lemma closing_algebra {x R P A B C εG εc κ a t : ℝ} (hx0 : 0 ≤ x) (hs2 : 214 * (κ * a) ^ 2 ≤ 1)
1526    (hxs : x ≤ 3 * (A + B + C)) (hA : A ≤ 25 * εG ^ 2) (hB : B ≤ 10 * P)
1527    (hP : P ≤ 16 / 9 * κ ^ 2 * R) (hR : R ≤ 2 * εc ^ 2 + 2 * (a ^ 2 * x))
1528    (hC : C ≤ (4 / 3 * t) ^ 2) (hPnn : 0 ≤ κ ^ 2) :
1529    x ≤ 150 * εG ^ 2 + 214 * κ ^ 2 * εc ^ 2 + 12 * t ^ 2 := by
1530  have hk : (κ * a) ^ 2 * x ≤ x / 214 := by
1531    have := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hs2 hx0
1532    linarith
1533  have h1 : κ ^ 2 * R ≤ κ ^ 2 * (2 * εc ^ 2 + 2 * (a ^ 2 * x)) := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hR hPnn
1534  have e1 : κ ^ 2 * (a ^ 2 * x) = (κ * a) ^ 2 * x := by ring
1535  have e2 : (4 / 3 * t) ^ 2 = 16 / 9 * t ^ 2 := by ring
1536  have h2 : 0 ≤ κ ^ 2 * εc ^ 2 := by positivity
1537  nlinarith [hxs, hA, hB, hP, h1, e1, e2, hk, h2]
1538

1540lemma intg_bdd {g : E → ℝ} (hm : Measurable g) {C : ℝ} (hb : ∀ x, |g x| ≤ C) : Integrable g μ :=
1541  Integrable.of_bound hm.aestronglyMeasurable C (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun x => by
1542    rw [Real.norm_eq_abs]; exact hb x)
1543
1544lemma sq_integral_le' {g : E → ℝ} (hm : Measurable g) {C : ℝ} (hb : ∀ x, |g x| ≤ C) :
1545    (∫ x, g x ∂μ) ^ 2 ≤ ∫ x, g x ^ 2 ∂μ := by
1546  have h2 : MemLp g 2 μ := MemLp.of_bound hm.aestronglyMeasurable C
1547    (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun x => by rw [Real.norm_eq_abs]; exact hb x)
1548  have h := variance_nonneg g μ
1549  rw [variance_eq_sub h2] at h
1550  simp only [Pi.pow_apply] at h
1551  linarith
1552
1553lemma intg_sq_bdd {g : E → ℝ} (hm : Measurable g) {C : ℝ} (hb : ∀ x, |g x| ≤ C) :
1554    Integrable (fun x => g x ^ 2) μ :=
1555  intg_bdd (hm.pow_const 2) (C := C ^ 2) fun x => by
1556    rw [abs_of_nonneg (sq_nonneg _)]
1557    calc g x ^ 2 = |g x| ^ 2 := (sq_abs _).symm
1558      _ ≤ C ^ 2 := pow_le_pow_left₀ (abs_nonneg _) (hb x) 2

1560/-- Normalisation is `L²`-Lipschitz on functions with values in `[3/4, 4/3]` and `[1, 4/3]`. -/
1561lemma ratio_sq {F F₁ : E → ℝ} (hF : Measurable F) (hF₁ : Measurable F₁)
1562    (hFb : ∀ x, 3 / 4 ≤ F x ∧ F x ≤ 4 / 3) (hF₁b : ∀ x, 1 ≤ F₁ x ∧ F₁ x ≤ 4 / 3) :
1563    ∫ x, (F x / ∫ y, F y ∂μ - F₁ x / ∫ y, F₁ y ∂μ) ^ 2 ∂μ ≤ 10 * ∫ x, (F x - F₁ x) ^ 2 ∂μ := by
1564  set Z := ∫ y, F y ∂μ with hZ
1565  set Z₁ := ∫ y, F₁ y ∂μ with hZ₁
1566  have hiF : Integrable F μ := intg_bdd hF (C := 4 / 3) fun x => by
1567    rw [abs_of_nonneg (by linarith [(hFb x).1])]; exact (hFb x).2
1568  have hiF₁ : Integrable F₁ μ := intg_bdd hF₁ (C := 4 / 3) fun x => by
1569    rw [abs_of_nonneg (by linarith [(hF₁b x).1])]; exact (hF₁b x).2
1570  have hZlo : 3 / 4 ≤ Z := by
1571    have := integral_mono (integrable_const (μ := μ) (3 / 4 : ℝ)) hiF fun x => (hFb x).1
1572    simpa using this
1573  have hZhi : Z ≤ 4 / 3 := by
1574    have := integral_mono hiF (integrable_const (μ := μ) (4 / 3 : ℝ)) fun x => (hFb x).2
1575    simpa using this
1576  have hZ₁lo : 1 ≤ Z₁ := by
1577    have := integral_mono (integrable_const (μ := μ) (1 : ℝ)) hiF₁ fun x => (hF₁b x).1
1578    simpa using this
1579  have hZ₁hi : Z₁ ≤ 4 / 3 := by
1580    have := integral_mono hiF₁ (integrable_const (μ := μ) (4 / 3 : ℝ)) fun x => (hF₁b x).2
1581    simpa using this
1582  have hΔ : ∀ x, |F x - F₁ x| ≤ 3 := fun x => by
1583    rw [abs_le]; constructor <;> linarith [hFb x, hF₁b x]
1584  have hmΔ : Measurable fun x => F x - F₁ x := hF.sub hF₁
1585  have hZdiff : (Z - Z₁) ^ 2 ≤ ∫ x, (F x - F₁ x) ^ 2 ∂μ := by
1586    have := sq_integral_le' (μ := μ) hmΔ hΔ
1587    rw [integral_sub hiF hiF₁] at this
1588    exact this
1589  have hpt : ∀ x, (F x / Z - F₁ x / Z₁) ^ 2 ≤ 32 / 9 * (F x - F₁ x) ^ 2 + 512 / 81 * (Z - Z₁) ^ 2 := by
1590    intro x
1591    have hZpos : 0 < Z := by linarith
1592    have hZ₁pos : 0 < Z₁ := by linarith
1593    have e : F x / Z - F₁ x / Z₁ = (F x - F₁ x) / Z + F₁ x * (Z₁ - Z) / (Z * Z₁) := by
1594      field_simp; ring
1595    rw [e]
1596    have h1 : |(F x - F₁ x) / Z| ≤ 4 / 3 * |F x - F₁ x| := by
1597      rw [abs_div, abs_of_pos hZpos, div_le_iff₀ hZpos]
1598      nlinarith [abs_nonneg (F x - F₁ x)]
1599    have h2 : |F₁ x * (Z₁ - Z) / (Z * Z₁)| ≤ 16 / 9 * |Z - Z₁| := by
1600      rw [abs_div, abs_mul, abs_of_pos (mul_pos hZpos hZ₁pos), abs_of_pos (by linarith [(hF₁b x).1] : 0 < F₁ x),
1601        abs_sub_comm Z₁ Z, div_le_iff₀ (mul_pos hZpos hZ₁pos)]
1602      have h3 : F₁ x ≤ 16 / 9 * (Z * Z₁) := by nlinarith [(hF₁b x).2]
1603      nlinarith [abs_nonneg (Z - Z₁)]
1604    set A1 := (F x - F₁ x) / Z with hA1
1605    set B1 := F₁ x * (Z₁ - Z) / (Z * Z₁) with hB1
1606    have h4 : (A1 + B1) ^ 2 ≤ 2 * A1 ^ 2 + 2 * B1 ^ 2 := by nlinarith [sq_nonneg (A1 - B1)]
1607    have h5 : A1 ^ 2 ≤ 16 / 9 * (F x - F₁ x) ^ 2 := by
1608      have := pow_le_pow_left₀ (abs_nonneg _) h1 2
1609      rw [sq_abs, mul_pow, sq_abs] at this
1610      linarith
1611    have h6 : B1 ^ 2 ≤ 256 / 81 * (Z - Z₁) ^ 2 := by
1612      have := pow_le_pow_left₀ (abs_nonneg _) h2 2
1613      rw [sq_abs, mul_pow, sq_abs] at this
1614      linarith
1615    linarith
1616  have hbd : ∀ x, |F x / Z - F₁ x / Z₁| ≤ 3 := by
1617    intro x
1618    have hZpos : 0 < Z := by linarith
1619    have hZ₁pos : 0 < Z₁ := by linarith
1620    have a1 : 0 ≤ F x / Z := div_nonneg (by linarith [(hFb x).1]) hZpos.le
1621    have a2 : F x / Z ≤ 2 := by rw [div_le_iff₀ hZpos]; nlinarith [(hFb x).2]
1622    have b1 : 0 ≤ F₁ x / Z₁ := div_nonneg (by linarith [(hF₁b x).1]) hZ₁pos.le
1623    have b2 : F₁ x / Z₁ ≤ 2 := by rw [div_le_iff₀ hZ₁pos]; nlinarith [(hF₁b x).2]
1624    rw [abs_le]; constructor <;> linarith
1625  have hmeas : Measurable fun x => F x / Z - F₁ x / Z₁ := (hF.div_const _).sub (hF₁.div_const _)
1626  have hI1 := intg_sq_bdd (μ := μ) hmeas hbd
1627  have hI2 := intg_sq_bdd (μ := μ) hmΔ hΔ
1628  calc ∫ x, (F x / Z - F₁ x / Z₁) ^ 2 ∂μ
1629      ≤ ∫ x, (32 / 9 * (F x - F₁ x) ^ 2 + 512 / 81 * (Z - Z₁) ^ 2) ∂μ :=
1630        integral_mono hI1 ((hI2.const_mul _).add (integrable_const _)) hpt
1631    _ = 32 / 9 * ∫ x, (F x - F₁ x) ^ 2 ∂μ + 512 / 81 * (Z - Z₁) ^ 2 := by
1632        rw [integral_add (hI2.const_mul _) (integrable_const _), integral_const_mul]
1633        simp
1634    _ ≤ 10 * ∫ x, (F x - F₁ x) ^ 2 ∂μ := by nlinarith

1638lemma exp_le_four_thirds {t : ℝ} (ht : t ≤ 1 / 4) : Real.exp t ≤ 4 / 3 := by
1639  have h1 : 3 / 4 ≤ Real.exp (-(1 / 4)) := by
1640    have := Real.add_one_le_exp (-(1 / 4 : ℝ)); linarith
1641  have h2 : Real.exp (1 / 4) * Real.exp (-(1 / 4)) = 1 := by rw [← Real.exp_add]; simp
1642  have h3 : Real.exp (1 / 4) ≤ 4 / 3 := by
1643    have hp := Real.exp_pos (1 / 4 : ℝ)
1644    nlinarith
1645  exact (Real.exp_le_exp.2 ht).trans h3
1646
1647lemma three_fourths_le_exp {t : ℝ} (ht : -(1 / 4) ≤ t) : 3 / 4 ≤ Real.exp t := by
1648  have h1 : 3 / 4 ≤ Real.exp (-(1 / 4)) := by
1649    have := Real.add_one_le_exp (-(1 / 4 : ℝ)); linarith
1650  exact h1.trans (Real.exp_le_exp.2 ht)
1651
1652lemma exp_sub_one_le_mul {t : ℝ} (ht0 : 0 ≤ t) (ht : t ≤ 1 / 4) : Real.exp t - 1 ≤ 4 / 3 * t := by
1653  have h1 := Real.add_one_le_exp (-t)
1654  have h2 : Real.exp t * Real.exp (-t) = 1 := by rw [← Real.exp_add]; simp
1655  have h3 := exp_le_four_thirds ht
1656  have hp := Real.exp_pos t
1657  have : Real.exp t - 1 ≤ t * Real.exp t := by nlinarith
1658  nlinarith

1660/-- `T₁ = F₁/∫F₁` is `η`-close to `1` in `L²` when `1 ≤ F₁ ≤ 1 + η`. -/
1661lemma ratio_one {F₁ : E → ℝ} (hF₁ : Measurable F₁) {η : ℝ} (hη : 0 ≤ η)
1662    (hb : ∀ x, 1 ≤ F₁ x ∧ F₁ x ≤ 1 + η) :
1663    ∫ x, (F₁ x / ∫ y, F₁ y ∂μ - 1) ^ 2 ∂μ ≤ η ^ 2 := by
1664  set Z₁ := ∫ y, F₁ y ∂μ with hZ₁
1665  have hiF₁ : Integrable F₁ μ := intg_bdd hF₁ (C := 1 + η) fun x => by
1666    rw [abs_of_nonneg (by linarith [(hb x).1])]; exact (hb x).2
1667  have hlo : 1 ≤ Z₁ := by
1668    have := integral_mono (integrable_const (μ := μ) (1 : ℝ)) hiF₁ fun x => (hb x).1
1669    simpa using this
1670  have hhi : Z₁ ≤ 1 + η := by
1671    have := integral_mono hiF₁ (integrable_const (μ := μ) (1 + η)) fun x => (hb x).2
1672    simpa using this
1673  have hbd : ∀ x, |F₁ x / Z₁ - 1| ≤ η := by
1674    intro x
1675    have hZpos : 0 < Z₁ := by linarith
1676    have e : F₁ x / Z₁ - 1 = (F₁ x - Z₁) / Z₁ := by field_simp
1677    rw [e, abs_div, abs_of_pos hZpos, div_le_iff₀ hZpos]
1678    have : |F₁ x - Z₁| ≤ η := by
1679      rw [abs_le]; constructor <;> linarith [(hb x).1, (hb x).2]
1680    nlinarith [abs_nonneg (F₁ x - Z₁)]
1681  have hpt : ∀ x, (F₁ x / Z₁ - 1) ^ 2 ≤ η ^ 2 := by
1682    intro x
1683    have := pow_le_pow_left₀ (abs_nonneg _) (hbd x) 2
1684    rwa [sq_abs] at this
1685  calc ∫ x, (F₁ x / Z₁ - 1) ^ 2 ∂μ ≤ ∫ _x, η ^ 2 ∂μ :=
1686        integral_mono (intg_sq_bdd (hF₁.div_const _ |>.sub_const 1) hbd) (integrable_const _) hpt
1687    _ = η ^ 2 := by simp

1691/-- **Closing estimate** (source `N:eq:L2-contraction` and the conclusion of Lemma 5.6): the density
1692`f = c e^{κH} G` with `G` the Laplace functional `≈ e^{-κθ Hν}` is `L²(μ)`-close to `1`, once `κ a`
1693is small.  Here `Hν = q*ν` is the cavity field and `‖Hν - q*f‖₂ ≤ εc`. -/
1694theorem closing_L2 {q : E → E → ℝ} (hqm : Measurable (Function.uncurry q)) (hq0 : ∀ x y, 0 ≤ q x y)
1695    (hqs : ∀ x y, q x y = q y x) {a : ℝ} (ha : 0 ≤ a) (hrow : ∀ x, ∫ y, q x y ∂μ ≤ a)
1696    (hqi : ∀ x, Integrable (q x) μ)
1697    {κ θ D : ℝ} (hκ : 0 ≤ κ) (hθ0 : 0 ≤ θ) (hθ1 : θ ≤ 1) (hD : 1 ≤ D) (hs : κ * a * D ≤ 1 / 4)
1698    (hs2 : 214 * (κ * a) ^ 2 ≤ 1)
1699    {Hν : E → ℝ} (hHνm : Measurable Hν) (hHν0 : ∀ x, 0 ≤ Hν x) (hHνD : ∀ x, Hν x ≤ D * a)
1700    {G f : E → ℝ} {c : ℝ} (hc : 0 < c) (hGm : Measurable G)
1701    (hf : ∀ x, f x = c * (Real.exp (κ * ∫ y, q x y ∂μ) * G x))
1702    (hf1 : ∫ x, f x ∂μ = 1)
1703    {εG : ℝ} (hG1 : ∀ x, |G x - Real.exp (-(κ * θ * Hν x))| ≤ εG)
1704    (hG2 : ∀ x, Real.exp (-(κ * θ * Hν x)) ≤ G x)
1705    {εc : ℝ} (hcnv : ∫ x, (Hν x - ∫ y, q x y * f y ∂μ) ^ 2 ∂μ ≤ εc ^ 2) :
1706    ∫ x, (f x - 1) ^ 2 ∂μ ≤ 150 * εG ^ 2 + 214 * κ ^ 2 * εc ^ 2 + 12 * (κ * (1 - θ) * a) ^ 2 := by
1707  have hne : Nonempty E := Measure.nonempty_of_neZero μ
1708  obtain ⟨x₀⟩ := hne
1709  have hεG : 0 ≤ εG := (abs_nonneg _).trans (hG1 x₀)
1710  -- the row field
1711  set H : E → ℝ := fun x => ∫ y, q x y ∂μ with hH
1712  have hHm : Measurable H := by
1713    simpa [hH] using measurable_rowInt' (μ := μ) hqm (h := fun _ => 1) measurable_const
1714  have hH0 : ∀ x, 0 ≤ H x := fun x => integral_nonneg fun y => hq0 x y
1715  have hHa : ∀ x, H x ≤ a := hrow
1716  have hκa : κ * a ≤ 1 / 4 := by
1717    have : κ * a ≤ κ * a * D := by nlinarith [mul_nonneg hκ ha]
1718    linarith
1719  -- the exponential fields
1720  set F : E → ℝ := fun x => Real.exp (κ * H x) * Real.exp (-(κ * θ * Hν x)) with hFdef
1721  set F₁ : E → ℝ := fun x => Real.exp (κ * H x) * Real.exp (-(κ * θ * H x)) with hF₁def
1722  have hFm : Measurable F := (Real.measurable_exp.comp (hHm.const_mul κ)).mul
1723    (Real.measurable_exp.comp ((hHνm.const_mul (κ * θ)).neg))
1724  have hF₁m : Measurable F₁ := (Real.measurable_exp.comp (hHm.const_mul κ)).mul
1725    (Real.measurable_exp.comp ((hHm.const_mul (κ * θ)).neg))
1726  have hwb : ∀ x, 0 ≤ κ * θ * Hν x ∧ κ * θ * Hν x ≤ 1 / 4 := by
1727    intro x
1728    refine ⟨mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg hκ hθ0) (hHν0 x), ?_⟩
1729    calc κ * θ * Hν x ≤ κ * 1 * (D * a) := by
1730          have := hHνD x
1731          have h0 := hHν0 x
1732          gcongr
1733      _ = κ * a * D := by ring
1734      _ ≤ 1 / 4 := hs
1735  have hub : ∀ x, 0 ≤ κ * H x ∧ κ * H x ≤ 1 / 4 := by
1736    intro x
1737    exact ⟨mul_nonneg hκ (hH0 x), (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (hHa x) hκ).trans hκa⟩
1738  have hFb : ∀ x, 3 / 4 ≤ F x ∧ F x ≤ 4 / 3 := by
1739    intro x
1740    have e : F x = Real.exp (κ * H x - κ * θ * Hν x) := by
1741      simp only [hFdef]; rw [← Real.exp_add]; ring_nf
1742    rw [e]
1743    constructor
1744    · exact three_fourths_le_exp (by linarith [(hub x).1, (hwb x).2])
1745    · exact exp_le_four_thirds (by linarith [(hub x).2, (hwb x).1])
1746  have hF₁b : ∀ x, 1 ≤ F₁ x ∧ F₁ x ≤ 1 + 4 / 3 * (κ * (1 - θ) * a) := by
1747    intro x
1748    have e : F₁ x = Real.exp (κ * (1 - θ) * H x) := by
1749      simp only [hF₁def]; rw [← Real.exp_add]; ring_nf
1750    rw [e]
1751    have h0 : 0 ≤ κ * (1 - θ) * H x := mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg hκ (by linarith)) (hH0 x)
1752    have h1 : κ * (1 - θ) * H x ≤ κ * (1 - θ) * a :=
1753      mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (hHa x) (mul_nonneg hκ (by linarith))
1754    have h2 : κ * (1 - θ) * a ≤ 1 / 4 := by
1755      have : κ * (1 - θ) * a ≤ κ * 1 * a := by gcongr; linarith
1756      linarith
1757    refine ⟨Real.one_le_exp h0, ?_⟩
1758    have := exp_sub_one_le_mul h0 (h1.trans h2)
1759    nlinarith [mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg hκ (by linarith : (0:ℝ) ≤ 1 - θ)) ha]
1760  -- bounds on G and f
1761  have hGb : ∀ x, 0 ≤ G x ∧ G x ≤ 1 + εG := by
1762    intro x
1763    have h1 := hG2 x
1764    have h2 : Real.exp (-(κ * θ * Hν x)) ≤ 1 := Real.exp_le_one_iff.2 (by linarith [(hwb x).1])
1765    have h3 := (abs_le.1 (hG1 x)).2
1766    exact ⟨(Real.exp_pos _).le.trans h1, by linarith⟩
1767  have hEb : ∀ x, 0 ≤ Real.exp (κ * H x) * G x ∧ Real.exp (κ * H x) * G x ≤ 4 / 3 * (1 + εG) := by
1768    intro x
1769    refine ⟨mul_nonneg (Real.exp_pos _).le (hGb x).1, ?_⟩
1770    exact mul_le_mul (exp_le_four_thirds (hub x).2) (hGb x).2 (hGb x).1 (by norm_num)
1771  have hEm : Measurable fun x => Real.exp (κ * H x) * G x :=
1772    (Real.measurable_exp.comp (hHm.const_mul κ)).mul hGm
1773  have hEi : Integrable (fun x => Real.exp (κ * H x) * G x) μ :=
1774    intg_bdd hEm (C := 4 / 3 * (1 + εG)) fun x => by rw [abs_of_nonneg (hEb x).1]; exact (hEb x).2
1775  set I := ∫ x, Real.exp (κ * H x) * G x ∂μ with hI
1776  have hcI : c * I = 1 := by
1777    rw [← hf1, ← integral_const_mul]
1778    exact integral_congr_ae (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun x => (hf x).symm)
1779  have hiF : Integrable F μ := intg_bdd hFm (C := 4 / 3) fun x => by
1780    rw [abs_of_nonneg (by linarith [(hFb x).1])]; exact (hFb x).2
1781  set Z := ∫ x, F x ∂μ with hZ
1782  have hZlo : 3 / 4 ≤ Z := by
1783    have := integral_mono (integrable_const (μ := μ) (3 / 4 : ℝ)) hiF fun x => (hFb x).1
1784    simpa using this
1785  have hZhi : Z ≤ 4 / 3 := by
1786    have := integral_mono hiF (integrable_const (μ := μ) (4 / 3 : ℝ)) fun x => (hFb x).2
1787    simpa using this
1788  have hFle : ∀ x, F x ≤ Real.exp (κ * H x) * G x := fun x =>
1789    mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (hG2 x) (Real.exp_pos _).le
1790  have hZI : Z ≤ I := integral_mono hiF hEi hFle
1791  have hc43 : c ≤ 4 / 3 := by nlinarith
1792  have hIZ : |I - Z| ≤ 4 / 3 * εG := by
1793    have e : I - Z = ∫ x, Real.exp (κ * H x) * (G x - Real.exp (-(κ * θ * Hν x))) ∂μ := by
1794      rw [hI, hZ, ← integral_sub hEi hiF]
1795      refine integral_congr_ae (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun x => ?_)
1796      simp only [hFdef]; ring
1797    rw [e]
1798    have hb : ∀ x, ‖Real.exp (κ * H x) * (G x - Real.exp (-(κ * θ * Hν x)))‖ ≤ 4 / 3 * εG := by
1799      intro x
1800      rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_mul, abs_of_pos (Real.exp_pos _)]
1801      exact mul_le_mul (exp_le_four_thirds (hub x).2) (hG1 x) (abs_nonneg _) (by norm_num)
1802    have := norm_integral_le_of_norm_le_const (μ := μ) (Filter.Eventually.of_forall hb)
1803    simpa using this
1804  have hcZ : |1 - c * Z| ≤ 16 / 9 * εG := by
1805    have : 1 - c * Z = c * (I - Z) := by rw [mul_sub, hcI]
1806    rw [this, abs_mul, abs_of_pos hc]
1807    calc c * |I - Z| ≤ 4 / 3 * (4 / 3 * εG) := mul_le_mul hc43 hIZ (abs_nonneg _) (by norm_num)
1808      _ = 16 / 9 * εG := by ring
1809  have hfT : ∀ x, |f x - F x / Z| ≤ 5 * εG := by
1810    intro x
1811    have hZpos : 0 < Z := by linarith
1812    have e : f x - F x / Z = c * Real.exp (κ * H x) * (G x - Real.exp (-(κ * θ * Hν x)))
1813        + F x * (c * Z - 1) / Z := by
1814      rw [hf x]; simp only [hFdef]; field_simp; ring
1815    rw [e]
1816    have h1 : |c * Real.exp (κ * H x) * (G x - Real.exp (-(κ * θ * Hν x)))| ≤ 16 / 9 * εG := by
1817      rw [abs_mul, abs_mul, abs_of_pos hc, abs_of_pos (Real.exp_pos _)]
1818      calc c * Real.exp (κ * H x) * |G x - Real.exp (-(κ * θ * Hν x))|
1819          ≤ (4 / 3 * (4 / 3)) * εG := by
1820            refine mul_le_mul (mul_le_mul hc43 (exp_le_four_thirds (hub x).2) (Real.exp_pos _).le (by norm_num))
1821              (hG1 x) (abs_nonneg _) (by norm_num)
1822        _ = 16 / 9 * εG := by ring
1823    have h2 : |F x * (c * Z - 1) / Z| ≤ 256 / 81 * εG := by
1824      rw [abs_div, abs_mul, abs_of_pos hZpos, abs_of_pos (by linarith [(hFb x).1] : 0 < F x),
1825        div_le_iff₀ hZpos, abs_sub_comm]
1826      have h3 : |1 - c * Z| ≤ 16 / 9 * εG := hcZ
1827      have h4 : F x ≤ 4 / 3 := (hFb x).2
1828      calc F x * |1 - c * Z| ≤ 4 / 3 * (16 / 9 * εG) :=
1829            mul_le_mul h4 h3 (abs_nonneg _) (by norm_num)
1830        _ ≤ 256 / 81 * εG * Z := by nlinarith
1831    calc |_ + _| ≤ _ := abs_add_le _ _
1832      _ ≤ 16 / 9 * εG + 256 / 81 * εG := add_le_add h1 h2
1833      _ ≤ 5 * εG := by nlinarith
1834  -- measurability and boundedness of f
1835  have hfm : Measurable f := by
1836    have : f = fun x => c * (Real.exp (κ * H x) * G x) := funext hf
1837    rw [this]; exact hEm.const_mul c
1838  have hfb : ∀ x, 0 ≤ f x ∧ f x ≤ 16 / 9 * (1 + εG) := by
1839    intro x
1840    rw [hf x]
1841    refine ⟨mul_nonneg hc.le (hEb x).1, ?_⟩
1842    calc c * (Real.exp (κ * H x) * G x) ≤ 4 / 3 * (4 / 3 * (1 + εG)) :=
1843          mul_le_mul hc43 (hEb x).2 (hEb x).1 (by norm_num)
1844      _ = 16 / 9 * (1 + εG) := by ring
1845  have hf1b : ∀ x, |f x - 1| ≤ 16 / 9 * (1 + εG) + 1 := fun x => by
1846    rw [abs_le]; constructor <;> linarith [(hfb x).1, (hfb x).2, hεG]
1847  -- the three pieces
1848  set Z₁ := ∫ x, F₁ x ∂μ with hZ₁
1849  set T : E → ℝ := fun x => F x / Z with hT
1850  set T₁ : E → ℝ := fun x => F₁ x / Z₁ with hT₁
1851  have hTm : Measurable T := hFm.div_const _
1852  have hT₁m : Measurable T₁ := hF₁m.div_const _
1853  have hZpos : 0 < Z := by linarith
1854  have hTb : ∀ x, |T x| ≤ 2 := fun x => by
1855    simp only [hT]
1856    rw [abs_of_nonneg (div_nonneg (by linarith [(hFb x).1]) hZpos.le), div_le_iff₀ hZpos]
1857    nlinarith [(hFb x).2]
1858  have hZ₁lo : 1 ≤ Z₁ := by
1859    have hi : Integrable F₁ μ := intg_bdd hF₁m (C := 2) fun x => by
1860      rw [abs_of_nonneg (by linarith [(hF₁b x).1])]
1861      have : 0 ≤ κ * (1 - θ) * a := mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg hκ (by linarith)) ha
1862      linarith [(hF₁b x).2, show 4 / 3 * (κ * (1 - θ) * a) ≤ 1 / 3 by
1863        have : κ * (1 - θ) * a ≤ κ * 1 * a := by gcongr; linarith
1864        linarith]
1865    have := integral_mono (integrable_const (μ := μ) (1 : ℝ)) hi fun x => (hF₁b x).1
1866    simpa using this
1867  have hZ₁pos : 0 < Z₁ := by linarith
1868  have hT₁b : ∀ x, |T₁ x| ≤ 2 := fun x => by
1869    simp only [hT₁]
1870    rw [abs_of_nonneg (div_nonneg (by linarith [(hF₁b x).1]) hZ₁pos.le), div_le_iff₀ hZ₁pos]
1871    have : 0 ≤ κ * (1 - θ) * a := mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg hκ (by linarith)) ha
1872    have h2 : κ * (1 - θ) * a ≤ 1 / 4 := by
1873      have : κ * (1 - θ) * a ≤ κ * 1 * a := by gcongr; linarith
1874      linarith
1875    nlinarith [(hF₁b x).2]
1876  -- (i) f vs T
1877  have Q1 : ∫ x, (f x - T x) ^ 2 ∂μ ≤ 25 * εG ^ 2 := by
1878    calc ∫ x, (f x - T x) ^ 2 ∂μ ≤ ∫ _x, 25 * εG ^ 2 ∂μ := by
1879          refine integral_mono (intg_sq_bdd (hfm.sub hTm) (C := 16 / 9 * (1 + εG) + 2) fun x => ?_)
1880            (integrable_const _) fun x => ?_
1881          · rw [abs_le]; constructor <;> linarith [(hfb x).1, (hfb x).2, hεG, (abs_le.1 (hTb x)).1,
1882              (abs_le.1 (hTb x)).2]
1883          · have h := pow_le_pow_left₀ (abs_nonneg _) (hfT x) 2
1884            rw [sq_abs, mul_pow] at h
1885            simp only [hT]; nlinarith
1886      _ = 25 * εG ^ 2 := by simp
1887  -- (ii) T vs T₁
1888  have Q2 : ∫ x, (T x - T₁ x) ^ 2 ∂μ ≤ 10 * ∫ x, (F x - F₁ x) ^ 2 ∂μ := by
1889    have hF₁b' : ∀ x, 1 ≤ F₁ x ∧ F₁ x ≤ 4 / 3 := by
1890      intro x
1891      refine ⟨(hF₁b x).1, ?_⟩
1892      have : 4 / 3 * (κ * (1 - θ) * a) ≤ 1 / 3 := by
1893        have : κ * (1 - θ) * a ≤ κ * 1 * a := by gcongr; linarith
1894        linarith
1895      linarith [(hF₁b x).2]
1896    exact ratio_sq hFm hF₁m hFb hF₁b'
1897  -- (iii) T₁ vs 1
1898  have Q3 : ∫ x, (T₁ x - 1) ^ 2 ∂μ ≤ (4 / 3 * (κ * (1 - θ) * a)) ^ 2 := by
1899    have hη : 0 ≤ 4 / 3 * (κ * (1 - θ) * a) :=
1900      mul_nonneg (by norm_num) (mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg hκ (by linarith)) ha)
1901    exact ratio_one hF₁m hη hF₁b
1902  -- (iv) F vs F₁ in terms of Hν - H
1903  have hFF₁ : ∀ x, (F x - F₁ x) ^ 2 ≤ 16 / 9 * κ ^ 2 * (Hν x - H x) ^ 2 := by
1904    intro x
1905    have h1 : |F x - F₁ x| ≤ 4 / 3 * (κ * |Hν x - H x|) := by
1906      have e : F x - F₁ x = Real.exp (κ * H x) *
1907          (Real.exp (-(κ * θ * Hν x)) - Real.exp (-(κ * θ * H x))) := by
1908        simp only [hFdef, hF₁def]; ring
1909      rw [e, abs_mul, abs_of_pos (Real.exp_pos _)]
1910      have h2 := abs_exp_neg_sub_le' (hwb x).1 (mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg hκ hθ0) (hH0 x))
1911      have h3 : |κ * θ * Hν x - κ * θ * H x| ≤ κ * |Hν x - H x| := by
1912        rw [← mul_sub, abs_mul, abs_of_nonneg (mul_nonneg hκ hθ0)]
1913        exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right (by nlinarith) (abs_nonneg _)
1914      exact mul_le_mul (exp_le_four_thirds (hub x).2) (h2.trans h3) (abs_nonneg _) (by norm_num)
1915    have := pow_le_pow_left₀ (abs_nonneg _) h1 2
1916    rw [sq_abs, mul_pow, mul_pow, sq_abs] at this
1917    calc (F x - F₁ x) ^ 2 ≤ _ := this
1918      _ = 16 / 9 * κ ^ 2 * (Hν x - H x) ^ 2 := by ring
1919  -- Schur: Hπ - H
1920  set Hπ : E → ℝ := fun x => ∫ y, q x y * f y ∂μ with hHπ
1921  have hHπm : Measurable Hπ := measurable_rowInt' hqm hfm
1922  have hπH : ∀ x, Hπ x - H x = ∫ y, q x y * (f y - 1) ∂μ := by
1923    intro x
1924    have h1 : Integrable (fun y => q x y * f y) μ := by
1925      refine Integrable.mono' ((hqi x).const_mul (16 / 9 * (1 + εG)))
1926        ((hqm.comp (measurable_const.prodMk measurable_id)).mul hfm).aestronglyMeasurable
1927        (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => ?_)
1928      rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_mul, abs_of_nonneg (hq0 x y), abs_of_nonneg (hfb y).1, mul_comm]
1929      exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right (hfb y).2 (hq0 x y)
1930    simp only [hHπ, hH, mul_sub, mul_one]
1931    rw [integral_sub h1 (hqi x)]
1932  have hSchur : ∫ x, (Hπ x - H x) ^ 2 ∂μ ≤ a ^ 2 * ∫ y, (f y - 1) ^ 2 ∂μ := by
1933    simp_rw [hπH]
1934    exact schur_sq hqm hq0 hqs ha hrow hqi (hfm.sub_const 1) (C := 16 / 9 * (1 + εG) + 1)
1935      (by linarith) hf1b
1936  -- integrability for assembly
1937  have hiQ : ∀ {g : E → ℝ}, Measurable g → ∀ {C : ℝ}, (∀ x, |g x| ≤ C) → Integrable (fun x => g x ^ 2) μ :=
1938    fun hg _ hb => intg_sq_bdd hg hb
1939  have hHπb : ∀ x, 0 ≤ Hπ x ∧ Hπ x ≤ 16 / 9 * (1 + εG) * a := by
1940    intro x
1941    have h1 : Integrable (fun y => q x y * f y) μ := by
1942      refine Integrable.mono' ((hqi x).const_mul (16 / 9 * (1 + εG)))
1943        ((hqm.comp (measurable_const.prodMk measurable_id)).mul hfm).aestronglyMeasurable
1944        (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => ?_)
1945      rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_mul, abs_of_nonneg (hq0 x y), abs_of_nonneg (hfb y).1, mul_comm]
1946      exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right (hfb y).2 (hq0 x y)
1947    refine ⟨integral_nonneg fun y => mul_nonneg (hq0 x y) (hfb y).1, ?_⟩
1948    calc Hπ x ≤ ∫ y, 16 / 9 * (1 + εG) * q x y ∂μ :=
1949          integral_mono h1 ((hqi x).const_mul _) fun y => by
1950            have := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (hfb y).2 (hq0 x y)
1951            linarith [mul_comm (q x y) (16 / 9 * (1 + εG))]
1952      _ = 16 / 9 * (1 + εG) * H x := by rw [integral_const_mul]
1953      _ ≤ 16 / 9 * (1 + εG) * a := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (hHa x) (by positivity)
1954  have hbd1 : ∀ x, |Hν x - Hπ x| ≤ D * a + 16 / 9 * (1 + εG) * a := by
1955    intro x
1956    rw [abs_le]; constructor <;> linarith [hHν0 x, hHνD x, (hHπb x).1, (hHπb x).2]
1957  have hbd2 : ∀ x, |Hπ x - H x| ≤ 16 / 9 * (1 + εG) * a + a := by
1958    intro x
1959    rw [abs_le]; constructor <;> linarith [hH0 x, hHa x, (hHπb x).1, (hHπb x).2]
1960  have hbd3 : ∀ x, |Hν x - H x| ≤ D * a + a := by
1961    intro x
1962    rw [abs_le]; constructor <;> linarith [hH0 x, hHa x, hHν0 x, hHνD x]
1963  have hI1 : Integrable (fun x => (Hν x - Hπ x) ^ 2) μ := intg_sq_bdd (hHνm.sub hHπm) hbd1
1964  have hI2 : Integrable (fun x => (Hπ x - H x) ^ 2) μ := intg_sq_bdd (hHπm.sub hHm) hbd2
1965  have hI3 : Integrable (fun x => (Hν x - H x) ^ 2) μ := intg_sq_bdd (hHνm.sub hHm) hbd3
1966  have hHν_H : ∫ x, (Hν x - H x) ^ 2 ∂μ ≤ 2 * εc ^ 2 + 2 * (a ^ 2 * ∫ y, (f y - 1) ^ 2 ∂μ) := by
1967    calc ∫ x, (Hν x - H x) ^ 2 ∂μ ≤ ∫ x, (2 * (Hν x - Hπ x) ^ 2 + 2 * (Hπ x - H x) ^ 2) ∂μ :=
1968          integral_mono hI3 ((hI1.const_mul 2).add (hI2.const_mul 2)) fun x =>
1969            sq_le_two_add (Hν x) (H x) (Hπ x)
1970      _ = 2 * ∫ x, (Hν x - Hπ x) ^ 2 ∂μ + 2 * ∫ x, (Hπ x - H x) ^ 2 ∂μ := by
1971          rw [integral_add (hI1.const_mul 2) (hI2.const_mul 2), integral_const_mul, integral_const_mul]
1972      _ ≤ _ := by linarith
1973  -- assembly
1974  have hiA : Integrable (fun x => (f x - T x) ^ 2) μ :=
1975    intg_sq_bdd (hfm.sub hTm) (C := 16 / 9 * (1 + εG) + 2) fun x => by
1976      rw [abs_le]; constructor <;> linarith [(hfb x).1, (hfb x).2, hεG, (abs_le.1 (hTb x)).1,
1977        (abs_le.1 (hTb x)).2]
1978  have hiB : Integrable (fun x => (T x - T₁ x) ^ 2) μ :=
1979    intg_sq_bdd (hTm.sub hT₁m) (C := 4) fun x => by
1980      rw [abs_le]; constructor <;> linarith [(abs_le.1 (hTb x)).1, (abs_le.1 (hTb x)).2,
1981        (abs_le.1 (hT₁b x)).1, (abs_le.1 (hT₁b x)).2]
1982  have hiC : Integrable (fun x => (T₁ x - 1) ^ 2) μ :=
1983    intg_sq_bdd (hT₁m.sub_const 1) (C := 3) fun x => by
1984      rw [abs_le]; constructor <;> linarith [(abs_le.1 (hT₁b x)).1, (abs_le.1 (hT₁b x)).2]
1985  have hiP : Integrable (fun x => (F x - F₁ x) ^ 2) μ :=
1986    intg_sq_bdd (hFm.sub hF₁m) (C := 3) fun x => by
1987      have h1 := hF₁b x
1988      have : 4 / 3 * (κ * (1 - θ) * a) ≤ 1 / 3 := by
1989        have : κ * (1 - θ) * a ≤ κ * 1 * a := by gcongr; linarith
1990        linarith
1991      rw [abs_le]; constructor <;> linarith [(hFb x).1, (hFb x).2, h1.1, h1.2]
1992  have hxsum : ∫ x, (f x - 1) ^ 2 ∂μ ≤ 3 * (∫ x, (f x - T x) ^ 2 ∂μ + ∫ x, (T x - T₁ x) ^ 2 ∂μ
1993      + ∫ x, (T₁ x - 1) ^ 2 ∂μ) := by
1994    have hiF1 : Integrable (fun x => (f x - 1) ^ 2) μ := intg_sq_bdd (hfm.sub_const 1) hf1b
1995    calc ∫ x, (f x - 1) ^ 2 ∂μ
1996        ≤ ∫ x, (3 * ((f x - T x) ^ 2 + (T x - T₁ x) ^ 2 + (T₁ x - 1) ^ 2)) ∂μ :=
1997          integral_mono hiF1 (((hiA.add hiB).add hiC).const_mul 3) fun x =>
1998            sq_le_three_add (f x) (T x) (T₁ x) 1
1999      _ = _ := by
2000          have hs1 : Integrable (fun x => (f x - T x) ^ 2 + (T x - T₁ x) ^ 2) μ := hiA.add hiB
2001          rw [integral_const_mul, integral_add hs1 hiC, integral_add hiA hiB]
2002  have hP : ∫ x, (F x - F₁ x) ^ 2 ∂μ ≤ 16 / 9 * κ ^ 2 * ∫ x, (Hν x - H x) ^ 2 ∂μ := by
2003    calc ∫ x, (F x - F₁ x) ^ 2 ∂μ ≤ ∫ x, 16 / 9 * κ ^ 2 * (Hν x - H x) ^ 2 ∂μ :=
2004          integral_mono hiP (hI3.const_mul _) hFF₁
2005      _ = _ := integral_const_mul _ _
2006  have hx0 : 0 ≤ ∫ x, (f x - 1) ^ 2 ∂μ := integral_nonneg fun y => sq_nonneg _
2007  have hHν_H' := hHν_H
2008  exact closing_algebra hx0 hs2 hxsum Q1 Q2 hP hHν_H' Q3 (sq_nonneg κ)
2009

2027/-- Jensen: `exp(-∫X) ≤ ∫ exp(-X)` for an integrable `X`. -/
2028lemma jensen_exp_neg {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] (P : Measure Ω) [IsProbabilityMeasure P]
2029    {X : Ω → ℝ} (hX : Measurable X) (hi : Integrable X P) (h0 : ∀ ω, 0 ≤ X ω) :
2030    Real.exp (-∫ ω, X ω ∂P) ≤ ∫ ω, Real.exp (-X ω) ∂P := by
2031  have hint2 : Integrable (fun ω => Real.exp (-X ω)) P :=
2032    Integrable.of_bound (Real.measurable_exp.comp hX.neg).aestronglyMeasurable 1
2033      (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun ω => by
2034        rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_pos (Real.exp_pos _)]
2035        exact Real.exp_le_one_iff.2 (by linarith [h0 ω]))
2036  set m := ∫ ω, X ω ∂P with hm
2037  have hI : Integrable (fun ω => Real.exp (-m) * (1 - (X ω - m))) P :=
2038    ((integrable_const (1:ℝ)).sub (hi.sub (integrable_const m))).const_mul _
2039  have hlin : ∫ ω, Real.exp (-m) * (1 - (X ω - m)) ∂P = Real.exp (-m) := by
2040    have h1 : Integrable (fun ω => X ω - m) P := hi.sub (integrable_const m)
2041    rw [integral_const_mul, integral_sub (f := fun _ => (1:ℝ)) (g := fun ω => X ω - m)
2042      (integrable_const _) h1, integral_sub (f := X) (g := fun _ => m) hi (integrable_const _)]
2043    simp [hm]
2044  calc Real.exp (-m) = ∫ ω, Real.exp (-m) * (1 - (X ω - m)) ∂P := hlin.symm
2045    _ ≤ ∫ ω, Real.exp (-X ω) ∂P := by
2046      refine integral_mono hI hint2 fun ω => ?_
2047      have := Real.add_one_le_exp (-(X ω - m))
2048      calc Real.exp (-m) * (1 - (X ω - m)) ≤ Real.exp (-m) * Real.exp (-(X ω - m)) :=
2049            mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (by linarith) (Real.exp_pos _).le
2050        _ = Real.exp (-X ω) := by rw [← Real.exp_add]; ring_nf
2051

2053/-- Jensen lower bound for the cavity functional. -/
2054theorem cavG_ge (V : WeakPotential μ) {β : ℝ} (hβ : 0 ≤ β) {N n : ℕ} (hN0 : 0 < N)
2055    (hN : n + Fintype.card B ≤ N) (hn : 0 < n) (a : B → E) (x : E) :
2056    Real.exp (-((β / N) * (n * ∫ z, V.q x z
2057        ∂((pinnedLaw V (β / N) n a).map (fun y => y ⟨0, hn⟩)))))
2058      ≤ cavG V (β / N) a n x := by
2059  have hlam : 0 ≤ β / N := div_nonneg hβ (Nat.cast_nonneg N)
2060  have hP := isProb_pinnedLaw V hlam n a
2061  set P := pinnedLaw V (β / N) n a with hPdef
2062  set i₀ : Fin n := ⟨0, hn⟩ with hi₀
2063  set ν := P.map (fun y => y i₀) with hν
2064  set D := Real.exp (V.M + V.M' + β * V.γ) with hD
2065  have hD0 : 0 ≤ D := (Real.exp_pos _).le
2066  have hνP : ∀ k : Fin n, P.map (fun y => y k) = ν := fun k => marginal_symm V hlam a k i₀
2067  have hνle : ν ≤ ENNReal.ofReal D • μ := marginal_le V hβ hN0 hN a i₀
2068  have hq0 : ∀ z, 0 ≤ V.q x z := fun z => V.q_nonneg x z
2069  have hqm : Measurable (V.q x) := V.measurable_q.comp (measurable_const.prodMk measurable_id)
2070  have hqi : Integrable (V.q x) μ := V.integrable_q x
2071  have : IsProbabilityMeasure ν := by rw [hν]; infer_instance
2072  have hmeas : ∀ k : Fin n, Measurable (fun y : Fin n → E => V.q x (y k)) :=
2073    fun k => hqm.comp (measurable_pi_apply k)
2074  have hint : ∀ k : Fin n, Integrable (fun y : Fin n → E => V.q x (y k)) P := by
2075    intro k
2076    have h1 : Integrable (V.q x) ν := (integral_le_of_le_smul hD0 hνle hq0 hqi).1
2077    rw [← hνP k] at h1
2078    exact (integrable_map_measure hqm.aestronglyMeasurable (measurable_pi_apply k).aemeasurable).1 h1
2079  have hmap : ∀ (g : E → ℝ), Measurable g → ∀ k : Fin n,
2080      ∫ y, g (y k) ∂P = ∫ z, g z ∂ν := by
2081    intro g hg k
2082    rw [← hνP k, integral_map (measurable_pi_apply k).aemeasurable hg.aestronglyMeasurable]
2083  have hXm : Measurable fun y : Fin n → E => (β / N) * ∑ k, V.q x (y k) :=
2084    measurable_const.mul (Finset.measurable_sum _ fun k _ => hmeas k)
2085  have hXi : Integrable (fun y : Fin n → E => (β / N) * ∑ k, V.q x (y k)) P :=
2086    (integrable_finset_sum _ fun k _ => hint k).const_mul _
2087  have hX0 : ∀ y : Fin n → E, 0 ≤ (β / N) * ∑ k, V.q x (y k) := fun y =>
2088    mul_nonneg hlam (Finset.sum_nonneg fun k _ => hq0 _)
2089  have key := jensen_exp_neg P hXm hXi hX0
2090  have e2 : ∫ y, (β / N) * ∑ k, V.q x (y k) ∂P = (β / N) * (n * ∫ z, V.q x z ∂ν) := by
2091    rw [integral_const_mul, integral_finset_sum _ fun k _ => hint k,
2092      Finset.sum_congr rfl fun k _ => hmap (V.q x) hqm k]
2093    simp
2094  rw [e2] at key
2095  have e1 : cavG V (β / N) a n x = ∫ y, Real.exp (-((β / N) * ∑ k, V.q x (y k))) ∂P := by
2096    simp [cavG, neg_mul, hPdef]
2097  rw [e1]
2098  exact key

2101lemma measurable_rowInt_meas (V : WeakPotential μ) (ν : Measure E) [IsFiniteMeasure ν] :
2102    Measurable fun x => ∫ y, V.q x y ∂ν := by
2103  have : Measurable fun p : E × E => V.q p.1 p.2 := V.measurable_q
2104  exact (this.stronglyMeasurable.integral_prod_right').measurable

2106set_option maxHeartbeats 1600000 in
2107/-- **Marginal bound, abstract form** (source `N:eq:weak-marginal`): assuming the kernel inputs
2108`hKA` (cavity cut estimate) and `hKB` (`L²` convolution estimate for two nearby marginals). -/
2109theorem marginal_L2 (V : WeakPotential μ) {κ₀ : ℝ} (hκ₀ : 0 ≤ κ₀) {n : ℕ} (hn : 0 < n)
2110    (hφ : ∀ x, V.φ x = κ₀ * ∫ y, V.q x y ∂μ) (hM0 : 0 ≤ V.M) (hM'0 : 0 ≤ V.M')
2111    (hκ : weakKappa V κ₀ ≤ 1 / 2)
2112    (hs : κ₀ * V.γ * Real.exp (V.M + V.M' + κ₀ * V.γ) ≤ 1 / 4)
2113    (hs2 : 214 * (κ₀ * V.γ) ^ 2 ≤ 1)
2114    {εG εc : ℝ}
2115    (hKA : ∀ x, 2 * ((κ₀ / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) * (n * (Real.exp (V.M + V.M' + κ₀ * V.γ)
2116          * ∫ z, max (V.q x z - ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) 0 ∂μ)))
2117        + (κ₀ / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) ^ 2 / 2 * (2 * n * (Real.exp (V.M + V.M' + κ₀ * V.γ)
2118          * ∫ z, (min (V.q x z) ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) ^ 2 ∂μ)) ≤ εG)
2119    (hKB : ∀ ν₁ ν₂ : Measure E, IsProbabilityMeasure ν₁ → IsProbabilityMeasure ν₂ →
2120        ν₁ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (V.M + V.M' + κ₀ * V.γ)) • μ →
2121        ν₂ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (V.M + V.M' + κ₀ * V.γ)) • μ →
2122        (∀ A, MeasurableSet A → |ν₁.real A - ν₂.real A| ≤ weakKappa V κ₀ / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) →
2123        ∫ x, (∫ y, V.q x y ∂ν₁ - ∫ y, V.q x y ∂ν₂) ^ 2 ∂μ ≤ εc ^ 2) :
2124    ∃ f : E → ℝ≥0∞,
2125      (pinnedLaw V (κ₀ / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) (n + 1) (fun e : Empty => e.elim)).map
2126          (fun y => y ⟨0, Nat.succ_pos n⟩) = μ.withDensity f ∧ Measurable f ∧
2127        (∃ K : ℝ, ∀ x, (f x).toReal ≤ K) ∧
2128        ∫ x, ((f x).toReal - 1) ^ 2 ∂μ
2129          ≤ 150 * εG ^ 2 + 214 * κ₀ ^ 2 * εc ^ 2 + 12 * (κ₀ * V.γ / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) ^ 2 := by
2130  set N : ℕ := n + 1 with hN
2131  have hN0 : 0 < N := Nat.succ_pos n
2132  have hNR : (0 : ℝ) < N := by exact_mod_cast hN0
2133  set lam : ℝ := κ₀ / N with hlamdef
2134  have hlam : 0 ≤ lam := div_nonneg hκ₀ hNR.le
2135  set a : Empty → E := fun e => e.elim with hadef
2136  set D : ℝ := Real.exp (V.M + V.M' + κ₀ * V.γ) with hD
2137  have hDge : 1 ≤ D := by
2138    refine Real.one_le_exp ?_
2139    have := V.γ_nonneg
2140    positivity
2141  have hD0 : 0 ≤ D := by linarith
2142  have hγ := V.γ_nonneg
2143  obtain ⟨c, hc0, hmarg⟩ := marginal_cavity V hlam a (n := n) ⟨0, Nat.succ_pos n⟩
2144  have hpin : ∀ x, pinField V lam a x = κ₀ * ∫ y, V.q x y ∂μ := by
2145    intro x; simp [pinField, hφ]
2146  set f : E → ℝ := fun x => c * (Real.exp (pinField V lam a x) * cavG V lam a n x) with hfdef
2147  have hGm : Measurable (cavG V lam a n) := measurable_cavG V hlam a n
2148  have hHm : Measurable fun x => ∫ y, V.q x y ∂μ := measurable_rowInt_meas V μ
2149  have hpinm : Measurable (pinField V lam a) := (isField_pinField V hlam a).measurable
2150  have hfm : Measurable f := by
2151    simp only [hfdef]
2152    exact ((Real.measurable_exp.comp hpinm).mul hGm).const_mul c
2153  have hf0 : ∀ x, 0 ≤ f x := fun x =>
2154    mul_nonneg hc0.le (mul_nonneg (Real.exp_pos _).le (cavG_nonneg V lam a n x))
2155  have hπ : (pinnedLaw V lam (n + 1) a).map (fun y => y ⟨0, Nat.succ_pos n⟩)
2156      = μ.withDensity (fun x => ENNReal.ofReal (f x)) := hmarg
2157  have hPN : IsProbabilityMeasure (pinnedLaw V lam (n + 1) a) := isProb_pinnedLaw V hlam (n + 1) a
2158  have hπprob : IsProbabilityMeasure (μ.withDensity (fun x => ENNReal.ofReal (f x))) := by
2159    rw [← hπ]; infer_instance
2160  have hlint : ∫⁻ x, ENNReal.ofReal (f x) ∂μ = 1 := by
2161    have := hπprob.measure_univ
2162    rwa [withDensity_apply _ MeasurableSet.univ, Measure.restrict_univ] at this
2163  have hf1 : ∫ x, f x ∂μ = 1 := by
2164    rw [integral_eq_lintegral_of_nonneg_ae (Filter.Eventually.of_forall hf0) hfm.aestronglyMeasurable,
2165      hlint]; simp
2166  -- the cavity marginal
2167  have hNcav : n + Fintype.card Empty ≤ N := by simp [hN]
2168  set i₀ : Fin n := ⟨0, hn⟩ with hi₀
2169  set ν : Measure E := (pinnedLaw V lam n a).map (fun y => y i₀) with hν
2170  have hνprob : IsProbabilityMeasure ν := by
2171    have := isProb_pinnedLaw V hlam n a
2172    rw [hν]; infer_instance
2173  have hνle : ν ≤ ENNReal.ofReal D • μ := marginal_le V hκ₀ hN0 hNcav a i₀
2174  have hq0 : ∀ x z, 0 ≤ V.q x z := V.q_nonneg
2175  have hqxm : ∀ x, Measurable (V.q x) := fun x =>
2176    V.measurable_q.comp (measurable_const.prodMk measurable_id)
2177  set Hν : E → ℝ := fun x => ∫ z, V.q x z ∂ν with hHν
2178  have hHνm : Measurable Hν := measurable_rowInt_meas V ν
2179  have hHν0 : ∀ x, 0 ≤ Hν x := fun x => integral_nonneg fun z => hq0 x z
2180  have hHνD : ∀ x, Hν x ≤ D * V.γ := by
2181    intro x
2182    have := (integral_le_of_le_smul hD0 hνle (hq0 x) (V.integrable_q x)).2
2183    exact this.trans (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (V.row_le x) hD0)
2184  -- the Laplace residual
2185  set θ : ℝ := (n : ℝ) / N with hθ
2186  have hθ0 : 0 ≤ θ := by positivity
2187  have hθ1 : θ ≤ 1 := by
2188    rw [hθ, div_le_one hNR]; simp [hN]
2189  have hexp : ∀ x, lam * (n * ∫ z, V.q x z ∂ν) = κ₀ * θ * Hν x := by
2190    intro x; simp only [hlamdef, hθ, hHν]; ring
2191  have hG1 : ∀ x, |cavG V lam a n x - Real.exp (-(κ₀ * θ * Hν x))| ≤ εG := by
2192    intro x
2193    have h := cavG_residual V hκ₀ hN0 hNcav hn a hκ x (L := (N : ℝ)) hNR.le
2194    rw [hexp] at h
2195    exact h.trans (hKA x)
2196  have hG2 : ∀ x, Real.exp (-(κ₀ * θ * Hν x)) ≤ cavG V lam a n x := by
2197    intro x
2198    have h := cavG_ge V hκ₀ hN0 hNcav hn a x
2199    rw [hexp] at h
2200    exact h
2201  -- the full marginal and the comparison
2202  set π : Measure E := μ.withDensity (fun x => ENNReal.ofReal (f x)) with hπdef
2203  have hπle : π ≤ ENNReal.ofReal D • μ := by
2204    have h := marginal_le V hκ₀ hN0 (n := n + 1) (B := Empty) (by simp [hN]) a
2205      (⟨0, Nat.succ_pos n⟩ : Fin (n + 1))
2206    rw [hπ] at h
2207    exact h
2208  have hTV : ∀ A, MeasurableSet A → |ν.real A - π.real A| ≤ weakKappa V κ₀ / N := by
2209    intro A hA
2210    have h := rvh_pin_removed V hκ₀ hN0 (n := n) (B := Empty) (by simp [hN]) a hκ
2211      (⟨0, Nat.succ_pos n⟩ : Fin (n + 1)) i₀ hA
2212    have hsym := marginal_symm V hlam a (n := n + 1) ((⟨0, Nat.succ_pos n⟩ : Fin (n + 1)).succAbove i₀)
2213      ⟨0, Nat.succ_pos n⟩
2214    rw [hsym, hπ] at h
2215    rw [abs_sub_comm]
2216    exact h
2217  have hcnv0 := hKB ν π hνprob hπprob hνle hπle hTV
2218  have hHπ : ∀ x, ∫ y, V.q x y ∂π = ∫ y, V.q x y * f y ∂μ := by
2219    intro x
2220    rw [hπdef, integral_withDensity_eq_integral_toReal_smul (hfm.ennreal_ofReal)
2221      (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun _ => ENNReal.ofReal_lt_top)]
2222    refine integral_congr_ae (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => ?_)
2223    simp only [smul_eq_mul, ENNReal.toReal_ofReal (hf0 y)]
2224    ring
2225  have hcnv : ∫ x, (Hν x - ∫ y, V.q x y * f y ∂μ) ^ 2 ∂μ ≤ εc ^ 2 := by
2226    simp_rw [← hHπ]
2227    exact hcnv0
2228  -- closing
2229  have hcl := closing_L2 (μ := μ) (q := V.q) V.measurable_q V.q_nonneg V.q_symm V.γ_nonneg V.row_le
2230    V.integrable_q hκ₀ hθ0 hθ1 hDge hs hs2 hHνm hHν0 hHνD hc0 hGm
2231    (fun x => by simp only [hfdef, hpin]) hf1 hG1 hG2 hcnv
2232  have hfK : ∀ x, f x ≤ c * Real.exp V.M := by
2233    intro x
2234    simp only [hfdef]
2235    have h1 : Real.exp (pinField V lam a x) ≤ Real.exp V.M :=
2236      Real.exp_le_exp.2 ((isField_pinField V hlam a).le x)
2237    have h2 := cavG_le_one V hlam a n x
2238    have h3 := cavG_nonneg V lam a n x
2239    calc c * (Real.exp (pinField V lam a x) * cavG V lam a n x)
2240        ≤ c * (Real.exp V.M * 1) := by
2241          refine mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left ?_ hc0.le
2242          exact mul_le_mul h1 h2 h3 (Real.exp_pos _).le
2243      _ = c * Real.exp V.M := by ring
2244  refine ⟨fun x => ENNReal.ofReal (f x), hπ, hfm.ennreal_ofReal, ⟨c * Real.exp V.M, fun x => ?_⟩, ?_⟩
2245  · rw [ENNReal.toReal_ofReal (hf0 x)]; exact hfK x
2246  have e1 : 1 - θ = 1 / N := by
2247    rw [hθ]; field_simp; simp [hN]
2248  have e2 : κ₀ * (1 - θ) * V.γ = κ₀ * V.γ / N := by rw [e1]; ring
2249  rw [e2] at hcl
2250  simp only [ENNReal.toReal_ofReal (hf0 _)]
2251  exact hcl
2252
2253end HRS.WeakRef.Abs
2254
2255end WM_C

2265variable {d : ℕ} {μ : Measure (Torus d)} [IsProbabilityMeasure μ] {a : ℝ}
2266  {q : Torus d → Torus d → ℝ}

2268/-- The weak potential of a weak kernel at coupling `κ₀`: `φ = κ₀ (q*μ)`. -/
2269def mkV (hK : WeakKernel μ a q) (ha : 0 ≤ a) {κ₀ : ℝ} (hκ₀ : 0 ≤ κ₀) : WeakPotential μ where
2270  φ := fun x => κ₀ * rowInt q μ x
2271  q := q
2272  M := κ₀ * a
2273  M' := 0
2274  γ := a
2275  measurable_φ := hK.measurable_rowInt.const_mul κ₀
2276  measurable_q := hK.meas
2277  q_nonneg := hK.nonneg
2278  q_symm := hK.symm
2279  γ_nonneg := ha
2280  φ_le := fun x => mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (hK.rowInt_le ha x) hκ₀
2281  integrable_φ := by
2282    refine Integrable.of_bound (hK.measurable_rowInt.const_mul κ₀).aestronglyMeasurable (κ₀ * a)
2283      (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun x => ?_)
2284    rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_nonneg (mul_nonneg hκ₀ (hK.rowInt_nonneg x))]
2285    exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (hK.rowInt_le ha x) hκ₀
2286  neg_le_integral_φ := by
2287    have : 0 ≤ ∫ x, κ₀ * rowInt q μ x ∂μ :=
2288      integral_nonneg fun x => mul_nonneg hκ₀ (hK.rowInt_nonneg x)
2289    simpa using this
2290  integrable_q := hK.integrable_row
2291  row_le := fun x => hK.rowInt_le ha x
2292
2293lemma pairWeight_eq_fieldWeight (hK : WeakKernel μ a q) (ha : 0 ≤ a) {κ₀ : ℝ} (hκ₀ : 0 ≤ κ₀)
2294    {N : ℕ} (hN : 0 < N) (X : Config d N) :
2295    pairWeight q N κ₀ μ X
2296      = ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (-(κ₀ * N * (∫ x, ∫ y, q x y ∂μ ∂μ) / 2)))
2297        * fieldWeight (mkV hK ha hκ₀) (pinField (mkV hK ha hκ₀) (κ₀ / N) (fun e : Empty => e.elim))
2298            (κ₀ / N) X := by
2299  unfold pairWeight fieldWeight
2300  rw [← ENNReal.ofReal_mul (Real.exp_pos _).le, ← Real.exp_add]
2301  congr 2
2302  rw [exponent_eq q μ κ₀ X hN]
2303  have hpe : pairEnergy (mkV hK ha hκ₀) X = (1 / 2) * pairSum q X := by
2304    simp [pairEnergy, pairSum, mkV]
2305  rw [hpe]
2306  have : (N : ℝ) ≠ 0 := by positivity
2307  simp only [pinField, mkV, Finset.univ_eq_empty, Finset.sum_empty, mul_zero, sub_zero, rowInt]
2308  simp only [← Finset.mul_sum]
2309  field_simp
2310  ring
2311
2312
2313lemma pairGibbsLaw_eq_pinnedLaw (hK : WeakKernel μ a q) (ha : 0 ≤ a) {κ₀ : ℝ} (hκ₀ : 0 ≤ κ₀)
2314    {N : ℕ} (hN : 0 < N) :
2315    pairGibbsLaw q N κ₀ μ
2316      = pinnedLaw (mkV hK ha hκ₀) (κ₀ / N) N (fun e : Empty => e.elim) := by
2317  set V := mkV hK ha hκ₀ with hV
2318  have hlam : 0 ≤ κ₀ / N := div_nonneg hκ₀ (Nat.cast_nonneg N)
2319  have hψ := isField_pinField V hlam (fun e : Empty => e.elim)
2320  set K₀ : ℝ≥0∞ := ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (-(κ₀ * N * (∫ x, ∫ y, q x y ∂μ ∂μ) / 2))) with hK₀
2321  have hK0 : K₀ ≠ 0 := (ENNReal.ofReal_pos.2 (Real.exp_pos _)).ne'
2322  have hKt : K₀ ≠ ⊤ := ENNReal.ofReal_ne_top
2323  have hpw : ∀ X : Config d N, pairWeight q N κ₀ μ X
2324      = K₀ * fieldWeight V (pinField V (κ₀ / N) (fun e : Empty => e.elim)) (κ₀ / N) X :=
2325    fun X => pairWeight_eq_fieldWeight hK ha hκ₀ hN X
2326  have hpp : pairPartition q N κ₀ μ
2327      = K₀ * fieldNorm V (pinField V (κ₀ / N) (fun e : Empty => e.elim)) (κ₀ / N) N := by
2328    unfold pairPartition fieldNorm
2329    simp_rw [hpw]
2330    exact lintegral_const_mul _ (measurable_fieldWeight hψ.measurable V _)
2331  unfold pairGibbsLaw pinnedLaw fieldLaw fieldDensity
2332  congr 1
2333  funext X
2334  rw [hpw, hpp, ENNReal.mul_div_mul_left _ _ hK0 hKt, div_eq_mul_inv]
2335
2336end HRS.WeakRef
2337
2338end WM_D

2340section WM_E
2341open MeasureTheory ProbabilityTheory Function

2345namespace HRS.WeakRef

2348variable {d : ℕ}

2350/-- The exponent of the pointwise envelope: `σ = s` for `s > 0` and `σ = d/4` for `s = 0`. -/
2351def sigmaOf (d : ℕ) (s : ℝ) : ℝ := if s = 0 then (d : ℝ) / 4 else s
2352
2353lemma continuous_of_lift'' {E : Type*} [TopologicalSpace E] {f : Torus d → E}
2354    (h : Continuous (Torus.lift f)) : Continuous f := by
2355  have h1 : IsOpenQuotientMap
2356      (Pi.map (fun _ : Fin d => ((↑) : ℝ → UnitAddCircle))) :=
2357    IsOpenQuotientMap.piMap fun _ => QuotientAddGroup.isOpenQuotientMap_mk
2358  have h2 : IsOpenQuotientMap (Torus.mk : Euc d → Torus d) :=
2359    h1.comp (PiLp.homeomorph 2 (fun _ : Fin d => ℝ)).isOpenQuotientMap
2360  exact h2.isQuotientMap.continuous_iff.2 h
2361
2362lemma exists_bound_smooth {r : Torus d → ℝ} (hr : Torus.IsSmooth r) : ∃ R : ℝ, 0 ≤ R ∧ ∀ x, |r x| ≤ R := by
2363  have hc : Continuous r := continuous_of_lift'' (show ContDiff ℝ ((⊤ : ℕ∞) : WithTop ℕ∞) (Torus.lift r) from hr).continuous
2364  obtain ⟨R, hR⟩ := isCompact_univ.exists_bound_of_continuousOn hc.continuousOn
2365  exact ⟨max R 0, le_max_right _ _, fun x => (hR x trivial).trans (le_max_left _ _)⟩
2366
2367
2368lemma sup_le_euc (y : Fin d → ℝ) : ‖y‖ ≤ ‖(WithLp.toLp 2 y : Euc d)‖ := by
2369  refine (pi_norm_le_iff_of_nonneg (norm_nonneg _)).2 fun i => ?_
2370  simpa using PiLp.norm_apply_le (WithLp.toLp 2 y : Euc d) i
2371
2372lemma euc_le_sup (y : Fin d → ℝ) : ‖(WithLp.toLp 2 y : Euc d)‖ ≤ Real.sqrt d * ‖y‖ := by
2373  rw [EuclideanSpace.norm_eq]
2374  have h : ∑ i, ‖(WithLp.toLp 2 y : Euc d) i‖ ^ 2 ≤ d * ‖y‖ ^ 2 := by
2375    calc ∑ i, ‖(WithLp.toLp 2 y : Euc d) i‖ ^ 2 ≤ ∑ _i : Fin d, ‖y‖ ^ 2 := by
2376          refine Finset.sum_le_sum fun i _ => ?_
2377          have : ‖(WithLp.toLp 2 y : Euc d) i‖ = ‖y i‖ := rfl
2378          rw [this]
2379          exact pow_le_pow_left₀ (norm_nonneg _) (norm_le_pi_norm y i) 2
2380      _ = d * ‖y‖ ^ 2 := by simp
2381  calc Real.sqrt (∑ i, ‖(WithLp.toLp 2 y : Euc d) i‖ ^ 2) ≤ Real.sqrt (d * ‖y‖ ^ 2) :=
2382        Real.sqrt_le_sqrt h
2383    _ = Real.sqrt d * ‖y‖ := by
2384        rw [Real.sqrt_mul (Nat.cast_nonneg d), Real.sqrt_sq (norm_nonneg _)]
2385
2386/-- Pointwise envelope of the Riesz kernel (source: `w = g + c_* ≤ C(1 + |x|^{-σ})`). -/
2387theorem riesz_envelope (hd : 1 ≤ d) {s : ℝ} (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) (c : ℝ) :
2388    ∃ B : ℝ, 1 ≤ B ∧ ∀ y ∈ Box d, y ≠ 0 →
2389      periodicRiesz d s (boxPt y) + c ≤ B * (1 + ‖y‖ ^ (-(sigmaOf d s))) := by
2390  have hd0 : (0 : ℝ) < d := by exact_mod_cast hd
2391  have hsqrt : 0 < Real.sqrt d := Real.sqrt_pos.2 hd0
2392  obtain ⟨C1, C2, hC1, hC2, hbox⟩ := CoercAux.periodicRiesz_box_bound_one hd hs0 hsd
2393  -- local bound: `g ≤ M₁ + M₂ ‖y‖^{-σ}`
2394  have key : ∃ M₁ M₂ : ℝ, 0 ≤ M₁ ∧ 0 ≤ M₂ ∧ ∀ y ∈ Box d, y ≠ 0 →
2395      periodicRiesz d s (boxPt y) ≤ M₁ + M₂ * ‖y‖ ^ (-(sigmaOf d s)) := by
2396    by_cases hs : s = 0
2397    · subst hs
2398      have hσ : sigmaOf d 0 = (d : ℝ) / 4 := by simp [sigmaOf]
2399      obtain ⟨r, δ, hδ, hr, hloc⟩ := KernelD1Aux.log_sing_d1 hd
2400      obtain ⟨R, hR0, hR⟩ := exists_bound_smooth hr
2401      set δ' := δ / Real.sqrt d with hδ'
2402      have hδ'0 : 0 < δ' := div_pos hδ hsqrt
2403      refine ⟨C1 * δ' ^ (-((0 + (d : ℝ)) / 2)) + C2 + R, 4 / d, by positivity, by positivity, ?_⟩
2404      intro y hy hy0
2405      have hyn : 0 < ‖y‖ := norm_pos_iff.2 hy0
2406      rw [hσ]
2407      by_cases hsmall : ‖y‖ < δ'
2408      · have hx : ‖(WithLp.toLp 2 y : Euc d)‖ < δ := by
2409          calc ‖(WithLp.toLp 2 y : Euc d)‖ ≤ Real.sqrt d * ‖y‖ := euc_le_sup y
2410            _ < Real.sqrt d * δ' := mul_lt_mul_of_pos_left hsmall hsqrt
2411            _ = δ := by rw [hδ']; field_simp
2412        have hx0 : (WithLp.toLp 2 y : Euc d) ≠ 0 := by
2413          intro h; apply hy0
2414          have := congrArg (fun x : Euc d => (x : Fin d → ℝ)) h
2415          simpa using this
2416        have hmk : boxPt y = Torus.mk (WithLp.toLp 2 y : Euc d) := rfl
2417        rw [hmk, hloc _ hx hx0]
2418        have h1 : Real.log ‖y‖ ≤ Real.log ‖(WithLp.toLp 2 y : Euc d)‖ :=
2419          Real.log_le_log hyn (sup_le_euc y)
2420        have h2 : -Real.log ‖y‖ ≤ ‖y‖ ^ (-((d : ℝ) / 4)) / ((d : ℝ) / 4) := by
2421          have := Real.log_le_rpow_div (inv_nonneg.2 hyn.le) (show 0 < (d : ℝ) / 4 by positivity)
2422          rw [Real.log_inv, Real.inv_rpow hyn.le] at this
2423          rwa [← Real.rpow_neg hyn.le] at this
2424        have h3 := hR (Torus.mk (WithLp.toLp 2 y : Euc d))
2425        have h4 := (abs_le.1 h3).2
2426        have hCC : 0 ≤ C1 * δ' ^ (-((0 + (d : ℝ)) / 2)) := mul_nonneg hC1 (Real.rpow_nonneg hδ'0.le _)
2427        have h5 : 0 ≤ ‖y‖ ^ (-((d : ℝ) / 4)) := Real.rpow_nonneg hyn.le _
2428        calc -Real.log ‖(WithLp.toLp 2 y : Euc d)‖ + r (Torus.mk (WithLp.toLp 2 y : Euc d))
2429            ≤ ‖y‖ ^ (-((d : ℝ) / 4)) / ((d : ℝ) / 4) + R := by linarith
2430          _ ≤ _ := by
2431            have : ‖y‖ ^ (-((d : ℝ) / 4)) / ((d : ℝ) / 4) = 4 / d * ‖y‖ ^ (-((d : ℝ) / 4)) := by
2432              field_simp
2433            rw [this]; linarith
2434      · push_neg at hsmall
2435        have h1 := hbox y hy hy0
2436        have h2 : ‖y‖ ^ (-((0 + (d : ℝ)) / 2)) ≤ δ' ^ (-((0 + (d : ℝ)) / 2)) :=
2437          Real.rpow_le_rpow_of_nonpos hδ'0 hsmall (by have : (0:ℝ) ≤ (0 + (d:ℝ)) / 2 := by positivity
2438                                                      linarith)
2439        have h5 : 0 ≤ ‖y‖ ^ (-((d : ℝ) / 4)) := Real.rpow_nonneg hyn.le _
2440        calc periodicRiesz d 0 (boxPt y) ≤ |periodicRiesz d 0 (boxPt y)| := le_abs_self _
2441          _ ≤ C1 * ‖y‖ ^ (-((0 + (d : ℝ)) / 2)) + C2 := h1
2442          _ ≤ C1 * δ' ^ (-((0 + (d : ℝ)) / 2)) + C2 := by gcongr
2443          _ ≤ _ := by
2444            have : 0 ≤ 4 / (d : ℝ) * ‖y‖ ^ (-((d : ℝ) / 4)) := by positivity
2445            linarith
2446    · have hs0' : 0 < s := lt_of_le_of_ne hs0 (Ne.symm hs)
2447      have hσ : sigmaOf d s = s := by simp [sigmaOf, hs]
2448      obtain ⟨r, δ, hδ, hr, hloc⟩ := KernelD1Aux.local_sing_d1 hd hs0' hsd
2449      obtain ⟨R, hR0, hR⟩ := exists_bound_smooth hr
2450      set δ' := δ / Real.sqrt d with hδ'
2451      have hδ'0 : 0 < δ' := div_pos hδ hsqrt
2452      refine ⟨C1 * δ' ^ (-((s + (d : ℝ)) / 2)) + C2 + R, 1, by positivity, by norm_num, ?_⟩
2453      intro y hy hy0
2454      have hyn : 0 < ‖y‖ := norm_pos_iff.2 hy0
2455      rw [hσ]
2456      by_cases hsmall : ‖y‖ < δ'
2457      · have hx : ‖(WithLp.toLp 2 y : Euc d)‖ < δ := by
2458          calc ‖(WithLp.toLp 2 y : Euc d)‖ ≤ Real.sqrt d * ‖y‖ := euc_le_sup y
2459            _ < Real.sqrt d * δ' := mul_lt_mul_of_pos_left hsmall hsqrt
2460            _ = δ := by rw [hδ']; field_simp
2461        have hx0 : (WithLp.toLp 2 y : Euc d) ≠ 0 := by
2462          intro h; apply hy0
2463          have := congrArg (fun x : Euc d => (x : Fin d → ℝ)) h
2464          simpa using this
2465        have hmk : boxPt y = Torus.mk (WithLp.toLp 2 y : Euc d) := rfl
2466        rw [hmk, hloc _ hx hx0]
2467        have h1 : ‖(WithLp.toLp 2 y : Euc d)‖ ^ (-s) ≤ ‖y‖ ^ (-s) :=
2468          Real.rpow_le_rpow_of_nonpos hyn (sup_le_euc y) (by linarith)
2469        have h4 := (abs_le.1 (hR (Torus.mk (WithLp.toLp 2 y : Euc d)))).2
2470        have hCC : 0 ≤ C1 * δ' ^ (-((s + (d : ℝ)) / 2)) := mul_nonneg hC1 (Real.rpow_nonneg hδ'0.le _)
2471        linarith
2472      · push_neg at hsmall
2473        have h1 := hbox y hy hy0
2474        have h2 : ‖y‖ ^ (-((s + (d : ℝ)) / 2)) ≤ δ' ^ (-((s + (d : ℝ)) / 2)) :=
2475          Real.rpow_le_rpow_of_nonpos hδ'0 hsmall (by have : (0:ℝ) ≤ (s + (d:ℝ)) / 2 := by positivity
2476                                                      linarith)
2477        have h5 : 0 ≤ ‖y‖ ^ (-s) := Real.rpow_nonneg hyn.le _
2478        calc periodicRiesz d s (boxPt y) ≤ |periodicRiesz d s (boxPt y)| := le_abs_self _
2479          _ ≤ C1 * ‖y‖ ^ (-((s + (d : ℝ)) / 2)) + C2 := h1
2480          _ ≤ C1 * δ' ^ (-((s + (d : ℝ)) / 2)) + C2 := by gcongr
2481          _ ≤ _ := by linarith
2482  obtain ⟨M₁, M₂, hM₁, hM₂, hM⟩ := key
2483  refine ⟨max (max (M₁ + |c|) M₂) 1, le_max_right _ _, fun y hy hy0 => ?_⟩
2484  have hyn : 0 < ‖y‖ := norm_pos_iff.2 hy0
2485  have h5 : 0 ≤ ‖y‖ ^ (-(sigmaOf d s)) := Real.rpow_nonneg hyn.le _
2486  have h1 := hM y hy hy0
2487  have hb1 : M₁ + |c| ≤ max (max (M₁ + |c|) M₂) 1 := (le_max_left _ _).trans (le_max_left _ _)
2488  have hb2 : M₂ ≤ max (max (M₁ + |c|) M₂) 1 := (le_max_right _ _).trans (le_max_left _ _)
2489  have hc := le_abs_self c
2490  nlinarith [mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hb2 h5, abs_nonneg c]
2491
2492end HRS.WeakRef
2493
2494end WM_E

2505/-- Polar coordinates for the sup norm on `ℝ^d`. -/
2506lemma polar_integral (hd : 1 ≤ d) (f : ℝ → ℝ) :
2507    ∫ y : Fin d → ℝ, f ‖y‖ = (d : ℝ) * 2 ^ d * ∫ t in Ioi (0 : ℝ), t ^ (d - 1) * f t := by
2508  have : Nonempty (Fin d) := ⟨⟨0, hd⟩⟩
2509  have h := integral_fun_norm_addHaar (volume : Measure (Fin d → ℝ)) f
2510  have hf : Module.finrank ℝ (Fin d → ℝ) = d := by simp
2511  rw [hf] at h
2512  have hb : (volume : Measure (Fin d → ℝ)).real (Metric.ball 0 1) = 2 ^ d := by
2513    rw [Measure.real, Real.volume_pi_ball _ one_pos]
2514    simp
2515  rw [hb] at h
2516  simpa [nsmul_eq_mul, smul_eq_mul, mul_assoc] using h
2517
2518lemma polar_integrable (hd : 1 ≤ d) {f : ℝ → ℝ} :
2519    Integrable (fun y : Fin d → ℝ => f ‖y‖) ↔
2520      IntegrableOn (fun t : ℝ => t ^ (d - 1) * f t) (Ioi 0) := by
2521  have : Nonempty (Fin d) := ⟨⟨0, hd⟩⟩
2522  have h := integrable_fun_norm_addHaar (volume : Measure (Fin d → ℝ)) (f := f)
2523  have hf : Module.finrank ℝ (Fin d → ℝ) = d := by simp
2524  rw [hf] at h
2525  simpa [smul_eq_mul] using h
2526
2527
2528lemma radial_pow_mul (hd : 1 ≤ d) {α t : ℝ} (ht : 0 < t) :
2529    t ^ (d - 1) * t ^ (-α) = t ^ ((d : ℝ) - 1 - α) := by
2530  have h1 : t ^ (d - 1) = t ^ (((d - 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) := (Real.rpow_natCast t (d - 1)).symm
2531  rw [h1, ← Real.rpow_add ht]
2532  congr 1
2533  rw [Nat.cast_sub hd]; push_cast; ring
2534
2535/-- Annulus integral of a power of the radius. -/
2536lemma polar_Ico_power (hd : 1 ≤ d) {α r R : ℝ} (hr : 0 < r) (hrR : r ≤ R) :
2537    ∫⁻ y : Fin d → ℝ, ENNReal.ofReal ((Ico r R).indicator (fun t => t ^ (-α)) ‖y‖)
2538      = ENNReal.ofReal ((d : ℝ) * 2 ^ d * ∫ t in r..R, t ^ ((d : ℝ) - 1 - α)) := by
2539  set f : ℝ → ℝ := (Ico r R).indicator (fun t => t ^ (-α)) with hf
2540  have hcont : ContinuousOn (fun t : ℝ => t ^ ((d : ℝ) - 1 - α)) (Icc r R) :=
2541    ContinuousOn.rpow_const continuousOn_id fun t ht =>
2542      Or.inl (show t ≠ 0 from ne_of_gt (hr.trans_le ht.1))
2543  have hIco : IntegrableOn (fun t : ℝ => t ^ ((d : ℝ) - 1 - α)) (Ico r R) :=
2544    hcont.integrableOn_Icc.mono_set Ico_subset_Icc_self
2545  have hfun : (fun t : ℝ => t ^ (d - 1) * f t)
2546      = (Ico r R).indicator (fun t : ℝ => t ^ ((d : ℝ) - 1 - α)) := by
2547    funext t
2548    by_cases ht : t ∈ Ico r R
2549    · simp only [hf, indicator_of_mem ht]
2550      exact radial_pow_mul hd (hr.trans_le ht.1)
2551    · simp [hf, indicator_of_notMem ht]
2552  have hintOn : IntegrableOn (fun t : ℝ => t ^ (d - 1) * f t) (Ioi 0) := by
2553    rw [hfun]
2554    exact (integrable_indicator_iff measurableSet_Ico).2 hIco |>.integrableOn
2555  have hint : Integrable (fun y : Fin d → ℝ => f ‖y‖) := (polar_integrable hd).2 hintOn
2556  have hnn : ∀ t, 0 ≤ f t := fun t => by
2557    by_cases ht : t ∈ Ico r R
2558    · simp only [hf, indicator_of_mem ht]
2559      exact Real.rpow_nonneg (hr.le.trans ht.1) _
2560    · simp [hf, indicator_of_notMem ht]
2561  rw [← ofReal_integral_eq_lintegral_ofReal hint (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => hnn _)]
2562  rw [polar_integral hd f]
2563  congr 2
2564  rw [hfun, setIntegral_indicator measurableSet_Ico]
2565  have : Ioi (0 : ℝ) ∩ Ico r R = Ico r R := by
2566    ext t; simp only [mem_inter_iff, mem_Ico, mem_Ioi]
2567    exact ⟨fun h => h.2, fun h => ⟨hr.trans_le h.1, h⟩⟩
2568  rw [this, intervalIntegral.integral_of_le hrR, integral_Ioc_eq_integral_Ioo, ← integral_Ico_eq_integral_Ioo]
2569
2570
2571/-- Ball integral of a power of the radius. -/
2572lemma polar_Ioo_power (hd : 1 ≤ d) {α ρ : ℝ} (hρ : 0 < ρ) (hα : α < d) :
2573    ∫⁻ y : Fin d → ℝ, ENNReal.ofReal ((Ioo 0 ρ).indicator (fun t => t ^ (-α)) ‖y‖)
2574      = ENNReal.ofReal ((d : ℝ) * 2 ^ d * (ρ ^ ((d : ℝ) - α) / ((d : ℝ) - α))) := by
2575  set f : ℝ → ℝ := (Ioo 0 ρ).indicator (fun t => t ^ (-α)) with hf
2576  have he : (-1 : ℝ) < (d : ℝ) - 1 - α := by linarith
2577  have hIoo : IntegrableOn (fun t : ℝ => t ^ ((d : ℝ) - 1 - α)) (Ioo 0 ρ) := by
2578    have := (intervalIntegral.intervalIntegrable_rpow' (a := 0) (b := ρ) he)
2579    exact (intervalIntegrable_iff_integrableOn_Ioc_of_le hρ.le |>.1 this).mono_set Ioo_subset_Ioc_self
2580  have hfun : (fun t : ℝ => t ^ (d - 1) * f t)
2581      = (Ioo 0 ρ).indicator (fun t : ℝ => t ^ ((d : ℝ) - 1 - α)) := by
2582    funext t
2583    by_cases ht : t ∈ Ioo 0 ρ
2584    · simp only [hf, indicator_of_mem ht]
2585      exact radial_pow_mul hd ht.1
2586    · simp [hf, indicator_of_notMem ht]
2587  have hintOn : IntegrableOn (fun t : ℝ => t ^ (d - 1) * f t) (Ioi 0) := by
2588    rw [hfun]
2589    exact (integrable_indicator_iff measurableSet_Ioo).2 hIoo |>.integrableOn
2590  have hint : Integrable (fun y : Fin d → ℝ => f ‖y‖) := (polar_integrable hd).2 hintOn
2591  have hnn : ∀ t, 0 ≤ f t := fun t => by
2592    by_cases ht : t ∈ Ioo 0 ρ
2593    · simp only [hf, indicator_of_mem ht]
2594      exact Real.rpow_nonneg ht.1.le _
2595    · simp [hf, indicator_of_notMem ht]
2596  rw [← ofReal_integral_eq_lintegral_ofReal hint (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => hnn _)]
2597  rw [polar_integral hd f]
2598  congr 2
2599  rw [hfun, setIntegral_indicator measurableSet_Ioo]
2600  have : Ioi (0 : ℝ) ∩ Ioo 0 ρ = Ioo 0 ρ := by
2601    ext t; simp only [mem_inter_iff, mem_Ioo, mem_Ioi]
2602    exact ⟨fun h => h.2, fun h => ⟨h.1, h⟩⟩
2603  rw [this, ← integral_Ioc_eq_integral_Ioo, ← intervalIntegral.integral_of_le hρ.le,
2604    integral_rpow (Or.inl he)]
2605  have h0 : (0 : ℝ) ^ ((d : ℝ) - 1 - α + 1) = 0 := Real.zero_rpow (by linarith)
2606  rw [h0]
2607  have : (d : ℝ) - 1 - α + 1 = (d : ℝ) - α := by ring
2608  rw [this]; ring

2611open HRS.KernelAux in
2612/-- Transfer of a torus integral to a radial integral on `ℝ^d`. -/
2613lemma lintegral_torus_le_radial (hd : 1 ≤ d) (F : Torus d → ℝ≥0∞) (φ : ℝ → ℝ)
2614    (hF : ∀ y ∈ Box d, y ≠ 0 → F (boxPt y) ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (φ ‖y‖)) :
2615    ∫⁻ z : Torus d, F z ≤ ∫⁻ y : Fin d → ℝ, ENNReal.ofReal (φ ‖y‖) := by
2616  rw [lintegral_torus_box]
2617  refine le_trans (lintegral_mono_ae ?_) (setLIntegral_le_lintegral _ _)
2618  filter_upwards [ae_restrict_of_ae (ae_ne_pi hd), ae_restrict_mem (measurableSet_Box d)] with y hy hyB
2619  exact hF y hyB hy

2622open HRS.KernelAux in
2623/-- First level-set integral: `∫ (w - L)_+ ≤ C L^{1 - d/σ}`. -/
2624theorem lev1 (hd : 1 ≤ d) {σ B : ℝ} (hσ0 : 0 < σ) (hσd : σ < d) (hB1 : 1 ≤ B)
2625    {w : Torus d → ℝ} (hw : ∀ y ∈ Box d, y ≠ 0 → w (boxPt y) ≤ B * (1 + ‖y‖ ^ (-σ))) :
2626    ∃ C : ℝ, 0 ≤ C ∧ ∀ L : ℝ, 0 < L →
2627      ∫⁻ z : Torus d, ENNReal.ofReal (max (w z - L) 0)
2628        ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (C * L ^ (1 - (d : ℝ) / σ)) := by
2629  have hB0 : 0 < B := by linarith
2630  have hdσ : 0 < (d : ℝ) - σ := by linarith
2631  set K : ℝ := (d : ℝ) * 2 ^ d / ((d : ℝ) - σ) with hK
2632  have hK0 : 0 ≤ K := by positivity
2633  refine ⟨(B * K + B * (1 + K)) * (2 * B) ^ ((d : ℝ) / σ - 1), by positivity, fun L hL => ?_⟩
2634  have hexp : 1 - (d : ℝ) / σ < 0 := by
2635    have : 1 < (d : ℝ) / σ := by rw [lt_div_iff₀ hσ0]; linarith
2636    linarith
2637  by_cases hbig : 2 * B ≤ L
2638  · -- radial cutoff at ρ
2639    set ρ : ℝ := (2 * B / L) ^ (1 / σ) with hρ
2640    have hρ0 : 0 < ρ := Real.rpow_pos_of_pos (by positivity) _
2641    have hρσ : ρ ^ (-σ) = L / (2 * B) := by
2642      rw [hρ, ← Real.rpow_mul (by positivity)]
2643      have : 1 / σ * (-σ) = -1 := by field_simp
2644      rw [this, Real.rpow_neg_one, inv_div]
2645    have hpt : ∀ y ∈ Box d, y ≠ 0 → ENNReal.ofReal (max (w (boxPt y) - L) 0)
2646        ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (B * (Ioo 0 ρ).indicator (fun t => t ^ (-σ)) ‖y‖) := by
2647      intro y hy hy0
2648      have ht : 0 < ‖y‖ := norm_pos_iff.2 hy0
2649      refine ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal ?_
2650      have hwy := hw y hy hy0
2651      by_cases hlt : ‖y‖ < ρ
2652      · rw [indicator_of_mem (show ‖y‖ ∈ Ioo 0 ρ from ⟨ht, hlt⟩)]
2653        refine max_le ?_ (mul_nonneg hB0.le (Real.rpow_nonneg ht.le _))
2654        nlinarith
2655      · push_neg at hlt
2656        rw [indicator_of_notMem (show ‖y‖ ∉ Ioo 0 ρ from fun h => absurd h.2 (not_lt.2 hlt))]
2657        have h1 : ‖y‖ ^ (-σ) ≤ ρ ^ (-σ) := Real.rpow_le_rpow_of_nonpos hρ0 hlt (by linarith)
2658        rw [hρσ] at h1
2659        have h2 : B * (L / (2 * B)) = L / 2 := by field_simp
2660        refine max_le ?_ (by simp)
2661        nlinarith
2662    calc ∫⁻ z : Torus d, ENNReal.ofReal (max (w z - L) 0)
2663        ≤ ∫⁻ y : Fin d → ℝ, ENNReal.ofReal (B * (Ioo 0 ρ).indicator (fun t => t ^ (-σ)) ‖y‖) :=
2664          lintegral_torus_le_radial hd _ (fun t => B * (Ioo 0 ρ).indicator (fun t => t ^ (-σ)) t) hpt
2665      _ = ENNReal.ofReal B * ∫⁻ y : Fin d → ℝ,
2666            ENNReal.ofReal ((Ioo 0 ρ).indicator (fun t => t ^ (-σ)) ‖y‖) := by
2667          rw [← lintegral_const_mul' _ _ ENNReal.ofReal_ne_top]
2668          refine lintegral_congr fun y => ?_
2669          rw [ENNReal.ofReal_mul hB0.le]
2670      _ = ENNReal.ofReal (B * ((d : ℝ) * 2 ^ d * (ρ ^ ((d : ℝ) - σ) / ((d : ℝ) - σ)))) := by
2671          rw [polar_Ioo_power hd hρ0 hσd, ← ENNReal.ofReal_mul hB0.le]
2672      _ ≤ _ := by
2673          refine ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal ?_
2674          have hρpow : ρ ^ ((d : ℝ) - σ) = (2 * B) ^ ((d : ℝ) / σ - 1) * L ^ (1 - (d : ℝ) / σ) := by
2675            rw [hρ, ← Real.rpow_mul (by positivity), Real.div_rpow (by positivity) hL.le,
2676              show (1 / σ * ((d : ℝ) - σ)) = (d : ℝ) / σ - 1 by field_simp,
2677              div_eq_mul_inv, ← Real.rpow_neg hL.le]
2678            congr 2; ring
2679          have : B * ((d : ℝ) * 2 ^ d * (ρ ^ ((d : ℝ) - σ) / ((d : ℝ) - σ)))
2680              = B * K * (2 * B) ^ ((d : ℝ) / σ - 1) * L ^ (1 - (d : ℝ) / σ) := by
2681            rw [hρpow, hK]; ring
2682          rw [this]
2683          have hp : 0 ≤ (2 * B) ^ ((d : ℝ) / σ - 1) * L ^ (1 - (d : ℝ) / σ) := by positivity
2684          nlinarith [mul_nonneg hB0.le hK0]
2685  · -- small `L`: use the global bound
2686    rw [not_le] at hbig
2687    have hpt : ∀ y ∈ Box d, y ≠ 0 → ENNReal.ofReal (max (w (boxPt y) - L) 0)
2688        ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (2 * B * (Ioo 0 1).indicator (fun t => t ^ (-σ)) ‖y‖) := by
2689      intro y hy hy0
2690      have ht : 0 < ‖y‖ := norm_pos_iff.2 hy0
2691      have hy1 : ‖y‖ < 1 := lt_of_le_of_lt (Box_norm_le hy) (by norm_num)
2692      refine ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal ?_
2693      rw [indicator_of_mem (show ‖y‖ ∈ Ioo 0 1 from ⟨ht, hy1⟩)]
2694      have hwy := hw y hy hy0
2695      have hu1 : 1 ≤ ‖y‖ ^ (-σ) := by
2696        have := Real.rpow_le_rpow_of_nonpos ht hy1.le (show -σ ≤ 0 by linarith)
2697        simpa using this
2698      refine max_le ?_ (by have := Real.rpow_nonneg ht.le (-σ); positivity)
2699      nlinarith
2700    calc ∫⁻ z : Torus d, ENNReal.ofReal (max (w z - L) 0)
2701        ≤ ∫⁻ y : Fin d → ℝ, ENNReal.ofReal (2 * B * (Ioo 0 1).indicator (fun t => t ^ (-σ)) ‖y‖) :=
2702          lintegral_torus_le_radial hd _ (fun t => 2 * B * (Ioo 0 1).indicator (fun t => t ^ (-σ)) t) hpt
2703      _ = ENNReal.ofReal (2 * B) * ∫⁻ y : Fin d → ℝ,
2704            ENNReal.ofReal ((Ioo 0 1).indicator (fun t => t ^ (-σ)) ‖y‖) := by
2705          rw [← lintegral_const_mul' _ _ ENNReal.ofReal_ne_top]
2706          refine lintegral_congr fun y => ?_
2707          rw [ENNReal.ofReal_mul (by positivity)]
2708      _ = ENNReal.ofReal (2 * B * ((d : ℝ) * 2 ^ d * (1 ^ ((d : ℝ) - σ) / ((d : ℝ) - σ)))) := by
2709          rw [polar_Ioo_power hd one_pos hσd, ← ENNReal.ofReal_mul (by positivity)]
2710      _ ≤ _ := by
2711          refine ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal ?_
2712          have h1 : (2 * B) ^ (1 - (d : ℝ) / σ) ≤ L ^ (1 - (d : ℝ) / σ) :=
2713            Real.rpow_le_rpow_of_nonpos hL hbig.le hexp.le
2714          have h2 : (2 * B) ^ ((d : ℝ) / σ - 1) * (2 * B) ^ (1 - (d : ℝ) / σ) = 1 := by
2715            rw [← Real.rpow_add (by positivity)]; simp
2716          have : 2 * B * ((d : ℝ) * 2 ^ d * (1 ^ ((d : ℝ) - σ) / ((d : ℝ) - σ))) = 2 * B * K := by
2717            rw [Real.one_rpow, hK]; ring
2718          rw [this]
2719          have hp : 0 ≤ (2 * B) ^ ((d : ℝ) / σ - 1) := by positivity
2720          have h3 : 2 * B * K ≤ (B * K + B * (1 + K)) * ((2 * B) ^ ((d : ℝ) / σ - 1) * (2 * B) ^ (1 - (d : ℝ) / σ)) := by
2721            rw [h2]; nlinarith [mul_nonneg hB0.le hK0]
2722          calc 2 * B * K ≤ _ := h3
2723            _ ≤ (B * K + B * (1 + K)) * (2 * B) ^ ((d : ℝ) / σ - 1) * L ^ (1 - (d : ℝ) / σ) := by
2724              rw [mul_assoc]
2725              refine mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left ?_ (by positivity)
2726              exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h1 hp
2727
