2729open HRS.KernelAux in
2730/-- Second level-set integral, reduced to an annulus integral. -/
2731theorem lev2_base (hd : 1 ≤ d) {σ B : ℝ} (hσ0 : 0 < σ) (hσd : σ < d) (hB1 : 1 ≤ B)
2732    {w : Torus d → ℝ} (hw : ∀ y ∈ Box d, y ≠ 0 → w (boxPt y) ≤ B * (1 + ‖y‖ ^ (-σ)))
2733    (hw0 : ∀ y ∈ Box d, y ≠ 0 → 0 ≤ w (boxPt y)) {L : ℝ} (hL : 1 ≤ L) :
2734    ∫⁻ z : Torus d, ENNReal.ofReal ((min (w z) L) ^ 2)
2735      ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (L ^ 2 * 2 ^ d * (L ^ (-(1 / σ))) ^ d + 2 * B ^ 2 * 2 ^ d
2736          + 2 * B ^ 2 * ((d : ℝ) * 2 ^ d * ∫ t in (L ^ (-(1 / σ)))..1, t ^ ((d : ℝ) - 1 - 2 * σ))) := by
2737  have hB0 : 0 < B := by linarith
2738  have hL0 : 0 < L := by linarith
2739  set r : ℝ := L ^ (-(1 / σ)) with hr
2740  have hr0 : 0 < r := Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hL0 _
2741  have hr1 : r ≤ 1 := Real.rpow_le_one_of_one_le_of_nonpos hL (by have : 0 < 1 / σ := by positivity
2742                                                                  linarith)
2743  have hrσ : r ^ (-σ) = L := by
2744    rw [hr, ← Real.rpow_mul hL0.le]
2745    have : -(1 / σ) * (-σ) = 1 := by field_simp
2746    rw [this, Real.rpow_one]
2747  have hpt : ∀ y ∈ Box d, y ≠ 0 → ENNReal.ofReal ((min (w (boxPt y)) L) ^ 2)
2748      ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (L ^ 2 * (Ioo 0 r).indicator (fun t => t ^ (-(0 : ℝ))) ‖y‖
2749          + 2 * B ^ 2 * (Ioo 0 1).indicator (fun t => t ^ (-(0 : ℝ))) ‖y‖
2750          + 2 * B ^ 2 * (Ico r 1).indicator (fun t => t ^ (-(2 * σ))) ‖y‖) := by
2751    intro y hy hy0
2752    have ht : 0 < ‖y‖ := norm_pos_iff.2 hy0
2753    have hy1 : ‖y‖ < 1 := lt_of_le_of_lt (Box_norm_le hy) (by norm_num)
2754    refine ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal ?_
2755    have h1 : (Ioo (0 : ℝ) 1).indicator (fun t => t ^ (-(0 : ℝ))) ‖y‖ = 1 := by
2756      rw [indicator_of_mem (show ‖y‖ ∈ Ioo 0 1 from ⟨ht, hy1⟩)]; simp
2757    rw [h1]
2758    have hw0y := hw0 y hy hy0
2759    have hwy := hw y hy hy0
2760    by_cases hlt : ‖y‖ < r
2761    · rw [indicator_of_mem (show ‖y‖ ∈ Ioo 0 r from ⟨ht, hlt⟩)]
2762      rw [indicator_of_notMem (show ‖y‖ ∉ Ico r 1 from fun h => absurd h.1 (not_le.2 hlt))]
2763      have : (min (w (boxPt y)) L) ^ 2 ≤ L ^ 2 := by
2764        have h0 : 0 ≤ min (w (boxPt y)) L := le_min hw0y hL0.le
2765        nlinarith [min_le_right (w (boxPt y)) L]
2766      simp only [Real.rpow_zero, neg_zero, mul_one, mul_zero, add_zero]
2767      linarith [sq_nonneg B]
2768    · push_neg at hlt
2769      rw [indicator_of_notMem (show ‖y‖ ∉ Ioo 0 r from fun h => absurd h.2 (not_lt.2 hlt))]
2770      rw [indicator_of_mem (show ‖y‖ ∈ Ico r 1 from ⟨hlt, hy1⟩)]
2771      have h0 : 0 ≤ min (w (boxPt y)) L := le_min hw0y hL0.le
2772      have h2 : (min (w (boxPt y)) L) ^ 2 ≤ (w (boxPt y)) ^ 2 := by
2773        nlinarith [min_le_left (w (boxPt y)) L]
2774      have h3 : (w (boxPt y)) ^ 2 ≤ (B * (1 + ‖y‖ ^ (-σ))) ^ 2 := by
2775        nlinarith
2776      have h4 : (B * (1 + ‖y‖ ^ (-σ))) ^ 2 ≤ 2 * B ^ 2 + 2 * B ^ 2 * ‖y‖ ^ (-(2 * σ)) := by
2777        have : ‖y‖ ^ (-(2 * σ)) = (‖y‖ ^ (-σ)) ^ 2 := by
2778          rw [← Real.rpow_natCast, ← Real.rpow_mul ht.le]; congr 1; push_cast; ring
2779        rw [this]
2780        nlinarith [sq_nonneg (B * (1 - ‖y‖ ^ (-σ)))]
2781      simp only [Real.rpow_zero, neg_zero, mul_one, mul_zero, zero_add]
2782      nlinarith [sq_nonneg L]
2783  have hd0 : (0 : ℝ) < d := by exact_mod_cast hd
2784  have hnn : ∀ (α : ℝ) (S : Set ℝ) (y : Fin d → ℝ), 0 ≤ S.indicator (fun t => t ^ (-α)) ‖y‖ := by
2785    intro α S y
2786    by_cases h : ‖y‖ ∈ S
2787    · rw [indicator_of_mem h]; exact Real.rpow_nonneg (norm_nonneg _) _
2788    · rw [indicator_of_notMem h]
2789  set g₁ : (Fin d → ℝ) → ℝ≥0∞ := fun y => ENNReal.ofReal ((Ioo 0 r).indicator (fun t => t ^ (-(0 : ℝ))) ‖y‖) with hg₁
2790  set g₂ : (Fin d → ℝ) → ℝ≥0∞ := fun y => ENNReal.ofReal ((Ioo 0 1).indicator (fun t => t ^ (-(0 : ℝ))) ‖y‖) with hg₂
2791  set g₃ : (Fin d → ℝ) → ℝ≥0∞ := fun y => ENNReal.ofReal ((Ico r 1).indicator (fun t => t ^ (-(2 * σ))) ‖y‖) with hg₃
2792  have hm1 : Measurable g₁ :=
2793    (((measurable_id.pow_const _).indicator measurableSet_Ioo).comp measurable_norm).ennreal_ofReal
2794  have hm2 : Measurable g₂ :=
2795    (((measurable_id.pow_const _).indicator measurableSet_Ioo).comp measurable_norm).ennreal_ofReal
2796  have hm3 : Measurable g₃ :=
2797    (((measurable_id.pow_const _).indicator measurableSet_Ico).comp measurable_norm).ennreal_ofReal
2798  have e1 : ∫⁻ y, g₁ y = ENNReal.ofReal ((d : ℝ) * 2 ^ d * (r ^ ((d : ℝ) - 0) / ((d : ℝ) - 0))) :=
2799    polar_Ioo_power hd hr0 (α := 0) hd0
2800  have e2 : ∫⁻ y, g₂ y = ENNReal.ofReal ((d : ℝ) * 2 ^ d * ((1 : ℝ) ^ ((d : ℝ) - 0) / ((d : ℝ) - 0))) :=
2801    polar_Ioo_power hd one_pos (α := 0) hd0
2802  have e3 : ∫⁻ y, g₃ y = ENNReal.ofReal ((d : ℝ) * 2 ^ d * ∫ t in r..1, t ^ ((d : ℝ) - 1 - 2 * σ)) :=
2803    polar_Ico_power hd (α := 2 * σ) hr0 hr1
2804  have hsum : ∀ y : Fin d → ℝ, ENNReal.ofReal (L ^ 2 * (Ioo 0 r).indicator (fun t => t ^ (-(0 : ℝ))) ‖y‖
2805          + 2 * B ^ 2 * (Ioo 0 1).indicator (fun t => t ^ (-(0 : ℝ))) ‖y‖
2806          + 2 * B ^ 2 * (Ico r 1).indicator (fun t => t ^ (-(2 * σ))) ‖y‖)
2807        = ENNReal.ofReal (L ^ 2) * g₁ y + ENNReal.ofReal (2 * B ^ 2) * g₂ y
2808          + ENNReal.ofReal (2 * B ^ 2) * g₃ y := by
2809    intro y
2810    have a1 := hnn 0 (Ioo 0 r) y
2811    have a2 := hnn 0 (Ioo 0 1) y
2812    have a3 := hnn (2 * σ) (Ico r 1) y
2813    have p1 : 0 ≤ L ^ 2 * (Ioo 0 r).indicator (fun t => t ^ (-(0 : ℝ))) ‖y‖ := mul_nonneg (sq_nonneg _) a1
2814    have p2 : 0 ≤ 2 * B ^ 2 * (Ioo 0 1).indicator (fun t => t ^ (-(0 : ℝ))) ‖y‖ :=
2815      mul_nonneg (by positivity) a2
2816    have p3 : 0 ≤ 2 * B ^ 2 * (Ico r 1).indicator (fun t => t ^ (-(2 * σ))) ‖y‖ :=
2817      mul_nonneg (by positivity) a3
2818    simp only [hg₁, hg₂, hg₃]
2819    rw [ENNReal.ofReal_add (add_nonneg p1 p2) p3, ENNReal.ofReal_add p1 p2,
2820      ENNReal.ofReal_mul (sq_nonneg L), ENNReal.ofReal_mul (by positivity : (0:ℝ) ≤ 2 * B ^ 2),
2821      ENNReal.ofReal_mul (by positivity : (0:ℝ) ≤ 2 * B ^ 2)]
2822  calc ∫⁻ z : Torus d, ENNReal.ofReal ((min (w z) L) ^ 2)
2823      ≤ ∫⁻ y : Fin d → ℝ, ENNReal.ofReal (L ^ 2 * (Ioo 0 r).indicator (fun t => t ^ (-(0 : ℝ))) ‖y‖
2824          + 2 * B ^ 2 * (Ioo 0 1).indicator (fun t => t ^ (-(0 : ℝ))) ‖y‖
2825          + 2 * B ^ 2 * (Ico r 1).indicator (fun t => t ^ (-(2 * σ))) ‖y‖) :=
2826        lintegral_torus_le_radial hd _ (fun t => L ^ 2 * (Ioo 0 r).indicator (fun t => t ^ (-(0 : ℝ))) t
2827          + 2 * B ^ 2 * (Ioo 0 1).indicator (fun t => t ^ (-(0 : ℝ))) t
2828          + 2 * B ^ 2 * (Ico r 1).indicator (fun t => t ^ (-(2 * σ))) t) hpt
2829    _ = ENNReal.ofReal (L ^ 2) * (∫⁻ y, g₁ y) + ENNReal.ofReal (2 * B ^ 2) * (∫⁻ y, g₂ y)
2830          + ENNReal.ofReal (2 * B ^ 2) * (∫⁻ y, g₃ y) := by
2831        simp_rw [hsum]
2832        have hA : Measurable fun y : Fin d → ℝ => ENNReal.ofReal (L ^ 2) * g₁ y := hm1.const_mul _
2833        have hB' : Measurable fun y : Fin d → ℝ => ENNReal.ofReal (2 * B ^ 2) * g₂ y := hm2.const_mul _
2834        rw [lintegral_add_left (f := fun y => ENNReal.ofReal (L ^ 2) * g₁ y + ENNReal.ofReal (2 * B ^ 2) * g₂ y)
2835          (hA.add hB'), lintegral_add_left (f := fun y => ENNReal.ofReal (L ^ 2) * g₁ y) hA,
2836          lintegral_const_mul _ hm1, lintegral_const_mul _ hm2, lintegral_const_mul _ hm3]
2837    _ = _ := by
2838        have hJ : 0 ≤ ∫ t in r..1, t ^ ((d : ℝ) - 1 - 2 * σ) :=
2839          intervalIntegral.integral_nonneg hr1 fun t ht => Real.rpow_nonneg (hr0.le.trans ht.1) _
2840        have hrd : r ^ ((d : ℝ) - 0) = r ^ d := by
2841          rw [sub_zero, Real.rpow_natCast]
2842        have h1d : (1 : ℝ) ^ ((d : ℝ) - 0) = 1 := Real.one_rpow _
2843        rw [e1, e2, e3, hrd, h1d]
2844        have q1 : 0 ≤ (d : ℝ) * 2 ^ d * (r ^ d / ((d : ℝ) - 0)) := by
2845          have := pow_nonneg hr0.le d; rw [sub_zero]; positivity
2846        have q2 : 0 ≤ (d : ℝ) * 2 ^ d * ((1 : ℝ) / ((d : ℝ) - 0)) := by rw [sub_zero]; positivity
2847        have q3 : 0 ≤ (d : ℝ) * 2 ^ d * ∫ t in r..1, t ^ ((d : ℝ) - 1 - 2 * σ) := by positivity
2848        rw [← ENNReal.ofReal_mul (sq_nonneg L), ← ENNReal.ofReal_mul (by positivity : (0:ℝ) ≤ 2 * B ^ 2),
2849          ← ENNReal.ofReal_mul (by positivity : (0:ℝ) ≤ 2 * B ^ 2),
2850          ← ENNReal.ofReal_add (mul_nonneg (sq_nonneg L) q1) (mul_nonneg (by positivity) q2),
2851          ← ENNReal.ofReal_add (add_nonneg (mul_nonneg (sq_nonneg L) q1) (mul_nonneg (by positivity) q2))
2852            (mul_nonneg (by positivity) q3)]
2853        congr 1
2854        rw [sub_zero]
2855        have hd0' : (d : ℝ) ≠ 0 := hd0.ne'
2856        field_simp
2857
2858
2859lemma ball_term {σ L : ℝ} (hσ0 : 0 < σ) (hL : 1 ≤ L) :
2860    L ^ 2 * 2 ^ d * (L ^ (-(1 / σ))) ^ d = 2 ^ d * L ^ (2 - (d : ℝ) / σ) := by
2861  have hL0 : 0 < L := by linarith
2862  have h1 : (L ^ (-(1 / σ))) ^ d = L ^ (-((d : ℝ) / σ)) := by
2863    rw [← Real.rpow_natCast, ← Real.rpow_mul hL0.le]; congr 1; ring
2864  rw [h1]
2865  have h2 : L ^ 2 = L ^ ((2 : ℝ)) := (Real.rpow_two L).symm
2866  have h3 : L ^ ((2 : ℝ)) * L ^ (-((d : ℝ) / σ)) = L ^ (2 - (d : ℝ) / σ) := by
2867    rw [← Real.rpow_add hL0, ← sub_eq_add_neg]
2868  rw [h2]
2869  calc L ^ ((2 : ℝ)) * 2 ^ d * L ^ (-((d : ℝ) / σ))
2870      = 2 ^ d * (L ^ ((2 : ℝ)) * L ^ (-((d : ℝ) / σ))) := by ring
2871    _ = _ := by rw [h3]
2872
2873lemma annulus_power {σ L : ℝ} (hσ0 : 0 < σ) (hL : 1 ≤ L) :
2874    (L ^ (-(1 / σ))) ^ ((d : ℝ) - 2 * σ) = L ^ (2 - (d : ℝ) / σ) := by
2875  have hL0 : 0 < L := by linarith
2876  rw [← Real.rpow_mul hL0.le]; congr 1; field_simp; ring
2877
2878
2879open HRS.KernelAux in
2880theorem lev2a (hd : 1 ≤ d) {σ B : ℝ} (hσ0 : 0 < σ) (hσd : σ < d) (hB1 : 1 ≤ B)
2881    {w : Torus d → ℝ} (hw : ∀ y ∈ Box d, y ≠ 0 → w (boxPt y) ≤ B * (1 + ‖y‖ ^ (-σ)))
2882    (hw0 : ∀ y ∈ Box d, y ≠ 0 → 0 ≤ w (boxPt y)) (h2 : 2 * σ < d) :
2883    ∃ C : ℝ, 0 ≤ C ∧ ∀ L : ℝ, 1 ≤ L →
2884      ∫⁻ z : Torus d, ENNReal.ofReal ((min (w z) L) ^ 2) ≤ ENNReal.ofReal C := by
2885  have hB0 : 0 < B := by linarith
2886  have he : (-1 : ℝ) < (d : ℝ) - 1 - 2 * σ := by linarith
2887  have hpos : 0 < (d : ℝ) - 2 * σ := by linarith
2888  refine ⟨2 ^ d + 2 * B ^ 2 * 2 ^ d + 2 * B ^ 2 * ((d : ℝ) * 2 ^ d / ((d : ℝ) - 2 * σ)), by positivity, fun L hL => ?_⟩
2889  refine (lev2_base hd hσ0 hσd hB1 hw hw0 hL).trans (ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal ?_)
2890  have hL0 : 0 < L := by linarith
2891  set r : ℝ := L ^ (-(1 / σ)) with hr
2892  have hr0 : 0 < r := Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hL0 _
2893  have hr1 : r ≤ 1 := Real.rpow_le_one_of_one_le_of_nonpos hL (by have : 0 < 1 / σ := by positivity
2894                                                                  linarith)
2895  rw [ball_term hσ0 hL]
2896  have h1 : L ^ (2 - (d : ℝ) / σ) ≤ 1 := by
2897    refine Real.rpow_le_one_of_one_le_of_nonpos hL ?_
2898    have : 2 < (d : ℝ) / σ := by rw [lt_div_iff₀ hσ0]; linarith
2899    linarith
2900  have hJ : ∫ t in r..1, t ^ ((d : ℝ) - 1 - 2 * σ) ≤ 1 / ((d : ℝ) - 2 * σ) := by
2901    rw [integral_rpow (Or.inl he)]
2902    have h3 : (d : ℝ) - 1 - 2 * σ + 1 = (d : ℝ) - 2 * σ := by ring
2903    rw [h3, Real.one_rpow]
2904    have : 0 ≤ r ^ ((d : ℝ) - 2 * σ) := Real.rpow_nonneg hr0.le _
2905    exact div_le_div_of_nonneg_right (by linarith) hpos.le
2906  have h2d : (0 : ℝ) ≤ 2 ^ d := by positivity
2907  have hcoef : 0 ≤ 2 * B ^ 2 * ((d : ℝ) * 2 ^ d) := by positivity
2908  have := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hJ hcoef
2909  have e1 : 2 * B ^ 2 * ((d : ℝ) * 2 ^ d / ((d : ℝ) - 2 * σ)) = 2 * B ^ 2 * ((d : ℝ) * 2 ^ d) * (1 / ((d : ℝ) - 2 * σ)) := by
2910    field_simp
2911  nlinarith [mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h1 h2d]
2912

2915theorem lev2b (hd : 1 ≤ d) {σ B : ℝ} (hσ0 : 0 < σ) (hσd : σ < d) (hB1 : 1 ≤ B)
2916    {w : Torus d → ℝ} (hw : ∀ y ∈ Box d, y ≠ 0 → w (boxPt y) ≤ B * (1 + ‖y‖ ^ (-σ)))
2917    (hw0 : ∀ y ∈ Box d, y ≠ 0 → 0 ≤ w (boxPt y)) (h2 : 2 * σ = d) :
2918    ∃ C : ℝ, 0 ≤ C ∧ ∀ L : ℝ, 1 ≤ L →
2919      ∫⁻ z : Torus d, ENNReal.ofReal ((min (w z) L) ^ 2)
2920        ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (C * Real.log (Real.exp 1 * L)) := by
2921  have hB0 : 0 < B := by linarith
2922  refine ⟨2 ^ d + 2 * B ^ 2 * 2 ^ d + 2 * B ^ 2 * ((d : ℝ) * 2 ^ d / σ), by positivity, fun L hL => ?_⟩
2923  refine (lev2_base hd hσ0 hσd hB1 hw hw0 hL).trans (ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal ?_)
2924  have hL0 : 0 < L := by linarith
2925  set r : ℝ := L ^ (-(1 / σ)) with hr
2926  have hr0 : 0 < r := Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hL0 _
2927  rw [ball_term hσ0 hL]
2928  have hexp : 2 - (d : ℝ) / σ = 0 := by
2929    have : (d : ℝ) = 2 * σ := h2.symm
2930    rw [this]; field_simp; ring
2931  rw [hexp, Real.rpow_zero, mul_one]
2932  have hlog : 0 ≤ Real.log L := Real.log_nonneg hL
2933  have hJ : ∫ t in r..1, t ^ ((d : ℝ) - 1 - 2 * σ) = Real.log L / σ := by
2934    have : ∀ t : ℝ, t ^ ((d : ℝ) - 1 - 2 * σ) = t⁻¹ := by
2935      intro t
2936      have : (d : ℝ) - 1 - 2 * σ = -1 := by rw [← h2]; ring
2937      rw [this, Real.rpow_neg_one]
2938    simp_rw [this]
2939    rw [integral_inv_of_pos hr0 one_pos, one_div, Real.log_inv, hr, Real.log_rpow hL0]
2940    field_simp
2941  rw [hJ]
2942  have hlogeL : Real.log (Real.exp 1 * L) = 1 + Real.log L := by
2943    rw [Real.log_mul (Real.exp_pos 1).ne' hL0.ne', Real.log_exp]
2944  rw [hlogeL]
2945  have h2d : (0 : ℝ) ≤ 2 ^ d := by positivity
2946  have hc1 : 0 ≤ 2 * B ^ 2 * 2 ^ d := by positivity
2947  have hc2 : 0 ≤ 2 * B ^ 2 * ((d : ℝ) * 2 ^ d / σ) := by positivity
2948  have e1 : 2 * B ^ 2 * ((d : ℝ) * 2 ^ d * (Real.log L / σ))
2949      = 2 * B ^ 2 * ((d : ℝ) * 2 ^ d / σ) * Real.log L := by ring
2950  rw [e1]
2951  nlinarith [mul_nonneg hc2 hlog]

2954theorem lev2c (hd : 1 ≤ d) {σ B : ℝ} (hσ0 : 0 < σ) (hσd : σ < d) (hB1 : 1 ≤ B)
2955    {w : Torus d → ℝ} (hw : ∀ y ∈ Box d, y ≠ 0 → w (boxPt y) ≤ B * (1 + ‖y‖ ^ (-σ)))
2956    (hw0 : ∀ y ∈ Box d, y ≠ 0 → 0 ≤ w (boxPt y)) (h2 : (d : ℝ) < 2 * σ) :
2957    ∃ C : ℝ, 0 ≤ C ∧ ∀ L : ℝ, 1 ≤ L →
2958      ∫⁻ z : Torus d, ENNReal.ofReal ((min (w z) L) ^ 2)
2959        ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (C * L ^ (2 - (d : ℝ) / σ)) := by
2960  have hB0 : 0 < B := by linarith
2961  have hpos : 0 < 2 * σ - (d : ℝ) := by linarith
2962  refine ⟨2 ^ d + 2 * B ^ 2 * 2 ^ d + 2 * B ^ 2 * ((d : ℝ) * 2 ^ d / (2 * σ - (d : ℝ))), by positivity, fun L hL => ?_⟩
2963  refine (lev2_base hd hσ0 hσd hB1 hw hw0 hL).trans (ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal ?_)
2964  have hL0 : 0 < L := by linarith
2965  set r : ℝ := L ^ (-(1 / σ)) with hr
2966  have hr0 : 0 < r := Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hL0 _
2967  have hr1 : r ≤ 1 := Real.rpow_le_one_of_one_le_of_nonpos hL (by have : 0 < 1 / σ := by positivity
2968                                                                  linarith)
2969  rw [ball_term hσ0 hL]
2970  have h1 : 1 ≤ L ^ (2 - (d : ℝ) / σ) := by
2971    refine Real.one_le_rpow hL ?_
2972    have : (d : ℝ) / σ < 2 := by rw [div_lt_iff₀ hσ0]; linarith
2973    linarith
2974  have hJ : ∫ t in r..1, t ^ ((d : ℝ) - 1 - 2 * σ) ≤ L ^ (2 - (d : ℝ) / σ) / (2 * σ - (d : ℝ)) := by
2975    have he : (d : ℝ) - 1 - 2 * σ ≠ -1 := by intro h; linarith
2976    rw [integral_rpow (Or.inr ⟨he, by
2977      rw [Set.mem_uIcc]; push Not; exact ⟨fun h => absurd h (not_le.2 hr0), fun h => absurd h (by norm_num)⟩⟩)]
2978    have h3 : (d : ℝ) - 1 - 2 * σ + 1 = (d : ℝ) - 2 * σ := by ring
2979    rw [h3, Real.one_rpow, annulus_power hσ0 hL]
2980    have : (1 - L ^ (2 - (d : ℝ) / σ)) / ((d : ℝ) - 2 * σ)
2981        = (L ^ (2 - (d : ℝ) / σ) - 1) / (2 * σ - (d : ℝ)) := by
2982      have : (d : ℝ) - 2 * σ ≠ 0 := by intro h; linarith
2983      field_simp; ring
2984    rw [this]
2985    exact div_le_div_of_nonneg_right (by linarith) hpos.le
2986  have h2d : (0 : ℝ) ≤ 2 ^ d := by positivity
2987  have hcoef : 0 ≤ 2 * B ^ 2 * ((d : ℝ) * 2 ^ d) := by positivity
2988  have := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hJ hcoef
2989  have e1 : 2 * B ^ 2 * ((d : ℝ) * 2 ^ d / (2 * σ - (d : ℝ)))
2990      = 2 * B ^ 2 * ((d : ℝ) * 2 ^ d) * (1 / (2 * σ - (d : ℝ))) := by field_simp
2991  have e2 : 2 * B ^ 2 * ((d : ℝ) * 2 ^ d) * (L ^ (2 - (d : ℝ) / σ) / (2 * σ - (d : ℝ)))
2992      = 2 * B ^ 2 * ((d : ℝ) * 2 ^ d / (2 * σ - (d : ℝ))) * L ^ (2 - (d : ℝ) / σ) := by ring
2993  have hLp : 0 ≤ L ^ (2 - (d : ℝ) / σ) := by positivity
2994  have hc2 : 0 ≤ 2 * B ^ 2 * ((d : ℝ) * 2 ^ d / (2 * σ - (d : ℝ))) := by positivity
2995  nlinarith [mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h1 h2d, mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h1 (show 0 ≤ 2 * B ^ 2 * 2 ^ d by positivity)]
2996

3001section WM_G
3002open MeasureTheory ProbabilityTheory Function
3003open scoped ENNReal NNReal BigOperators
3004set_option linter.unusedSectionVars false

3006namespace HRS.WeakRef.Abs
3007
3008variable {E : Type*} [MeasurableSpace E] {μ : Measure E} [IsProbabilityMeasure μ]
3009  {q : E → E → ℝ}
3010
3011/-- `L²` estimate of `q * |h|` from row bounds of the two pieces `min(q,N)` and `(q - N)_+`. -/
3012theorem conv_abstract (hqm : Measurable (Function.uncurry q)) (hq0 : ∀ x y, 0 ≤ q x y)
3013    (hqs : ∀ x y, q x y = q y x) (hqi : ∀ x, Integrable (q x) μ) {N a₂ b₁ : ℝ} (hN : 0 ≤ N)
3014    (ha₂ : 0 ≤ a₂) (hb₁ : 0 ≤ b₁)
3015    (hrow2 : ∀ x, ∫ y, max (q x y - N) 0 ∂μ ≤ a₂)
3016    (hrow1 : ∀ x, ∫ y, (min (q x y) N) ^ 2 ∂μ ≤ b₁)
3017    {h : E → ℝ} (hh : Measurable h) {M ε : ℝ} (hM : 0 ≤ M) (hhM : ∀ y, |h y| ≤ M)
3018    (hε : ∫ y, |h y| ∂μ ≤ ε) :
3019    ∫ x, (∫ y, q x y * |h y| ∂μ) ^ 2 ∂μ ≤ 2 * (ε ^ 2 * b₁ + a₂ ^ 2 * (M * ε)) := by
3020  set q₁ : E → E → ℝ := fun x y => min (q x y) N with hq₁
3021  set q₂ : E → E → ℝ := fun x y => max (q x y - N) 0 with hq₂
3022  have hq₁m : Measurable (Function.uncurry q₁) := hqm.min measurable_const
3023  have hq₂m : Measurable (Function.uncurry q₂) := (hqm.sub_const N).max measurable_const
3024  have hq₁0 : ∀ x y, 0 ≤ q₁ x y := fun x y => le_min (hq0 x y) hN
3025  have hq₂0 : ∀ x y, 0 ≤ q₂ x y := fun x y => le_max_right _ _
3026  have hq₁s : ∀ x y, q₁ x y = q₁ y x := fun x y => by simp only [hq₁, hqs x y]
3027  have hq₂s : ∀ x y, q₂ x y = q₂ y x := fun x y => by simp only [hq₂, hqs x y]
3028  have hq₁N : ∀ x y, q₁ x y ≤ N := fun x y => min_le_right _ _
3029  have hq₂q : ∀ x y, q₂ x y ≤ q x y := fun x y => max_le (by linarith [hq0 x y]) (hq0 x y)
3030  have hqx : ∀ x, Measurable (q x) := fun x => hqm.comp (measurable_const.prodMk measurable_id)
3031  have hq₁x : ∀ x, Measurable (q₁ x) := fun x => hq₁m.comp (measurable_const.prodMk measurable_id)
3032  have hq₂x : ∀ x, Measurable (q₂ x) := fun x => hq₂m.comp (measurable_const.prodMk measurable_id)
3033  have hq₂i : ∀ x, Integrable (q₂ x) μ := fun x =>
3034    Integrable.mono' (hqi x) (hq₂x x).aestronglyMeasurable (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => by
3035      rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_nonneg (hq₂0 x y)]; exact hq₂q x y)
3036  have habs : Measurable fun y => |h y| := hh.abs
3037  have hM' : ∀ y, |(|h y|)| ≤ M := fun y => by rw [abs_abs]; exact hhM y
3038  -- integrability of the pieces
3039  have hi₁ : ∀ x, Integrable (fun y => q₁ x y * |h y|) μ := fun x =>
3040    intg_bdd ((hq₁x x).mul habs) (C := N * M) fun y => by
3041      rw [abs_of_nonneg (mul_nonneg (hq₁0 x y) (abs_nonneg _))]
3042      exact mul_le_mul (hq₁N x y) (hhM y) (abs_nonneg _) hN
3043  have hi₂ : ∀ x, Integrable (fun y => q₂ x y * |h y|) μ := fun x =>
3044    Integrable.mono' ((hqi x).mul_const M) ((hq₂x x).mul habs).aestronglyMeasurable
3045      (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => by
3046        rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_nonneg (mul_nonneg (hq₂0 x y) (abs_nonneg _))]
3047        exact mul_le_mul (hq₂q x y) (hhM y) (abs_nonneg _) (hq0 x y))
3048  have hsplit : ∀ x, ∫ y, q x y * |h y| ∂μ = ∫ y, q₁ x y * |h y| ∂μ + ∫ y, q₂ x y * |h y| ∂μ := by
3049    intro x
3050    rw [← integral_add (hi₁ x) (hi₂ x)]
3051    refine integral_congr_ae (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => ?_)
3052    simp only [hq₁, hq₂]
3053    rw [← add_mul]
3054    congr 1
3055    rcases le_total (q x y) N with h1 | h1
3056    · simp [min_eq_left h1, max_eq_right (by linarith : q x y - N ≤ 0)]
3057    · simp [min_eq_right h1, max_eq_left (by linarith : 0 ≤ q x y - N)]
3058  -- Part 2: Schur
3059  have hB2 : ∫ x, (∫ y, q₂ x y * |h y| ∂μ) ^ 2 ∂μ ≤ a₂ ^ 2 * (M * ε) := by
3060    have hs := schur_sq (μ := μ) hq₂m hq₂0 hq₂s ha₂ hrow2 hq₂i habs hM hM'
3061    refine hs.trans (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left ?_ (sq_nonneg _))
3062    have hsq : ∀ y, |h y| ^ 2 ≤ M * |h y| := fun y => by
3063      nlinarith [abs_nonneg (h y), hhM y]
3064    calc ∫ y, |h y| ^ 2 ∂μ ≤ ∫ y, M * |h y| ∂μ :=
3065          integral_mono (intg_sq_bdd habs (C := M) hM') (hh.abs.const_mul M |> fun hm => intg_bdd hm (C := M * M)
3066            (fun y => by
3067              rw [abs_of_nonneg (mul_nonneg hM (abs_nonneg _))]
3068              exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (hhM y) hM)) hsq
3069      _ = M * ∫ y, |h y| ∂μ := integral_const_mul _ _
3070      _ ≤ M * ε := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hε hM
3071  -- Part 1: Cauchy-Schwarz and Fubini
3072  have hint0 : Integrable (fun y => |h y|) μ := intg_bdd habs (C := M) hM'
3073  have hε0 : 0 ≤ ε := (integral_nonneg fun y => abs_nonneg _).trans hε
3074  have hpt1 : ∀ x, (∫ y, q₁ x y * |h y| ∂μ) ^ 2 ≤ ε * ∫ y, |h y| * q₁ x y ^ 2 ∂μ := by
3075    intro x
3076    have h1 := cs_weight (μ := μ) habs (fun y => abs_nonneg _) hint0 (hq₁x x) hN (fun y => by
3077      rw [abs_of_nonneg (hq₁0 x y)]; exact hq₁N x y)
3078    have e : ∫ y, q₁ x y * |h y| ∂μ = ∫ y, |h y| * q₁ x y ∂μ := by
3079      simp_rw [mul_comm (q₁ x _)]
3080    rw [e]
3081    refine h1.trans (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hε (integral_nonneg fun y => mul_nonneg (abs_nonneg _) (sq_nonneg _)))
3082  have hprod : Integrable (fun p : E × E => |h p.2| * q₁ p.1 p.2 ^ 2) (μ.prod μ) := by
3083    refine Integrable.of_bound ((habs.comp measurable_snd).mul ((hq₁m.comp measurable_id).pow_const 2)).aestronglyMeasurable
3084      (M * N ^ 2) (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun p => ?_)
3085    rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_nonneg (mul_nonneg (abs_nonneg _) (sq_nonneg _))]
3086    refine mul_le_mul (hhM _) ?_ (sq_nonneg _) hM
3087    have := hq₁0 p.1 p.2; have := hq₁N p.1 p.2
3088    nlinarith
3089  have hJm : Measurable fun x => ∫ y, |h y| * q₁ x y ^ 2 ∂μ := by
3090    have : Measurable fun p : E × E => |h p.2| * q₁ p.1 p.2 ^ 2 :=
3091      (habs.comp measurable_snd).mul ((hq₁m.comp measurable_id).pow_const 2)
3092    exact (this.stronglyMeasurable.integral_prod_right').measurable
3093  have hJi : Integrable (fun x => ∫ y, |h y| * q₁ x y ^ 2 ∂μ) μ := hprod.integral_prod_left
3094  have hJb : ∫ x, ∫ y, |h y| * q₁ x y ^ 2 ∂μ ∂μ ≤ ε * b₁ := by
3095    rw [integral_integral_swap hprod]
3096    have : ∀ y, ∫ x, |h y| * q₁ x y ^ 2 ∂μ ≤ |h y| * b₁ := by
3097      intro y
3098      rw [integral_const_mul]
3099      refine mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left ?_ (abs_nonneg _)
3100      calc ∫ x, q₁ x y ^ 2 ∂μ = ∫ x, (min (q y x) N) ^ 2 ∂μ := by
3101            refine integral_congr_ae (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun x => ?_)
3102            simp only [hq₁, hqs x y]
3103        _ ≤ b₁ := hrow1 y
3104    calc ∫ y, ∫ x, |h y| * q₁ x y ^ 2 ∂μ ∂μ ≤ ∫ y, |h y| * b₁ ∂μ :=
3105          integral_mono hprod.integral_prod_right (hint0.mul_const _) this
3106      _ = (∫ y, |h y| ∂μ) * b₁ := integral_mul_const _ _
3107      _ ≤ ε * b₁ := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hε hb₁
3108  have hA1m : Measurable fun x => ∫ y, q₁ x y * |h y| ∂μ := measurable_rowInt' hq₁m habs
3109  have hA1b : ∀ x, |∫ y, q₁ x y * |h y| ∂μ| ≤ N * M := fun x => by
3110    have := norm_integral_le_of_norm_le_const (μ := μ) (f := fun y => q₁ x y * |h y|) (C := N * M)
3111      (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => by
3112        rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_nonneg (mul_nonneg (hq₁0 x y) (abs_nonneg _))]
3113        exact mul_le_mul (hq₁N x y) (hhM y) (abs_nonneg _) hN)
3114    simpa using this
3115  have hA2m : Measurable fun x => ∫ y, q₂ x y * |h y| ∂μ := measurable_rowInt' hq₂m habs
3116  have hB1 : ∫ x, (∫ y, q₁ x y * |h y| ∂μ) ^ 2 ∂μ ≤ ε ^ 2 * b₁ := by
3117    calc ∫ x, (∫ y, q₁ x y * |h y| ∂μ) ^ 2 ∂μ ≤ ∫ x, ε * ∫ y, |h y| * q₁ x y ^ 2 ∂μ ∂μ :=
3118          integral_mono (intg_sq_bdd hA1m hA1b) (hJi.const_mul ε) hpt1
3119      _ = ε * ∫ x, ∫ y, |h y| * q₁ x y ^ 2 ∂μ ∂μ := integral_const_mul _ _
3120      _ ≤ ε * (ε * b₁) := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hJb hε0
3121      _ = ε ^ 2 * b₁ := by ring
3122  -- combine
3123  have hA2b : ∀ x, |∫ y, q₂ x y * |h y| ∂μ| ≤ M * a₂ := fun x => by
3124    have h1 : ∫ y, q₂ x y * |h y| ∂μ ≤ ∫ y, M * q₂ x y ∂μ :=
3125      integral_mono (hi₂ x) ((hq₂i x).const_mul M) fun y => by
3126        have := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (hhM y) (hq₂0 x y)
3127        linarith [mul_comm (q₂ x y) M]
3128    have h2 : ∫ y, M * q₂ x y ∂μ = M * ∫ y, q₂ x y ∂μ := integral_const_mul _ _
3129    have h3 : 0 ≤ ∫ y, q₂ x y * |h y| ∂μ := integral_nonneg fun y => mul_nonneg (hq₂0 x y) (abs_nonneg _)
3130    rw [abs_of_nonneg h3]
3131    have h4 : ∫ y, q₂ x y ∂μ ≤ a₂ := hrow2 x
3132    nlinarith
3133  have hpt : ∀ x, (∫ y, q x y * |h y| ∂μ) ^ 2
3134      ≤ 2 * (∫ y, q₁ x y * |h y| ∂μ) ^ 2 + 2 * (∫ y, q₂ x y * |h y| ∂μ) ^ 2 := by
3135    intro x
3136    rw [hsplit x]
3137    nlinarith [sq_nonneg (∫ y, q₁ x y * |h y| ∂μ - ∫ y, q₂ x y * |h y| ∂μ)]
3138  have hAm : Measurable fun x => ∫ y, q x y * |h y| ∂μ := measurable_rowInt' hqm habs
3139  have hAb : ∀ x, |∫ y, q x y * |h y| ∂μ| ≤ N * M + M * a₂ := fun x => by
3140    rw [hsplit x]
3141    exact (abs_add_le _ _).trans (add_le_add (hA1b x) (hA2b x))
3142  calc ∫ x, (∫ y, q x y * |h y| ∂μ) ^ 2 ∂μ
3143      ≤ ∫ x, (2 * (∫ y, q₁ x y * |h y| ∂μ) ^ 2 + 2 * (∫ y, q₂ x y * |h y| ∂μ) ^ 2) ∂μ :=
3144        integral_mono (intg_sq_bdd hAm hAb) (((intg_sq_bdd hA1m hA1b).const_mul 2).add
3145          ((intg_sq_bdd hA2m hA2b).const_mul 2)) hpt
3146    _ = 2 * ∫ x, (∫ y, q₁ x y * |h y| ∂μ) ^ 2 ∂μ + 2 * ∫ x, (∫ y, q₂ x y * |h y| ∂μ) ^ 2 ∂μ := by
3147        rw [integral_add ((intg_sq_bdd hA1m hA1b).const_mul 2) ((intg_sq_bdd hA2m hA2b).const_mul 2),
3148          integral_const_mul, integral_const_mul]
3149    _ ≤ 2 * (ε ^ 2 * b₁ + a₂ ^ 2 * (M * ε)) := by linarith

3152/-- A probability measure `≤ D μ` has a bounded measurable density. -/
3153lemma exists_density_le {ν : Measure E} {D : ℝ} (hD : 0 ≤ D) (hν : ν ≤ ENNReal.ofReal D • μ) :
3154    ∃ g : E → ℝ, Measurable g ∧ (∀ y, 0 ≤ g y ∧ g y ≤ D) ∧
3155      ν = μ.withDensity (fun y => ENNReal.ofReal (g y)) := by
3156  have hac : ν ≪ μ := Measure.absolutelyContinuous_of_le_smul hν
3157  haveI : IsFiniteMeasure ν := by
3158    refine ⟨?_⟩
3159    calc ν Set.univ ≤ (ENNReal.ofReal D • μ) Set.univ := hν _
3160      _ < ⊤ := by simp
3161  have hrn : ν.rnDeriv μ ≤ᵐ[μ] fun _ => ENNReal.ofReal D := by
3162    refine ae_le_of_forall_setLIntegral_le_of_sigmaFinite (Measure.measurable_rnDeriv _ _) fun A hA _ => ?_
3163    rw [Measure.setLIntegral_rnDeriv hac, setLIntegral_const]
3164    calc ν A ≤ (ENNReal.ofReal D • μ) A := hν A
3165      _ = ENNReal.ofReal D * μ A := by simp
3166  have hfin : ∀ᵐ y ∂μ, ν.rnDeriv μ y < ⊤ := Measure.rnDeriv_lt_top _ _
3167  refine ⟨fun y => min D (ν.rnDeriv μ y).toReal, ?_, fun y => ⟨le_min hD ENNReal.toReal_nonneg, min_le_left _ _⟩, ?_⟩
3168  · exact measurable_const.min (Measure.measurable_rnDeriv _ _).ennreal_toReal
3169  · conv_lhs => rw [← Measure.withDensity_rnDeriv_eq ν μ hac]
3170    refine withDensity_congr_ae ?_
3171    filter_upwards [hrn, hfin] with y h1 h2
3172    have h3 : (ν.rnDeriv μ y).toReal ≤ D := by
3173      have := ENNReal.toReal_mono ENNReal.ofReal_ne_top h1
3174      rwa [ENNReal.toReal_ofReal hD] at this
3175    rw [min_eq_right h3, ENNReal.ofReal_toReal h2.ne]
3176

3178lemma integral_density {g : E → ℝ} (hgm : Measurable g) (hg0 : ∀ y, 0 ≤ g y) {f : E → ℝ} :
3179    ∫ y, f y ∂(μ.withDensity (fun y => ENNReal.ofReal (g y))) = ∫ y, g y * f y ∂μ := by
3180  rw [integral_withDensity_eq_integral_toReal_smul hgm.ennreal_ofReal
3181    (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun _ => ENNReal.ofReal_lt_top)]
3182  refine integral_congr_ae (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => ?_)
3183  simp only [smul_eq_mul, ENNReal.toReal_ofReal (hg0 y)]

3185lemma real_density {g : E → ℝ} (hgm : Measurable g) (hg0 : ∀ y, 0 ≤ g y) (A : Set E)
3186    (hA : MeasurableSet A) :
3187    (μ.withDensity (fun y => ENNReal.ofReal (g y))).real A = ∫ y in A, g y ∂μ := by
3188  rw [Measure.real, withDensity_apply _ hA,
3189    integral_eq_lintegral_of_nonneg_ae (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => hg0 y)
3190      hgm.aestronglyMeasurable]

3193/-- Measure form of the `L²` convolution estimate. -/
3194theorem conv_measures (hqm : Measurable (Function.uncurry q)) (hq0 : ∀ x y, 0 ≤ q x y)
3195    (hqs : ∀ x y, q x y = q y x) (hqi : ∀ x, Integrable (q x) μ) {N a₂ b₁ D ε₀ : ℝ} (hN : 0 ≤ N)
3196    (ha₂ : 0 ≤ a₂) (hb₁ : 0 ≤ b₁)
3197    (hrow2 : ∀ x, ∫ y, max (q x y - N) 0 ∂μ ≤ a₂)
3198    (hrow1 : ∀ x, ∫ y, (min (q x y) N) ^ 2 ∂μ ≤ b₁) (hD : 0 ≤ D)
3199    (ν₁ ν₂ : Measure E) (h₁ : ν₁ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal D • μ) (h₂ : ν₂ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal D • μ)
3200    (hTV : ∀ A, MeasurableSet A → |ν₁.real A - ν₂.real A| ≤ ε₀) :
3201    ∫ x, (∫ y, q x y ∂ν₁ - ∫ y, q x y ∂ν₂) ^ 2 ∂μ
3202      ≤ 2 * ((2 * ε₀) ^ 2 * b₁ + a₂ ^ 2 * (D * (2 * ε₀))) := by
3203  obtain ⟨g₁, hg₁m, hg₁, rfl⟩ := exists_density_le hD h₁
3204  obtain ⟨g₂, hg₂m, hg₂, rfl⟩ := exists_density_le hD h₂
3205  set h : E → ℝ := fun y => g₁ y - g₂ y with hhdef
3206  have hhm : Measurable h := hg₁m.sub hg₂m
3207  have hhM : ∀ y, |h y| ≤ D := fun y => by
3208    rw [abs_le]; constructor <;> linarith [(hg₁ y).1, (hg₁ y).2, (hg₂ y).1, (hg₂ y).2]
3209  have hqx : ∀ x, Measurable (q x) := fun x => hqm.comp (measurable_const.prodMk measurable_id)
3210  have hε₀ : 0 ≤ ε₀ := (abs_nonneg _).trans (hTV Set.univ MeasurableSet.univ)
3211  -- L¹ bound
3212  have hg₁i : Integrable g₁ μ := intg_bdd hg₁m (C := D) fun y => by
3213    rw [abs_of_nonneg (hg₁ y).1]; exact (hg₁ y).2
3214  have hg₂i : Integrable g₂ μ := intg_bdd hg₂m (C := D) fun y => by
3215    rw [abs_of_nonneg (hg₂ y).1]; exact (hg₂ y).2
3216  have hL1 : ∫ y, |h y| ∂μ ≤ 2 * ε₀ := by
3217    set S : Set E := {y | g₂ y < g₁ y} with hS
3218    have hSm : MeasurableSet S := measurableSet_lt hg₂m hg₁m
3219    have hhi : Integrable (fun y => |h y|) μ := intg_bdd hhm.abs (C := D) fun y => by
3220      rw [abs_abs]; exact hhM y
3221    rw [← integral_add_compl hSm hhi]
3222    have e1 : ∫ y in S, |h y| ∂μ = ∫ y in S, g₁ y ∂μ - ∫ y in S, g₂ y ∂μ := by
3223      rw [← integral_sub hg₁i.integrableOn hg₂i.integrableOn]
3224      refine setIntegral_congr_fun hSm fun y hy => ?_
3225      simp only [hhdef]; rw [abs_of_pos (sub_pos.2 hy)]
3226    have e2 : ∫ y in Sᶜ, |h y| ∂μ = ∫ y in Sᶜ, g₂ y ∂μ - ∫ y in Sᶜ, g₁ y ∂μ := by
3227      rw [← integral_sub hg₂i.integrableOn hg₁i.integrableOn]
3228      refine setIntegral_congr_fun hSm.compl fun y hy => ?_
3229      have : g₁ y ≤ g₂ y := by simpa [hS] using hy
3230      simp only [hhdef]; rw [abs_of_nonpos (sub_nonpos.2 this)]; ring
3231    rw [e1, e2]
3232    have t1 := hTV S hSm
3233    have t2 := hTV Sᶜ hSm.compl
3234    rw [real_density hg₁m (fun y => (hg₁ y).1) S hSm, real_density hg₂m (fun y => (hg₂ y).1) S hSm] at t1
3235    rw [real_density hg₁m (fun y => (hg₁ y).1) Sᶜ hSm.compl,
3236      real_density hg₂m (fun y => (hg₂ y).1) Sᶜ hSm.compl] at t2
3237    rw [abs_le] at t1 t2
3238    linarith [t1.2, t2.1]
3239  -- the difference of fields
3240  have hdiff : ∀ x, ∫ y, q x y ∂(μ.withDensity (fun y => ENNReal.ofReal (g₁ y)))
3241      - ∫ y, q x y ∂(μ.withDensity (fun y => ENNReal.ofReal (g₂ y))) = ∫ y, q x y * h y ∂μ := by
3242    intro x
3243    rw [integral_density hg₁m (fun y => (hg₁ y).1), integral_density hg₂m (fun y => (hg₂ y).1)]
3244    have i1 : Integrable (fun y => g₁ y * q x y) μ :=
3245      Integrable.mono' ((hqi x).const_mul D) (hg₁m.mul (hqx x)).aestronglyMeasurable
3246        (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => by
3247          rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_nonneg (mul_nonneg (hg₁ y).1 (hq0 x y))]
3248          exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right (hg₁ y).2 (hq0 x y))
3249    have i2 : Integrable (fun y => g₂ y * q x y) μ :=
3250      Integrable.mono' ((hqi x).const_mul D) (hg₂m.mul (hqx x)).aestronglyMeasurable
3251        (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => by
3252          rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_nonneg (mul_nonneg (hg₂ y).1 (hq0 x y))]
3253          exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right (hg₂ y).2 (hq0 x y))
3254    rw [← integral_sub i1 i2]
3255    refine integral_congr_ae (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => ?_)
3256    simp only [hhdef]; ring
3257  have hsq : ∀ x, (∫ y, q x y ∂(μ.withDensity (fun y => ENNReal.ofReal (g₁ y)))
3258      - ∫ y, q x y ∂(μ.withDensity (fun y => ENNReal.ofReal (g₂ y)))) ^ 2
3259      ≤ (∫ y, q x y * |h y| ∂μ) ^ 2 := by
3260    intro x
3261    rw [hdiff x]
3262    have hb : |∫ y, q x y * h y ∂μ| ≤ ∫ y, q x y * |h y| ∂μ := by
3263      refine (abs_integral_le_integral_abs).trans ?_
3264      refine integral_mono_of_nonneg (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => abs_nonneg _) ?_
3265        (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => ?_)
3266      · exact Integrable.mono' ((hqi x).const_mul D) ((hqx x).mul hhm.abs).aestronglyMeasurable
3267          (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => by
3268            rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_nonneg (mul_nonneg (hq0 x y) (abs_nonneg _))]
3269            exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (hhM y) (hq0 x y) |>.trans (le_of_eq (mul_comm _ _)))
3270      · show |q x y * h y| ≤ q x y * |h y|
3271        rw [abs_mul, abs_of_nonneg (hq0 x y)]
3272    have := pow_le_pow_left₀ (abs_nonneg _) hb 2
3273    rwa [sq_abs] at this
3274  have hqrow : ∀ x, ∫ y, q x y ∂μ ≤ N + a₂ := by
3275    intro x
3276    have h1 : ∫ y, q x y ∂μ ≤ ∫ y, (N + max (q x y - N) 0) ∂μ :=
3277      integral_mono (hqi x) ((integrable_const N).add (Integrable.mono' (hqi x)
3278        ((hqx x).sub_const N |>.max measurable_const).aestronglyMeasurable
3279        (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => by
3280          rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_nonneg (le_max_right _ _)]
3281          exact max_le (by linarith [hq0 x y]) (hq0 x y)))) fun y => by
3282        rcases le_total (q x y) N with h | h
3283        · rw [max_eq_right (by linarith)]; linarith
3284        · rw [max_eq_left (by linarith)]; linarith
3285    have h2 : ∫ y, (N + max (q x y - N) 0) ∂μ = N + ∫ y, max (q x y - N) 0 ∂μ := by
3286      rw [integral_add (integrable_const N) (Integrable.mono' (hqi x)
3287        ((hqx x).sub_const N |>.max measurable_const).aestronglyMeasurable
3288        (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => by
3289          rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_nonneg (le_max_right _ _)]
3290          exact max_le (by linarith [hq0 x y]) (hq0 x y)))]
3291      simp
3292    linarith [hrow2 x]
3293  have hG0 : ∀ x, 0 ≤ ∫ y, q x y * |h y| ∂μ := fun x =>
3294    integral_nonneg fun y => mul_nonneg (hq0 x y) (abs_nonneg _)
3295  have hGb : ∀ x, |∫ y, q x y * |h y| ∂μ| ≤ D * (N + a₂) := by
3296    intro x
3297    rw [abs_of_nonneg (hG0 x)]
3298    have hint : Integrable (fun y => q x y * |h y|) μ :=
3299      Integrable.mono' ((hqi x).const_mul D) ((hqx x).mul hhm.abs).aestronglyMeasurable
3300        (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => by
3301          rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_nonneg (mul_nonneg (hq0 x y) (abs_nonneg _))]
3302          exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (hhM y) (hq0 x y) |>.trans (le_of_eq (mul_comm _ _)))
3303    calc ∫ y, q x y * |h y| ∂μ ≤ ∫ y, D * q x y ∂μ :=
3304          integral_mono hint ((hqi x).const_mul D) fun y => by
3305            have := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (hhM y) (hq0 x y)
3306            linarith [mul_comm (q x y) D]
3307      _ = D * ∫ y, q x y ∂μ := integral_const_mul _ _
3308      _ ≤ D * (N + a₂) := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (hqrow x) hD
3309  have hGm : Measurable fun x => ∫ y, q x y * |h y| ∂μ := measurable_rowInt' hqm hhm.abs
3310  have hmain := conv_abstract hqm hq0 hqs hqi hN ha₂ hb₁ hrow2 hrow1 hhm hD hhM hL1
3311  calc ∫ x, (∫ y, q x y ∂(μ.withDensity (fun y => ENNReal.ofReal (g₁ y)))
3312        - ∫ y, q x y ∂(μ.withDensity (fun y => ENNReal.ofReal (g₂ y)))) ^ 2 ∂μ
3313      ≤ ∫ x, (∫ y, q x y * |h y| ∂μ) ^ 2 ∂μ :=
3314        integral_mono_of_nonneg (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun x => sq_nonneg _)
3315          (intg_sq_bdd hGm hGb) (Filter.Eventually.of_forall hsq)
3316    _ ≤ _ := hmain

3331lemma sigmaOf_pos (hd : 1 ≤ d) {s : ℝ} (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) : 0 < sigmaOf d s := by
3332  unfold sigmaOf
3333  have : (0 : ℝ) < d := by exact_mod_cast hd
3334  split_ifs with h
3335  · positivity
3336  · exact lt_of_le_of_ne hs0 (Ne.symm h)
3337
3338lemma sigmaOf_lt (hd : 1 ≤ d) {s : ℝ} (hsd : s < d) : sigmaOf d s < d := by
3339  unfold sigmaOf
3340  have : (0 : ℝ) < d := by exact_mod_cast hd
3341  split_ifs with h
3342  · linarith
3343  · exact hsd
3344
3345lemma two_sigmaOf_lt_iff (hd : 1 ≤ d) {s : ℝ} (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) :
3346    2 * sigmaOf d s < d ↔ s < (d : ℝ) / 2 := by
3347  have : (0 : ℝ) < d := by exact_mod_cast hd
3348  unfold sigmaOf
3349  split_ifs with h
3350  · subst h; constructor <;> intro _ <;> linarith
3351  · constructor <;> intro h' <;> linarith
3352
3353lemma sigmaOf_eq (hd : 1 ≤ d) {s : ℝ} (hs : (d : ℝ) / 2 ≤ s) : sigmaOf d s = s := by
3354  have : (0 : ℝ) < d := by exact_mod_cast hd
3355  unfold sigmaOf
3356  rw [if_neg]
3357  intro h; subst h; linarith
3358
3359lemma inv_le_deltaN (hd : 1 ≤ d) {s : ℝ} (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) {N : ℕ} (hN : 1 ≤ N) :
3360    (N : ℝ)⁻¹ ≤ deltaN d s N := by
3361  have hN' : (1 : ℝ) ≤ N := by exact_mod_cast hN
3362  have hNpos : (0 : ℝ) < N := by linarith
3363  have hd0 : (0 : ℝ) < d := by exact_mod_cast hd
3364  unfold deltaN
3365  split_ifs with h1 h2
3366  · exact le_rfl
3367  · have : 1 ≤ Real.log (Real.exp 1 * N) := by
3368      rw [Real.log_mul (Real.exp_pos 1).ne' hNpos.ne', Real.log_exp]
3369      have := Real.log_nonneg hN'
3370      linarith
3371    have h0 : 0 ≤ (N : ℝ)⁻¹ := by positivity
3372    nlinarith
3373  · push Not at h1
3374    have hs : 0 < s := by
3375      rcases lt_or_eq_of_le h1 with h | h
3376      · linarith
3377      · have : s ≠ (d : ℝ) / 2 := h2
3378        exact absurd h.symm this
3379    have h3 : (d : ℝ) / 2 < s := lt_of_le_of_ne h1 (Ne.symm h2)
3380    have h4 : -1 ≤ 1 - (d : ℝ) / s := by
3381      have : (d : ℝ) / s ≤ 2 := by rw [div_le_iff₀ hs]; linarith
3382      linarith
3383    rw [← Real.rpow_neg_one]
3384    exact Real.rpow_le_rpow_of_exponent_le hN' h4
3385
3386lemma rpow_le_deltaN (hd : 1 ≤ d) {s : ℝ} (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) {N : ℕ} (hN : 1 ≤ N) :
3387    (N : ℝ) ^ (1 - (d : ℝ) / sigmaOf d s) ≤ deltaN d s N := by
3388  have hN' : (1 : ℝ) ≤ N := by exact_mod_cast hN
3389  have hNpos : (0 : ℝ) < N := by linarith
3390  have hd0 : (0 : ℝ) < d := by exact_mod_cast hd
3391  have hσ0 := sigmaOf_pos hd hs0
3392  unfold deltaN
3393  split_ifs with h1 h2
3394  · -- s < d/2
3395    have h2σ : 2 * sigmaOf d s < d := (two_sigmaOf_lt_iff hd hs0).2 h1
3396    have h3 : 1 - (d : ℝ) / sigmaOf d s ≤ -1 := by
3397      have : 2 ≤ (d : ℝ) / sigmaOf d s := by rw [le_div_iff₀ hσ0]; linarith
3398      linarith
3399    rw [← Real.rpow_neg_one]
3400    exact Real.rpow_le_rpow_of_exponent_le hN' h3
3401  · have hσ : sigmaOf d s = s := sigmaOf_eq hd (by rw [h2])
3402    rw [hσ]
3403    have : 1 - (d : ℝ) / s = -1 := by
3404      have hs : 0 < s := by rw [h2]; positivity
3405      rw [h2]; field_simp; ring
3406    rw [this, Real.rpow_neg_one]
3407    have : 1 ≤ Real.log (Real.exp 1 * N) := by
3408      rw [Real.log_mul (Real.exp_pos 1).ne' hNpos.ne', Real.log_exp]
3409      have := Real.log_nonneg hN'
3410      linarith
3411    have h0 : 0 ≤ (N : ℝ)⁻¹ := by positivity
3412    nlinarith
3413  · push Not at h1
3414    have hσ : sigmaOf d s = s := sigmaOf_eq hd h1
3415    rw [hσ]
3416
3417
3418open HRS.KernelAux in
3419theorem kernel_levels (hd : 1 ≤ d) {s : ℝ} (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) (cstar : ℝ)
3420    (hw : ∀ z : Torus d, z ≠ 0 → 1 ≤ periodicRiesz d s z + cstar) :
3421    ∃ C₁ C₂ : ℝ, 0 ≤ C₁ ∧ 0 ≤ C₂ ∧
3422      (∀ L : ℝ, 0 < L → ∫⁻ z : Torus d, ENNReal.ofReal (max (periodicRiesz d s z + cstar - L) 0)
3423          ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (C₁ * L ^ (1 - (d : ℝ) / sigmaOf d s))) ∧
3424      (∀ N : ℕ, 2 ≤ N → ∫⁻ z : Torus d, ENNReal.ofReal ((min (periodicRiesz d s z + cstar) N) ^ 2)
3425          ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (C₂ * (N * deltaN d s N))) := by
3426  have hd0 : (0 : ℝ) < d := by exact_mod_cast hd
3427  have hσ0 := sigmaOf_pos hd hs0
3428  have hσd := sigmaOf_lt hd hsd
3429  obtain ⟨B, hB1, hB⟩ := riesz_envelope hd hs0 hsd cstar
3430  set w : Torus d → ℝ := fun z => periodicRiesz d s z + cstar with hwdef
3431  have hwB : ∀ y ∈ Box d, y ≠ 0 → w (boxPt y) ≤ B * (1 + ‖y‖ ^ (-(sigmaOf d s))) := hB
3432  have hw0 : ∀ y ∈ Box d, y ≠ 0 → 0 ≤ w (boxPt y) := fun y hy hy0 => by
3433    have := hw (boxPt y) (fun h => hy0 (boxPt_eq_zero hy h))
3434    simp only [hwdef]; linarith
3435  obtain ⟨C₁, hC₁, hlev1⟩ := lev1 hd hσ0 hσd hB1 hwB
3436  have h2 : ∃ C₂ : ℝ, 0 ≤ C₂ ∧ ∀ N : ℕ, 2 ≤ N → ∫⁻ z : Torus d,
3437      ENNReal.ofReal ((min (periodicRiesz d s z + cstar) N) ^ 2)
3438        ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (C₂ * (N * deltaN d s N)) := by
3439    rcases lt_trichotomy s ((d : ℝ) / 2) with h | h | h
3440    · have h2σ : 2 * sigmaOf d s < d := (two_sigmaOf_lt_iff hd hs0).2 h
3441      obtain ⟨C, hC, hlev⟩ := lev2a hd hσ0 hσd hB1 hwB hw0 h2σ
3442      refine ⟨C, hC, fun N hN => ?_⟩
3443      have hN1 : (1 : ℝ) ≤ N := by exact_mod_cast (show 1 ≤ N by omega)
3444      have hNpos : (0 : ℝ) < N := by linarith
3445      have : (N : ℝ) * deltaN d s N = 1 := by
3446        unfold deltaN; rw [if_pos h]; field_simp
3447      rw [this, mul_one]
3448      exact hlev N hN1
3449    · have hσ : sigmaOf d s = s := sigmaOf_eq hd (by rw [h])
3450      have h2σ : 2 * sigmaOf d s = d := by rw [hσ, h]; ring
3451      obtain ⟨C, hC, hlev⟩ := lev2b hd hσ0 hσd hB1 hwB hw0 h2σ
3452      refine ⟨C, hC, fun N hN => ?_⟩
3453      have hN1 : (1 : ℝ) ≤ N := by exact_mod_cast (show 1 ≤ N by omega)
3454      have hNpos : (0 : ℝ) < N := by linarith
3455      have : (N : ℝ) * deltaN d s N = Real.log (Real.exp 1 * N) := by
3456        unfold deltaN; rw [if_neg (not_lt.2 h.ge), if_pos h]; field_simp
3457      rw [this]
3458      exact hlev N hN1
3459    · have hσ : sigmaOf d s = s := sigmaOf_eq hd h.le
3460      have h2σ : (d : ℝ) < 2 * sigmaOf d s := by rw [hσ]; linarith
3461      obtain ⟨C, hC, hlev⟩ := lev2c hd hσ0 hσd hB1 hwB hw0 h2σ
3462      refine ⟨C, hC, fun N hN => ?_⟩
3463      have hN1 : (1 : ℝ) ≤ N := by exact_mod_cast (show 1 ≤ N by omega)
3464      have hNpos : (0 : ℝ) < N := by linarith
3465      have : (N : ℝ) * deltaN d s N = (N : ℝ) ^ (2 - (d : ℝ) / sigmaOf d s) := by
3466        unfold deltaN; rw [if_neg (not_lt.2 h.le), if_neg h.ne', hσ]
3467        have : (2 : ℝ) - (d : ℝ) / s = 1 + (1 - (d : ℝ) / s) := by ring
3468        rw [this, Real.rpow_add hNpos, Real.rpow_one]
3469      rw [this]
3470      exact hlev N hN1
3471  obtain ⟨C₂, hC₂, h2'⟩ := h2
3472  exact ⟨C₁, C₂, hC₁, hC₂, hlev1, h2'⟩
3473
3474end HRS.WeakRef

3476end WM_H

3487lemma ae_ne_withDensityReal (hd : 1 ≤ d) (ρ : Torus d → ℝ) (x : Torus d) :
3488    ∀ᵐ y ∂(withDensityReal ρ), y ≠ x := by
3489  have h : ∀ᵐ y ∂(volume : Measure (Torus d)), y ≠ x := by
3490    rw [ae_iff]
3491    simpa using volume_singleton_torus hd x
3492  exact (withDensity_absolutelyContinuous _ _).ae_le h

3494/-- Row bounds of a kernel dominated by the Riesz envelope. -/
3495theorem row_bounds (hd : 1 ≤ d) {s : ℝ} (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) (cstar : ℝ)
3496    (hw : ∀ z : Torus d, z ≠ 0 → 1 ≤ periodicRiesz d s z + cstar) (A Λ₀ : ℝ) (hΛ₀ : 0 ≤ Λ₀) :
3497    ∃ Ca Cb : ℝ, 0 ≤ Ca ∧ 0 ≤ Cb ∧
3498      ∀ (ρ : Torus d → ℝ) [IsProbabilityMeasure (withDensityReal ρ)], (∀ x, ρ x ≤ Λ₀) →
3499        ∀ q : Torus d → Torus d → ℝ, Measurable (Function.uncurry q) → (∀ x y, 0 ≤ q x y) →
3500        (∀ x y, q x y ≤ A * (periodicRiesz d s (x - y) + cstar)) →
3501        ∀ N : ℕ, 2 ≤ N → ∀ x : Torus d,
3502          ∫ y, max (q x y - N) 0 ∂(withDensityReal ρ) ≤ Ca * deltaN d s N ∧
3503          ∫ y, (min (q x y) N) ^ 2 ∂(withDensityReal ρ) ≤ Cb * (N * deltaN d s N) := by
3504  obtain ⟨C₁, C₂, hC₁, hC₂, hlev1, hlev2⟩ := kernel_levels hd hs0 hsd cstar hw
3505  set Ā : ℝ := max A 1 with hĀ
3506  have hĀ1 : 1 ≤ Ā := le_max_right _ _
3507  have hĀ0 : 0 < Ā := by linarith
3508  have hAĀ : A ≤ Ā := le_max_left _ _
3509  set e : ℝ := 1 - (d : ℝ) / sigmaOf d s with he
3510  refine ⟨Λ₀ * Ā * C₁ * Ā ^ (-e), Λ₀ * Ā ^ 2 * C₂, by positivity, by positivity, ?_⟩
3511  intro ρ _ hρ q hqm hq0 hqA N hN x
3512  set μ := withDensityReal ρ with hμ
3513  have hN1 : (1 : ℝ) ≤ N := by exact_mod_cast (show 1 ≤ N by omega)
3514  have hNpos : (0 : ℝ) < N := by linarith
3515  set w : Torus d → ℝ := fun z => periodicRiesz d s z + cstar with hwdef
3516  have hqx : Measurable (q x) := hqm.comp (measurable_const.prodMk measurable_id)
3517  have hae := ae_ne_withDensityReal hd ρ x
3518  have hpt : ∀ y, y ≠ x → q x y ≤ Ā * w (x - y) ∧ 0 ≤ w (x - y) := by
3519    intro y hy
3520    have h1 := hw (x - y) (sub_ne_zero.2 (Ne.symm hy))
3521    have h2 := hqA x y
3522    have h0 : 0 ≤ w (x - y) := by simp only [hwdef]; linarith
3523    refine ⟨h2.trans ?_, h0⟩
3524    exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hAĀ h0
3525  constructor
3526  · -- tail
3527    have hnn : ∀ y, 0 ≤ max (q x y - N) 0 := fun y => le_max_right _ _
3528    have hm : Measurable fun y => max (q x y - N) 0 := (hqx.sub_const _).max measurable_const
3529    rw [integral_eq_lintegral_of_nonneg_ae (Filter.Eventually.of_forall hnn) hm.aestronglyMeasurable]
3530    have hle : ∫⁻ y, ENNReal.ofReal (max (q x y - N) 0) ∂μ
3531        ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (Λ₀ * Ā * C₁ * (N / Ā) ^ e) := by
3532      calc ∫⁻ y, ENNReal.ofReal (max (q x y - N) 0) ∂μ
3533          ≤ ∫⁻ y, (ENNReal.ofReal Ā * ENNReal.ofReal (max (w (x - y) - N / Ā) 0)) ∂μ := by
3534            refine lintegral_mono_ae ?_
3535            filter_upwards [hae] with y hy
3536            obtain ⟨h1, h0⟩ := hpt y hy
3537            rw [← ENNReal.ofReal_mul hĀ0.le]
3538            refine ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal (max_le ?_ (mul_nonneg hĀ0.le (le_max_right _ _)))
3539            have : Ā * (w (x - y) - N / Ā) ≤ Ā * max (w (x - y) - N / Ā) 0 :=
3540              mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (le_max_left _ _) hĀ0.le
3541            have e2 : Ā * (w (x - y) - N / Ā) = Ā * w (x - y) - N := by field_simp
3542            linarith
3543        _ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal Λ₀ * ∫⁻ z, (ENNReal.ofReal Ā * ENNReal.ofReal (max (w z - N / Ā) 0)) :=
3544            KernelAux2.lintegral_conv_le ρ Λ₀ hρ
3545              (fun z => ENNReal.ofReal Ā * ENNReal.ofReal (max (w z - N / Ā) 0)) x
3546        _ = ENNReal.ofReal Λ₀ * (ENNReal.ofReal Ā * ∫⁻ z, ENNReal.ofReal (max (w z - N / Ā) 0)) := by
3547            rw [lintegral_const_mul' _ _ ENNReal.ofReal_ne_top]
3548        _ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal Λ₀ * (ENNReal.ofReal Ā * ENNReal.ofReal (C₁ * (N / Ā) ^ e)) := by
3549            gcongr
3550            exact hlev1 _ (by positivity)
3551        _ = _ := by
3552            rw [← ENNReal.ofReal_mul hĀ0.le, ← ENNReal.ofReal_mul hΛ₀]
3553            congr 1; ring
3554    have hbd : Λ₀ * Ā * C₁ * (N / Ā) ^ e ≤ Λ₀ * Ā * C₁ * Ā ^ (-e) * deltaN d s N := by
3555      have h1 : (N / Ā) ^ e = (N : ℝ) ^ e * Ā ^ (-e) := by
3556        rw [Real.div_rpow hNpos.le hĀ0.le, Real.rpow_neg hĀ0.le, div_eq_mul_inv]
3557      rw [h1]
3558      have h2 := rpow_le_deltaN hd hs0 hsd (N := N) (by omega)
3559      have h3 : 0 ≤ Λ₀ * Ā * C₁ * Ā ^ (-e) := by positivity
3560      calc Λ₀ * Ā * C₁ * ((N : ℝ) ^ e * Ā ^ (-e)) = Λ₀ * Ā * C₁ * Ā ^ (-e) * (N : ℝ) ^ e := by ring
3561        _ ≤ _ := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h2 h3
3562    have hpos : 0 ≤ Λ₀ * Ā * C₁ * (N / Ā) ^ e := by positivity
3563    calc (∫⁻ y, ENNReal.ofReal (max (q x y - N) 0) ∂μ).toReal
3564        ≤ (ENNReal.ofReal (Λ₀ * Ā * C₁ * (N / Ā) ^ e)).toReal :=
3565          ENNReal.toReal_mono ENNReal.ofReal_ne_top hle
3566      _ = Λ₀ * Ā * C₁ * (N / Ā) ^ e := ENNReal.toReal_ofReal hpos
3567      _ ≤ _ := hbd
3568  · -- second moment
3569    have hnn : ∀ y, 0 ≤ (min (q x y) N) ^ 2 := fun y => sq_nonneg _
3570    have hm : Measurable fun y => (min (q x y) N) ^ 2 := (hqx.min measurable_const).pow_const 2
3571    rw [integral_eq_lintegral_of_nonneg_ae (Filter.Eventually.of_forall hnn) hm.aestronglyMeasurable]
3572    have hle : ∫⁻ y, ENNReal.ofReal ((min (q x y) N) ^ 2) ∂μ
3573        ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (Λ₀ * Ā ^ 2 * (C₂ * (N * deltaN d s N))) := by
3574      calc ∫⁻ y, ENNReal.ofReal ((min (q x y) N) ^ 2) ∂μ
3575          ≤ ∫⁻ y, (ENNReal.ofReal (Ā ^ 2) * ENNReal.ofReal ((min (w (x - y)) N) ^ 2)) ∂μ := by
3576            refine lintegral_mono_ae ?_
3577            filter_upwards [hae] with y hy
3578            obtain ⟨h1, h0⟩ := hpt y hy
3579            rw [← ENNReal.ofReal_mul (sq_nonneg _)]
3580            refine ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal ?_
3581            have hq0' := hq0 x y
3582            have h2 : min (q x y) N ≤ Ā * min (w (x - y)) N := by
3583              rw [mul_min_of_nonneg _ _ hĀ0.le]
3584              exact le_min ((min_le_left _ _).trans h1)
3585                ((min_le_right _ _).trans (le_mul_of_one_le_left hNpos.le hĀ1))
3586            have h3 : 0 ≤ min (q x y) N := le_min hq0' hNpos.le
3587            have := pow_le_pow_left₀ h3 h2 2
3588            calc (min (q x y) N) ^ 2 ≤ (Ā * min (w (x - y)) N) ^ 2 := this
3589              _ = Ā ^ 2 * (min (w (x - y)) N) ^ 2 := by ring
3590        _ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal Λ₀ * ∫⁻ z, (ENNReal.ofReal (Ā ^ 2) * ENNReal.ofReal ((min (w z) N) ^ 2)) :=
3591            KernelAux2.lintegral_conv_le ρ Λ₀ hρ
3592              (fun z => ENNReal.ofReal (Ā ^ 2) * ENNReal.ofReal ((min (w z) N) ^ 2)) x
3593        _ = ENNReal.ofReal Λ₀ * (ENNReal.ofReal (Ā ^ 2) * ∫⁻ z, ENNReal.ofReal ((min (w z) N) ^ 2)) := by
3594            rw [lintegral_const_mul' _ _ ENNReal.ofReal_ne_top]
3595        _ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal Λ₀ * (ENNReal.ofReal (Ā ^ 2) * ENNReal.ofReal (C₂ * (N * deltaN d s N))) := by
3596            gcongr
3597            exact hlev2 N hN
3598        _ = _ := by
3599            rw [← ENNReal.ofReal_mul (sq_nonneg _), ← ENNReal.ofReal_mul hΛ₀]
3600            congr 1; ring
3601    have hdel : 0 ≤ deltaN d s N := deltaN_nonneg s (by omega)
3602    have hpos : 0 ≤ Λ₀ * Ā ^ 2 * (C₂ * (N * deltaN d s N)) := by positivity
3603    calc (∫⁻ y, ENNReal.ofReal ((min (q x y) N) ^ 2) ∂μ).toReal
3604        ≤ (ENNReal.ofReal (Λ₀ * Ā ^ 2 * (C₂ * (N * deltaN d s N)))).toReal :=
3605          ENNReal.toReal_mono ENNReal.ofReal_ne_top hle
3606      _ = Λ₀ * Ā ^ 2 * (C₂ * (N * deltaN d s N)) := ENNReal.toReal_ofReal hpos
3607      _ = Λ₀ * Ā ^ 2 * C₂ * (N * deltaN d s N) := by ring
3608
3609end HRS.WeakRef

3623lemma continuous_of_lift3 {E : Type*} [TopologicalSpace E] {f : Torus d → E}
3624    (h : Continuous (Torus.lift f)) : Continuous f := by

3626      (Pi.map (fun _ : Fin d => ((↑) : ℝ → UnitAddCircle))) :=
3627    IsOpenQuotientMap.piMap fun _ => QuotientAddGroup.isOpenQuotientMap_mk

3629    h1.comp (PiLp.homeomorph 2 (fun _ : Fin d => ℝ)).isOpenQuotientMap
3630  exact h2.isQuotientMap.continuous_iff.2 h

3632lemma le_cbNorm_zero {ρ : Torus d → ℝ} (hρ : Torus.IsSmooth ρ) (x : Torus d) :
3633    ρ x ≤ Torus.cbNorm 0 ρ := by

3638    obtain ⟨R, hR⟩ := isCompact_univ.exists_bound_of_continuousOn hc.continuousOn
3639    refine ⟨R, ?_⟩
3640    rintro _ ⟨y, rfl⟩
3641    show ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ 0 (Torus.lift ρ) y‖ ≤ R
3642    rw [norm_iteratedFDeriv_zero]
3643    exact hR (Torus.mk y) trivial
3644  have h := le_ciSup hbdd (Torus.rep x)
3645  have e : ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ 0 (Torus.lift ρ) (Torus.rep x)‖ = |ρ x| := by
3646    rw [norm_iteratedFDeriv_zero]
3647    simp [Torus.lift, Torus.mk_rep]
3648  rw [e] at h
3649  exact (le_abs_self _).trans h

3652lemma ka_alg {κ₀ D Ca Cb δ T1 T2 n NR : ℝ} (hκ₀ : 0 ≤ κ₀) (hD0 : 0 ≤ D) (hn0 : 0 ≤ n)
3653    (hNR : 0 < NR) (hnN : n ≤ NR) (hδ : 0 ≤ δ) (hT10 : 0 ≤ T1) (hT20 : 0 ≤ T2)
3654    (hT1 : T1 ≤ Ca * δ) (hT2 : T2 ≤ Cb * (NR * δ)) :
3655    2 * ((κ₀ / NR) * (n * (D * T1))) + (κ₀ / NR) ^ 2 / 2 * (2 * n * (D * T2))
3656      ≤ (2 * κ₀ * D * Ca + κ₀ ^ 2 * D * Cb) * δ := by
3657  have hr : n / NR ≤ 1 := by rw [div_le_one hNR]; exact hnN
3658  have hr0 : 0 ≤ n / NR := div_nonneg hn0 hNR.le
3659  have e1 : 2 * ((κ₀ / NR) * (n * (D * T1))) = 2 * κ₀ * (n / NR) * D * T1 := by
3660    field_simp
3661  have e2 : (κ₀ / NR) ^ 2 / 2 * (2 * n * (D * T2)) = κ₀ ^ 2 * (n / NR) * D * T2 / NR := by
3662    field_simp
3663  rw [e1, e2]
3664  have a1 : 2 * κ₀ * (n / NR) * D * T1 ≤ 2 * κ₀ * D * (Ca * δ) := by
3665    have h1 : 2 * κ₀ * (n / NR) * D * T1 ≤ 2 * κ₀ * 1 * D * T1 := by
3666      have : 0 ≤ 2 * κ₀ * D * T1 := by positivity
3667      nlinarith
3668    have h2 : 2 * κ₀ * D * T1 ≤ 2 * κ₀ * D * (Ca * δ) := by
3669      exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hT1 (by positivity)
3670    linarith
3671  have a2 : κ₀ ^ 2 * (n / NR) * D * T2 / NR ≤ κ₀ ^ 2 * D * (Cb * δ) := by
3672    rw [div_le_iff₀ hNR]
3673    have h1 : κ₀ ^ 2 * (n / NR) * D * T2 ≤ κ₀ ^ 2 * 1 * D * T2 := by
3674      have : 0 ≤ κ₀ ^ 2 * D * T2 := by positivity
3675      nlinarith
3676    have h2 : κ₀ ^ 2 * D * T2 ≤ κ₀ ^ 2 * D * (Cb * (NR * δ)) :=
3677      mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hT2 (by positivity)
3678    have e3 : κ₀ ^ 2 * D * (Cb * (NR * δ)) = κ₀ ^ 2 * D * (Cb * δ) * NR := by ring
3679    linarith
3680  have e4 : (2 * κ₀ * D * Ca + κ₀ ^ 2 * D * Cb) * δ = 2 * κ₀ * D * (Ca * δ) + κ₀ ^ 2 * D * (Cb * δ) := by ring
3681  linarith
3682
3683lemma kb_alg {ε₀ Ca Cb D E δ NR : ℝ} (hε₀ : 0 ≤ ε₀) (h2ε : 2 * ε₀ ≤ 1 / NR) (hNR : 1 ≤ NR)
3684    (hCa : 0 ≤ Ca) (hCb : 0 ≤ Cb) (hD0 : 0 ≤ D) (hDE : D ≤ E) (hδ : 0 ≤ δ) (hδ1 : NR⁻¹ ≤ δ) :
3685    2 * ((2 * ε₀) ^ 2 * (Cb * (NR * δ)) + (Ca * δ) ^ 2 * (D * (2 * ε₀)))
3686      ≤ 2 * (Cb + Ca ^ 2 * E) * δ ^ 2 := by
3687  have hNR0 : 0 < NR := by linarith
3688  have hE0 : 0 ≤ E := hD0.trans hDE
3689  have h1 : (2 * ε₀) ^ 2 ≤ (1 / NR) ^ 2 := pow_le_pow_left₀ (by positivity) h2ε 2
3690  have h2 : (2 * ε₀) ^ 2 * (Cb * (NR * δ)) ≤ (1 / NR) ^ 2 * (Cb * (NR * δ)) :=
3691    mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right h1 (by positivity)
3692  have h3 : (1 / NR) ^ 2 * (Cb * (NR * δ)) = Cb * δ * NR⁻¹ := by field_simp
3693  have h4 : Cb * δ * NR⁻¹ ≤ Cb * δ * δ := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hδ1 (by positivity)
3694  have h5 : (Ca * δ) ^ 2 * (D * (2 * ε₀)) ≤ (Ca * δ) ^ 2 * (E * 1) := by
3695    refine mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left ?_ (by positivity)
3696    have : 2 * ε₀ ≤ 1 := h2ε.trans (by rw [div_le_one hNR0]; exact hNR)
3697    exact mul_le_mul hDE this (by positivity) hE0
3698  have h6 : (Ca * δ) ^ 2 * (E * 1) = Ca ^ 2 * E * δ ^ 2 := by ring
3699  nlinarith
3700
3701
3702lemma final_alg {κ₀ P D E Ca Cb δ Kc a NR : ℝ} (hκ₀ : 0 ≤ κ₀) (hκP : κ₀ ≤ P) (hD0 : 0 ≤ D)
3703    (hDE : D ≤ E) (hCa : 0 ≤ Ca) (hCb : 0 ≤ Cb) (hδ : 0 ≤ δ) (ha0 : 0 ≤ a) (ha1 : a ≤ 1)
3704    (hNR : 0 < NR) (hδ1 : NR⁻¹ ≤ δ) :
3705    150 * ((2 * κ₀ * D * Ca + κ₀ ^ 2 * D * Cb) * δ) ^ 2 + 214 * κ₀ ^ 2 * (Kc * δ) ^ 2
3706        + 12 * (κ₀ * a / NR) ^ 2
3707      ≤ (150 * (2 * P * E * Ca + P ^ 2 * E * Cb) ^ 2 + 214 * P ^ 2 * Kc ^ 2 + 12 * P ^ 2) * δ ^ 2 := by
3708  have hP0 : 0 ≤ P := hκ₀.trans hκP
3709  have hE0 : 0 ≤ E := hD0.trans hDE
3710  have h1 : 0 ≤ 2 * κ₀ * D * Ca + κ₀ ^ 2 * D * Cb := by positivity
3711  have h2 : 2 * κ₀ * D * Ca + κ₀ ^ 2 * D * Cb ≤ 2 * P * E * Ca + P ^ 2 * E * Cb := by
3712    have a1 : 2 * κ₀ * D * Ca ≤ 2 * P * E * Ca := by gcongr
3713    have a2 : κ₀ ^ 2 * D * Cb ≤ P ^ 2 * E * Cb := by gcongr
3714    linarith
3715  have h3 : ((2 * κ₀ * D * Ca + κ₀ ^ 2 * D * Cb) * δ) ^ 2
3716      ≤ ((2 * P * E * Ca + P ^ 2 * E * Cb) * δ) ^ 2 :=
3717    pow_le_pow_left₀ (by positivity) (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right h2 hδ) 2
3718  have h4 : κ₀ ^ 2 * (Kc * δ) ^ 2 ≤ P ^ 2 * (Kc * δ) ^ 2 :=
3719    mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right (pow_le_pow_left₀ hκ₀ hκP 2) (sq_nonneg _)
3720  have h5 : κ₀ * a / NR ≤ P * δ := by
3721    have : κ₀ * a ≤ P := by
3722      calc κ₀ * a ≤ κ₀ * 1 := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left ha1 hκ₀
3723        _ ≤ P := by linarith
3724    rw [div_eq_mul_inv]
3725    exact mul_le_mul this hδ1 (by positivity) hP0
3726  have h6 : (κ₀ * a / NR) ^ 2 ≤ (P * δ) ^ 2 :=
3727    pow_le_pow_left₀ (by positivity) h5 2
3728  have e1 : ((2 * P * E * Ca + P ^ 2 * E * Cb) * δ) ^ 2 = (2 * P * E * Ca + P ^ 2 * E * Cb) ^ 2 * δ ^ 2 := by ring
3729  have e2 : (Kc * δ) ^ 2 = Kc ^ 2 * δ ^ 2 := by ring
3730  have e3 : (P * δ) ^ 2 = P ^ 2 * δ ^ 2 := by ring
3731  nlinarith [h3, h4, h6, e1, e2, e3]
3732
3733end HRS.WeakRef
3734
3735end WM_J

3746  {B : Type*} [Fintype B]

3748/-- Disintegration of the `(n+1)`-particle ensemble with respect to coordinate `i`: integral of a
3749function of `(y_i, y_{-i})`. -/
3750theorem lintegral_pinned_succ (V : WeakPotential μ) {lam : ℝ} (hlam : 0 ≤ lam) (a : B → E) {n : ℕ}
3751    (i : Fin (n + 1)) (Φ : E → (Fin n → E) → ℝ≥0∞)
3752    (hΦ : Measurable fun p : E × (Fin n → E) => Φ p.1 p.2) :
3753    ∫⁻ y, Φ (y i) (fun k => y (i.succAbove k)) ∂(pinnedLaw V lam (n + 1) a)
3754      = ∫⁻ x, pinWeight V lam a n x * ∫⁻ y, Φ x y ∂(pinnedLaw V lam n (pinsWith a x)) ∂μ := by
3755  have hψ := isField_pinField V hlam a
3756  have hψx : ∀ x, IsField μ (pinField V lam (pinsWith a x)) V.M
3757      (V.M' + lam * Fintype.card (Option B) * V.γ) := fun x => isField_pinField V hlam _
3758  change ∫⁻ y, Φ (y i) (fun k => y (i.succAbove k)) ∂(fieldLaw V (pinField V lam a) lam (n + 1)) = _
3759  unfold fieldLaw
3760  set e := MeasurableEquiv.piFinSuccAbove (fun _ : Fin (n + 1) => E) i with he
3761  have hmp := measurePreserving_piFinSuccAbove (fun _ : Fin (n + 1) => μ) i
3762  have hsymm : ∀ (x : E) (y : Fin n → E), e.symm (x, y) = insertAt i x y := fun _ _ => rfl
3763  have hdens := measurable_fieldDensity hψ.measurable V lam (n + 1)
3764  have hΨ : Measurable fun y : Fin (n + 1) → E => Φ (y i) (fun k => y (i.succAbove k)) :=
3765    hΦ.comp (show Measurable fun y : Fin (n + 1) → E => (y i, fun k => y (i.succAbove k)) from
3766      (measurable_pi_apply i).prodMk (measurable_pi_iff.2 fun k => measurable_pi_apply _))
3767  rw [lintegral_withDensity_eq_lintegral_mul _ hdens hΨ]
3768  set h : (Fin (n + 1) → E) → ℝ≥0∞ := fun y =>
3769    fieldDensity V (pinField V lam a) lam (n + 1) y * Φ (y i) (fun k => y (i.succAbove k)) with hh
3770  have hhm : Measurable h := hdens.mul hΨ
3771  have step1 : ∫⁻ y, h y ∂(Measure.pi fun _ : Fin (n + 1) => μ)
3772      = ∫⁻ p, h (e.symm p) ∂(μ.prod (Measure.pi fun _ : Fin n => μ)) := by
3773    have := hmp.lintegral_comp_emb e.measurableEmbedding (fun p => h (e.symm p))
3774    rw [← this]
3775    exact lintegral_congr fun y => by rw [e.symm_apply_apply]
3776  change ∫⁻ y, h y ∂(Measure.pi fun _ : Fin (n + 1) => μ) = _
3777  rw [step1, lintegral_prod (fun p => h (e.symm p)) (hhm.comp e.symm.measurable).aemeasurable]
3778  refine lintegral_congr fun x => ?_
3779  simp only [hsymm]
3780  have hΦx : Measurable fun y : Fin n → E => Φ x y := hΦ.comp (measurable_const.prodMk measurable_id)
3781  have hx : ∀ y : Fin n → E, h (insertAt i x y)
3782      = (ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (pinField V lam a x)) * (fieldNorm V (pinField V lam a) lam (n + 1))⁻¹)
3783        * (fieldWeight V (pinField V lam (pinsWith a x)) lam y * Φ x y) := by
3784    intro y
3785    simp only [hh, insertAt_same]
3786    have : (fun k => insertAt i x y (i.succAbove k)) = y := funext fun k => insertAt_succAbove i x y k
3787    rw [this]
3788    unfold fieldDensity
3789    rw [fieldWeight_insertAt]; ring
3790  simp_rw [hx]
3791  have hNx := fieldNorm_pos (hψx x).measurable V lam n
3792  have hNxt := fieldNorm_lt_top (V := V) (hψx x) hlam n
3793  rw [lintegral_const_mul' _ _ (ENNReal.mul_ne_top ENNReal.ofReal_ne_top
3794    (ENNReal.inv_ne_top.2 (fieldNorm_pos hψ.measurable V lam (n + 1)).ne'))]
3795  change _ = _ * ∫⁻ y, Φ x y ∂(fieldLaw V (pinField V lam (pinsWith a x)) lam n)
3796  unfold fieldLaw
3797  rw [lintegral_withDensity_eq_lintegral_mul _ (measurable_fieldDensity (hψx x).measurable V lam n) hΦx]
3798  have hfd : ∀ y, ((fun y => fieldDensity V (pinField V lam (pinsWith a x)) lam n y) * fun y => Φ x y) y
3799      = (fieldNorm V (pinField V lam (pinsWith a x)) lam n)⁻¹
3800        * (fieldWeight V (pinField V lam (pinsWith a x)) lam y * Φ x y) := by
3801    intro y
3802    simp only [Pi.mul_apply]
3803    unfold fieldDensity; ring
3804  rw [lintegral_congr hfd, lintegral_const_mul' _ _ (ENNReal.inv_ne_top.2 hNx.ne')]
3805  unfold pinWeight
3806  have hcancel : fieldNorm V (pinField V lam (pinsWith a x)) lam n
3807      * (fieldNorm V (pinField V lam (pinsWith a x)) lam n)⁻¹ = 1 :=
3808    ENNReal.mul_inv_cancel hNx.ne' hNxt.ne
3809  set Nx := fieldNorm V (pinField V lam (pinsWith a x)) lam n
3810  set I := ∫⁻ (a_1 : Fin n → E), fieldWeight V (pinField V lam (pinsWith a x)) lam a_1 * Φ x a_1
3811    ∂Measure.pi fun x => μ
3812  calc ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (pinField V lam a x)) * (fieldNorm V (pinField V lam a) lam (n + 1))⁻¹ * I
3813      = ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (pinField V lam a x)) * (fieldNorm V (pinField V lam a) lam (n + 1))⁻¹
3814        * ((Nx * Nx⁻¹) * I) := by rw [hcancel, one_mul]
3815    _ = _ := by ring
3816
3817
3818/-- Marginals of a field ensemble whose field has the extra decay `ψ ≤ M - ε`. -/
3819theorem marginal_le_field {ψ : E → ℝ} {M M₁ lam Λ : ℝ} (V : WeakPotential μ)
3820    (hψ : IsField μ ψ M M₁) (hlam : 0 ≤ lam) {n : ℕ} (hΛ : M₁ + lam * n * V.γ ≤ Λ)
3821    (ε : E → ℝ) (hε0 : ∀ z, 0 ≤ ε z) (hψε : ∀ z, ψ z ≤ M - ε z) (i : Fin n) :
3822    (fieldLaw V ψ lam n).map (fun y => y i)
3823      ≤ μ.withDensity (fun z => ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (M + Λ) * Real.exp (-ε z))) := by
3824  have hP : IsProbabilityMeasure (fieldLaw V ψ lam n) :=
3825    (fieldSpec (V := V) hψ hlam n).isProb
3826  refine Measure.le_iff.2 fun A hA => ?_
3827  rw [Measure.map_apply (measurable_pi_apply _) hA]
3828  have hS : MeasurableSet ((fun y : Fin n → E => y i) ⁻¹' A) := measurable_pi_apply _ hA
3829  have hinv := invariant_density (μ := μ) (W := fieldDensity V ψ lam n)
3830    (measurable_fieldDensity hψ.measurable V _ n) (fieldDensity_pos hψ hlam n)
3831    (fieldDensity_le hψ hlam n) i
3832    (φ := ((fun y : Fin n → E => y i) ⁻¹' A).indicator 1) (measurable_one.indicator hS)
3833  change ∫⁻ x, ∫⁻ z, ((fun y : Fin n → E => y i) ⁻¹' A).indicator (1 : (Fin n → E) → ℝ≥0∞)
3834      (update x i z) ∂(siteKernel μ (fieldDensity V ψ lam n) i x) ∂(fieldLaw V ψ lam n)
3835      = ∫⁻ x, ((fun y : Fin n → E => y i) ⁻¹' A).indicator (1 : (Fin n → E) → ℝ≥0∞) x
3836          ∂(fieldLaw V ψ lam n) at hinv
3837  have h1 : ∀ x : Fin n → E, ∫⁻ z, ((fun y : Fin n → E => y i) ⁻¹' A).indicator (1 : (Fin n → E) → ℝ≥0∞)
3838      (update x i z) ∂(siteKernel μ (fieldDensity V ψ lam n) i x)
3839      = siteKernel μ (fieldDensity V ψ lam n) i x A := by
3840    intro x
3841    rw [← lintegral_indicator_one hA]
3842    refine lintegral_congr fun z => ?_
3843    by_cases hz : z ∈ A
3844    · simp [Set.indicator_apply, hz]
3845    · simp [Set.indicator_apply, hz]
3846  simp_rw [h1] at hinv
3847  have hsite : ∀ x : Fin n → E, siteKernel μ (fieldDensity V ψ lam n) i x A
3848      ≤ (μ.withDensity (fun z => ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (M + Λ) * Real.exp (-ε z)))) A := by
3849    intro x
3850    rw [siteKernel_fieldDensity hψ hlam, withDensity_apply _ hA, withDensity_apply _ hA]
3851    refine lintegral_mono fun z => ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal ?_
3852    have h1 := exp_le_integral_siteU (V := V) hψ hlam i x
3853    have h2 : Real.exp (-Λ) ≤ ∫ z', siteU V ψ lam i x z' ∂μ :=
3854      le_trans (Real.exp_le_exp.2 (by linarith)) h1
3855    rw [div_le_iff₀ (lt_of_lt_of_le (Real.exp_pos _) h2)]
3856    have h3 : siteU V ψ lam i x z ≤ Real.exp (M - ε z) := by
3857      unfold siteU
3858      refine Real.exp_le_exp.2 ?_
3859      have := mul_nonneg hlam (sumq_nonneg V i x z)
3860      linarith [hψε z]
3861    calc siteU V ψ lam i x z ≤ Real.exp (M - ε z) := h3
3862      _ = Real.exp (M + Λ) * Real.exp (-ε z) * Real.exp (-Λ) := by
3863          rw [← Real.exp_add, ← Real.exp_add]; congr 1; ring
3864      _ ≤ _ := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h2 (by positivity)
3865  calc (fieldLaw V ψ lam n) ((fun y : Fin n → E => y i) ⁻¹' A)
3866      = ∫⁻ x, ((fun y : Fin n → E => y i) ⁻¹' A).indicator (1 : (Fin n → E) → ℝ≥0∞) x
3867          ∂(fieldLaw V ψ lam n) := by rw [lintegral_indicator_one hS]
3868    _ = ∫⁻ x, siteKernel μ (fieldDensity V ψ lam n) i x A ∂(fieldLaw V ψ lam n) := hinv.symm
3869    _ ≤ ∫⁻ _x, (μ.withDensity (fun z => ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (M + Λ) * Real.exp (-ε z)))) A
3870          ∂(fieldLaw V ψ lam n) := lintegral_mono hsite
3871    _ = _ := by rw [lintegral_const, measure_univ, mul_one]
3872
3873
3874/-- Marginals of the pinned ensemble: density `≤ C e^{-λ q(z, x₀)}` with respect to `μ`. -/
3875theorem pinned_marginal_le_decay (V : WeakPotential μ) {β : ℝ} (hβ : 0 ≤ β) {N n : ℕ} (hN0 : 0 < N)
3876    (hN : n + (Fintype.card B + 1) ≤ N) (a : B → E) (x₀ : E) (i : Fin n) :
3877    (pinnedLaw V (β / N) n (pinsWith a x₀)).map (fun y => y i)
3878      ≤ μ.withDensity (fun z => ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (V.M + V.M' + β * V.γ)
3879          * Real.exp (-((β / N) * V.q z x₀)))) := by
3880  have hlam : 0 ≤ β / N := div_nonneg hβ (Nat.cast_nonneg N)
3881  have hψ := isField_pinField V hlam (pinsWith a x₀)
3882  have hΛ := weak_Lambda_le V hβ hN0 (B := Option B) (n := n) (by simpa [Fintype.card_option] using hN)
3883  have hψa := isField_pinField V hlam a
3884  have := marginal_le_field V hψ hlam (n := n) (Λ := V.M' + β * V.γ)
3885    (by
3886      have h := hΛ
3887      simpa using h)
3888    (fun z => (β / N) * V.q z x₀) (fun z => mul_nonneg hlam (V.q_nonneg _ _))
3889    (fun z => by
3890      rw [pinField_pinsWith]
3891      linarith [hψa.le z]) i
3892  rw [← add_assoc] at this
3893  exact this
3894
3895
3896/-- Wu's inequality for `G = N⁻¹ ∑_{i<j} q - ∑ H`, reduced to second moments of the single-coordinate
3897fluctuation `g_i = N⁻¹ ∑_{l ≠ i} q(x_i, x_l) - H(x_i)` (source `N:eq:row-variance`, first step). -/
3898theorem evariance_G_le (V : WeakPotential μ) {κ₀ : ℝ} (hκ₀ : 0 ≤ κ₀) {n : ℕ}
3899    (hκ : weakKappa V κ₀ ≤ 1 / 2) (H : E → ℝ) (hHm : Measurable H)
3900    (G : (Fin (n + 1) → E) → ℝ)
3901    (hG : ∀ X, G X = (1 / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) * pairEnergy V X - ∑ k, H (X k)) :
3902    evariance G (pinnedLaw V (κ₀ / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) (n + 1) (fun e : Empty => e.elim))
3903      ≤ 2 * ∑ i : Fin (n + 1), ∫⁻ x, ‖(1 / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) * ∑ l ∈ Finset.univ.erase i,
3904            V.q (x i) (x l) - H (x i)‖ₑ ^ 2
3905          ∂(pinnedLaw V (κ₀ / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) (n + 1) (fun e : Empty => e.elim)) := by
3906  have hN0 : 0 < n + 1 := Nat.succ_pos n
3907  have hlam : 0 ≤ κ₀ / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ) := div_nonneg hκ₀ (Nat.cast_nonneg (n + 1))
3908  set a₀ : Empty → E := fun e => e.elim with ha₀
3909  set P := pinnedLaw V (κ₀ / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) (n + 1) a₀ with hP
3910  have hψ := isField_pinField V hlam a₀
3911  have hGm : Measurable G := by
3912    have : G = fun X => (1 / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) * pairEnergy V X - ∑ k, H (X k) := funext hG
3913    rw [this]
3914    exact (measurable_pairEnergy V |>.const_mul _).sub
3915      (Finset.measurable_sum _ fun k _ => hHm.comp (measurable_pi_apply k))
3916  have hW := weak_resampling V hκ₀ hN0 (n := n + 1) (B := Empty) (by simp) a₀ hκ G hGm
3917  refine hW.trans ?_
3918  refine mul_le_mul' le_rfl (Finset.sum_le_sum fun (i : Fin (n + 1)) (_ : i ∈ Finset.univ) => ?_)
3919
3920  -- the one-coordinate observable
3921  set Ψ : (Fin (n + 1) → E) → ℝ≥0∞ := fun X =>
3922    ‖(1 / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) * ∑ l ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, V.q (X i) (X l) - H (X i)‖ₑ ^ 2 with hΨ
3923  have hΨm : Measurable Ψ := by
3924    refine (Measurable.enorm ?_).pow_const 2
3925    refine (measurable_const.mul (Finset.measurable_sum _ fun l _ => ?_)).sub
3926      (hHm.comp (measurable_pi_apply i))
3927    exact V.measurable_q.comp (show Measurable fun X : Fin (n + 1) → E => (X i, X l) from
3928      (measurable_pi_apply i).prodMk (measurable_pi_apply l))
3929  have hsite : ∀ x : Fin (n + 1) → E,
3930      evariance (fun z => G (update x i z)) (siteKernel μ (pinnedDensity V (κ₀ / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) (n + 1) a₀) i x)
3931        ≤ ∫⁻ z, Ψ (update x i z) ∂(siteKernel μ (pinnedDensity V (κ₀ / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) (n + 1) a₀) i x) := by
3932    intro x
3933    have hprob : IsProbabilityMeasure (siteKernel μ (pinnedDensity V (κ₀ / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) (n + 1) a₀) i x) := by
3934      have := (fieldSpec (V := V) hψ hlam (n + 1)).isMarkov i
3935      exact (fieldSpec_G (V := V) hψ hlam (n + 1) i ▸ this).isProbabilityMeasure x
3936    set g : E → ℝ := fun z => (1 / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) * ∑ l ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, V.q z (x l) - H z with hgdef
3937    have hgm : Measurable g := by
3938      refine (measurable_const.mul (Finset.measurable_sum _ fun l _ => ?_)).sub hHm
3939      exact V.measurable_q.comp (measurable_id.prodMk measurable_const)
3940    have hGupd : (fun z => G (update x i z)) = fun z => g z
3941        + ((1 / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) * HRS.External.pairRest V i x - ∑ k ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, H (x k)) := by
3942      funext z
3943      rw [hG, pairEnergy_update, sum_update_psi]
3944      simp only [hgdef]; ring
3945    rw [hGupd, evariance_add_const' _ hgm.aestronglyMeasurable]
3946    refine (evariance_le_lintegral_sub _ hgm.aestronglyMeasurable 0).trans ?_
3947    refine le_of_eq (lintegral_congr fun z => ?_)
3948    simp only [hΨ, sub_zero, hgdef]
3949    have e1 : ∑ l ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, V.q (update x i z i) (update x i z l)
3950        = ∑ l ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, V.q z (x l) :=
3951      Finset.sum_congr rfl fun l hl => by simp [Finset.ne_of_mem_erase hl]
3952    rw [e1, update_self]
3953  calc ∫⁻ x, evariance (fun z => G (update x i z)) (siteKernel μ (pinnedDensity V (κ₀ / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) (n + 1) a₀) i x) ∂P
3954      ≤ ∫⁻ x, ∫⁻ z, Ψ (update x i z) ∂(siteKernel μ (pinnedDensity V (κ₀ / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) (n + 1) a₀) i x) ∂P :=
3955        lintegral_mono hsite
3956    _ = ∫⁻ x, Ψ x ∂P := by
3957        have := (fieldSpec (V := V) hψ hlam (n + 1)).invariant i Ψ hΨm
3958        exact this
3959
3960end HRS.WeakRef.Abs

3975lemma enorm_sq_eq (a : ℝ) : ‖a‖ₑ ^ 2 = ENNReal.ofReal (a ^ 2) := by
3976  rw [← enorm_pow, Real.enorm_of_nonneg (sq_nonneg _)]
3977
3978lemma enorm_sq_le (a m : ℝ) : ‖a‖ₑ ^ 2 ≤ 2 * ‖a - m‖ₑ ^ 2 + 2 * ‖m‖ₑ ^ 2 := by
3979  rw [enorm_sq_eq, enorm_sq_eq, enorm_sq_eq]
3980  have h : a ^ 2 ≤ 2 * (a - m) ^ 2 + 2 * m ^ 2 := by nlinarith [sq_nonneg (a - 2 * m)]
3981  calc ENNReal.ofReal (a ^ 2) ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (2 * (a - m) ^ 2 + 2 * m ^ 2) :=
3982        ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal h
3983    _ = 2 * ENNReal.ofReal ((a - m) ^ 2) + 2 * ENNReal.ofReal (m ^ 2) := by
3984        rw [ENNReal.ofReal_add (by positivity) (by positivity), ENNReal.ofReal_mul (by norm_num),
3985          ENNReal.ofReal_mul (by norm_num)]
3986        simp

3988lemma sq_mul_exp_le {u lam : ℝ} (hu : 0 ≤ u) (hlam : 0 < lam) :
3989    u ^ 2 * Real.exp (-(lam * u)) ≤ (2 / lam * Real.exp (-1)) ^ 2 := by
3990  have h1 := Real.mul_exp_neg_le_exp_neg_one (lam * u / 2)
3991  have h2 : u * Real.exp (-(lam * u / 2)) = 2 / lam * ((lam * u / 2) * Real.exp (-(lam * u / 2))) := by
3992    field_simp
3993  have h3 : u * Real.exp (-(lam * u / 2)) ≤ 2 / lam * Real.exp (-1) := by
3994    rw [h2]; exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h1 (by positivity)
3995  have h4 : u ^ 2 * Real.exp (-(lam * u)) = (u * Real.exp (-(lam * u / 2))) ^ 2 := by
3996    rw [mul_pow, ← Real.exp_nat_mul]; congr 2; push_cast; ring
3997  rw [h4]
3998  exact pow_le_pow_left₀ (by positivity) h3 2

4000/-- Second moment of the fluctuation of the row sum under the pinned ensemble. -/
4001theorem cond_moment (V : WeakPotential μ) {κ₀ : ℝ} (hκ₀ : 0 < κ₀) {n : ℕ} (hn : 0 < n)
4002    (hκ : weakKappa V κ₀ ≤ 1 / 2) (a₀ : Empty → E) (x : E) (h : ℝ) :
4003    ∫⁻ y, ‖(1 / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) * ∑ k, V.q x (y k) - h‖ₑ ^ 2
4004        ∂(pinnedLaw V (κ₀ / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) n (pinsWith a₀ x))
4005      ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (4 * n * Real.exp (V.M + V.M' + κ₀ * V.γ) / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ) ^ 2
4006            * ∫ z, V.q x z ^ 2 * Real.exp (-((κ₀ / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) * V.q z x)) ∂μ)
4007        + 2 * ‖(n / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) * ∫ z, V.q x z
4008            ∂((pinnedLaw V (κ₀ / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) n (pinsWith a₀ x)).map (fun y => y ⟨0, hn⟩)) - h‖ₑ ^ 2 := by
4009  haveI : NeZero n := ⟨hn.ne'⟩
4010  set Nr : ℝ := ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ) with hNr
4011  have hNr0 : 0 < Nr := by positivity
4012  have hN0 : 0 < n + 1 := Nat.succ_pos n
4013  set lam : ℝ := κ₀ / Nr with hlamdef
4014  have hlam : 0 ≤ lam := div_nonneg hκ₀.le hNr0.le
4015  have hlam0 : 0 < lam := div_pos hκ₀ hNr0
4016  set D : ℝ := Real.exp (V.M + V.M' + κ₀ * V.γ) with hD
4017  have hD0 : 0 ≤ D := (Real.exp_pos _).le
4018  set P' := pinnedLaw V lam n (pinsWith a₀ x) with hP'
4019  have hP'prob : IsProbabilityMeasure P' := isProb_pinnedLaw V hlam n (pinsWith a₀ x)
4020  set π : Measure E := P'.map (fun y => y ⟨0, hn⟩) with hπ
4021  have hqx : Measurable (V.q x) := V.measurable_q.comp (measurable_const.prodMk measurable_id)
4022  have hqm : ∀ k : Fin n, Measurable fun y : Fin n → E => V.q x (y k) :=
4023    fun k => hqx.comp (measurable_pi_apply k)
4024  set Y : (Fin n → E) → ℝ := fun y => ∑ k, V.q x (y k) with hY
4025  have hYm : Measurable Y := Finset.measurable_sum _ fun k _ => hqm k
4026  set Z : (Fin n → E) → ℝ := fun y => (1 / Nr) * Y y - h with hZ
4027  have hZm : Measurable Z := (hYm.const_mul _).sub_const _
4028  -- the marginal bound
4029  have hπle := pinned_marginal_le_decay V hκ₀.le (N := n + 1) (n := n) hN0
4030    (by simp) a₀ x ⟨0, hn⟩
4031  have hπle' : π ≤ μ.withDensity (fun z => ENNReal.ofReal (D * Real.exp (-(lam * V.q z x)))) := hπle
4032  -- Step A
4033  have hA : ∫⁻ y, ‖Z y‖ₑ ^ 2 ∂P' ≤ 2 * evariance Z P' + 2 * ‖∫ y, Z y ∂P'‖ₑ ^ 2 := by
4034    calc ∫⁻ y, ‖Z y‖ₑ ^ 2 ∂P'
4035        ≤ ∫⁻ y, (2 * ‖Z y - ∫ y, Z y ∂P'‖ₑ ^ 2 + 2 * ‖∫ y, Z y ∂P'‖ₑ ^ 2) ∂P' :=
4036          lintegral_mono fun y => enorm_sq_le _ _
4037      _ = 2 * ∫⁻ y, ‖Z y - ∫ y, Z y ∂P'‖ₑ ^ 2 ∂P' + 2 * ‖∫ y, Z y ∂P'‖ₑ ^ 2 := by
4038          rw [lintegral_add_right _ measurable_const, lintegral_const_mul' _ _ (by simp),
4039            lintegral_const, measure_univ, mul_one]
4040      _ = _ := rfl
4041  -- Step C: evariance of Z
4042  have hC : evariance Z P' = ENNReal.ofReal ((1 / Nr) ^ 2) * evariance Y P' := by
4043    have : Z = fun y => (1 / Nr) * Y y + (-h) := funext fun y => by simp only [hZ]; ring
4044    rw [this, evariance_add_const' _ ((hYm.const_mul _).aestronglyMeasurable), evariance_mul]
4045  -- Step D: Wu linear
4046  have hD' : evariance Y P' ≤ 2 * n * evariance (V.q x) π := by
4047    have h1 := weak_linear_variance_symm V hκ₀.le hN0 (n := n) (N := n + 1) (B := Option Empty)
4048      (by simp [Fintype.card_option]) (pinsWith a₀ x) hκ (V.q x) hqx
4049    have h0 : P'.map (fun y => y ⟨0, hn⟩) = P'.map (fun y => y (0 : Fin n)) :=
4050      marginal_symm V hlam (pinsWith a₀ x) ⟨0, hn⟩ 0
4051    rw [hπ, h0]
4052    exact h1
4053  -- Step E: second moment of q under the marginal
4054  have hQ : evariance (V.q x) π ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (D * ∫ z, V.q x z ^ 2 * Real.exp (-(lam * V.q z x)) ∂μ) := by
4055    refine (evariance_le_lintegral_sub π hqx.aestronglyMeasurable 0).trans ?_
4056    have hgm : Measurable fun z => ENNReal.ofReal (D * Real.exp (-(lam * V.q z x))) := by
4057      refine ENNReal.measurable_ofReal.comp (measurable_const.mul (Real.measurable_exp.comp ?_))
4058      exact (measurable_const.mul (V.measurable_q.comp (measurable_id.prodMk measurable_const))).neg
4059    have hfm : Measurable fun z => ‖V.q x z - 0‖ₑ ^ 2 := ((hqx.sub_const 0).enorm).pow_const 2
4060    have hint : Integrable (fun z => D * (V.q x z ^ 2 * Real.exp (-(lam * V.q z x)))) μ := by
4061      refine Integrable.of_bound ?_ (D * (2 / lam * Real.exp (-1)) ^ 2) (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun z => ?_)
4062      · exact (measurable_const.mul ((hqx.pow_const 2).mul (Real.measurable_exp.comp
4063          ((measurable_const.mul (V.measurable_q.comp (measurable_id.prodMk measurable_const))).neg)))).aestronglyMeasurable
4064      · have hq0 := V.q_nonneg x z
4065        have := sq_mul_exp_le hq0 hlam0
4066        rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_nonneg (by have := Real.exp_pos (-(lam * V.q z x)); positivity)]
4067        have e : V.q z x = V.q x z := V.q_symm _ _
4068        rw [e]
4069        exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left this hD0
4070    calc ∫⁻ z, ‖V.q x z - 0‖ₑ ^ 2 ∂π
4071        ≤ ∫⁻ z, ‖V.q x z - 0‖ₑ ^ 2 ∂(μ.withDensity (fun z => ENNReal.ofReal (D * Real.exp (-(lam * V.q z x))))) :=
4072          lintegral_mono' hπle' le_rfl
4073      _ = ∫⁻ z, ENNReal.ofReal (D * Real.exp (-(lam * V.q z x))) * ‖V.q x z - 0‖ₑ ^ 2 ∂μ :=
4074          lintegral_withDensity_eq_lintegral_mul _ hgm hfm
4075      _ = ∫⁻ z, ENNReal.ofReal (D * (V.q x z ^ 2 * Real.exp (-(lam * V.q z x)))) ∂μ := by
4076          refine lintegral_congr fun z => ?_
4077          rw [sub_zero, enorm_sq_eq, ← ENNReal.ofReal_mul (by positivity)]
4078          congr 1; ring
4079      _ = ENNReal.ofReal (∫ z, D * (V.q x z ^ 2 * Real.exp (-(lam * V.q z x))) ∂μ) :=
4080          (ofReal_integral_eq_lintegral_ofReal hint (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun z => by
4081            have := Real.exp_pos (-(lam * V.q z x)); positivity)).symm
4082      _ = _ := by rw [integral_const_mul]
4083  -- the mean
4084  have hπD : π ≤ ENNReal.ofReal D • μ := by
4085    refine hπle'.trans ?_
4086    rw [← withDensity_const]
4087    refine withDensity_mono (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun z => ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal ?_)
4088    have : Real.exp (-(lam * V.q z x)) ≤ 1 :=
4089      Real.exp_le_one_iff.2 (by have := mul_nonneg hlam (V.q_nonneg z x); linarith)
4090    nlinarith
4091  have hqπ : Integrable (V.q x) π :=
4092    (integral_le_of_le_smul hD0 hπD (fun z => V.q_nonneg x z) (V.integrable_q x)).1
4093  have hπk : ∀ k : Fin n, P'.map (fun y => y k) = π := fun k => marginal_symm V hlam _ k ⟨0, hn⟩
4094  have hint : ∀ k : Fin n, Integrable (fun y : Fin n → E => V.q x (y k)) P' := by
4095    intro k
4096    have h1 := hqπ
4097    rw [← hπk k] at h1
4098    exact (integrable_map_measure hqx.aestronglyMeasurable (measurable_pi_apply k).aemeasurable).1 h1
4099  have hmean : ∫ y, Z y ∂P' = (n / Nr) * ∫ z, V.q x z ∂π - h := by
4100    have e1 : ∫ y, Y y ∂P' = n * ∫ z, V.q x z ∂π := by
4101      simp only [hY]
4102      rw [integral_finset_sum _ fun k _ => hint k]
4103      have : ∀ k : Fin n, ∫ y, V.q x (y k) ∂P' = ∫ z, V.q x z ∂π := fun k => by
4104        rw [← hπk k, integral_map (measurable_pi_apply k).aemeasurable hqx.aestronglyMeasurable]
4105      rw [Finset.sum_congr rfl fun k _ => this k]
4106      simp
4107    have hYi : Integrable Y P' := by simp only [hY]; exact integrable_finset_sum _ fun k _ => hint k
4108    simp only [hZ]
4109    rw [integral_sub (hYi.const_mul _) (integrable_const _), integral_const_mul, e1]
4110    simp only [integral_const, probReal_univ, smul_eq_mul, one_mul]
4111    ring
4112  -- assemble
4113  calc ∫⁻ y, ‖(1 / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) * ∑ k, V.q x (y k) - h‖ₑ ^ 2 ∂P'
4114      = ∫⁻ y, ‖Z y‖ₑ ^ 2 ∂P' := rfl
4115    _ ≤ 2 * evariance Z P' + 2 * ‖∫ y, Z y ∂P'‖ₑ ^ 2 := hA
4116    _ ≤ 2 * (ENNReal.ofReal ((1 / Nr) ^ 2) * (2 * n * ENNReal.ofReal (D * ∫ z, V.q x z ^ 2 * Real.exp (-(lam * V.q z x)) ∂μ)))
4117          + 2 * ‖∫ y, Z y ∂P'‖ₑ ^ 2 := by
4118        rw [hC]
4119        gcongr
4120        exact hD'.trans (mul_le_mul' le_rfl hQ)
4121    _ = _ := by
4122        rw [hmean]
4123        congr 1
4124        have hQ0 : 0 ≤ ∫ z, V.q x z ^ 2 * Real.exp (-(lam * V.q z x)) ∂μ :=
4125          integral_nonneg fun z => mul_nonneg (sq_nonneg _) (Real.exp_pos _).le
4126        have : (2 : ℝ≥0∞) * (ENNReal.ofReal ((1 / Nr) ^ 2) * (2 * (n : ℝ≥0∞) * ENNReal.ofReal (D * ∫ z, V.q x z ^ 2 * Real.exp (-(lam * V.q z x)) ∂μ)))
4127            = ENNReal.ofReal (2 * ((1 / Nr) ^ 2 * (2 * n * (D * ∫ z, V.q x z ^ 2 * Real.exp (-(lam * V.q z x)) ∂μ)))) := by
4128          rw [ENNReal.ofReal_mul (by norm_num : (0:ℝ) ≤ 2),
4129            ENNReal.ofReal_mul (by positivity : (0:ℝ) ≤ (1 / Nr) ^ 2),
4130            ENNReal.ofReal_mul (by positivity : (0:ℝ) ≤ 2 * n)]
4131          simp [ENNReal.ofReal_natCast]
4132        rw [this]
4133        congr 1
4134        field_simp
4135        ring
4136
4137end HRS.WeakRef.Abs
