4152lemma measurable_pinWeight (V : WeakPotential μ) {lam : ℝ} (hlam : 0 ≤ lam) (a : B → E) (n : ℕ) :
4153    Measurable (pinWeight V lam a n) := by
4154  have hψ := isField_pinField V hlam a
4155  have h1 : pinWeight V lam a n = fun x =>
4156      fieldNorm V (pinField V lam a) lam n * ENNReal.ofReal (cavG V lam a n x)
4157        * ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (pinField V lam a x))
4158        * (fieldNorm V (pinField V lam a) lam (n + 1))⁻¹ := by
4159    funext x
4160    unfold pinWeight
4161    rw [fieldNorm_pin_eq V hlam a x n]
4162  rw [h1]
4163  have hpm : Measurable (pinField V lam a) := hψ.measurable
4164  exact (((measurable_const.mul (measurable_cavG V hlam a n).ennreal_ofReal).mul
4165    (Real.measurable_exp.comp hpm).ennreal_ofReal).mul measurable_const)

4167/-- The density of a marginal of the pinned ensemble is `≤ D` almost everywhere. -/
4168lemma pinWeight_le_ae (V : WeakPotential μ) {β : ℝ} (hβ : 0 ≤ β) {N n : ℕ} (hN0 : 0 < N)
4169    (hN : n + 1 + Fintype.card B ≤ N) (a : B → E) :
4170    ∀ᵐ x ∂μ, pinWeight V (β / N) a n x ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (V.M + V.M' + β * V.γ)) := by
4171  have hlam : 0 ≤ β / N := div_nonneg hβ (Nat.cast_nonneg N)
4172  refine ae_le_of_forall_setLIntegral_le_of_sigmaFinite (measurable_pinWeight V hlam a n) ?_
4173  intro A hA _
4174  have h1 := marginal_self_succ V hlam a (n := n) 0
4175  have h2 := marginal_le V hβ hN0 hN a (0 : Fin (n + 1))
4176  rw [h1] at h2
4177  have := h2 A
4178  rw [withDensity_apply _ hA] at this
4179  rw [setLIntegral_const]
4180  simpa using this

4183lemma sum_erase_succAbove {n : ℕ} (i : Fin (n + 1)) (F : Fin (n + 1) → ℝ) :
4184    ∑ l ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, F l = ∑ k : Fin n, F (i.succAbove k) := by

4189lemma msq_le {r θ hp H' Th γ Nr : ℝ} (hr0 : 0 ≤ r) (hr1 : r ≤ 1) (hθ : |θ| ≤ Th)
4190    (hH0 : 0 ≤ H') (hHa : H' ≤ γ) (hNr : 0 < Nr) :
4191    (r * θ + r * hp - H' / Nr) ^ 2 ≤ 3 * Th ^ 2 + 3 * hp ^ 2 + 3 * γ ^ 2 / Nr ^ 2 := by
4192  have h1 : (r * θ) ^ 2 ≤ Th ^ 2 := by
4193    have : |r * θ| ≤ Th := by
4194      rw [abs_mul, abs_of_nonneg hr0]
4195      calc r * |θ| ≤ 1 * Th := mul_le_mul hr1 hθ (abs_nonneg _) (by norm_num)
4196        _ = Th := one_mul _
4197    have := pow_le_pow_left₀ (abs_nonneg _) this 2
4198    rwa [sq_abs] at this
4199  have h2 : (r * hp) ^ 2 ≤ hp ^ 2 := by
4200    rw [mul_pow]
4201    have : r ^ 2 ≤ 1 := by nlinarith
4202    nlinarith [sq_nonneg hp]
4203  have h3 : (H' / Nr) ^ 2 ≤ γ ^ 2 / Nr ^ 2 := by
4204    rw [div_pow]
4205    exact div_le_div_of_nonneg_right (pow_le_pow_left₀ hH0 hHa 2) (by positivity)
4206  have key : ∀ a b c : ℝ, (a + b - c) ^ 2 ≤ 3 * a ^ 2 + 3 * b ^ 2 + 3 * c ^ 2 := by
4207    intro a b c; nlinarith [sq_nonneg (a - b), sq_nonneg (a + c), sq_nonneg (b + c)]
4208  have := key (r * θ) (r * hp) (H' / Nr)
4209  have e : 3 * γ ^ 2 / Nr ^ 2 = 3 * (γ ^ 2 / Nr ^ 2) := by ring
4210  rw [e]
4211  nlinarith

4214/-- Second moment of the single-coordinate fluctuation `g_i` under the full ensemble. -/
4215theorem second_moment_gi (V : WeakPotential μ) {κ₀ : ℝ} (hκ₀ : 0 < κ₀) {n : ℕ} (hn : 0 < n)
4216    (hκ : weakKappa V κ₀ ≤ 1 / 2) (a₀ : Empty → E) (i : Fin (n + 1))
4217    {Qb Thb εf : ℝ}
4218    (hQb : ∀ x, ∫ z, V.q x z ^ 2 * Real.exp (-((κ₀ / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) * V.q z x)) ∂μ ≤ Qb)
4219    (hThb : ∀ (x : E) (ν₁ ν₂ : Measure E), IsProbabilityMeasure ν₁ → IsProbabilityMeasure ν₂ →
4220        ν₁ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (V.M + V.M' + κ₀ * V.γ)) • μ →
4221        ν₂ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (V.M + V.M' + κ₀ * V.γ)) • μ →
4222        (∀ A, MeasurableSet A → |ν₁.real A - ν₂.real A|
4223          ≤ 2 * weakKappa V κ₀ / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) →
4224        |∫ z, V.q x z ∂ν₁ - ∫ z, V.q x z ∂ν₂| ≤ Thb)
4225    (g : E → ℝ) (hgm : Measurable g) (hg : ∀ y, 0 ≤ g y ∧ g y ≤ Real.exp (V.M + V.M' + κ₀ * V.γ))
4226    (hgπ : (pinnedLaw V (κ₀ / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) (n + 1) a₀).map (fun y => y i)
4227      = μ.withDensity (fun y => ENNReal.ofReal (g y)))
4228    (hgf : ∫ y, (g y - 1) ^ 2 ∂μ ≤ εf ^ 2) :
4229    ∫⁻ x, ‖(1 / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) * ∑ l ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, V.q (x i) (x l)
4230          - (∫ y, V.q (x i) y ∂μ)‖ₑ ^ 2
4231        ∂(pinnedLaw V (κ₀ / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) (n + 1) a₀)
4232      ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (4 * n * Real.exp (V.M + V.M' + κ₀ * V.γ) / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ) ^ 2 * Qb
4233          + 6 * Thb ^ 2 + 6 * V.γ ^ 2 / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ) ^ 2
4234          + 6 * Real.exp (V.M + V.M' + κ₀ * V.γ) * V.γ ^ 2 * εf ^ 2) := by
4235  haveI : NeZero n := ⟨hn.ne'⟩
4236  set Nr : ℝ := ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ) with hNr
4237  have hNr0 : 0 < Nr := by positivity
4238  have hN0 : 0 < n + 1 := Nat.succ_pos n
4239  set lam : ℝ := κ₀ / Nr with hlamdef
4240  have hlam : 0 ≤ lam := div_nonneg hκ₀.le hNr0.le
4241  set D : ℝ := Real.exp (V.M + V.M' + κ₀ * V.γ) with hD
4242  have hD0 : 0 ≤ D := (Real.exp_pos _).le
4243  set P := pinnedLaw V lam (n + 1) a₀ with hP
4244  have hPprob : IsProbabilityMeasure P := isProb_pinnedLaw V hlam (n + 1) a₀
4245  set H : E → ℝ := fun x => ∫ y, V.q x y ∂μ with hH
4246  have hHm : Measurable H := measurable_rowInt_meas V μ
4247  have hH0 : ∀ x, 0 ≤ H x := fun x => integral_nonneg fun y => V.q_nonneg x y
4248  have hHa : ∀ x, H x ≤ V.γ := fun x => V.row_le x
4249  have hγ0 := V.γ_nonneg
4250  set π : Measure E := P.map (fun y => y i) with hπ
4251  have hqx : ∀ x, Measurable (V.q x) := fun x =>
4252    V.measurable_q.comp (measurable_const.prodMk measurable_id)
4253  -- Step 1: disintegration over the value of coordinate `i`
4254  set Φ : E → (Fin n → E) → ℝ≥0∞ := fun x y =>
4255    ‖(1 / Nr) * ∑ k, V.q x (y k) - H x‖ₑ ^ 2 with hΦ
4256  have hΦm : Measurable fun p : E × (Fin n → E) => Φ p.1 p.2 := by
4257    refine (Measurable.enorm ?_).pow_const 2
4258    refine (measurable_const.mul (Finset.measurable_sum _ fun k _ => ?_)).sub (hHm.comp measurable_fst)
4259    exact V.measurable_q.comp (measurable_fst.prodMk ((measurable_pi_apply k).comp measurable_snd))
4260  have e1 : ∀ X : Fin (n + 1) → E, ‖(1 / Nr) * ∑ l ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, V.q (X i) (X l)
4261        - (∫ y, V.q (X i) y ∂μ)‖ₑ ^ 2 = Φ (X i) (fun k => X (i.succAbove k)) := by
4262    intro X
4263    simp only [hΦ, hH]
4264    rw [sum_erase_succAbove i (fun l => V.q (X i) (X l))]
4265  simp_rw [e1]
4266  rw [lintegral_pinned_succ V hlam a₀ i Φ hΦm]
4267  -- the field of the full marginal
4268  set Hπ : E → ℝ := fun x => ∫ z, V.q x z ∂π with hHπdef
4269  have hHπ : ∀ x, Hπ x = ∫ z, V.q x z * g z ∂μ := by
4270    intro x
4271    simp only [hHπdef]
4272    rw [hgπ, integral_density hgm (fun y => (hg y).1)]
4273    refine integral_congr_ae (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun z => ?_)
4274    ring
4275  have hDg : ∀ y, |g y - 1| ≤ D + 1 := fun y => by
4276    rw [abs_le]; constructor <;> linarith [(hg y).1, (hg y).2]
4277  have hHπH : ∀ x, Hπ x - H x = ∫ z, V.q x z * (g z - 1) ∂μ := by
4278    intro x
4279    rw [hHπ x]
4280    have h1 : Integrable (fun z => V.q x z * g z) μ :=
4281      Integrable.mono' ((V.integrable_q x).const_mul D) ((hqx x).mul hgm).aestronglyMeasurable
4282        (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun z => by
4283          rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_nonneg (mul_nonneg (V.q_nonneg x z) (hg z).1), mul_comm]
4284          exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right (hg z).2 (V.q_nonneg x z))
4285    simp only [hH, mul_sub, mul_one]
4286    rw [integral_sub h1 (V.integrable_q x)]
4287  have hSchur : ∫ x, (Hπ x - H x) ^ 2 ∂μ ≤ V.γ ^ 2 * εf ^ 2 := by
4288    simp_rw [hHπH]
4289    have hs := schur_sq (μ := μ) V.measurable_q V.q_nonneg V.q_symm hγ0 V.row_le V.integrable_q
4290      (hgm.sub_const 1) (C := D + 1) (by linarith) hDg
4291    refine hs.trans ?_
4292    exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hgf (sq_nonneg _)
4293  haveI : Nonempty E := Measure.nonempty_of_neZero μ
4294  obtain ⟨x₀⟩ := (inferInstance : Nonempty E)
4295  have hQb0 : 0 ≤ Qb := (integral_nonneg fun z => mul_nonneg (sq_nonneg _) (Real.exp_pos _).le).trans (hQb x₀)
4296  set c₁ : ℝ := 4 * n * D / Nr ^ 2 with hc₁
4297  have hc₁0 : 0 ≤ c₁ := by positivity
4298  have hNrn : Nr = (n : ℝ) + 1 := by simp [hNr]
4299  have hπprob : IsProbabilityMeasure π := by rw [hπ]; infer_instance
4300  have hπD : π ≤ ENNReal.ofReal D • μ := by
4301    have := marginal_le V hκ₀.le hN0 (n := n + 1) (B := Empty) (by simp) a₀ i
4302    exact this
4303  have hx : ∀ x, ∫⁻ y, Φ x y ∂(pinnedLaw V lam n (pinsWith a₀ x))
4304      ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (c₁ * Qb) + ENNReal.ofReal (6 * Thb ^ 2 + 6 * V.γ ^ 2 / Nr ^ 2)
4305        + 6 * ENNReal.ofReal ((Hπ x - H x) ^ 2) := by
4306    intro x
4307    have hcm := cond_moment V hκ₀ hn hκ a₀ x (H x)
4308    set πx : Measure E := (pinnedLaw V lam n (pinsWith a₀ x)).map (fun y => y ⟨0, hn⟩) with hπx
4309    have hπxprob : IsProbabilityMeasure πx := by
4310      have := isProb_pinnedLaw V hlam n (pinsWith a₀ x)
4311      rw [hπx]; infer_instance
4312    have hπxD : πx ≤ ENNReal.ofReal D • μ :=
4313      marginal_le V hκ₀.le hN0 (n := n) (B := Option Empty) (N := n + 1)
4314        (by simp [Fintype.card_option]) (pinsWith a₀ x) ⟨0, hn⟩
4315    have hTV : ∀ A, MeasurableSet A → |πx.real A - π.real A| ≤ 2 * weakKappa V κ₀ / Nr := by
4316      intro A hA
4317      have h1 := rvh_pin_comparison V hκ₀.le hN0 (n := n) (B := Empty) (by simp) a₀ hκ i ⟨0, hn⟩ x hA
4318      have hsym : (pinnedLaw V lam (n + 1) a₀).map (fun y => y (i.succAbove ⟨0, hn⟩))
4319          = π := marginal_symm V hlam a₀ _ i
4320      rw [hsym] at h1
4321      exact h1
4322    have hθ := hThb x πx π hπxprob hπprob hπxD hπD hTV
4323    have hMsq := msq_le (r := (n : ℝ) / Nr) (θ := ∫ z, V.q x z ∂πx - ∫ z, V.q x z ∂π)
4324      (hp := Hπ x - H x) (H' := H x) (Th := Thb) (γ := V.γ) (Nr := Nr)
4325      (by positivity) (by rw [div_le_one hNr0, hNrn]; linarith) hθ (hH0 x) (hHa x) hNr0
4326    have hMeq : (n : ℝ) / Nr * ∫ z, V.q x z ∂πx - H x
4327        = (n : ℝ) / Nr * (∫ z, V.q x z ∂πx - ∫ z, V.q x z ∂π) + (n : ℝ) / Nr * (Hπ x - H x)
4328          - H x / Nr := by
4329      have : Hπ x = ∫ z, V.q x z ∂π := rfl
4330      rw [this]
4331      field_simp
4332      rw [hNrn]; ring
4333    refine hcm.trans ?_
4334    have hA : ENNReal.ofReal (4 * n * D / Nr ^ 2 * ∫ z, V.q x z ^ 2 * Real.exp (-(lam * V.q z x)) ∂μ)
4335        ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (c₁ * Qb) :=
4336      ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (hQb x) hc₁0)
4337    have hB : 2 * ‖(n : ℝ) / Nr * ∫ z, V.q x z ∂πx - H x‖ₑ ^ 2
4338        ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (6 * Thb ^ 2 + 6 * V.γ ^ 2 / Nr ^ 2) + 6 * ENNReal.ofReal ((Hπ x - H x) ^ 2) := by
4339      rw [enorm_sq_eq, hMeq]
4340      have h1 : ENNReal.ofReal (((n : ℝ) / Nr * (∫ z, V.q x z ∂πx - ∫ z, V.q x z ∂π)
4341          + (n : ℝ) / Nr * (Hπ x - H x) - H x / Nr) ^ 2)
4342          ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (3 * Thb ^ 2 + 3 * (Hπ x - H x) ^ 2 + 3 * V.γ ^ 2 / Nr ^ 2) :=
4343        ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal hMsq
4344      have hThb0 : 0 ≤ Thb := (abs_nonneg _).trans hθ
4345      calc 2 * ENNReal.ofReal _ ≤ 2 * ENNReal.ofReal (3 * Thb ^ 2 + 3 * (Hπ x - H x) ^ 2 + 3 * V.γ ^ 2 / Nr ^ 2) :=
4346            mul_le_mul' le_rfl h1
4347        _ = ENNReal.ofReal (6 * Thb ^ 2 + 6 * V.γ ^ 2 / Nr ^ 2) + 6 * ENNReal.ofReal ((Hπ x - H x) ^ 2) := by
4348            have e : 2 * (3 * Thb ^ 2 + 3 * (Hπ x - H x) ^ 2 + 3 * V.γ ^ 2 / Nr ^ 2)
4349                = (6 * Thb ^ 2 + 6 * V.γ ^ 2 / Nr ^ 2) + 6 * (Hπ x - H x) ^ 2 := by ring
4350            rw [show (2 : ℝ≥0∞) * ENNReal.ofReal (3 * Thb ^ 2 + 3 * (Hπ x - H x) ^ 2 + 3 * V.γ ^ 2 / Nr ^ 2)
4351                = ENNReal.ofReal (2 * (3 * Thb ^ 2 + 3 * (Hπ x - H x) ^ 2 + 3 * V.γ ^ 2 / Nr ^ 2)) by
4352              rw [ENNReal.ofReal_mul (by norm_num)]; simp, e,
4353              ENNReal.ofReal_add (by positivity) (by positivity), ENNReal.ofReal_mul (by norm_num)]
4354            simp
4355    calc _ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (c₁ * Qb)
4356          + 2 * ‖(n : ℝ) / Nr * ∫ z, V.q x z ∂πx - H x‖ₑ ^ 2 := by
4357          gcongr
4358      _ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (c₁ * Qb) + (ENNReal.ofReal (6 * Thb ^ 2 + 6 * V.γ ^ 2 / Nr ^ 2)
4359          + 6 * ENNReal.ofReal ((Hπ x - H x) ^ 2)) := by gcongr
4360      _ = _ := by ring
4361  -- integrate against the density of coordinate `i`
4362  have hwm := measurable_pinWeight V hlam a₀ n
4363  have hwle := pinWeight_le_ae V hκ₀.le hN0 (n := n) (B := Empty) (by simp) a₀
4364  have hw1 : ∫⁻ x, pinWeight V lam a₀ n x ∂μ = 1 := lintegral_pinWeight V hlam a₀ i ⟨0, hn⟩
4365  -- measurability and boundedness of `Hπ - H`
4366  have hHπm : Measurable Hπ := by
4367    have : Hπ = fun x => ∫ z, V.q x z * g z ∂μ := funext hHπ
4368    rw [this]; exact measurable_rowInt' V.measurable_q hgm
4369  have hHπb : ∀ x, |Hπ x - H x| ≤ D * V.γ + V.γ := by
4370    intro x
4371    have h1 : 0 ≤ Hπ x := by rw [hHπ x]; exact integral_nonneg fun z => mul_nonneg (V.q_nonneg x z) (hg z).1
4372    have h2 : Hπ x ≤ D * V.γ := by
4373      rw [hHπ x]
4374      calc ∫ z, V.q x z * g z ∂μ ≤ ∫ z, D * V.q x z ∂μ :=
4375            integral_mono (Integrable.mono' ((V.integrable_q x).const_mul D) ((hqx x).mul hgm).aestronglyMeasurable
4376              (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun z => by
4377                rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_nonneg (mul_nonneg (V.q_nonneg x z) (hg z).1), mul_comm]
4378                exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right (hg z).2 (V.q_nonneg x z)))
4379              ((V.integrable_q x).const_mul D) fun z => by
4380              have := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (hg z).2 (V.q_nonneg x z)
4381              linarith [mul_comm (V.q x z) D]
4382        _ = D * ∫ z, V.q x z ∂μ := integral_const_mul _ _
4383        _ ≤ D * V.γ := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (V.row_le x) hD0
4384    rw [abs_le]; constructor <;> linarith [hH0 x, hHa x]
4385  have hint : Integrable (fun x => (Hπ x - H x) ^ 2) μ := intg_sq_bdd (hHπm.sub hHm) hHπb
4386  have hlint : ∫⁻ x, ENNReal.ofReal ((Hπ x - H x) ^ 2) ∂μ = ENNReal.ofReal (∫ x, (Hπ x - H x) ^ 2 ∂μ) :=
4387    (ofReal_integral_eq_lintegral_ofReal hint (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun x => sq_nonneg _)).symm
4388  have hAm : Measurable fun x => ENNReal.ofReal ((Hπ x - H x) ^ 2) :=
4389    (((hHπm.sub hHm).pow_const 2)).ennreal_ofReal
4390  set A₀ : ℝ≥0∞ := ENNReal.ofReal (c₁ * Qb) + ENNReal.ofReal (6 * Thb ^ 2 + 6 * V.γ ^ 2 / Nr ^ 2) with hA₀
4391  calc ∫⁻ x, pinWeight V lam a₀ n x * ∫⁻ y, Φ x y ∂(pinnedLaw V lam n (pinsWith a₀ x)) ∂μ
4392      ≤ ∫⁻ x, pinWeight V lam a₀ n x * (A₀ + 6 * ENNReal.ofReal ((Hπ x - H x) ^ 2)) ∂μ :=
4393        lintegral_mono fun x => mul_le_mul' le_rfl (hx x)
4394    _ = A₀ + 6 * ∫⁻ x, pinWeight V lam a₀ n x * ENNReal.ofReal ((Hπ x - H x) ^ 2) ∂μ := by
4395        have e : ∀ x, pinWeight V lam a₀ n x * (A₀ + 6 * ENNReal.ofReal ((Hπ x - H x) ^ 2))
4396            = pinWeight V lam a₀ n x * A₀ + 6 * (pinWeight V lam a₀ n x * ENNReal.ofReal ((Hπ x - H x) ^ 2)) := by
4397          intro x; ring
4398        simp_rw [e]
4399        rw [lintegral_add_left (f := fun x => pinWeight V lam a₀ n x * A₀) (hwm.mul measurable_const),
4400          lintegral_mul_const _ hwm, hw1, one_mul, lintegral_const_mul' 6 _ (by norm_num)]
4401    _ ≤ A₀ + 6 * (ENNReal.ofReal D * ∫⁻ x, ENNReal.ofReal ((Hπ x - H x) ^ 2) ∂μ) := by
4402        gcongr
4403        rw [← lintegral_const_mul _ hAm]
4404        refine lintegral_mono_ae ?_
4405        filter_upwards [hwle] with x hx'
4406        exact mul_le_mul' hx' le_rfl
4407    _ = A₀ + ENNReal.ofReal (6 * D * ∫ x, (Hπ x - H x) ^ 2 ∂μ) := by
4408        have hI0 : 0 ≤ ∫ x, (Hπ x - H x) ^ 2 ∂μ := integral_nonneg fun x => sq_nonneg _
4409        rw [hlint, ENNReal.ofReal_mul (by positivity : (0:ℝ) ≤ 6 * D), ENNReal.ofReal_mul (by norm_num : (0:ℝ) ≤ 6)]
4410        simp [mul_assoc]
4411    _ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (4 * n * D / Nr ^ 2 * Qb + 6 * Thb ^ 2 + 6 * V.γ ^ 2 / Nr ^ 2 + 6 * D * V.γ ^ 2 * εf ^ 2) := by
4412        have hThb0 : 0 ≤ Thb := by
4413          have := hThb x₀ π π hπprob hπprob hπD hπD (fun A hA => by
4414            rw [sub_self, abs_zero]
4415            exact div_nonneg (mul_nonneg (by norm_num) (weakKappa_nonneg V hκ₀.le)) hNr0.le)
4416          exact (abs_nonneg _).trans this
4417        have hI : 6 * D * ∫ x, (Hπ x - H x) ^ 2 ∂μ ≤ 6 * D * (V.γ ^ 2 * εf ^ 2) :=
4418          mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hSchur (by positivity)
4419        have hI0 : 0 ≤ ∫ x, (Hπ x - H x) ^ 2 ∂μ := integral_nonneg fun x => sq_nonneg _
4420        have hp1 : 0 ≤ c₁ * Qb := mul_nonneg hc₁0 hQb0
4421        have hp2 : 0 ≤ 6 * Thb ^ 2 + 6 * V.γ ^ 2 / Nr ^ 2 := by positivity
4422        rw [hA₀, ← ENNReal.ofReal_add hp1 hp2,
4423          ← ENNReal.ofReal_add (add_nonneg hp1 hp2) (mul_nonneg (by positivity) hI0)]
4424        refine ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal ?_
4425        rw [hc₁]
4426        nlinarith
4427
4428end HRS.WeakRef.Abs

4432section WM_N
4433open MeasureTheory ProbabilityTheory Function
4434open scoped ENNReal NNReal BigOperators
4435set_option linter.unusedSectionVars false

4437namespace HRS.WeakRef.Abs
4438open HRS.External
4439
4440variable {E : Type*} [MeasurableSpace E] {μ : Measure E} [IsProbabilityMeasure μ]
4441  {B : Type*} [Fintype B]
4442
4443/-- **Variance bound** (source `N:eq:pressure-variance`, abstract form). -/
4444theorem variance_G_le (V : WeakPotential μ) {κ₀ : ℝ} (hκ₀ : 0 < κ₀) {n : ℕ} (hn : 0 < n)
4445    (hκ : weakKappa V κ₀ ≤ 1 / 2) (a₀ : Empty → E)
4446    {Qb Thb εf : ℝ}
4447    (hQb : ∀ x, ∫ z, V.q x z ^ 2 * Real.exp (-((κ₀ / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) * V.q z x)) ∂μ ≤ Qb)
4448    (hThb : ∀ (x : E) (ν₁ ν₂ : Measure E), IsProbabilityMeasure ν₁ → IsProbabilityMeasure ν₂ →
4449        ν₁ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (V.M + V.M' + κ₀ * V.γ)) • μ →
4450        ν₂ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (V.M + V.M' + κ₀ * V.γ)) • μ →
4451        (∀ A, MeasurableSet A → |ν₁.real A - ν₂.real A|
4452          ≤ 2 * weakKappa V κ₀ / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) →
4453        |∫ z, V.q x z ∂ν₁ - ∫ z, V.q x z ∂ν₂| ≤ Thb)
4454    (g : E → ℝ) (hgm : Measurable g) (hg : ∀ y, 0 ≤ g y ∧ g y ≤ Real.exp (V.M + V.M' + κ₀ * V.γ))
4455    (hgπ : (pinnedLaw V (κ₀ / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) (n + 1) a₀).map (fun y => y ⟨0, Nat.succ_pos n⟩)
4456      = μ.withDensity (fun y => ENNReal.ofReal (g y)))
4457    (hgf : ∫ y, (g y - 1) ^ 2 ∂μ ≤ εf ^ 2)
4458    (G : (Fin (n + 1) → E) → ℝ)
4459    (hG : ∀ X, G X = (1 / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) * pairEnergy V X - ∑ k, ∫ y, V.q (X k) y ∂μ) :
4460    variance G (pinnedLaw V (κ₀ / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) (n + 1) a₀)
4461      ≤ 2 * ((n : ℝ) + 1) * (4 * n * Real.exp (V.M + V.M' + κ₀ * V.γ) / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ) ^ 2 * Qb
4462          + 6 * Thb ^ 2 + 6 * V.γ ^ 2 / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ) ^ 2
4463          + 6 * Real.exp (V.M + V.M' + κ₀ * V.γ) * V.γ ^ 2 * εf ^ 2) := by
4464  set Nr : ℝ := ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ) with hNr
4465  have hNr0 : 0 < Nr := by positivity
4466  have hlam : 0 ≤ κ₀ / Nr := div_nonneg hκ₀.le hNr0.le
4467  set D : ℝ := Real.exp (V.M + V.M' + κ₀ * V.γ) with hD
4468  set P := pinnedLaw V (κ₀ / Nr) (n + 1) a₀ with hP
4469  have hPprob : IsProbabilityMeasure P := isProb_pinnedLaw V hlam (n + 1) a₀
4470  set H : E → ℝ := fun x => ∫ y, V.q x y ∂μ with hH
4471  have hHm : Measurable H := measurable_rowInt_meas V μ
4472  set S : ℝ := 4 * n * D / Nr ^ 2 * Qb + 6 * Thb ^ 2 + 6 * V.γ ^ 2 / Nr ^ 2 + 6 * D * V.γ ^ 2 * εf ^ 2 with hS
4473  have hev := evariance_G_le V hκ₀.le hκ H hHm G hG
4474  haveI : Nonempty E := Measure.nonempty_of_neZero μ
4475  obtain ⟨x₀⟩ := (inferInstance : Nonempty E)
4476  have hQb0 : 0 ≤ Qb := (integral_nonneg fun z => mul_nonneg (sq_nonneg _) (Real.exp_pos _).le).trans (hQb x₀)
4477  have hS0 : 0 ≤ S := by
4478    have : 0 ≤ D := (Real.exp_pos _).le
4479    simp only [hS]; positivity
4480  have hterm : ∀ i : Fin (n + 1), ∫⁻ x, ‖(1 / Nr) * ∑ l ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, V.q (x i) (x l)
4481        - (∫ y, V.q (x i) y ∂μ)‖ₑ ^ 2 ∂P ≤ ENNReal.ofReal S := by
4482    intro i
4483    have hgi : P.map (fun y => y i) = μ.withDensity (fun y => ENNReal.ofReal (g y)) := by
4484      rw [← hgπ]; exact marginal_symm V hlam a₀ i ⟨0, Nat.succ_pos n⟩
4485    exact second_moment_gi V hκ₀ hn hκ a₀ i hQb hThb g hgm hg hgi hgf
4486  have hbound : evariance G P ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (2 * ((n : ℝ) + 1) * S) := by
4487    refine hev.trans ?_
4488    calc 2 * ∑ i : Fin (n + 1), ∫⁻ x, ‖(1 / Nr) * ∑ l ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, V.q (x i) (x l)
4489          - (∫ y, V.q (x i) y ∂μ)‖ₑ ^ 2 ∂P
4490        ≤ 2 * ∑ _i : Fin (n + 1), ENNReal.ofReal S :=
4491          mul_le_mul' le_rfl (Finset.sum_le_sum fun i _ => hterm i)
4492      _ = ENNReal.ofReal (2 * ((n : ℝ) + 1) * S) := by
4493          rw [Finset.sum_const, Finset.card_univ, Fintype.card_fin, nsmul_eq_mul]
4494          have e : ENNReal.ofReal (2 * ((n : ℝ) + 1)) = 2 * ((n : ℝ≥0∞) + 1) := by
4495            rw [ENNReal.ofReal_mul (by norm_num), ENNReal.ofReal_add (by positivity) (by norm_num)]
4496            simp
4497          rw [ENNReal.ofReal_mul (by positivity), e]
4498          push_cast
4499          ring
4500  have hGm : Measurable G := by
4501    have : G = fun X => (1 / Nr) * pairEnergy V X - ∑ k, H (X k) := funext hG
4502    rw [this]
4503    exact (measurable_pairEnergy V |>.const_mul _).sub
4504      (Finset.measurable_sum _ fun k _ => hHm.comp (measurable_pi_apply k))
4505  have hlt : evariance G P < ⊤ := lt_of_le_of_lt hbound ENNReal.ofReal_lt_top
4506  have hmem : MemLp G 2 P := (evariance_lt_top_iff_memLp hGm.aestronglyMeasurable).1 hlt
4507  have h2 := hbound
4508  rw [← ofReal_variance hmem] at h2
4509  have hS2 : 0 ≤ 2 * ((n : ℝ) + 1) * S := by positivity
4510  exact (ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal_iff hS2).1 h2
4511
4512end HRS.WeakRef.Abs

4523/-- ODE comparison: `ψ'' ≤ V` on `(0, β]` with `W' = V`, `W(0) = 0` gives `ψ(β) ≤ ψ(0) + β (ψ'(0) + W(β))`. -/
4524theorem ode_bound {ψ m v V W : ℝ → ℝ} {β : ℝ} (hβ : 0 < β)
4525    (hψ : ∀ t, 0 < t → HasDerivAt ψ (m t) t) (hm : ∀ t, 0 < t → HasDerivAt m (v t) t)
4526    (hv : ∀ t ∈ Ioc 0 β, v t ≤ V t) (hV0 : ∀ t ∈ Ioc 0 β, 0 ≤ V t)
4527    (hW : ∀ t, 0 < t → HasDerivAt W (V t) t) (hWc : ContinuousWithinAt W (Ici 0) 0) (hW0 : W 0 = 0)
4528    (hψc : ContinuousWithinAt ψ (Ici 0) 0) (hmc : ContinuousWithinAt m (Ici 0) 0) :
4529    ψ β ≤ ψ 0 + β * (m 0 + W β) := by
4530  have hcontOn : ∀ f : ℝ → ℝ, (∀ t, 0 < t → ContinuousAt f t) → ContinuousWithinAt f (Ici 0) 0 →
4531      ContinuousOn f (Icc 0 β) := by
4532    intro f hf hf0 t ht
4533    rcases eq_or_lt_of_le ht.1 with h | h
4534    · rw [← h]; exact hf0.mono Icc_subset_Ici_self
4535    · exact (hf t h).continuousWithinAt
4536  have hint : ∀ t ∈ interior (Icc 0 β), 0 < t ∧ t < β := fun t ht => by
4537    rw [interior_Icc] at ht; exact ht
4538  have hWc' := hcontOn W (fun t ht => (hW t ht).continuousAt) hWc
4539  have hmc' := hcontOn m (fun t ht => (hm t ht).continuousAt) hmc
4540  have hψc' := hcontOn ψ (fun t ht => (hψ t ht).continuousAt) hψc
4541  -- `W` is monotone on `[0, β]`
4542  have hWmono : MonotoneOn W (Icc 0 β) := by
4543    refine monotoneOn_of_deriv_nonneg (convex_Icc 0 β) hWc'
4544      (fun t ht => (hW t (hint t ht).1).differentiableAt.differentiableWithinAt) fun t ht => ?_
4545    rw [(hW t (hint t ht).1).deriv]
4546    exact hV0 t ⟨(hint t ht).1, (hint t ht).2.le⟩
4547  -- `m - W` is antitone on `[0, β]`
4548  have hk : AntitoneOn (fun t => m t - W t) (Icc 0 β) := by
4549    refine antitoneOn_of_deriv_nonpos (convex_Icc 0 β) (hmc'.sub hWc')
4550      (fun t ht => ((hm t (hint t ht).1).sub (hW t (hint t ht).1)).differentiableAt.differentiableWithinAt)
4551      fun t ht => ?_
4552    have hd : HasDerivAt (fun t => m t - W t) (v t - V t) t :=
4553      (hm t (hint t ht).1).sub (hW t (hint t ht).1)
4554    rw [hd.deriv]
4555    have := hv t ⟨(hint t ht).1, (hint t ht).2.le⟩
4556    linarith
4557  have hmW : ∀ t ∈ Icc 0 β, m t ≤ m 0 + W β := by
4558    intro t ht
4559    have h1 := hk (show (0 : ℝ) ∈ Icc 0 β from ⟨le_rfl, hβ.le⟩) ht ht.1
4560    have h2 := hWmono ht (show β ∈ Icc 0 β from ⟨hβ.le, le_rfl⟩) ht.2
4561    simp only [hW0, sub_zero] at h1
4562    linarith
4563  -- `ψ - c t` is antitone
4564  have hh : AntitoneOn (fun t => ψ t - (m 0 + W β) * t) (Icc 0 β) := by
4565    refine antitoneOn_of_deriv_nonpos (convex_Icc 0 β)
4566      (hψc'.sub (continuousOn_const.mul continuousOn_id))
4567      (fun t ht => ((hψ t (hint t ht).1).sub ((hasDerivAt_id t).const_mul _)).differentiableAt.differentiableWithinAt)
4568      fun t ht => ?_
4569    have hd : HasDerivAt (fun t => ψ t - (m 0 + W β) * t) (m t - (m 0 + W β)) t := by
4570      have := (hψ t (hint t ht).1).sub ((hasDerivAt_id t).const_mul (m 0 + W β))
4571      convert this using 1 <;> first | rfl | (simp; done) | (funext y; simp) | ring
4572    rw [hd.deriv]
4573    have := hmW t ⟨(hint t ht).1.le, (hint t ht).2.le⟩
4574    linarith
4575  have := hh (show (0 : ℝ) ∈ Icc 0 β from ⟨le_rfl, hβ.le⟩) (show β ∈ Icc 0 β from ⟨hβ.le, le_rfl⟩) hβ.le
4576  simp only [mul_zero, sub_zero] at this
4577  linarith
4578
4579
4580lemma moment_bound {K t : ℝ} (hK : 0 ≤ K) (ht : 0 < t) (m : ℕ) :
4581    ∃ C : ℝ, 0 ≤ C ∧ ∀ g : ℝ, -K ≤ g → ∀ s : ℝ, t / 2 ≤ s → s ≤ 2 * t →
4582      |g| ^ m * Real.exp (-(s * g)) ≤ C := by
4583  refine ⟨Real.exp (4 * K * t) * ((m.factorial : ℝ) * (2 / t) ^ m), by positivity, ?_⟩
4584  intro g hg s hs1 hs2
4585  set w : ℝ := g + 2 * K with hw
4586  have hw0 : 0 ≤ w := by linarith
4587  have hgw : |g| ≤ w := by
4588    rw [abs_le]; constructor <;> linarith
4589  have h1 : |g| ^ m ≤ w ^ m := pow_le_pow_left₀ (abs_nonneg _) hgw m
4590  have h2 : Real.exp (-(s * g)) ≤ Real.exp (4 * K * t) * Real.exp (-(t / 2 * w)) := by
4591    rw [← Real.exp_add]
4592    refine Real.exp_le_exp.2 ?_
4593    have : -(s * g) = 2 * K * s - s * w := by rw [hw]; ring
4594    rw [this]
4595    nlinarith [mul_nonneg hK (by linarith : 0 ≤ t), mul_nonneg hw0 (by linarith : 0 ≤ s - t / 2)]
4596  have h3 : w ^ m * Real.exp (-(t / 2 * w)) ≤ (m.factorial : ℝ) * (2 / t) ^ m := by
4597    have := Real.pow_div_factorial_le_exp (t / 2 * w) (by positivity) m
4598    have hf : (0 : ℝ) < m.factorial := by exact_mod_cast Nat.factorial_pos m
4599    have h4 : (t / 2 * w) ^ m * Real.exp (-(t / 2 * w)) ≤ m.factorial := by
4600      have := (div_le_iff₀ hf).1 this
4601      calc (t / 2 * w) ^ m * Real.exp (-(t / 2 * w))
4602          ≤ (Real.exp (t / 2 * w) * m.factorial) * Real.exp (-(t / 2 * w)) :=
4603            mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right this (Real.exp_pos _).le
4604        _ = m.factorial := by
4605            rw [mul_comm (Real.exp _), mul_assoc, ← Real.exp_add]; simp
4606    have e : w ^ m * Real.exp (-(t / 2 * w)) = (2 / t) ^ m * ((t / 2 * w) ^ m * Real.exp (-(t / 2 * w))) := by
4607      rw [← mul_assoc, ← mul_pow]
4608      have : 2 / t * (t / 2 * w) = w := by field_simp
4609      rw [this]
4610    rw [e]
4611    nlinarith [pow_pos (show (0 : ℝ) < 2 / t by positivity) m]
4612  calc |g| ^ m * Real.exp (-(s * g)) ≤ w ^ m * (Real.exp (4 * K * t) * Real.exp (-(t / 2 * w))) :=
4613        mul_le_mul h1 h2 (Real.exp_pos _).le (by positivity)
4614    _ = Real.exp (4 * K * t) * (w ^ m * Real.exp (-(t / 2 * w))) := by ring
4615    _ ≤ _ := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h3 (Real.exp_pos _).le
4616

4618lemma integrable_expMoment {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] (P : Measure Ω) [IsProbabilityMeasure P]
4619    {G : Ω → ℝ} (hG : Measurable G) {K : ℝ} (hK0 : 0 ≤ K) (hK : ∀ ω, -K ≤ G ω) (k : ℕ)
4620    {t : ℝ} (ht : 0 < t) :
4621    Integrable (fun ω => G ω ^ k * Real.exp (-(t * G ω))) P := by
4622  obtain ⟨C, hC0, hC⟩ := moment_bound hK0 ht k
4623  refine Integrable.of_bound ((hG.pow_const k).mul (Real.measurable_exp.comp
4624    (measurable_const.mul hG).neg)).aestronglyMeasurable C (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun ω => ?_)
4625  rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_mul, abs_pow, abs_of_pos (Real.exp_pos _)]
4626  exact hC (G ω) (hK ω) t (by linarith) (by linarith)

4628lemma hasDerivAt_expInt {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] (P : Measure Ω) [IsProbabilityMeasure P]
4629    {G : Ω → ℝ} (hG : Measurable G) {K : ℝ} (hK0 : 0 ≤ K) (hK : ∀ ω, -K ≤ G ω) (k : ℕ)
4630    {t : ℝ} (ht : 0 < t) :
4631    HasDerivAt (fun s => ∫ ω, G ω ^ k * Real.exp (-(s * G ω)) ∂P)
4632      (-∫ ω, G ω ^ (k + 1) * Real.exp (-(t * G ω)) ∂P) t := by
4633  obtain ⟨C, hC0, hC⟩ := moment_bound hK0 ht (k + 1)
4634  have hmeas : ∀ s : ℝ, Measurable fun ω => G ω ^ k * Real.exp (-(s * G ω)) := fun s =>
4635    (hG.pow_const k).mul (Real.measurable_exp.comp (measurable_const.mul hG).neg)
4636  have hmeas' : ∀ s : ℝ, Measurable fun ω => -(G ω ^ (k + 1) * Real.exp (-(s * G ω))) := fun s =>
4637    ((hG.pow_const (k + 1)).mul (Real.measurable_exp.comp (measurable_const.mul hG).neg)).neg
4638  have := hasDerivAt_integral_of_dominated_loc_of_deriv_le (μ := P) (𝕜 := ℝ)
4639    (F := fun s ω => G ω ^ k * Real.exp (-(s * G ω)))
4640    (F' := fun s ω => -(G ω ^ (k + 1) * Real.exp (-(s * G ω))))
4641    (bound := fun _ => C) (x₀ := t) (s := Metric.ball t (t / 2)) (Metric.ball_mem_nhds t (by linarith))
4642    (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun s => (hmeas s).aestronglyMeasurable)
4643    (integrable_expMoment P hG hK0 hK k ht) (hmeas' t).aestronglyMeasurable
4644    (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun ω s hs => by
4645      rw [Metric.mem_ball, Real.dist_eq, abs_lt] at hs
4646      rw [norm_neg, Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_mul, abs_pow, abs_of_pos (Real.exp_pos _)]
4647      exact hC (G ω) (hK ω) s (by linarith) (by linarith))
4648    (integrable_const C)
4649    (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun ω s hs => by
4650      have h1 : HasDerivAt (fun s : ℝ => -(s * G ω)) (-G ω) s := by
4651        have := ((hasDerivAt_id s).mul_const (G ω)).neg
4652        convert this using 1 <;> first | (funext y; simp) | simp
4653      have h2 := (h1.exp).const_mul (G ω ^ k)
4654      convert h2 using 1
4655      ring)
4656  have h := this.2
4657  rw [integral_neg] at h
4658  exact h

4661section LogPartition

4663variable {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] (P : Measure Ω) (G : Ω → ℝ)

4665/-- Moments `∫ G^k e^{-s G}`. -/
4666def expZ (k : ℕ) (s : ℝ) : ℝ := ∫ ω, G ω ^ k * Real.exp (-(s * G ω)) ∂P
4667
4668def logZ (s : ℝ) : ℝ := Real.log (expZ P G 0 s)
4669
4670def mFun (s : ℝ) : ℝ := -(expZ P G 1 s) / expZ P G 0 s
4671
4672def vFun (s : ℝ) : ℝ := expZ P G 2 s / expZ P G 0 s - (expZ P G 1 s / expZ P G 0 s) ^ 2
4673
4674variable [IsProbabilityMeasure P]
4675
4676lemma expZ_zero_pos (hG : Measurable G) {K : ℝ} (hK0 : 0 ≤ K) (hK : ∀ ω, -K ≤ G ω) {t : ℝ} (ht : 0 < t) :
4677    0 < expZ P G 0 t := by
4678  unfold expZ
4679  have hi := integrable_expMoment P hG hK0 hK 0 ht
4680  simp only [pow_zero, one_mul] at hi ⊢
4681  exact (integral_pos_iff_support_of_nonneg (fun ω => (Real.exp_pos _).le) hi).2 (by
4682    have : Function.support (fun ω => Real.exp (-(t * G ω))) = Set.univ :=
4683      Set.eq_univ_of_forall fun ω => (Real.exp_pos _).ne'
4684    rw [this]; simp)
4685
4686lemma hasDerivAt_logZ (hG : Measurable G) {K : ℝ} (hK0 : 0 ≤ K) (hK : ∀ ω, -K ≤ G ω) {t : ℝ}
4687    (ht : 0 < t) : HasDerivAt (logZ P G) (mFun P G t) t := by
4688  have h0 := hasDerivAt_expInt P hG hK0 hK 0 ht
4689  have hp := expZ_zero_pos P G hG hK0 hK ht
4690  have h1 : HasDerivAt (expZ P G 0) (-expZ P G 1 t) t := h0
4691  have := h1.log hp.ne'
4692  unfold mFun logZ
4693  convert this using 1
4694
4695lemma hasDerivAt_mFun (hG : Measurable G) {K : ℝ} (hK0 : 0 ≤ K) (hK : ∀ ω, -K ≤ G ω) {t : ℝ}
4696    (ht : 0 < t) : HasDerivAt (mFun P G) (vFun P G t) t := by
4697  have hp := expZ_zero_pos P G hG hK0 hK ht
4698  have h0 : HasDerivAt (expZ P G 0) (-expZ P G 1 t) t := hasDerivAt_expInt P hG hK0 hK 0 ht
4699  have h1 : HasDerivAt (expZ P G 1) (-expZ P G 2 t) t := hasDerivAt_expInt P hG hK0 hK 1 ht
4700  have := (h1.neg).div h0 hp.ne'
4701  unfold mFun vFun
4702  convert this using 1
4703  simp only [Pi.neg_apply]
4704  field_simp

4708lemma continuousWithinAt_expZ (hG : Measurable G) (hGi : Integrable G P) {K : ℝ} (hK0 : 0 ≤ K)
4709    (hK : ∀ ω, -K ≤ G ω) {k : ℕ} (hk : k ≤ 1) :
4710    ContinuousWithinAt (expZ P G k) (Set.Ici 0) 0 := by
4711  have hint : Integrable (fun ω => (|G ω| + 1) * Real.exp K) P :=
4712    ((hGi.abs.add (integrable_const 1)).mul_const _)
4713  have hmeas : ∀ s : ℝ, Measurable fun ω => G ω ^ k * Real.exp (-(s * G ω)) := fun s =>
4714    (hG.pow_const k).mul (Real.measurable_exp.comp (measurable_const.mul hG).neg)
4715  unfold expZ
4716  refine continuousWithinAt_of_dominated (bound := fun ω => (|G ω| + 1) * Real.exp K)
4717    (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun s => (hmeas s).aestronglyMeasurable) ?_ hint
4718    (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun ω => ?_)
4719  · have : ∀ᶠ s in nhdsWithin (0 : ℝ) (Set.Ici 0), s < 1 :=
4720      (nhdsWithin_le_nhds (Iio_mem_nhds (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) < 1)))
4721    filter_upwards [this, self_mem_nhdsWithin] with s hs hs0
4722    refine Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun ω => ?_
4723    rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_mul, abs_pow, abs_of_pos (Real.exp_pos _)]
4724    have h1 : Real.exp (-(s * G ω)) ≤ Real.exp K := by
4725      refine Real.exp_le_exp.2 ?_
4726      have := hK ω
4727      have hs0' : 0 ≤ s := hs0
4728      nlinarith
4729    have h2 : |G ω| ^ k ≤ |G ω| + 1 := by
4730      interval_cases k
4731      · simp
4732      · simp
4733    exact mul_le_mul h2 h1 (Real.exp_pos _).le (by positivity)
4734  · refine Continuous.continuousWithinAt ?_
4735    fun_prop

4739lemma expZ_zero_zero : expZ P G 0 0 = 1 := by
4740  unfold expZ; simp

4742lemma expZ_one_zero : expZ P G 1 0 = ∫ ω, G ω ∂P := by
4743  unfold expZ; simp

4745/-- Upper bound for the log-partition function from a bound on the variance along the path. -/
4746theorem logZ_upper (hG : Measurable G) (hGi : Integrable G P) {K : ℝ} (hK0 : 0 ≤ K)
4747    (hK : ∀ ω, -K ≤ G ω) {β : ℝ} (hβ : 0 < β) {V W : ℝ → ℝ}
4748    (hv : ∀ t ∈ Set.Ioc 0 β, vFun P G t ≤ V t) (hV0 : ∀ t ∈ Set.Ioc 0 β, 0 ≤ V t)
4749    (hW : ∀ t, 0 < t → HasDerivAt W (V t) t) (hWc : ContinuousWithinAt W (Set.Ici 0) 0) (hW0 : W 0 = 0) :
4750    logZ P G β ≤ β * (-(∫ ω, G ω ∂P) + W β) := by
4751  have h0 := continuousWithinAt_expZ P G hG hGi hK0 hK (k := 0) (by norm_num)
4752  have h1 := continuousWithinAt_expZ P G hG hGi hK0 hK (k := 1) le_rfl
4753  have hz : expZ P G 0 0 = 1 := expZ_zero_zero P G
4754  have hψc : ContinuousWithinAt (logZ P G) (Set.Ici 0) 0 := by
4755    unfold logZ
4756    refine ContinuousWithinAt.log h0 ?_
4757    rw [hz]; norm_num
4758  have hmc : ContinuousWithinAt (mFun P G) (Set.Ici 0) 0 := by
4759    unfold mFun
4760    refine ContinuousWithinAt.div h1.neg h0 ?_
4761    rw [hz]; norm_num
4762  have := ode_bound (ψ := logZ P G) (m := mFun P G) (v := vFun P G) (V := V) (W := W) hβ
4763    (fun t ht => hasDerivAt_logZ P G hG hK0 hK ht) (fun t ht => hasDerivAt_mFun P G hG hK0 hK ht)
4764    hv hV0 hW hWc hW0 hψc hmc
4765  have e1 : logZ P G 0 = 0 := by unfold logZ; rw [hz]; simp
4766  have e2 : mFun P G 0 = -(∫ ω, G ω ∂P) := by
4767    unfold mFun; rw [hz, expZ_one_zero]; simp
4768  rw [e1, e2] at this
4769  linarith
4770
4771end LogPartition

4786/-- `ψ₂(L)`: the order of `∫ min(w, L)²`. -/
4787def psi2 (d : ℕ) (s : ℝ) (L : ℝ) : ℝ :=
4788  if s < (d : ℝ) / 2 then 1
4789  else if s = (d : ℝ) / 2 then Real.log (Real.exp 1 * L)
4790  else L ^ (2 - (d : ℝ) / s)
4791
4792lemma mul_deltaN_eq_psi2 (s : ℝ) {N : ℕ} (hN : 1 ≤ N) :
4793    (N : ℝ) * deltaN d s N = psi2 d s N := by
4794  have hNpos : (0 : ℝ) < N := by exact_mod_cast hN
4795  unfold deltaN psi2
4796  split_ifs with h1 h2
4797  · field_simp
4798  · field_simp
4799  · have : (2 : ℝ) - (d : ℝ) / s = 1 + (1 - (d : ℝ) / s) := by ring
4800    rw [this, Real.rpow_add hNpos, Real.rpow_one]
4801
4802open HRS.KernelAux in
4803theorem kernel_levels_real (hd : 1 ≤ d) {s : ℝ} (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) (cstar : ℝ)
4804    (hw : ∀ z : Torus d, z ≠ 0 → 1 ≤ periodicRiesz d s z + cstar) :
4805    ∃ C₂ : ℝ, 0 ≤ C₂ ∧ ∀ L : ℝ, 1 ≤ L →
4806      ∫⁻ z : Torus d, ENNReal.ofReal ((min (periodicRiesz d s z + cstar) L) ^ 2)
4807        ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (C₂ * psi2 d s L) := by
4808  have hd0 : (0 : ℝ) < d := by exact_mod_cast hd
4809  have hσ0 := sigmaOf_pos hd hs0
4810  have hσd := sigmaOf_lt hd hsd
4811  obtain ⟨B, hB1, hB⟩ := riesz_envelope hd hs0 hsd cstar
4812  set w : Torus d → ℝ := fun z => periodicRiesz d s z + cstar with hwdef
4813  have hwB : ∀ y ∈ Box d, y ≠ 0 → w (boxPt y) ≤ B * (1 + ‖y‖ ^ (-(sigmaOf d s))) := hB
4814  have hw0 : ∀ y ∈ Box d, y ≠ 0 → 0 ≤ w (boxPt y) := fun y hy hy0 => by
4815    have := hw (boxPt y) (fun h => hy0 (boxPt_eq_zero hy h))
4816    simp only [hwdef]; linarith
4817  rcases lt_trichotomy s ((d : ℝ) / 2) with h | h | h
4818  · have h2σ : 2 * sigmaOf d s < d := (two_sigmaOf_lt_iff hd hs0).2 h
4819    obtain ⟨C, hC, hlev⟩ := lev2a hd hσ0 hσd hB1 hwB hw0 h2σ
4820    refine ⟨C, hC, fun L hL => ?_⟩
4821    have : psi2 d s L = 1 := by unfold psi2; rw [if_pos h]
4822    rw [this, mul_one]
4823    exact hlev L hL
4824  · have hσ : sigmaOf d s = s := sigmaOf_eq hd (by rw [h])
4825    have h2σ : 2 * sigmaOf d s = d := by rw [hσ, h]; ring
4826    obtain ⟨C, hC, hlev⟩ := lev2b hd hσ0 hσd hB1 hwB hw0 h2σ
4827    refine ⟨C, hC, fun L hL => ?_⟩
4828    have : psi2 d s L = Real.log (Real.exp 1 * L) := by
4829      unfold psi2; rw [if_neg (not_lt.2 h.ge), if_pos h]
4830    rw [this]
4831    exact hlev L hL
4832  · have hσ : sigmaOf d s = s := sigmaOf_eq hd h.le
4833    have h2σ : (d : ℝ) < 2 * sigmaOf d s := by rw [hσ]; linarith
4834    obtain ⟨C, hC, hlev⟩ := lev2c hd hσ0 hσd hB1 hwB hw0 h2σ
4835    refine ⟨C, hC, fun L hL => ?_⟩
4836    have : psi2 d s L = L ^ (2 - (d : ℝ) / sigmaOf d s) := by
4837      unfold psi2; rw [if_neg (not_lt.2 h.le), if_neg h.ne', hσ]
4838    rw [this]
4839    exact hlev L hL
4840
4841
4842/-- Real-level row bounds of a kernel dominated by the Riesz envelope. -/
4843theorem row_bounds_real (hd : 1 ≤ d) {s : ℝ} (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) (cstar : ℝ)
4844    (hw : ∀ z : Torus d, z ≠ 0 → 1 ≤ periodicRiesz d s z + cstar) (A Λ₀ : ℝ) (hΛ₀ : 0 ≤ Λ₀) :
4845    ∃ Ca Cb : ℝ, 0 ≤ Ca ∧ 0 ≤ Cb ∧
4846      ∀ (ρ : Torus d → ℝ) [IsProbabilityMeasure (withDensityReal ρ)], (∀ x, ρ x ≤ Λ₀) →
4847        ∀ q : Torus d → Torus d → ℝ, Measurable (Function.uncurry q) → (∀ x y, 0 ≤ q x y) →
4848        (∀ x y, q x y ≤ A * (periodicRiesz d s (x - y) + cstar)) → ∀ x : Torus d,
4849          (∀ L : ℝ, 0 < L → ∫ y, max (q x y - L) 0 ∂(withDensityReal ρ)
4850              ≤ Ca * L ^ (1 - (d : ℝ) / sigmaOf d s)) ∧
4851          (∀ L : ℝ, 1 ≤ L → ∫ y, (min (q x y) L) ^ 2 ∂(withDensityReal ρ) ≤ Cb * psi2 d s L) := by
4852  obtain ⟨C₁, C₂, hC₁, hC₂, hlev1, -⟩ := kernel_levels hd hs0 hsd cstar hw
4853  obtain ⟨C₂', hC₂', hlev2⟩ := kernel_levels_real hd hs0 hsd cstar hw
4854  set Ā : ℝ := max A 1 with hĀ
4855  have hĀ1 : 1 ≤ Ā := le_max_right _ _
4856  have hĀ0 : 0 < Ā := by linarith
4857  have hAĀ : A ≤ Ā := le_max_left _ _
4858  set e : ℝ := 1 - (d : ℝ) / sigmaOf d s with he
4859  refine ⟨Λ₀ * Ā * C₁ * Ā ^ (-e), Λ₀ * Ā ^ 2 * C₂', by positivity, by positivity, ?_⟩
4860  intro ρ _ hρ q hqm hq0 hqA x
4861  set μ := withDensityReal ρ with hμ
4862  set w : Torus d → ℝ := fun z => periodicRiesz d s z + cstar with hwdef
4863  have hqx : Measurable (q x) := hqm.comp (measurable_const.prodMk measurable_id)
4864  have hae := ae_ne_withDensityReal hd ρ x
4865  have hpt : ∀ y, y ≠ x → q x y ≤ Ā * w (x - y) ∧ 0 ≤ w (x - y) := by
4866    intro y hy
4867    have h1 := hw (x - y) (sub_ne_zero.2 (Ne.symm hy))
4868    have h2 := hqA x y
4869    have h0 : 0 ≤ w (x - y) := by simp only [hwdef]; linarith
4870    refine ⟨h2.trans ?_, h0⟩
4871    exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hAĀ h0
4872  constructor
4873  · intro L hL
4874    have hnn : ∀ y, 0 ≤ max (q x y - L) 0 := fun y => le_max_right _ _
4875    have hm : Measurable fun y => max (q x y - L) 0 := (hqx.sub_const _).max measurable_const
4876    rw [integral_eq_lintegral_of_nonneg_ae (Filter.Eventually.of_forall hnn) hm.aestronglyMeasurable]
4877    have hle : ∫⁻ y, ENNReal.ofReal (max (q x y - L) 0) ∂μ
4878        ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (Λ₀ * Ā * C₁ * (L / Ā) ^ e) := by
4879      calc ∫⁻ y, ENNReal.ofReal (max (q x y - L) 0) ∂μ
4880          ≤ ∫⁻ y, (ENNReal.ofReal Ā * ENNReal.ofReal (max (w (x - y) - L / Ā) 0)) ∂μ := by
4881            refine lintegral_mono_ae ?_
4882            filter_upwards [hae] with y hy
4883            obtain ⟨h1, h0⟩ := hpt y hy
4884            rw [← ENNReal.ofReal_mul hĀ0.le]
4885            refine ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal (max_le ?_ (mul_nonneg hĀ0.le (le_max_right _ _)))
4886            have : Ā * (w (x - y) - L / Ā) ≤ Ā * max (w (x - y) - L / Ā) 0 :=
4887              mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (le_max_left _ _) hĀ0.le
4888            have e2 : Ā * (w (x - y) - L / Ā) = Ā * w (x - y) - L := by field_simp
4889            linarith
4890        _ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal Λ₀ * ∫⁻ z, (ENNReal.ofReal Ā * ENNReal.ofReal (max (w z - L / Ā) 0)) :=
4891            KernelAux2.lintegral_conv_le ρ Λ₀ hρ
4892              (fun z => ENNReal.ofReal Ā * ENNReal.ofReal (max (w z - L / Ā) 0)) x
4893        _ = ENNReal.ofReal Λ₀ * (ENNReal.ofReal Ā * ∫⁻ z, ENNReal.ofReal (max (w z - L / Ā) 0)) := by
4894            rw [lintegral_const_mul' _ _ ENNReal.ofReal_ne_top]
4895        _ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal Λ₀ * (ENNReal.ofReal Ā * ENNReal.ofReal (C₁ * (L / Ā) ^ e)) := by
4896            gcongr
4897            exact hlev1 _ (by positivity)
4898        _ = _ := by
4899            rw [← ENNReal.ofReal_mul hĀ0.le, ← ENNReal.ofReal_mul hΛ₀]
4900            congr 1; ring
4901    have hbd : Λ₀ * Ā * C₁ * (L / Ā) ^ e = Λ₀ * Ā * C₁ * Ā ^ (-e) * L ^ e := by
4902      rw [Real.div_rpow hL.le hĀ0.le, Real.rpow_neg hĀ0.le, div_eq_mul_inv]; ring
4903    have hpos : 0 ≤ Λ₀ * Ā * C₁ * (L / Ā) ^ e := by positivity
4904    calc (∫⁻ y, ENNReal.ofReal (max (q x y - L) 0) ∂μ).toReal
4905        ≤ (ENNReal.ofReal (Λ₀ * Ā * C₁ * (L / Ā) ^ e)).toReal :=
4906          ENNReal.toReal_mono ENNReal.ofReal_ne_top hle
4907      _ = Λ₀ * Ā * C₁ * (L / Ā) ^ e := ENNReal.toReal_ofReal hpos
4908      _ = _ := hbd
4909  · intro L hL
4910    have hNpos : (0 : ℝ) < L := by linarith
4911    have hnn : ∀ y, 0 ≤ (min (q x y) L) ^ 2 := fun y => sq_nonneg _
4912    have hm : Measurable fun y => (min (q x y) L) ^ 2 := (hqx.min measurable_const).pow_const 2
4913    rw [integral_eq_lintegral_of_nonneg_ae (Filter.Eventually.of_forall hnn) hm.aestronglyMeasurable]
4914    have hle : ∫⁻ y, ENNReal.ofReal ((min (q x y) L) ^ 2) ∂μ
4915        ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (Λ₀ * Ā ^ 2 * (C₂' * psi2 d s L)) := by
4916      calc ∫⁻ y, ENNReal.ofReal ((min (q x y) L) ^ 2) ∂μ
4917          ≤ ∫⁻ y, (ENNReal.ofReal (Ā ^ 2) * ENNReal.ofReal ((min (w (x - y)) L) ^ 2)) ∂μ := by
4918            refine lintegral_mono_ae ?_
4919            filter_upwards [hae] with y hy
4920            obtain ⟨h1, h0⟩ := hpt y hy
4921            rw [← ENNReal.ofReal_mul (sq_nonneg _)]
4922            refine ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal ?_
4923            have hq0' := hq0 x y
4924            have h2 : min (q x y) L ≤ Ā * min (w (x - y)) L := by
4925              rw [mul_min_of_nonneg _ _ hĀ0.le]
4926              exact le_min ((min_le_left _ _).trans h1)
4927                ((min_le_right _ _).trans (le_mul_of_one_le_left hNpos.le hĀ1))
4928            have h3 : 0 ≤ min (q x y) L := le_min hq0' hNpos.le
4929            have := pow_le_pow_left₀ h3 h2 2
4930            calc (min (q x y) L) ^ 2 ≤ (Ā * min (w (x - y)) L) ^ 2 := this
4931              _ = Ā ^ 2 * (min (w (x - y)) L) ^ 2 := by ring
4932        _ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal Λ₀ * ∫⁻ z, (ENNReal.ofReal (Ā ^ 2) * ENNReal.ofReal ((min (w z) L) ^ 2)) :=
4933            KernelAux2.lintegral_conv_le ρ Λ₀ hρ
4934              (fun z => ENNReal.ofReal (Ā ^ 2) * ENNReal.ofReal ((min (w z) L) ^ 2)) x
4935        _ = ENNReal.ofReal Λ₀ * (ENNReal.ofReal (Ā ^ 2) * ∫⁻ z, ENNReal.ofReal ((min (w z) L) ^ 2)) := by
4936            rw [lintegral_const_mul' _ _ ENNReal.ofReal_ne_top]
4937        _ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal Λ₀ * (ENNReal.ofReal (Ā ^ 2) * ENNReal.ofReal (C₂' * psi2 d s L)) := by
4938            gcongr
4939            exact hlev2 L hL
4940        _ = _ := by
4941            rw [← ENNReal.ofReal_mul (sq_nonneg _), ← ENNReal.ofReal_mul hΛ₀]
4942            congr 1; ring
4943    have hdel : 0 ≤ psi2 d s L := by
4944      unfold psi2
4945      split_ifs with h1 h2
4946      · norm_num
4947      · have := Real.log_nonneg (show (1 : ℝ) ≤ Real.exp 1 * L by
4948          have := Real.add_one_le_exp (1 : ℝ); nlinarith [Real.exp_pos 1])
4949        exact this
4950      · exact Real.rpow_nonneg hNpos.le _
4951    have hpos : 0 ≤ Λ₀ * Ā ^ 2 * (C₂' * psi2 d s L) := by positivity
4952    calc (∫⁻ y, ENNReal.ofReal ((min (q x y) L) ^ 2) ∂μ).toReal
4953        ≤ (ENNReal.ofReal (Λ₀ * Ā ^ 2 * (C₂' * psi2 d s L))).toReal :=
4954          ENNReal.toReal_mono ENNReal.ofReal_ne_top hle
4955      _ = Λ₀ * Ā ^ 2 * (C₂' * psi2 d s L) := ENNReal.toReal_ofReal hpos
4956      _ = Λ₀ * Ā ^ 2 * C₂' * psi2 d s L := by ring
4957
4958end HRS.WeakRef
4959

4970variable {E : Type*} [MeasurableSpace E] {μ : Measure E} [IsProbabilityMeasure μ]
4971
4972/-- Difference of the fields of two close measures: truncation at level `L`. -/
4973theorem theta_abstract {q : E → E → ℝ} (hqm : Measurable (Function.uncurry q))
4974    (hq0 : ∀ x y, 0 ≤ q x y) (hqi : ∀ x, Integrable (q x) μ) {L a₂ D ε₀ : ℝ} (hL : 0 ≤ L)
4975    (hrow2 : ∀ x, ∫ y, max (q x y - L) 0 ∂μ ≤ a₂) (hD : 0 ≤ D)
4976    (x : E) (ν₁ ν₂ : Measure E) [IsProbabilityMeasure ν₁] [IsProbabilityMeasure ν₂]
4977    (h₁ : ν₁ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal D • μ) (h₂ : ν₂ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal D • μ)
4978    (hTV : ∀ A, MeasurableSet A → |ν₁.real A - ν₂.real A| ≤ ε₀) :
4979    |∫ y, q x y ∂ν₁ - ∫ y, q x y ∂ν₂| ≤ ε₀ * L + 2 * (D * a₂) := by
4980  have hqx : Measurable (q x) := hqm.comp (measurable_const.prodMk measurable_id)
4981  have hmin : Measurable fun y => min (q x y) L := hqx.min measurable_const
4982  have hmax : Measurable fun y => max (q x y - L) 0 := (hqx.sub_const L).max measurable_const
4983  have hmaxi : Integrable (fun y => max (q x y - L) 0) μ :=
4984    Integrable.mono' (hqi x) hmax.aestronglyMeasurable (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => by
4985      rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_nonneg (le_max_right _ _)]
4986      exact max_le (by linarith [hq0 x y]) (hq0 x y))
4987  have hmini : ∀ ν : Measure E, IsProbabilityMeasure ν → Integrable (fun y => min (q x y) L) ν :=
4988    fun ν _ => intg_bdd hmin (C := L) fun y => by
4989      rw [abs_of_nonneg (le_min (hq0 x y) hL)]; exact min_le_right _ _
4990  have hsplit : ∀ (ν : Measure E) [IsProbabilityMeasure ν] (D' : ℝ), 0 ≤ D' → ν ≤ ENNReal.ofReal D' • μ →
4991      ∫ y, q x y ∂ν = ∫ y, min (q x y) L ∂ν + ∫ y, max (q x y - L) 0 ∂ν ∧
4992      0 ≤ ∫ y, max (q x y - L) 0 ∂ν ∧ ∫ y, max (q x y - L) 0 ∂ν ≤ D' * a₂ := by
4993    intro ν _ D' hD' hν
4994    have hi := integral_le_of_le_smul hD' hν (fun y => le_max_right _ _) hmaxi
4995    refine ⟨?_, integral_nonneg fun y => le_max_right _ _, ?_⟩
4996    · rw [← integral_add (hmini ν inferInstance) hi.1]
4997      refine integral_congr_ae (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => ?_)
4998      simp only
4999      rcases le_total (q x y) L with h | h
5000      · rw [min_eq_left h, max_eq_right (by linarith)]; ring
5001      · rw [min_eq_right h, max_eq_left (by linarith)]; ring
5002    · exact hi.2.trans (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (hrow2 x) hD')
5003  obtain ⟨e₁, n₁, u₁⟩ := hsplit ν₁ D hD h₁
5004  obtain ⟨e₂, n₂, u₂⟩ := hsplit ν₂ D hD h₂
5005  have hosc : ∀ z z', |min (q x z) L - min (q x z') L| ≤ L := fun z z' => by
5006    rw [abs_le]
5007    have a1 := le_min (hq0 x z) hL
5008    have a2 := le_min (hq0 x z') hL
5009    have b1 := min_le_right (q x z) L
5010    have b2 := min_le_right (q x z') L
5011    constructor <;> linarith
5012  have hmain := abs_integral_sub_le_of_tv ν₁ ν₂ hL hTV hmin hosc
5013  rw [e₁, e₂]
5014  have : ∫ y, min (q x y) L ∂ν₁ + ∫ y, max (q x y - L) 0 ∂ν₁
5015      - (∫ y, min (q x y) L ∂ν₂ + ∫ y, max (q x y - L) 0 ∂ν₂)
5016      = (∫ y, min (q x y) L ∂ν₁ - ∫ y, min (q x y) L ∂ν₂)
5017        + (∫ y, max (q x y - L) 0 ∂ν₁ - ∫ y, max (q x y - L) 0 ∂ν₂) := by ring
5018  rw [this]
5019  calc |(∫ y, min (q x y) L ∂ν₁ - ∫ y, min (q x y) L ∂ν₂)
5020        + (∫ y, max (q x y - L) 0 ∂ν₁ - ∫ y, max (q x y - L) 0 ∂ν₂)|
5021      ≤ |∫ y, min (q x y) L ∂ν₁ - ∫ y, min (q x y) L ∂ν₂|
5022        + |∫ y, max (q x y - L) 0 ∂ν₁ - ∫ y, max (q x y - L) 0 ∂ν₂| := abs_add_le _ _
5023    _ ≤ ε₀ * L + 2 * (D * a₂) := by
5024        have h3 : |∫ y, max (q x y - L) 0 ∂ν₁ - ∫ y, max (q x y - L) 0 ∂ν₂| ≤ 2 * (D * a₂) := by
5025          rw [abs_le]; constructor <;> linarith
5026        linarith
5027
5028end HRS.WeakRef.Abs
5029
5030end WM_Q

5032section WM_R
5033open MeasureTheory ProbabilityTheory Function Set
5034open scoped ENNReal NNReal BigOperators
5035set_option linter.unusedSectionVars false
5036
5037namespace HRS.WeakRef
5038
5039variable {d : ℕ} {μ : Measure (Torus d)} [IsProbabilityMeasure μ] {a : ℝ} {q : Torus d → Torus d → ℝ}
5040
5041/-- The energy `G = N F_N^q(·, μ)`. -/
5042def enG (q : Torus d → Torus d → ℝ) (μ : Measure (Torus d)) (N : ℕ) (X : Config d N) : ℝ :=
5043  N * modulatedEnergy q X μ
5044
5045lemma WeakKernel.measurable_energy (h : WeakKernel μ a q) (N : ℕ) :
5046    Measurable (fun X : Config d N => modulatedEnergy q X μ) := by
5047  unfold modulatedEnergy
5048  have h1 : Measurable (fun X : Config d N => ∑ i, ∑ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, q (X i) (X j)) :=
5049    Finset.measurable_sum _ fun i _ => Finset.measurable_sum _ fun j _ => by
5050      have := h.meas.comp (f := fun X : Config d N => (X i, X j))
5051        ((measurable_pi_apply i).prodMk (measurable_pi_apply j))
5052      exact this
5053  have h2 : Measurable (fun X : Config d N => ∑ i, ∫ y, q (X i) y ∂μ) :=
5054    Finset.measurable_sum _ fun i _ => h.measurable_rowInt.comp (measurable_pi_apply i)
5055  exact (((h1.const_mul _).sub (h2.const_mul _)).add_const _)
5056
5057lemma WeakKernel.measurable_enG (h : WeakKernel μ a q) (N : ℕ) : Measurable (enG q μ N) :=
5058  (h.measurable_energy N).const_mul _
5059
5060lemma WeakKernel.enG_ge (h : WeakKernel μ a q) (ha : 0 ≤ a) {N : ℕ} (hN : 0 < N) (X : Config d N) :
5061    -((N : ℝ) * a) ≤ enG q μ N X := by
5062  have hN' : (0 : ℝ) < N := by exact_mod_cast hN
5063  unfold enG modulatedEnergy
5064  have h1 : 0 ≤ ∑ i, ∑ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, q (X i) (X j) :=
5065    Finset.sum_nonneg fun i _ => Finset.sum_nonneg fun j _ => h.nonneg _ _
5066  have h2 : ∑ i, ∫ y, q (X i) y ∂μ ≤ N * a := by
5067    have : ∀ i, ∫ y, q (X i) y ∂μ ≤ a := fun i => h.rowInt_le ha (X i)
5068    calc ∑ i, ∫ y, q (X i) y ∂μ ≤ ∑ _i : Fin N, a := Finset.sum_le_sum fun i _ => this i
5069      _ = N * a := by simp
5070  have h3 : 0 ≤ ∫ x, ∫ y, q x y ∂μ ∂μ :=
5071    integral_nonneg fun x => integral_nonneg fun y => h.nonneg _ _
5072  have h4 : 0 ≤ 1 / (2 * (N : ℝ) ^ 2) * ∑ i, ∑ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, q (X i) (X j) := by positivity
5073  have h5 : 1 / (N : ℝ) * ∑ i, ∫ y, q (X i) y ∂μ ≤ a := by
5074    calc 1 / (N : ℝ) * ∑ i, ∫ y, q (X i) y ∂μ ≤ 1 / (N : ℝ) * (N * a) :=
5075          mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h2 (by positivity)
5076      _ = a := by field_simp
5077  have : -a ≤ 1 / (2 * (N : ℝ) ^ 2) * ∑ i, ∑ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, q (X i) (X j)
5078      - 1 / (N : ℝ) * ∑ i, ∫ y, q (X i) y ∂μ + 1 / 2 * ∫ x, ∫ y, q x y ∂μ ∂μ := by linarith
5079  calc -((N : ℝ) * a) = N * (-a) := by ring
5080    _ ≤ _ := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left this hN'.le
5081
5082lemma WeakKernel.integral_enG (h : WeakKernel μ a q) {N : ℕ} (hN : 1 ≤ N) :
5083    ∫ X, enG q μ N X ∂(Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ)
5084      = -(1 / 2) * ∫ p : Torus d × Torus d, q p.1 p.2 ∂(μ.prod μ) := by
5085  unfold enG
5086  rw [integral_const_mul, h.integral_energy hN]
5087  have : (N : ℝ) ≠ 0 := by positivity
5088  field_simp
5089
5090lemma WeakKernel.integrable_enG (h : WeakKernel μ a q) (N : ℕ) :
5091    Integrable (enG q μ N) (Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ) :=
5092  (h.integrable_energy N).const_mul _
5093
5094lemma WeakKernel.prod_le (h : WeakKernel μ a q) (ha : 0 ≤ a) :
5095    ∫ p : Torus d × Torus d, q p.1 p.2 ∂(μ.prod μ) ≤ a := by
5096  rw [integral_prod _ h.integrable_prod]
5097  calc ∫ x, ∫ y, q (x, y).1 (x, y).2 ∂μ ∂μ ≤ ∫ _x, a ∂μ :=
5098        integral_mono_of_nonneg (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun x => integral_nonneg fun y => h.nonneg _ _)
5099          (integrable_const _) (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun x => h.rowInt_le ha x)
5100    _ = a := by simp
5101
5102lemma WeakKernel.prod_nonneg (h : WeakKernel μ a q) :
5103    0 ≤ ∫ p : Torus d × Torus d, q p.1 p.2 ∂(μ.prod μ) :=
5104  integral_nonneg fun p => h.nonneg _ _
5105
5106lemma WeakKernel.pairPartition_eq (h : WeakKernel μ a q) (ha : 0 ≤ a) {t : ℝ} (ht : 0 < t) {N : ℕ}
5107    (hN : 0 < N) :
5108    pairPartition q N t μ
5109      = ENNReal.ofReal (expZ (Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ) (enG q μ N) 0 t) := by
5110  have hK0 : 0 ≤ (N : ℝ) * a := by positivity
5111  have hi := integrable_expMoment (Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ) (h.measurable_enG N) hK0
5112    (h.enG_ge ha hN) 0 ht
5113  unfold expZ pairPartition
5114  simp only [pow_zero, one_mul] at hi ⊢
5115  rw [ofReal_integral_eq_lintegral_ofReal hi (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun ω => (Real.exp_pos _).le)]
5116  refine lintegral_congr fun X => ?_
5117  unfold pairWeight enG
5118  congr 2
5119  ring
5120
5121end HRS.WeakRef

5125section WM_S

5127open scoped ENNReal NNReal BigOperators
5128set_option linter.unusedSectionVars false

5130namespace HRS.WeakRef

5132variable {d : ℕ} {μ : Measure (Torus d)} [IsProbabilityMeasure μ] {a : ℝ} {q : Torus d → Torus d → ℝ}
5133
5134lemma WeakKernel.integral_gibbs (h : WeakKernel μ a q) (ha : 0 ≤ a) {t : ℝ} (ht : 0 < t) {N : ℕ}
5135    (hN : 0 < N) (g : Config d N → ℝ) :
5136    ∫ X, g X ∂(pairGibbsLaw q N t μ)
5137      = ∫ X, (Real.exp (-(t * enG q μ N X)) / expZ (Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ) (enG q μ N) 0 t) * g X
5138          ∂(Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ) := by
5139  set Pm : Measure (Config d N) := Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ with hPm
5140  set Z := expZ Pm (enG q μ N) 0 t with hZ
5141  have hK0 : 0 ≤ (N : ℝ) * a := by positivity
5142  have hZpos : 0 < Z := expZ_zero_pos Pm (enG q μ N) (h.measurable_enG N) hK0 (h.enG_ge ha hN) ht
5143  have hpp := h.pairPartition_eq ha ht hN
5144  unfold pairGibbsLaw
5145  rw [integral_withDensity_eq_integral_toReal_smul]
5146  · refine integral_congr_ae (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun X => ?_)
5147    simp only [smul_eq_mul]
5148    congr 1
5149    rw [hpp, ENNReal.toReal_div, ENNReal.toReal_ofReal hZpos.le]
5150    unfold pairWeight
5151    rw [ENNReal.toReal_ofReal (Real.exp_pos _).le]
5152    simp only [enG]
5153    congr 3
5154    ring
5155  · exact (h.measurable_pairWeight N t).div_const _
5156  · refine Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun X => ?_
5157    rw [hpp]
5158    refine ENNReal.div_lt_top ENNReal.ofReal_ne_top ?_
5159    exact (ENNReal.ofReal_pos.2 hZpos).ne'
5160
5161lemma WeakKernel.vFun_eq (h : WeakKernel μ a q) (ha : 0 ≤ a) {t : ℝ} (ht : 0 < t) {N : ℕ}
5162    (hN : 0 < N) :
5163    vFun (Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ) (enG q μ N) t
5164      = variance (enG q μ N) (pairGibbsLaw q N t μ) := by
5165  set Pm : Measure (Config d N) := Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ with hPm
5166  set G := enG q μ N with hG
5167  set Z := expZ Pm G 0 t with hZ
5168  have hK0 : 0 ≤ (N : ℝ) * a := by positivity
5169  have hGm := h.measurable_enG N
5170  have hZpos : 0 < Z := expZ_zero_pos Pm G hGm hK0 (h.enG_ge ha hN) ht
5171  have hI := fun k => integrable_expMoment Pm hGm hK0 (h.enG_ge ha hN) k ht
5172  set E1 := expZ Pm G 1 t with hE1
5173  set E2 := expZ Pm G 2 t with hE2
5174  set m : ℝ := E1 / Z with hm
5175  have key := h.integral_gibbs ha ht hN G
5176  rw [← hG, ← hPm, ← hZ] at key
5177  have hmean : ∫ X, G X ∂(pairGibbsLaw q N t μ) = m := by
5178    rw [key]
5179    have : ∀ X, Real.exp (-(t * G X)) / Z * G X = (1 / Z) * (G X ^ 1 * Real.exp (-(t * G X))) := by
5180      intro X; ring
5181    simp_rw [this]
5182    rw [integral_const_mul]
5183    show 1 / Z * E1 = _
5184    rw [hm]; ring
5185  have key2 := h.integral_gibbs ha ht hN (fun X => (G X - m) ^ 2)
5186  rw [← hG, ← hPm, ← hZ] at key2
5187  rw [variance_eq_integral hGm.aemeasurable, hmean, key2]
5188  have e : ∀ X, Real.exp (-(t * G X)) / Z * (G X - m) ^ 2
5189      = (1 / Z) * (G X ^ 2 * Real.exp (-(t * G X))) - (2 * m / Z) * (G X ^ 1 * Real.exp (-(t * G X)))
5190        + (m ^ 2 / Z) * (G X ^ 0 * Real.exp (-(t * G X))) := by
5191    intro X; ring
5192  simp_rw [e]
5193  have i2 : Integrable (fun X => 1 / Z * (G X ^ 2 * Real.exp (-(t * G X)))) Pm := (hI 2).const_mul _
5194  have i1 : Integrable (fun X => 2 * m / Z * (G X ^ 1 * Real.exp (-(t * G X)))) Pm := (hI 1).const_mul _
5195  have i0 : Integrable (fun X => m ^ 2 / Z * (G X ^ 0 * Real.exp (-(t * G X)))) Pm := (hI 0).const_mul _
5196  have i21 : Integrable (fun X => 1 / Z * (G X ^ 2 * Real.exp (-(t * G X)))
5197      - 2 * m / Z * (G X ^ 1 * Real.exp (-(t * G X)))) Pm := i2.sub i1
5198  rw [integral_add i21 i0, integral_sub i2 i1, integral_const_mul, integral_const_mul,
5199    integral_const_mul]
5200  show E2 / Z - (E1 / Z) ^ 2 = 1 / Z * E2 - 2 * m / Z * E1 + m ^ 2 / Z * Z
5201  rw [← hm]
5202  have hE : E1 = m * Z := by rw [hm]; field_simp
5203  field_simp
5204  linear_combination (2 * m) * hE
5205
5206end HRS.WeakRef
5207
5208end WM_S

5217variable {d : ℕ} {μ : Measure (Torus d)} [IsProbabilityMeasure μ] {a : ℝ} {q : Torus d → Torus d → ℝ}
5218
5219theorem WeakKernel.pairPartition_upper (h : WeakKernel μ a q) (ha : 0 ≤ a) {N : ℕ} (hN : 0 < N)
5220    {β : ℝ} (hβ : 0 < β) {V W : ℝ → ℝ}
5221    (hv : ∀ t ∈ Ioc 0 β, variance (enG q μ N) (pairGibbsLaw q N t μ) ≤ V t)
5222    (hV0 : ∀ t ∈ Ioc 0 β, 0 ≤ V t)
5223    (hW : ∀ t, 0 < t → HasDerivAt W (V t) t) (hWc : ContinuousWithinAt W (Ici 0) 0) (hW0 : W 0 = 0) :
5224    pairPartition q N β μ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (β * (a / 2 + W β))) := by
5225  set Pm : Measure (Config d N) := Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ with hPm
5226  have hK0 : 0 ≤ (N : ℝ) * a := by positivity
5227  have hlog := logZ_upper Pm (enG q μ N) (h.measurable_enG N) (h.integrable_enG N) hK0
5228    (h.enG_ge ha hN) hβ (V := V) (W := W)
5229    (fun t ht => by rw [h.vFun_eq ha ht.1 hN]; exact hv t ht) hV0 hW hWc hW0
5230  have hZpos : 0 < expZ Pm (enG q μ N) 0 β :=
5231    expZ_zero_pos Pm (enG q μ N) (h.measurable_enG N) hK0 (h.enG_ge ha hN) hβ
5232  rw [h.pairPartition_eq ha hβ hN]
5233  refine ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal ?_
5234  have hexp : expZ Pm (enG q μ N) 0 β = Real.exp (logZ Pm (enG q μ N) β) := by
5235    unfold logZ; rw [Real.exp_log hZpos]
5236  rw [hexp]
5237  refine Real.exp_le_exp.2 (hlog.trans ?_)
5238  rw [h.integral_enG hN]
5239  have := h.prod_le ha
5240  have h0 := h.prod_nonneg
5241  have : β * (-(-(1 / 2) * ∫ p : Torus d × Torus d, q p.1 p.2 ∂(μ.prod μ)) + W β)
5242      ≤ β * (a / 2 + W β) := by
5243    refine mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left ?_ hβ.le
5244    linarith
5245  exact this
5246
5247end HRS.WeakRef

5260lemma one_le_log_eL {L : ℝ} (hL : 1 ≤ L) : 1 ≤ Real.log (Real.exp 1 * L) := by
5261  have hLp : 0 < L := by linarith
5262  rw [Real.log_mul (Real.exp_pos 1).ne' hLp.ne', Real.log_exp]
5263  have := Real.log_nonneg hL
5264  linarith
5265
5266lemma psi2_ge_one (hd : 1 ≤ d) {s : ℝ} (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) {L : ℝ} (hL : 1 ≤ L) : 1 ≤ psi2 d s L := by
5267  have hLp : 0 < L := by linarith
5268  have hd0 : (0 : ℝ) < d := by exact_mod_cast hd
5269  unfold psi2
5270  split_ifs with h1 h2
5271  · exact le_rfl
5272  · exact one_le_log_eL hL
5273  · push Not at h1
5274    have h3 : (d : ℝ) / 2 < s := lt_of_le_of_ne h1 (Ne.symm h2)
5275    have hs : 0 < s := by linarith
5276    have : 0 ≤ 2 - (d : ℝ) / s := by
5277      have : (d : ℝ) / s ≤ 2 := by rw [div_le_iff₀ hs]; linarith
5278      linarith
5279    exact Real.one_le_rpow hL this
5280
5281lemma deltaN_le_one (hd : 1 ≤ d) {s : ℝ} (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) {N : ℕ} (hN : 1 ≤ N) : deltaN d s N ≤ 1 := by
5282  have hN' : (1 : ℝ) ≤ N := by exact_mod_cast hN
5283  have hNpos : (0 : ℝ) < N := by linarith
5284  have hd0 : (0 : ℝ) < d := by exact_mod_cast hd
5285  unfold deltaN
5286  split_ifs with h1 h2
5287  · exact inv_le_one_of_one_le₀ hN'
5288  · rw [inv_mul_le_iff₀ hNpos, mul_one, Real.log_mul (Real.exp_pos 1).ne' hNpos.ne', Real.log_exp]
5289    have := Real.log_le_sub_one_of_pos hNpos
5290    linarith
5291  · push Not at h1
5292    have h3 : (d : ℝ) / 2 < s := lt_of_le_of_ne h1 (Ne.symm h2)
5293    have hs : 0 < s := by linarith
5294    have : 1 - (d : ℝ) / s ≤ 0 := by
5295      have : 1 ≤ (d : ℝ) / s := by
5296        rw [le_div_iff₀ hs]
5297        linarith
5298      linarith
5299    exact Real.rpow_le_one_of_one_le_of_nonpos hN' this
5300
5301
5302/-- `N^{2σ/d - 1} ≤ ψ₂(N)`, in the form used for the field-difference term. -/
5303lemma sq_rpow_le_psi2 (hd : 1 ≤ d) {s : ℝ} (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) {N : ℝ} (hN : 1 ≤ N) :
5304    N * (N ^ (sigmaOf d s / d - 1)) ^ 2 ≤ psi2 d s N := by
5305  have hNp : 0 < N := by linarith
5306  have hd0 : (0 : ℝ) < d := by exact_mod_cast hd
5307  have hσ0 := sigmaOf_pos hd hs0
5308  have e : N * (N ^ (sigmaOf d s / d - 1)) ^ 2 = N ^ (2 * sigmaOf d s / d - 1) := by
5309    have h1 : (N ^ (sigmaOf d s / d - 1)) ^ 2 = N ^ ((sigmaOf d s / d - 1) * 2) := by
5310      rw [Real.rpow_mul hNp.le]; norm_cast
5311    rw [h1]
5312    calc N * N ^ ((sigmaOf d s / d - 1) * 2) = N ^ (1 : ℝ) * N ^ ((sigmaOf d s / d - 1) * 2) := by
5313          rw [Real.rpow_one]
5314      _ = N ^ (1 + (sigmaOf d s / d - 1) * 2) := (Real.rpow_add hNp _ _).symm
5315      _ = _ := by congr 1; ring
5316  rw [e]
5317  unfold psi2
5318  split_ifs with h1 h2
5319  · have h2σ : 2 * sigmaOf d s < d := (two_sigmaOf_lt_iff hd hs0).2 h1
5320    have : 2 * sigmaOf d s / d - 1 ≤ 0 := by
5321      have : 2 * sigmaOf d s / d ≤ 1 := by rw [div_le_iff₀ hd0]; linarith
5322      linarith
5323    exact Real.rpow_le_one_of_one_le_of_nonpos hN this
5324  · have hσ : sigmaOf d s = s := sigmaOf_eq hd (by rw [h2])
5325    have : 2 * sigmaOf d s / d - 1 = 0 := by rw [hσ, h2]; field_simp; ring
5326    rw [this, Real.rpow_zero]
5327    exact one_le_log_eL hN
5328  · push Not at h1
5329    have hσ : sigmaOf d s = s := sigmaOf_eq hd h1
5330    rw [hσ]
5331    have h3 : (d : ℝ) / 2 < s := lt_of_le_of_ne h1 (Ne.symm h2)
5332    have hs : 0 < s := by linarith
5333    refine Real.rpow_le_rpow_of_exponent_le hN ?_
5334    -- 2 s/d - 1 ≤ 2 - d/s
5335    have key : 2 * s / d - 1 ≤ 2 - (d : ℝ) / s := by
5336      have e2 : 2 * s / d - 1 - (2 - (d : ℝ) / s) = (2 * s - d) * (s - d) / (d * s) := by
5337        field_simp
5338      have : (2 * s - d) * (s - d) / (d * s) ≤ 0 :=
5339        div_nonpos_of_nonpos_of_nonneg (mul_nonpos_of_nonneg_of_nonpos (by linarith) (by linarith))
5340          (by positivity)
5341      linarith
5342    exact key
5343
5344end HRS.WeakRef

5346end WM_U

5357lemma exists_W_lt {s : ℝ} (h : s < (d : ℝ) / 2) {P : ℝ} (hP : 1 ≤ P) :
5358    ∃ Cw : ℝ, 0 ≤ Cw ∧ ∀ N : ℕ, 1 ≤ N → ∃ W : ℝ → ℝ,
5359      (∀ t, 0 < t → HasDerivAt W (psi2 d s (2 * N * P / t)) t) ∧
5360      ContinuousWithinAt W (Ici 0) 0 ∧ W 0 = 0 ∧
5361      ∀ β, 0 < β → β ≤ P → W β ≤ Cw * psi2 d s N := by
5362  refine ⟨P, by linarith, fun N hN => ⟨fun t => t, fun t ht => ?_, continuous_id.continuousWithinAt, rfl, ?_⟩⟩
5363  · have : psi2 d s (2 * N * P / t) = 1 := by unfold psi2; rw [if_pos h]
5364    rw [this]; exact hasDerivAt_id t
5365  · intro β hβ hβP
5366    have : psi2 d s N = 1 := by unfold psi2; rw [if_pos h]
5367    rw [this]; linarith
5368
5369lemma exists_W_eq {s : ℝ} (h : s = (d : ℝ) / 2) {P βm : ℝ} (hP : 1 ≤ P) (hβm : 0 < βm) :
5370    ∃ Cw : ℝ, 0 ≤ Cw ∧ ∀ N : ℕ, 1 ≤ N → ∃ W : ℝ → ℝ,
5371      (∀ t, 0 < t → HasDerivAt W (psi2 d s (2 * N * P / t)) t) ∧
5372      ContinuousWithinAt W (Ici 0) 0 ∧ W 0 = 0 ∧
5373      ∀ β, βm ≤ β → β ≤ P → W β ≤ Cw * psi2 d s N := by
5374  set K0 : ℝ := 2 + Real.log (2 * P) + |Real.log βm| with hK0
5375  have hlogP : 0 ≤ Real.log (2 * P) := Real.log_nonneg (by linarith)
5376  have hK01 : 1 ≤ K0 := by have := abs_nonneg (Real.log βm); rw [hK0]; linarith
5377  refine ⟨P * K0, by positivity, fun N hN => ?_⟩
5378  have hN' : (1 : ℝ) ≤ N := by exact_mod_cast hN
5379  have hNp : (0 : ℝ) < N := by linarith
5380  have hP0 : 0 < P := by linarith
5381  set c : ℝ := 2 * N * P with hc
5382  have hcp : 0 < c := by positivity
5383  have hpsi : ∀ t : ℝ, 0 < t → psi2 d s (c / t) = 1 + Real.log c - Real.log t := by
5384    intro t ht
5385    unfold psi2
5386    rw [if_neg (by rw [h]; exact lt_irrefl _), if_pos h, Real.log_mul (Real.exp_pos 1).ne' (by positivity),
5387      Real.log_exp, Real.log_div hcp.ne' ht.ne']
5388    ring
5389  refine ⟨fun t => t * (2 + Real.log c - Real.log t), fun t ht => ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
5390  · rw [hpsi t ht]
5391    have h1 : HasDerivAt (fun t : ℝ => 2 + Real.log c - Real.log t) (-(t⁻¹)) t := by
5392      have := (Real.hasDerivAt_log ht.ne').const_sub (2 + Real.log c)
5393      simpa using this
5394    have := (hasDerivAt_id t).mul h1
5395    refine (this : HasDerivAt (fun t : ℝ => t * (2 + Real.log c - Real.log t)) _ t).congr_deriv ?_
5396    simp only [id]
5397    field_simp
5398    ring
5399  · have h1 : Continuous fun t : ℝ => t * (2 + Real.log c) - t * Real.log t :=
5400      (continuous_id.mul continuous_const).sub Real.continuous_mul_log
5401    refine h1.continuousWithinAt.congr (fun t _ => by ring) (by simp)
5402  · simp
5403  · intro β hβ hβP
5404    have hβ0 : 0 < β := lt_of_lt_of_le hβm hβ
5405    have hlogβ : -Real.log β ≤ |Real.log βm| := by
5406      have : Real.log βm ≤ Real.log β := Real.log_le_log hβm hβ
5407      have h2 := neg_abs_le (Real.log βm)
5408      linarith
5409    have hlogc : Real.log c = Real.log (2 * P) + Real.log N := by
5410      rw [hc, mul_right_comm, Real.log_mul (by positivity) hNp.ne']
5411    have hψN : psi2 d s N = 1 + Real.log N := by
5412      unfold psi2
5413      rw [if_neg (by rw [h]; exact lt_irrefl _), if_pos h, Real.log_mul (Real.exp_pos 1).ne' hNp.ne', Real.log_exp]
5414    have hlN : 0 ≤ Real.log N := Real.log_nonneg hN'
5415    set B : ℝ := K0 + Real.log N with hB
5416    have hB0 : 0 ≤ B := by positivity
5417    have hbr : 2 + Real.log c - Real.log β ≤ B := by
5418      rw [hlogc, hB, hK0]; linarith
5419    calc β * (2 + Real.log c - Real.log β) ≤ β * B := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hbr hβ0.le
5420      _ ≤ P * B := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hβP hB0
5421      _ ≤ P * K0 * psi2 d s N := by
5422          rw [hψN, hB]
5423          have : K0 + Real.log N ≤ K0 * (1 + Real.log N) := by nlinarith
5424          nlinarith
5425
5426lemma exists_W_gt {s : ℝ} (hs1 : (d : ℝ) / 2 < s) (hsd : s < d) {P : ℝ} (hP : 1 ≤ P) :
5427    ∃ Cw : ℝ, 0 ≤ Cw ∧ ∀ N : ℕ, 1 ≤ N → ∃ W : ℝ → ℝ,
5428      (∀ t, 0 < t → HasDerivAt W (psi2 d s (2 * N * P / t)) t) ∧
5429      ContinuousWithinAt W (Ici 0) 0 ∧ W 0 = 0 ∧
5430      ∀ β, 0 < β → β ≤ P → W β ≤ Cw * psi2 d s N := by
5431  have hd0 : (0 : ℝ) < d := by
5432    have : (0 : ℝ) ≤ d := Nat.cast_nonneg d
5433    rcases this.lt_or_eq with h | h
5434    · exact h
5435    · rw [← h] at hs1 hsd; linarith
5436  have hs : 0 < s := by linarith
5437  set k : ℝ := 2 - (d : ℝ) / s with hk
5438  have hk0 : 0 < k := by
5439    have : (d : ℝ) / s < 2 := by rw [div_lt_iff₀ hs]; linarith
5440    linarith
5441  have hk1 : k < 1 := by
5442    have : 1 < (d : ℝ) / s := by rw [lt_div_iff₀ hs]; linarith
5443    linarith
5444  have hP0 : 0 < P := by linarith
5445  refine ⟨(2 * P) ^ k * P ^ (1 - k) / (1 - k), by positivity, fun N hN => ?_⟩
5446  have hN' : (1 : ℝ) ≤ N := by exact_mod_cast hN
5447  have hNp : (0 : ℝ) < N := by linarith
5448  set c : ℝ := 2 * N * P with hc
5449  have hcp : 0 < c := by positivity
5450  have hpsi : ∀ t : ℝ, 0 < t → psi2 d s (c / t) = c ^ k * t ^ (-k) := by
5451    intro t ht
5452    unfold psi2
5453    rw [if_neg (by push Not; exact hs1.le), if_neg hs1.ne', Real.div_rpow hcp.le ht.le, Real.rpow_neg ht.le,
5454      div_eq_mul_inv]
5455  have h1k : 0 < 1 - k := by linarith
5456  refine ⟨fun t => c ^ k / (1 - k) * t ^ (1 - k), fun t ht => ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
5457  · rw [hpsi t ht]
5458    have := (Real.hasDerivAt_rpow_const (x := t) (p := 1 - k) (Or.inl ht.ne')).const_mul (c ^ k / (1 - k))
5459    convert this using 1
5460    have : 1 - k - 1 = -k := by ring
5461    rw [this]
5462    field_simp
5463  · have : Continuous fun t : ℝ => c ^ k / (1 - k) * t ^ (1 - k) :=
5464      continuous_const.mul (Real.continuous_rpow_const h1k.le)
5465    exact this.continuousWithinAt
5466  · simp only [Real.zero_rpow h1k.ne', mul_zero]
5467  · intro β hβ hβP
5468    have hψN : psi2 d s N = N ^ k := by
5469      unfold psi2
5470      rw [if_neg (by push Not; exact hs1.le), if_neg hs1.ne']
5471    rw [hψN]
5472    have h2 : β ^ (1 - k) ≤ P ^ (1 - k) := Real.rpow_le_rpow hβ.le hβP h1k.le
5473    have h3 : c ^ k = (2 * P) ^ k * N ^ k := by
5474      rw [hc, mul_right_comm, Real.mul_rpow (by positivity) hNp.le]
5475    calc c ^ k / (1 - k) * β ^ (1 - k) ≤ c ^ k / (1 - k) * P ^ (1 - k) :=
5476          mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h2 (by positivity)
5477      _ = (2 * P) ^ k * P ^ (1 - k) / (1 - k) * N ^ k := by rw [h3]; ring
5478
5479end HRS.WeakRef

5481end WM_V

5483section WM_W
5484open MeasureTheory ProbabilityTheory Function Set

5488namespace HRS.WeakRef.Abs
5489open HRS.External
5490
5491variable {E : Type*} [MeasurableSpace E] {μ : Measure E} [IsProbabilityMeasure μ]
5492
5493/-- The marginal of the full pinned law is dominated by `e^{M+M'+κ₀γ} μ`. -/
5494theorem pinned_marginal_le_const (V : WeakPotential μ) {κ₀ : ℝ} (hκ₀ : 0 ≤ κ₀) {n : ℕ} (hn : 0 < n)
5495    (i : Fin n) :
5496    (pinnedLaw V (κ₀ / n) n (fun e : Empty => e.elim)).map (fun y => y i)
5497      ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (V.M + V.M' + κ₀ * V.γ)) • μ := by
5498  have hlam : 0 ≤ κ₀ / n := div_nonneg hκ₀ (Nat.cast_nonneg n)
5499  have hψ := isField_pinField V hlam (fun e : Empty => e.elim)
5500  have hn' : (n : ℝ) ≠ 0 := by positivity
5501  have := marginal_le_field V hψ hlam (n := n) (Λ := V.M' + κ₀ * V.γ)
5502    (by
5503      simp only [Fintype.card_empty, Nat.cast_zero, mul_zero, zero_mul, add_zero]
5504      have : κ₀ / n * n * V.γ = κ₀ * V.γ := by field_simp
5505      linarith)
5506    (fun _ => 0) (fun _ => le_rfl) (fun z => by simpa using hψ.le z) i
5507  have e : (fun z : E => ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (V.M + (V.M' + κ₀ * V.γ)) * Real.exp (-(0 : ℝ))))
5508      = fun _ : E => ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (V.M + V.M' + κ₀ * V.γ)) := by
5509    funext z; rw [neg_zero, Real.exp_zero, mul_one, add_assoc]
5510  rw [e, withDensity_const] at this
5511  exact this
5512
5513/-- Clamping a density bounded by `D`. -/
5514theorem clamp_density {ν : Measure E} {f : E → ℝ≥0∞} (hf : Measurable f) (hν : ν = μ.withDensity f)
5515    {D : ℝ} (hD : 0 ≤ D) (hle : ν ≤ ENNReal.ofReal D • μ) :
5516    ∃ g : E → ℝ, Measurable g ∧ (∀ y, 0 ≤ g y ∧ g y ≤ D) ∧
5517      ν = μ.withDensity (fun y => ENNReal.ofReal (g y)) ∧
5518      ∫ y, (g y - 1) ^ 2 ∂μ = ∫ y, ((f y).toReal - 1) ^ 2 ∂μ := by
5519  have hae : f ≤ᵐ[μ] fun _ => ENNReal.ofReal D := by
5520    refine ae_le_of_forall_setLIntegral_le_of_sigmaFinite hf fun s hs _ => ?_
5521    rw [← withDensity_apply _ hs, ← hν, setLIntegral_const]
5522    have := hle s
5523    simpa [mul_comm] using this
5524  set g : E → ℝ := fun y => max 0 (min (f y).toReal D) with hg
5525  have hfm : Measurable fun y => (f y).toReal := hf.ennreal_toReal
5526  have hgm : Measurable g := measurable_const.max (hfm.min measurable_const)
5527  have hgeq : ∀ᵐ y ∂μ, g y = (f y).toReal := by
5528    filter_upwards [hae] with y hy
5529    have hlt : f y ≠ ⊤ := ne_top_of_le_ne_top ENNReal.ofReal_ne_top hy
5530    have : (f y).toReal ≤ D := by
5531      have := ENNReal.toReal_mono ENNReal.ofReal_ne_top hy
5532      rwa [ENNReal.toReal_ofReal hD] at this
5533    simp only [hg]
5534    rw [min_eq_left this, max_eq_right ENNReal.toReal_nonneg]
5535  refine ⟨g, hgm, fun y => ⟨le_max_left _ _, max_le hD (min_le_right _ _)⟩, ?_, ?_⟩
5536  · rw [hν]
5537    refine withDensity_congr_ae ?_
5538    filter_upwards [hgeq, hae] with y hy hy2
5539    rw [hy, ENNReal.ofReal_toReal (ne_top_of_le_ne_top ENNReal.ofReal_ne_top hy2)]
5540  · refine integral_congr_ae ?_
5541    filter_upwards [hgeq] with y hy
5542    rw [hy]
5543
5544end HRS.WeakRef.Abs
5545
5546end WM_W

5555lemma sq_exp_le_min {x lam L : ℝ} (hx : 0 ≤ x) (hlam : 0 < lam) (hL : 2 ≤ lam * L) :
5556    x ^ 2 * Real.exp (-(lam * x)) ≤ (min x L) ^ 2 := by
5557  have hL0 : 0 ≤ L := by
5558    by_contra h
5559    push Not at h
5560    nlinarith
5561  rcases le_total x L with h | h
5562  · rw [min_eq_left h]
5563    have : Real.exp (-(lam * x)) ≤ 1 := Real.exp_le_one_iff.2 (by nlinarith)
5564    nlinarith [sq_nonneg x]
5565  · rw [min_eq_right h]
5566    have h1 : (lam * x) ^ 2 / 2 ≤ Real.exp (lam * x) := by
5567      have := Real.quadratic_le_exp_of_nonneg (show 0 ≤ lam * x by positivity)
5568      nlinarith [sq_nonneg (lam * x)]
5569    have h2 : x ^ 2 * Real.exp (-(lam * x)) ≤ 2 / lam ^ 2 := by
5570      rw [Real.exp_neg, ← div_eq_mul_inv, div_le_iff₀ (Real.exp_pos _)]
5571      have : 2 / lam ^ 2 * Real.exp (lam * x) ≥ 2 / lam ^ 2 * ((lam * x) ^ 2 / 2) :=
5572        mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h1 (by positivity)
5573      have e : 2 / lam ^ 2 * ((lam * x) ^ 2 / 2) = x ^ 2 := by field_simp
5574      linarith
5575    have h3 : 2 / lam ^ 2 ≤ L ^ 2 := by
5576      rw [div_le_iff₀ (by positivity)]
5577      nlinarith
5578    linarith
5579
5580lemma var_alg {Nr m D E a Qb Thb εf Cb pL c₁ pN Cm δ : ℝ} (hNr : Nr = m + 1) (hm : 0 ≤ m)
5581    (hD0 : 0 ≤ D) (hDE : D ≤ E) (hQb0 : 0 ≤ Qb) (hQb : Qb ≤ Cb * pL) (hCb : 0 ≤ Cb)
5582    (hTh : Nr * Thb ^ 2 ≤ c₁ * pN) (ha0 : 0 ≤ a) (ha1 : a ≤ 1) (hδ0 : 0 ≤ δ) (hδ1 : δ ≤ 1)
5583    (hNδ : Nr * δ = pN) (hpN : 1 ≤ pN) (hεf : εf = Cm * δ) (hc₁ : 0 ≤ c₁) (hCm : 0 ≤ Cm) :
5584    2 * (m + 1) * (4 * m * D / Nr ^ 2 * Qb + 6 * Thb ^ 2 + 6 * a ^ 2 / Nr ^ 2 + 6 * D * a ^ 2 * εf ^ 2)
5585      ≤ 8 * E * Cb * pL + (12 * c₁ + 12 + 12 * E * Cm ^ 2) * pN := by
5586  have hNr1 : 1 ≤ Nr := by linarith
5587  have hNrp : 0 < Nr := by linarith
5588  have hE0 : 0 ≤ E := hD0.trans hDE
5589  rw [← hNr]
5590  have hmN : m ≤ Nr := by linarith
5591  have t1 : 2 * Nr * (4 * m * D / Nr ^ 2 * Qb) ≤ 8 * E * (Cb * pL) := by
5592    have : 2 * Nr * (4 * m * D / Nr ^ 2 * Qb) = 8 * D * Qb * (m / Nr) := by field_simp; ring
5593    rw [this]
5594    have hr : m / Nr ≤ 1 := (div_le_one hNrp).2 hmN
5595    have hr0 : 0 ≤ m / Nr := by positivity
5596    calc 8 * D * Qb * (m / Nr) ≤ 8 * D * Qb * 1 := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hr (by positivity)
5597      _ = 8 * D * Qb := by ring
5598      _ ≤ 8 * E * (Cb * pL) := by
5599          have := mul_le_mul hDE hQb hQb0 hE0
5600          nlinarith
5601  have t2 : 2 * Nr * (6 * Thb ^ 2) ≤ 12 * (c₁ * pN) := by nlinarith
5602  have t3 : 2 * Nr * (6 * a ^ 2 / Nr ^ 2) ≤ 12 * pN := by
5603    have : 2 * Nr * (6 * a ^ 2 / Nr ^ 2) = 12 * a ^ 2 / Nr := by field_simp; ring
5604    rw [this, div_le_iff₀ hNrp]
5605    have : a ^ 2 ≤ 1 := by nlinarith
5606    nlinarith
5607  have t4 : 2 * Nr * (6 * D * a ^ 2 * εf ^ 2) ≤ 12 * E * Cm ^ 2 * pN := by
5608    rw [hεf, mul_pow]
5609    have h1 : a ^ 2 ≤ 1 := by nlinarith
5610    have h2 : δ ^ 2 ≤ δ := by nlinarith
5611    have h3 : D * a ^ 2 ≤ E := by nlinarith
5612    have : 2 * Nr * (6 * D * a ^ 2 * (Cm ^ 2 * δ ^ 2)) = 12 * (D * a ^ 2) * Cm ^ 2 * (Nr * δ ^ 2) := by ring
5613    rw [this]
5614    have h4 : Nr * δ ^ 2 ≤ pN := by rw [← hNδ]; nlinarith
5615    have h5 : 0 ≤ D * a ^ 2 := by positivity
5616    have h6 : 12 * (D * a ^ 2) * Cm ^ 2 ≤ 12 * E * Cm ^ 2 := by nlinarith [sq_nonneg Cm]
5617    exact mul_le_mul h6 h4 (by positivity) (by positivity)
5618  have e : 2 * Nr * (4 * m * D / Nr ^ 2 * Qb + 6 * Thb ^ 2 + 6 * a ^ 2 / Nr ^ 2 + 6 * D * a ^ 2 * εf ^ 2)
5619      = 2 * Nr * (4 * m * D / Nr ^ 2 * Qb) + 2 * Nr * (6 * Thb ^ 2) + 2 * Nr * (6 * a ^ 2 / Nr ^ 2)
5620        + 2 * Nr * (6 * D * a ^ 2 * εf ^ 2) := by ring
5621  rw [e]
5622  nlinarith
5623
5624end HRS.WeakRef

5626end WM_X

5628section WM_Y
5629open MeasureTheory ProbabilityTheory Function Set
5630open scoped ENNReal NNReal BigOperators
5631set_option linter.unusedSectionVars false

5633namespace HRS.WeakRef
5634open HRS.External
