5636variable {d : ℕ}
5637
5638set_option maxHeartbeats 800000 in
5639theorem variance_at (hd : 1 ≤ d) {s : ℝ} (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d)
5640    {μ : Measure (Torus d)} [IsProbabilityMeasure μ] {a : ℝ} {q : Torus d → Torus d → ℝ}
5641    (hK : WeakKernel μ a q) (ha0 : 0 ≤ a) (ha1 : a ≤ 1)
5642    {Ca Cb Cm P E : ℝ} (hCa : 0 ≤ Ca) (hCb : 0 ≤ Cb) (hCm : 0 ≤ Cm) (hP : 1 ≤ P)
5643    (hE : E = Real.exp (2 * P))
5644    (hr1 : ∀ x, ∀ L : ℝ, 0 < L → ∫ y, max (q x y - L) 0 ∂μ ≤ Ca * L ^ (1 - (d : ℝ) / sigmaOf d s))
5645    (hr2 : ∀ x, ∀ L : ℝ, 1 ≤ L → ∫ y, (min (q x y) L) ^ 2 ∂μ ≤ Cb * psi2 d s L)
5646    {N : ℕ} (hN : 2 ≤ N) {t : ℝ} (ht : 0 < t) (htP : t ≤ P) (hta : t * a ≤ 1 / (16 * E))
5647    (hmarg : ∃ f : Torus d → ℝ≥0∞,
5648      (pairGibbsLaw q N t μ).map (fun X => X ⟨0, by omega⟩) = μ.withDensity f ∧ Measurable f ∧
5649        ∫ x, ((f x).toReal - 1) ^ 2 ∂μ ≤ (Cm * deltaN d s N) ^ 2) :
5650    variance (enG q μ N) (pairGibbsLaw q N t μ)
5651      ≤ 8 * E * Cb * psi2 d s (2 * N * P / t)
5652        + (12 * (1 + 2 * E * Ca) ^ 2 + 12 + 12 * E * Cm ^ 2) * psi2 d s N := by
5653  obtain ⟨n, rfl⟩ : ∃ n, N = n + 1 := ⟨N - 1, by omega⟩
5654  have hn : 0 < n := by omega
5655  have hd0 : (0 : ℝ) < d := by exact_mod_cast hd
5656  have hσ0 := sigmaOf_pos hd hs0
5657  have hE1 : 1 ≤ E := by rw [hE]; exact Real.one_le_exp (by linarith)
5658  have hE0 : 0 < E := by linarith
5659  have hNR : (0 : ℝ) < ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ) := by positivity
5660  have hN1R : (1 : ℝ) ≤ ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ) := by exact_mod_cast (show 1 ≤ n + 1 by omega)
5661  set V := mkV hK ha0 ht.le with hV
5662  have hVM : V.M = t * a := rfl
5663  have hVM' : V.M' = 0 := rfl
5664  have hVγ : V.γ = a := rfl
5665  have hVq : V.q = q := rfl
5666  set D : ℝ := Real.exp (V.M + V.M' + t * V.γ) with hDdef
5667  have hta0 : 0 ≤ t * a := mul_nonneg ht.le ha0
5668  have hDe : D = Real.exp (t * a + t * a) := by
5669    simp only [hDdef, hVM, hVM', hVγ]; congr 1; ring
5670  have htaP : t * a ≤ P := by
5671    have : 1 / (16 * E) ≤ 1 := by rw [div_le_one (by positivity)]; linarith
5672    linarith
5673  have hDE : D ≤ E := by
5674    rw [hDe, hE]; exact Real.exp_le_exp.2 (by linarith)
5675  have hD1 : 1 ≤ D := by rw [hDe]; exact Real.one_le_exp (by linarith)
5676  have hD0 : 0 ≤ D := by linarith
5677  have hκ : weakKappa V t ≤ 1 / 2 := by
5678    have : weakKappa V t = 4 * D * (t * a) := by
5679      simp only [weakKappa, hDdef, hVγ]
5680    rw [this]
5681    have h1 : D * (t * a) ≤ E * (1 / (16 * E)) := mul_le_mul hDE hta hta0 hE0.le
5682    have h2 : E * (1 / (16 * E)) = 1 / 16 := by field_simp
5683    linarith
5684  have hκ0 : 0 ≤ weakKappa V t := weakKappa_nonneg V ht.le
5685  rw [pairGibbsLaw_eq_pinnedLaw hK ha0 ht.le (Nat.succ_pos n)] at hmarg ⊢
5686  obtain ⟨f, hfmap, hfm, hfint⟩ := hmarg
5687  have hle := Abs.pinned_marginal_le_const V ht.le (Nat.succ_pos n) ⟨0, Nat.succ_pos n⟩
5688  obtain ⟨g, hgm, hg, hgπ, hgf⟩ := Abs.clamp_density hfm hfmap hD0 hle
5689  -- the energy and the centred version
5690  set Nr : ℝ := ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ) with hNr
5691  set I₂ : ℝ := ∫ p : Torus d × Torus d, q p.1 p.2 ∂(μ.prod μ) with hI₂
5692  have hI₂' : ∫ x, ∫ y, q x y ∂μ ∂μ = I₂ := by
5693    rw [hI₂, integral_prod _ hK.integrable_prod]
5694  set G : Config d (n + 1) → ℝ := fun X => enG q μ (n + 1) X - Nr / 2 * I₂ with hGdef
5695  have hG : ∀ X, G X = (1 / Nr) * pairEnergy V X - ∑ k, ∫ y, V.q (X k) y ∂μ := by
5696    intro X
5697    simp only [hGdef, enG, modulatedEnergy, pairEnergy, hVq, hI₂']
5698    rw [← hNr]
5699    field_simp
5700    ring
5701  -- Qb
5702  set L : ℝ := 2 * Nr * P / t with hL
5703  have hLge : 2 * Nr ≤ L := by
5704    rw [hL, le_div_iff₀ ht]
5705    nlinarith
5706  have hL1 : 1 ≤ L := by linarith
5707  have hQb : ∀ x, ∫ z, V.q x z ^ 2 * Real.exp (-((t / Nr) * V.q z x)) ∂μ ≤ Cb * psi2 d s L := by
5708    intro x
5709    refine le_trans ?_ (hr2 x L hL1)
5710    have hqx : Measurable (q x) := hK.measurable_left x
5711    have hmin : Measurable fun y => (min (q x y) L) ^ 2 := (hqx.min measurable_const).pow_const 2
5712    have hint : Integrable (fun y => (min (q x y) L) ^ 2) μ :=
5713      Abs.intg_bdd hmin (C := L ^ 2) fun y => by
5714        rw [abs_of_nonneg (sq_nonneg _)]
5715        have h0 : 0 ≤ min (q x y) L := le_min (hK.nonneg x y) (by linarith)
5716        exact pow_le_pow_left₀ h0 (min_le_right _ _) 2
5717    refine integral_mono_of_nonneg (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun z => ?_) hint
5718      (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun z => ?_)
5719    · exact mul_nonneg (sq_nonneg _) (Real.exp_pos _).le
5720    · simp only [hVq]
5721      rw [hK.symm z x]
5722      refine sq_exp_le_min (hK.nonneg x z) (by positivity) ?_
5723      have : t / Nr * L = 2 * P := by rw [hL]; field_simp
5724      rw [this]; linarith
5725  -- Thb
5726  set σ := sigmaOf d s with hσ
5727  set Lp : ℝ := Nr ^ (σ / d) with hLp
5728  have hLp0 : 0 < Lp := Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hNR _
5729  have hexp1 : Lp / Nr = Nr ^ (σ / d - 1) := by
5730    rw [hLp, Real.rpow_sub hNR, Real.rpow_one]
5731  have hexp2 : Lp ^ (1 - (d : ℝ) / σ) = Nr ^ (σ / d - 1) := by
5732    rw [hLp, ← Real.rpow_mul hNR.le]
5733    congr 1
5734    field_simp
5735  have hThb : ∀ (x : Torus d) (ν₁ ν₂ : Measure (Torus d)), IsProbabilityMeasure ν₁ → IsProbabilityMeasure ν₂ →
5736      ν₁ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (V.M + V.M' + t * V.γ)) • μ →
5737      ν₂ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (V.M + V.M' + t * V.γ)) • μ →
5738      (∀ A, MeasurableSet A → |ν₁.real A - ν₂.real A| ≤ 2 * weakKappa V t / Nr) →
5739      |∫ z, V.q x z ∂ν₁ - ∫ z, V.q x z ∂ν₂| ≤ (1 + 2 * E * Ca) * Nr ^ (σ / d - 1) := by
5740    intro x ν₁ ν₂ h1 h2 hν₁ hν₂ hTV
5741    have := Abs.theta_abstract (μ := μ) (q := q) hK.meas hK.nonneg hK.integrable_row (L := Lp)
5742      (a₂ := Ca * Lp ^ (1 - (d : ℝ) / σ)) (D := D) (ε₀ := 2 * weakKappa V t / Nr) hLp0.le
5743      (fun y => hr1 y Lp hLp0) hD0 x ν₁ ν₂ hν₁ hν₂ hTV
5744    refine this.trans ?_
5745    rw [hexp2]
5746    have e1 : 2 * weakKappa V t / Nr * Lp ≤ Nr ^ (σ / d - 1) := by
5747      rw [div_mul_eq_mul_div, div_le_iff₀ hNR]
5748      have : Nr ^ (σ / d - 1) * Nr = Lp := by
5749        rw [← hexp1]; field_simp
5750      rw [this]
5751      have : 2 * weakKappa V t ≤ 1 := by linarith
5752      nlinarith
5753    have e2 : 2 * (D * (Ca * Nr ^ (σ / d - 1))) ≤ 2 * E * Ca * Nr ^ (σ / d - 1) := by
5754      have h0 : 0 ≤ Nr ^ (σ / d - 1) := Real.rpow_nonneg hNR.le _
5755      have : D * (Ca * Nr ^ (σ / d - 1)) ≤ E * (Ca * Nr ^ (σ / d - 1)) :=
5756        mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hDE (by positivity)
5757      nlinarith
5758    nlinarith
5759  have hgf' : ∫ y, (g y - 1) ^ 2 ∂μ ≤ (Cm * deltaN d s (n + 1)) ^ 2 := by rw [hgf]; exact hfint
5760  have hvar := Abs.variance_G_le V ht hn hκ (fun e : Empty => e.elim) (Qb := Cb * psi2 d s L)
5761    (Thb := (1 + 2 * E * Ca) * Nr ^ (σ / d - 1)) (εf := Cm * deltaN d s (n + 1)) hQb hThb g hgm hg hgπ hgf' G hG
5762  have hGm : Measurable G := ((hK.measurable_enG (n + 1)).sub_const _)
5763  have hvc : variance (enG q μ (n + 1)) (pinnedLaw V (t / Nr) (n + 1) (fun e : Empty => e.elim))
5764      = variance G (pinnedLaw V (t / Nr) (n + 1) (fun e : Empty => e.elim)) := by
5765    have hP : IsProbabilityMeasure (pinnedLaw V (t / Nr) (n + 1) (fun e : Empty => e.elim)) :=
5766      isProb_pinnedLaw V (div_nonneg ht.le hNR.le) _ _
5767    have := variance_add_const hGm.aestronglyMeasurable (Nr / 2 * I₂)
5768      (μ := pinnedLaw V (t / Nr) (n + 1) (fun e : Empty => e.elim))
5769    have e : (fun ω => G ω + Nr / 2 * I₂) = enG q μ (n + 1) := by
5770      funext X; simp only [hGdef]; ring
5771    rw [e] at this
5772    exact this
5773  rw [hvc]
5774  refine hvar.trans ?_
5775  -- arithmetic
5776  have hδ0 := deltaN_nonneg (d := d) s (show 1 ≤ n + 1 by omega)
5777  have hδ1 := deltaN_le_one hd hs0 hsd (show 1 ≤ n + 1 by omega)
5778  have hNδ := mul_deltaN_eq_psi2 (d := d) s (show 1 ≤ n + 1 by omega)
5779  have hpN := psi2_ge_one hd hs0 hN1R
5780  have hTh : Nr * ((1 + 2 * E * Ca) * Nr ^ (σ / d - 1)) ^ 2 ≤ (1 + 2 * E * Ca) ^ 2 * psi2 d s Nr := by
5781    have := sq_rpow_le_psi2 hd hs0 hsd hN1R
5782    rw [mul_pow]
5783    have h2 : 0 ≤ (1 + 2 * E * Ca) ^ 2 := sq_nonneg _
5784    calc Nr * ((1 + 2 * E * Ca) ^ 2 * (Nr ^ (σ / d - 1)) ^ 2)
5785        = (1 + 2 * E * Ca) ^ 2 * (Nr * (Nr ^ (σ / d - 1)) ^ 2) := by ring
5786      _ ≤ (1 + 2 * E * Ca) ^ 2 * psi2 d s Nr := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left this h2
5787  have hQb0 : 0 ≤ Cb * psi2 d s L := mul_nonneg hCb (by linarith [psi2_ge_one hd hs0 hL1])
5788  have := var_alg (Nr := Nr) (m := (n : ℝ)) (D := D) (E := E) (a := a) (Qb := Cb * psi2 d s L)
5789    (Thb := (1 + 2 * E * Ca) * Nr ^ (σ / d - 1)) (εf := Cm * deltaN d s (n + 1)) (Cb := Cb)
5790    (pL := psi2 d s L) (c₁ := (1 + 2 * E * Ca) ^ 2) (pN := psi2 d s Nr) (Cm := Cm)
5791    (δ := deltaN d s (n + 1)) (by rw [hNr]; push_cast; ring) (Nat.cast_nonneg n) hD0 hDE hQb0 le_rfl hCb
5792    hTh ha0 ha1 hδ0 hδ1 hNδ hpN rfl (sq_nonneg _) hCm
5793  exact this
5794
5795end HRS.WeakRef

5799section WM_Z
5800open MeasureTheory ProbabilityTheory Function Set
5801open scoped ENNReal NNReal BigOperators
5802set_option linter.unusedSectionVars false

5804namespace HRS.WeakRef

5806variable {d : ℕ}
5807
5808lemma exists_W (hd : 1 ≤ d) {s : ℝ} (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) {P βm : ℝ} (hP : 1 ≤ P) (hβm : 0 < βm) :
5809    ∃ Cw : ℝ, 0 ≤ Cw ∧ ∀ N : ℕ, 1 ≤ N → ∃ W : ℝ → ℝ,
5810      (∀ t, 0 < t → HasDerivAt W (psi2 d s (2 * N * P / t)) t) ∧
5811      ContinuousWithinAt W (Ici 0) 0 ∧ W 0 = 0 ∧
5812      ∀ β, βm ≤ β → β ≤ P → W β ≤ Cw * psi2 d s N := by
5813  rcases lt_trichotomy s ((d : ℝ) / 2) with h | h | h
5814  · obtain ⟨Cw, hC, H⟩ := exists_W_lt (d := d) h hP
5815    refine ⟨Cw, hC, fun N hN => ?_⟩
5816    obtain ⟨W, h1, h2, h3, h4⟩ := H N hN
5817    exact ⟨W, h1, h2, h3, fun β hβ hβP => h4 β (lt_of_lt_of_le hβm hβ) hβP⟩
5818  · exact exists_W_eq (d := d) h hP hβm
5819  · obtain ⟨Cw, hC, H⟩ := exists_W_gt (d := d) h hsd hP
5820    refine ⟨Cw, hC, fun N hN => ?_⟩
5821    obtain ⟨W, h1, h2, h3, h4⟩ := H N hN
5822    exact ⟨W, h1, h2, h3, fun β hβ hβP => h4 β (lt_of_lt_of_le hβm hβ) hβP⟩
5823
5824end HRS.WeakRef
5825end WM_Z

5831/-- Strong form of the marginal bound `N:eq:weak-marginal` (real-valued `L²` integral, with a measurable
5832bounded density), proved by the cavity formula, the Laplace residual, the `L²` convolution estimate and the
5833Schur contraction (source lines 6206-6268). -/
5834theorem weak_marginal_strong (hd : 1 ≤ d) (s : ℝ) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d)
5835    (cstar : ℝ) (hw : ∀ z : Torus d, z ≠ 0 → 1 ≤ periodicRiesz d s z + cstar)
5836    (βm βp : ℝ) (hβm : 0 < βm) (c₀ : ℝ) (hc₀ : 0 < c₀) (Λ : ℕ → ℝ) (A : ℝ) :
5837    ∃ a₀ C : ℝ, 0 < a₀ ∧
5838      ∀ ρ : Torus d → ℝ, IsProbDensity ρ → ∀ [IsProbabilityMeasure (withDensityReal ρ)],
5839        Torus.IsSmooth ρ → (∀ x, c₀ ≤ ρ x) →
5840        (∀ k : ℕ, Torus.cbNorm k ρ ≤ Λ k) →
5841      ∀ q : Torus d → Torus d → ℝ, Measurable (Function.uncurry q) → (∀ x y, q x y = q y x) →
5842        (∀ x y, 0 ≤ q x y) → (∀ x y, q x y ≤ A * (periodicRiesz d s (x - y) + cstar)) →
5843      ∀ a : ℝ, 0 ≤ a → a < a₀ →
5844        (∀ x, ∫⁻ y, ENNReal.ofReal (q x y) ∂(withDensityReal ρ) ≤ ENNReal.ofReal a) →
5845      ∀ N : ℕ, ∀ hN : 2 ≤ N, ∀ β ∈ Set.Icc βm βp, ∀ τ ∈ Set.Icc (0 : ℝ) 1,
5846        ∃ f : Torus d → ℝ≥0∞,
5847          (pairGibbsLaw q N (τ * β) (withDensityReal ρ)).map (fun X => X ⟨0, by omega⟩)
5848            = (withDensityReal ρ).withDensity f ∧ Measurable f ∧ (∃ K : ℝ, ∀ x, (f x).toReal ≤ K) ∧
5849          ∫ x, ((f x).toReal - 1) ^ 2 ∂(withDensityReal ρ) ≤ (C * deltaN d s N) ^ 2 := by
5850  set Λ₀ : ℝ := max (Λ 0) 1 with hΛ₀
5851  have hΛ₀0 : 0 ≤ Λ₀ := by have := le_max_right (Λ 0) 1; linarith
5852  obtain ⟨Ca, Cb, hCa, hCb, hrows⟩ := WeakRef.row_bounds hd hs0 hsd cstar hw A Λ₀ hΛ₀0
5853  set P : ℝ := max βp 0 + 1 with hP
5854  have hP1 : 1 ≤ P := by have := le_max_right βp 0; linarith
5855  have hP0 : 0 < P := by linarith
5856  set E : ℝ := Real.exp (2 * P) with hE
5857  have hE1 : 1 ≤ E := Real.one_le_exp (by linarith)
5858  have hE0 : 0 < E := by linarith
5859  set G₁ : ℝ := 2 * P * E * Ca + P ^ 2 * E * Cb with hG₁
5860  have hG₁0 : 0 ≤ G₁ := by positivity
5861  set Kc : ℝ := Real.sqrt (2 * (Cb + Ca ^ 2 * E)) with hKc
5862  have hKc2 : Kc ^ 2 = 2 * (Cb + Ca ^ 2 * E) := Real.sq_sqrt (by positivity)
5863  have hKc0 : 0 ≤ Kc := Real.sqrt_nonneg _
5864  set C₀ : ℝ := 150 * G₁ ^ 2 + 214 * P ^ 2 * Kc ^ 2 + 12 * P ^ 2 with hC₀
5865  set C : ℝ := Real.sqrt C₀ with hCdef
5866  have hC₀0 : 0 ≤ C₀ := by positivity
5867  have hC2 : C ^ 2 = C₀ := Real.sq_sqrt hC₀0
5868  refine ⟨min 1 (1 / (16 * E * P)), C, lt_min one_pos (by positivity), ?_⟩
5869  intro ρ hρ hprob hsm hlow hcb q hqm hqs hq0 hqA a ha0 ha hrow N hN β hβ τ hτ
5870  set μ : Measure (Torus d) := withDensityReal ρ with hμ
5871  have hρle : ∀ x, ρ x ≤ Λ₀ := fun x =>
5872    (WeakRef.le_cbNorm_zero hsm x).trans ((hcb 0).trans (le_max_left _ _))
5873  have hK : WeakRef.WeakKernel μ a q := ⟨hqm, hqs, hq0, hrow⟩
5874  have hβ0 : 0 < β := lt_of_lt_of_le hβm hβ.1
5875  have hκ₀ : 0 ≤ τ * β := mul_nonneg hτ.1 hβ0.le
5876  have hκP : τ * β ≤ P := by
5877    have h1 : τ * β ≤ 1 * β := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hτ.2 hβ0.le
5878    have h2 := hβ.2
5879    have h3 := le_max_left βp 0
5880    linarith
5881  have ha1 : a < 1 := lt_of_lt_of_le ha (min_le_left _ _)
5882  have haE : a < 1 / (16 * E * P) := lt_of_lt_of_le ha (min_le_right _ _)
5883  have hnN : 0 < N := by omega
5884  obtain ⟨n, rfl⟩ : ∃ n, N = n + 1 := ⟨N - 1, by omega⟩
5885  have hn : 0 < n := by omega
5886  set κ₀ : ℝ := τ * β with hκ₀def
5887  set V := WeakRef.mkV hK ha0 hκ₀ with hV
5888  have hNR : (0 : ℝ) < ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ) := by positivity
5889  have hN1R : (1 : ℝ) ≤ ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ) := by exact_mod_cast (show 1 ≤ n + 1 by omega)
5890  have hκaE : κ₀ * a ≤ 1 / (16 * E) := by
5891    have h1 : κ₀ * a ≤ P * a := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hκP ha0
5892    have h2 : P * a ≤ P * (1 / (16 * E * P)) := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left haE.le hP0.le
5893    have h3 : P * (1 / (16 * E * P)) = 1 / (16 * E) := by field_simp
5894    linarith
5895  have hκa0 : 0 ≤ κ₀ * a := mul_nonneg hκ₀ ha0
5896  set D : ℝ := Real.exp (V.M + V.M' + κ₀ * V.γ) with hDdef
5897  have hVM : V.M = κ₀ * a := rfl
5898  have hVM' : V.M' = 0 := rfl
5899  have hVγ : V.γ = a := rfl
5900  have hDe : D = Real.exp (κ₀ * a + κ₀ * a) := by
5901    simp only [hDdef, hVM, hVM', hVγ]; congr 1; ring
5902  have hDE : D ≤ E := by
5903    rw [hDe]
5904    refine Real.exp_le_exp.2 ?_
5905    have : κ₀ * a ≤ P := by nlinarith
5906    linarith
5907  have hD1 : 1 ≤ D := by rw [hDe]; exact Real.one_le_exp (by linarith)
5908  have hs : κ₀ * V.γ * D ≤ 1 / 4 := by
5909    rw [hVγ]
5910    have : κ₀ * a * D ≤ (1 / (16 * E)) * E := mul_le_mul hκaE hDE (by linarith) (by positivity)
5911    have h2 : (1 / (16 * E)) * E = 1 / 16 := by field_simp
5912    linarith
5913  have hs2 : 214 * (κ₀ * V.γ) ^ 2 ≤ 1 := by
5914    rw [hVγ]
5915    have h1 : κ₀ * a ≤ 1 / 16 := by
5916      have : 1 / (16 * E) ≤ 1 / 16 := by
5917        rw [div_le_div_iff₀ (by positivity) (by norm_num)]; nlinarith
5918      linarith
5919    have h2 : (κ₀ * a) ^ 2 ≤ (1 / 16) ^ 2 := pow_le_pow_left₀ hκa0 h1 2
5920    nlinarith
5921  have hκ : External.weakKappa V κ₀ ≤ 1 / 2 := by
5922    have : External.weakKappa V κ₀ = 4 * D * (κ₀ * a) := by
5923      simp only [External.weakKappa, hDdef, hVγ]
5924    rw [this]
5925    have h1 : D * (κ₀ * a) ≤ E * (1 / (16 * E)) := mul_le_mul hDE hκaE hκa0 hE0.le
5926    have h2 : E * (1 / (16 * E)) = 1 / 16 := by field_simp
5927    linarith
5928  set δ : ℝ := deltaN d s (n + 1) with hδdef
5929  have hδ0 : 0 ≤ δ := deltaN_nonneg s (by omega)
5930  have hδ1 : (((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ))⁻¹ ≤ δ := WeakRef.inv_le_deltaN hd hs0 hsd (by omega)
5931  have hrowsx := fun x => hrows ρ hρle q hqm hq0 hqA (n + 1) hN x
5932  have hT1 : ∀ x, ∫ y, max (q x y - ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) 0 ∂μ ≤ Ca * δ := fun x => (hrowsx x).1
5933  have hT2 : ∀ x, ∫ y, (min (q x y) ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) ^ 2 ∂μ ≤ Cb * (((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ) * δ) :=
5934    fun x => (hrowsx x).2
5935  have hT10 : ∀ x, 0 ≤ ∫ y, max (q x y - ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) 0 ∂μ :=
5936    fun x => integral_nonneg fun y => le_max_right _ _
5937  have hT20 : ∀ x, 0 ≤ ∫ y, (min (q x y) ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) ^ 2 ∂μ :=
5938    fun x => integral_nonneg fun y => sq_nonneg _
5939  have hnN : (n : ℝ) ≤ ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ) := by exact_mod_cast Nat.le_succ n
5940  have hD0 : 0 ≤ D := by linarith
5941  set εG : ℝ := (2 * κ₀ * D * Ca + κ₀ ^ 2 * D * Cb) * δ with hεG
5942  have hKA : ∀ x, 2 * ((κ₀ / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) * (n * (D * ∫ z, max (V.q x z - ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) 0 ∂μ)))
5943        + (κ₀ / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) ^ 2 / 2 * (2 * n * (D * ∫ z, (min (V.q x z) ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) ^ 2 ∂μ))
5944        ≤ εG := fun x =>
5945    WeakRef.ka_alg hκ₀ hD0 (Nat.cast_nonneg n) hNR hnN hδ0 (hT10 x) (hT20 x) (hT1 x) (hT2 x)
5946  set εc : ℝ := Kc * δ with hεc
5947  have hKB : ∀ ν₁ ν₂ : Measure (Torus d), IsProbabilityMeasure ν₁ → IsProbabilityMeasure ν₂ →
5948        ν₁ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal D • μ → ν₂ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal D • μ →
5949        (∀ A, MeasurableSet A → |ν₁.real A - ν₂.real A| ≤ External.weakKappa V κ₀ / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) →
5950        ∫ x, (∫ y, V.q x y ∂ν₁ - ∫ y, V.q x y ∂ν₂) ^ 2 ∂μ ≤ εc ^ 2 := by
5951    intro ν₁ ν₂ _ _ h₁ h₂ hTV
5952    have hκ0 : 0 ≤ External.weakKappa V κ₀ := External.weakKappa_nonneg V hκ₀
5953    have hcv := WeakRef.Abs.conv_measures (μ := μ) (q := q) hqm hq0 hqs hK.integrable_row
5954      (N := ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) (a₂ := Ca * δ) (b₁ := Cb * (((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ) * δ)) (D := D)
5955      (ε₀ := External.weakKappa V κ₀ / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) hNR.le (by positivity)
5956      (by have := hδ0; positivity) (fun x => hT1 x) (fun x => hT2 x) hD0 ν₁ ν₂ h₁ h₂ hTV
5957    refine hcv.trans ?_
5958    have hε0 : 0 ≤ External.weakKappa V κ₀ / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ) := div_nonneg hκ0 hNR.le
5959    have h2ε : 2 * (External.weakKappa V κ₀ / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) ≤ 1 / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ) := by
5960      rw [← mul_div_assoc]
5961      exact div_le_div_of_nonneg_right (by linarith) hNR.le
5962    have := WeakRef.kb_alg hε0 h2ε hN1R hCa hCb hD0 hDE hδ0 hδ1
5963    have hKcδ : εc ^ 2 = 2 * (Cb + Ca ^ 2 * E) * δ ^ 2 := by rw [hεc, mul_pow, hKc2]
5964    rw [hKcδ]
5965    exact this
5966  obtain ⟨f, hfmap, hfm, ⟨K, hK'⟩, hfint⟩ := WeakRef.Abs.marginal_L2 V hκ₀ hn (fun x => rfl)
5967    (by show 0 ≤ κ₀ * a; exact hκa0) (le_refl _) hκ hs hs2 hKA hKB
5968  have hfin := WeakRef.final_alg (Kc := Kc) (a := a) hκ₀ hκP hD0 hDE hCa hCb hδ0 ha0 ha1.le hNR hδ1
5969  have hCδ : (C * δ) ^ 2 = C₀ * δ ^ 2 := by rw [mul_pow, hC2]
5970  have hbound : ∫ x, ((f x).toReal - 1) ^ 2 ∂μ ≤ (C * δ) ^ 2 := by
5971    rw [hVγ] at hfint
5972    calc ∫ x, ((f x).toReal - 1) ^ 2 ∂μ
5973        ≤ 150 * εG ^ 2 + 214 * κ₀ ^ 2 * εc ^ 2 + 12 * (κ₀ * a / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) ^ 2 := hfint
5974      _ ≤ (150 * (2 * P * E * Ca + P ^ 2 * E * Cb) ^ 2 + 214 * P ^ 2 * Kc ^ 2 + 12 * P ^ 2) * δ ^ 2 := hfin
5975      _ = (C * δ) ^ 2 := by rw [hCδ, hC₀, hG₁]
5976  have hmap : (pairGibbsLaw q (n + 1) (τ * β) μ).map (fun X => X ⟨0, by omega⟩)
5977      = μ.withDensity f := by
5978    rw [WeakRef.pairGibbsLaw_eq_pinnedLaw hK ha0 hκ₀ (Nat.succ_pos n)]
5979    exact hfmap
5980  exact ⟨f, hmap, hfm, ⟨K, hK'⟩, hbound⟩

5983/-- Marginal bound `N:eq:weak-marginal` of Lemma 5.6 (source lines 6206-6268), from `weak_marginal_strong`. -/
5984theorem weak_marginal_aux (hd : 1 ≤ d) (s : ℝ) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d)
5985    (cstar : ℝ) (hw : ∀ z : Torus d, z ≠ 0 → 1 ≤ periodicRiesz d s z + cstar)
5986    (βm βp : ℝ) (hβm : 0 < βm) (c₀ : ℝ) (hc₀ : 0 < c₀) (Λ : ℕ → ℝ) (A : ℝ) :
5987    ∃ a₀ C : ℝ, 0 < a₀ ∧
5988      ∀ ρ : Torus d → ℝ, IsProbDensity ρ → ∀ [IsProbabilityMeasure (withDensityReal ρ)],
5989        Torus.IsSmooth ρ → (∀ x, c₀ ≤ ρ x) →
5990        (∀ k : ℕ, Torus.cbNorm k ρ ≤ Λ k) →
5991      ∀ q : Torus d → Torus d → ℝ, Measurable (Function.uncurry q) → (∀ x y, q x y = q y x) →
5992        (∀ x y, 0 ≤ q x y) → (∀ x y, q x y ≤ A * (periodicRiesz d s (x - y) + cstar)) →
5993      ∀ a : ℝ, 0 ≤ a → a < a₀ →
5994        (∀ x, ∫⁻ y, ENNReal.ofReal (q x y) ∂(withDensityReal ρ) ≤ ENNReal.ofReal a) →
5995      ∀ N : ℕ, ∀ hN : 2 ≤ N, ∀ β ∈ Set.Icc βm βp, ∀ τ ∈ Set.Icc (0 : ℝ) 1,
5996        ∃ f : Torus d → ℝ≥0∞,
5997          (pairGibbsLaw q N (τ * β) (withDensityReal ρ)).map (fun X => X ⟨0, by omega⟩)
5998            = (withDensityReal ρ).withDensity f ∧
5999          ∫⁻ x, ‖(f x).toReal - 1‖ₑ ^ 2 ∂(withDensityReal ρ)
6000            ≤ ENNReal.ofReal ((C * deltaN d s N) ^ 2) := by
6001  obtain ⟨a₀, C, ha₀, h⟩ := weak_marginal_strong hd s hs0 hsd cstar hw βm βp hβm c₀ hc₀ Λ A
6002  refine ⟨a₀, C, ha₀, ?_⟩
6003  intro ρ hρ hprob hsm hlow hcb q hqm hqs hq0 hqA a ha0 ha hrow N hN β hβ τ hτ
6004  obtain ⟨f, hfmap, hfm, ⟨K, hK'⟩, hbound⟩ := h ρ hρ hsm hlow hcb q hqm hqs hq0 hqA a ha0 ha hrow N hN β hβ τ hτ
6005  refine ⟨f, hfmap, ?_⟩
6006  set μ : Measure (Torus d) := withDensityReal ρ with hμ
6007  have hfm' : Measurable fun x => (f x).toReal := hfm.ennreal_toReal
6008  have hint : Integrable (fun x => ((f x).toReal - 1) ^ 2) μ := by
6009    refine Integrable.of_bound ((hfm'.sub_const 1).pow_const 2).aestronglyMeasurable ((max K 0 + 1) ^ 2)
6010      (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun x => ?_)
6011    rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_nonneg (sq_nonneg _)]
6012    have h0 : 0 ≤ (f x).toReal := ENNReal.toReal_nonneg
6013    have h1 := hK' x
6014    have h2 : |(f x).toReal - 1| ≤ max K 0 + 1 := by
6015      rw [abs_le]; constructor <;> linarith [le_max_left K 0, le_max_right K 0]
6016    calc ((f x).toReal - 1) ^ 2 = |(f x).toReal - 1| ^ 2 := (sq_abs _).symm
6017      _ ≤ (max K 0 + 1) ^ 2 := pow_le_pow_left₀ (abs_nonneg _) h2 2
6018  have e : ∀ x, ‖(f x).toReal - 1‖ₑ ^ 2 = ENNReal.ofReal (((f x).toReal - 1) ^ 2) := by
6019    intro x
6020    rw [Real.enorm_eq_ofReal_abs, ← ENNReal.ofReal_pow (abs_nonneg _), sq_abs]
6021  simp_rw [e]
6022  rw [← ofReal_integral_eq_lintegral_ofReal hint (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun x => sq_nonneg _)]
6023  exact ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal hbound
6024

6026/-- Lemma 5.6 `N:lem:weak` (source lines 6156-6172), without the pinned/cavity clause. -/
6027theorem weak_reference_estimates (hd : 1 ≤ d) (s : ℝ) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d)
6028    (cstar : ℝ) (hw : ∀ z : Torus d, z ≠ 0 → 1 ≤ periodicRiesz d s z + cstar)
6029    (βm βp : ℝ) (hβm : 0 < βm) (c₀ : ℝ) (hc₀ : 0 < c₀) (Λ : ℕ → ℝ) (A : ℝ) :
6030    ∃ a₀ C : ℝ, 0 < a₀ ∧
6031      ∀ ρ : Torus d → ℝ, IsProbDensity ρ → ∀ [IsProbabilityMeasure (withDensityReal ρ)], Torus.IsSmooth ρ → (∀ x, c₀ ≤ ρ x) →
6032        (∀ k : ℕ, Torus.cbNorm k ρ ≤ Λ k) →
6033      ∀ q : Torus d → Torus d → ℝ, Measurable (Function.uncurry q) → (∀ x y, q x y = q y x) →
6034        (∀ x y, 0 ≤ q x y) → (∀ x y, q x y ≤ A * (periodicRiesz d s (x - y) + cstar)) →
6035      ∀ a : ℝ, 0 ≤ a → a < a₀ →
6036        (∀ x, ∫⁻ y, ENNReal.ofReal (q x y) ∂(withDensityReal ρ) ≤ ENNReal.ofReal a) →
6037      ∀ N : ℕ, ∀ hN : 2 ≤ N, ∀ β ∈ Set.Icc βm βp, ∀ τ ∈ Set.Icc (0 : ℝ) 1,
6038        let μ : Measure (Torus d) := withDensityReal ρ
6039        let W := pairWeight q N (τ * β) μ
6040        let R := pairGibbsLaw q N (τ * β) μ
6041        -- conditional densities bounded by `C μ`
6042        (∀ (i : Fin N) (x : Config d N), External.siteKernel μ W i x ≤ ENNReal.ofReal C • μ) ∧
6043        -- interdependence coefficients `≤ C τ β a / N`, with row (and, by symmetry, column) sums `< 1/2`
6044        (∀ i j : Fin N, i ≠ j → ∀ x y : Config d N, (∀ k, k ≠ j → x k = y k) →
6045          ∀ S : Set (Torus d), MeasurableSet S →
6046            |(External.siteKernel μ W i x).real S - (External.siteKernel μ W i y).real S| ≤
6047              C * τ * β * a / N) ∧
6048        C * τ * β * a < 1 / 2 ∧
6049        -- first-marginal bound `N:eq:weak-marginal`
6050        (∃ f : Torus d → ℝ≥0∞, R.map (fun X => X ⟨0, by omega⟩) = μ.withDensity f ∧
6051          ∫⁻ x, ‖(f x).toReal - 1‖ₑ ^ 2 ∂μ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal ((C * deltaN d s N) ^ 2)) ∧
6052        -- Wu's resampling inequality for `ℝ_{N,τ}`, `N:eq:weak-pi`
6053        (∀ F : Config d N → ℝ, Measurable F →
6054          ProbabilityTheory.evariance F R ≤
6055            2 * ∑ i, ∫⁻ x, ProbabilityTheory.evariance (fun z => F (Function.update x i z))
6056              (External.siteKernel μ W i x) ∂(R : Measure (Config d N))) := by
6057  obtain ⟨a₁, C₂, ha₁, hmarg⟩ := weak_marginal_aux hd s hs0 hsd cstar hw βm βp hβm c₀ hc₀ Λ A
6058  set C₁ : ℝ := 4 * Real.exp (2 * βp) with hC₁
6059  set C : ℝ := max C₁ |C₂| with hC
6060  have hC₁C : C₁ ≤ C := le_max_left _ _
6061  have hC₂C : |C₂| ≤ C := le_max_right _ _
6062  have hC₁pos : 0 < C₁ := by positivity
6063  have hCpos : 0 < C := lt_of_lt_of_le hC₁pos hC₁C
6064  set B : ℝ := max βp 0 + 1 with hB
6065  have hBpos : 0 < B := by have := le_max_right βp 0; linarith
6066  have hβpB : βp ≤ B := by have := le_max_left βp 0; linarith
6067  have hCB : 0 < 1 / (4 * C * B) := by positivity
6068  refine ⟨min (min a₁ 1) (1 / (4 * C * B)), C, lt_min (lt_min ha₁ one_pos) hCB, ?_⟩
6069  intro ρ hρ hprob hsm hlow hcb q hqm hqs hq0 hqA a ha0 ha hrow N hN β hβ τ hτ
6070  intro μ W R
6071  have : IsProbabilityMeasure μ := hprob
6072  have hK : WeakRef.WeakKernel μ a q := ⟨hqm, hqs, hq0, hrow⟩
6073  have hN0 : 0 < N := by omega
6074  have ha₁' : a < a₁ := lt_of_lt_of_le ha ((min_le_left _ _).trans (min_le_left _ _))
6075  have ha1 : a < 1 := lt_of_lt_of_le ha ((min_le_left _ _).trans (min_le_right _ _))
6076  have haB : a < 1 / (4 * C * B) := lt_of_lt_of_le ha (min_le_right _ _)
6077  have hβ0 : 0 < β := lt_of_lt_of_le hβm hβ.1
6078  have hβ'0 : 0 ≤ τ * β := mul_nonneg hτ.1 hβ0.le
6079  have hβ'p : τ * β ≤ βp := by
6080    have : τ * β ≤ 1 * β := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hτ.2 hβ0.le
6081    linarith [hβ.2]
6082  have hβ'a : τ * β * a ≤ βp := by
6083    have : τ * β * a ≤ τ * β * 1 := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left ha1.le hβ'0
6084    linarith
6085  have hexp : Real.exp (2 * (τ * β * a)) ≤ C := by
6086    have h1 : Real.exp (2 * (τ * β * a)) ≤ Real.exp (2 * βp) := Real.exp_le_exp.2 (by linarith)
6087    have : Real.exp (2 * βp) ≤ C₁ := by
6088      have := Real.exp_pos (2 * βp); linarith
6089    linarith
6090  have hsmall : C * τ * β * a < 1 / 2 := by
6091    have h1 : C * τ * β * a ≤ C * B * a := by
6092      have : τ * β ≤ B := hβ'p.trans hβpB
6093      have h2 : C * (τ * β) ≤ C * B := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left this hCpos.le
6094      have := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right h2 ha0
6095      calc C * τ * β * a = C * (τ * β) * a := by ring
6096        _ ≤ _ := this
6097    have h2 : C * B * a < C * B * (1 / (4 * C * B)) :=
6098      mul_lt_mul_of_pos_left haB (by positivity)
6099    have h3 : C * B * (1 / (4 * C * B)) = 1 / 4 := by field_simp
6100    linarith
6101  have hκ0 : 0 ≤ C * τ * β * a := by
6102    have : 0 ≤ τ * β * a := mul_nonneg hβ'0 ha0
6103    calc (0 : ℝ) ≤ C * (τ * β * a) := mul_nonneg hCpos.le this
6104      _ = C * τ * β * a := by ring
6105  have hcoef : ∀ i j : Fin N, i ≠ j → ∀ x y : Config d N, (∀ k, k ≠ j → x k = y k) →
6106      ∀ S : Set (Torus d), MeasurableSet S →
6107        |(External.siteKernel μ W i x).real S - (External.siteKernel μ W i y).real S| ≤
6108          C * τ * β * a / N := by
6109    intro i j hij x y hxy S hS
6110    refine (hK.siteKernel_coeff ha0 hβ'0 hN0 i j hij x y hxy S hS).trans ?_
6111    refine div_le_div_of_nonneg_right ?_ (Nat.cast_nonneg N)
6112    have h1 : 0 ≤ τ * β * a := mul_nonneg hβ'0 ha0
6113    have h2 : 4 * Real.exp (2 * (τ * β * a)) ≤ C := by
6114      have := Real.exp_pos (2 * (τ * β * a))
6115      have h3 : Real.exp (2 * (τ * β * a)) ≤ Real.exp (2 * βp) := Real.exp_le_exp.2 (by linarith)
6116      linarith
6117    calc 4 * Real.exp (2 * (τ * β * a)) * (τ * β) * a
6118        = (4 * Real.exp (2 * (τ * β * a))) * (τ * β * a) := by ring
6119      _ ≤ C * (τ * β * a) := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right h2 h1
6120      _ = C * τ * β * a := by ring
6121  refine ⟨fun i x => hK.siteKernel_le ha0 hβ'0 hN0 i x hexp, hcoef, hsmall, ?_, ?_⟩
6122  · obtain ⟨f, hf1, hf2⟩ := hmarg ρ hρ hsm hlow hcb q hqm hqs hq0 hqA a ha0 ha₁' hrow N hN β hβ τ hτ
6123    refine ⟨f, hf1, hf2.trans (ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal ?_)⟩
6124    rw [mul_pow, mul_pow]
6125    refine mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right ?_ (sq_nonneg _)
6126    exact sq_le_sq.2 (by rwa [abs_of_pos hCpos])
6127  · intro F hF
6128    exact hK.wu_clause ha0 hβ'0 hN0 (C * τ * β * a) hκ0 hsmall.le hcoef F hF

6131theorem weak_pressure_upper_proof (hd : 1 ≤ d) (s : ℝ) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d)
6132    (cstar : ℝ) (hw : ∀ z : Torus d, z ≠ 0 → 1 ≤ periodicRiesz d s z + cstar)
6133    (βm βp : ℝ) (hβm : 0 < βm) (c₀ : ℝ) (hc₀ : 0 < c₀) (Λ : ℕ → ℝ) (A : ℝ) :
6134    ∃ a₀ C : ℝ, 0 < a₀ ∧
6135      ∀ ρ : Torus d → ℝ, IsProbDensity ρ → Torus.IsSmooth ρ → (∀ x, c₀ ≤ ρ x) →
6136        (∀ k : ℕ, Torus.cbNorm k ρ ≤ Λ k) →
6137      ∀ q : Torus d → Torus d → ℝ, Measurable (Function.uncurry q) → (∀ x y, q x y = q y x) →
6138        (∀ x y, 0 ≤ q x y) → (∀ x y, q x y ≤ A * (periodicRiesz d s (x - y) + cstar)) →
6139      ∀ a : ℝ, 0 ≤ a → a < a₀ →
6140        (∀ x, ∫⁻ y, ENNReal.ofReal (q x y) ∂(withDensityReal ρ) ≤ ENNReal.ofReal a) →
6141      ∀ N : ℕ, 2 ≤ N → ∀ β ∈ Set.Icc βm βp,
6142        pairPartition q N β (withDensityReal ρ) ≤
6143          ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (C * N * deltaN d s N)) := by
6144  obtain ⟨a₀', Cm, ha₀', hmarg⟩ := weak_marginal_strong hd s hs0 hsd cstar hw βm βp hβm c₀ hc₀ Λ A
6145  set Λ₀ : ℝ := max (Λ 0) 1 with hΛ₀
6146  have hΛ₀0 : 0 ≤ Λ₀ := by have := le_max_right (Λ 0) 1; linarith
6147  obtain ⟨Ca, Cb, hCa, hCb, hrows⟩ := WeakRef.row_bounds_real hd hs0 hsd cstar hw A Λ₀ hΛ₀0
6148  set P : ℝ := max βp 0 + 1 with hP
6149  have hP1 : 1 ≤ P := by have := le_max_right βp 0; linarith
6150  have hP0 : 0 < P := by linarith
6151  set E : ℝ := Real.exp (2 * P) with hE
6152  have hE1 : 1 ≤ E := Real.one_le_exp (by linarith)
6153  have hE0 : 0 < E := by linarith
6154  obtain ⟨Cw, hCw0, hWex⟩ := WeakRef.exists_W hd hs0 hsd hP1 hβm
6155  set Cm' : ℝ := |Cm| with hCm'
6156  have hCm0 : 0 ≤ Cm' := abs_nonneg _
6157  set A₁ : ℝ := 8 * E * Cb with hA₁
6158  set A₂ : ℝ := 12 * (1 + 2 * E * Ca) ^ 2 + 12 + 12 * E * Cm' ^ 2 with hA₂
6159  have hA₁0 : 0 ≤ A₁ := by positivity
6160  have hA₂0 : 0 ≤ A₂ := by positivity
6161  refine ⟨min a₀' (min 1 (1 / (16 * E * P))), P * (1 / 2 + A₁ * Cw + A₂ * P),
6162    lt_min ha₀' (lt_min one_pos (by positivity)), ?_⟩
6163  intro ρ hρ hsm hlow hcb q hqm hqs hq0 hqA a ha0 ha hrow N hN β hβ
6164  haveI hprob : IsProbabilityMeasure (withDensityReal ρ) := isProb_withDensityReal hρ
6165  set μ : Measure (Torus d) := withDensityReal ρ with hμ
6166  have hK : WeakRef.WeakKernel μ a q := ⟨hqm, hqs, hq0, hrow⟩
6167  have hρle : ∀ x, ρ x ≤ Λ₀ := fun x =>
6168    (WeakRef.le_cbNorm_zero hsm x).trans ((hcb 0).trans (le_max_left _ _))
6169  have hrowsx := fun x => hrows ρ hρle q hqm hq0 hqA x
6170  have ha₁ : a < a₀' := lt_of_lt_of_le ha (min_le_left _ _)
6171  have ha1 : a < 1 := lt_of_lt_of_le ha ((min_le_right _ _).trans (min_le_left _ _))
6172  have haE : a < 1 / (16 * E * P) :=
6173    lt_of_lt_of_le ha ((min_le_right _ _).trans (min_le_right _ _))
6174  have hβ0 : 0 < β := lt_of_lt_of_le hβm hβ.1
6175  have hβP : β ≤ P := by have := hβ.2; have := le_max_left βp 0; linarith
6176  have hN1 : 1 ≤ N := by omega
6177  obtain ⟨W₁, hW₁d, hW₁c, hW₁0, hW₁b⟩ := hWex N hN1
6178  have hpsiN := WeakRef.psi2_ge_one hd hs0 (show (1 : ℝ) ≤ N by exact_mod_cast hN1)
6179  have hNδ := WeakRef.mul_deltaN_eq_psi2 (d := d) s hN1
6180  set V : ℝ → ℝ := fun t => A₁ * WeakRef.psi2 d s (2 * N * P / t) + A₂ * WeakRef.psi2 d s N with hVdef
6181  set W : ℝ → ℝ := fun t => A₁ * W₁ t + A₂ * WeakRef.psi2 d s N * t with hWdef
6182  have hup := hK.pairPartition_upper ha0 (show 0 < N by omega) hβ0 (V := V) (W := W)
6183    ?_ ?_ ?_ ?_ ?_
6184  · refine hup.trans (ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal (Real.exp_le_exp.2 ?_))
6185    have hWβ : W β ≤ (A₁ * Cw + A₂ * P) * WeakRef.psi2 d s N := by
6186      have h1 := hW₁b β hβ.1 hβP
6187      have h2 : A₂ * WeakRef.psi2 d s N * β ≤ A₂ * P * WeakRef.psi2 d s N := by
6188        have : 0 ≤ A₂ * WeakRef.psi2 d s N := by positivity
6189        nlinarith
6190      simp only [hWdef]
6191      nlinarith [mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h1 hA₁0]
6192    have hS0 : 0 ≤ (1 / 2 + A₁ * Cw + A₂ * P) * WeakRef.psi2 d s N := by
6193      have : 0 ≤ 1 / 2 + A₁ * Cw + A₂ * P := by positivity
6194      exact mul_nonneg this (by linarith)
6195    have hx : a / 2 + W β ≤ (1 / 2 + A₁ * Cw + A₂ * P) * WeakRef.psi2 d s N := by nlinarith
6196    calc β * (a / 2 + W β) ≤ β * ((1 / 2 + A₁ * Cw + A₂ * P) * WeakRef.psi2 d s N) :=
6197          mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hx hβ0.le
6198      _ ≤ P * ((1 / 2 + A₁ * Cw + A₂ * P) * WeakRef.psi2 d s N) := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hβP hS0
6199      _ = P * (1 / 2 + A₁ * Cw + A₂ * P) * ((N : ℝ) * deltaN d s N) := by rw [hNδ]; ring
6200      _ = _ := by ring
6201  · intro t ht
6202    have htβ : t ≤ β := ht.2
6203    have htP : t ≤ P := htβ.trans hβP
6204    have hτ : t / β ∈ Set.Icc (0 : ℝ) 1 := ⟨div_nonneg ht.1.le hβ0.le, (div_le_one hβ0).2 htβ⟩
6205    obtain ⟨f, hf1, hf2, -, hf4⟩ :=
6206      hmarg ρ hρ hsm hlow hcb q hqm hqs hq0 hqA a ha0 ha₁ hrow N hN β hβ (t / β) hτ
6207    rw [div_mul_cancel₀ _ hβ0.ne'] at hf1
6208    have hta : t * a ≤ 1 / (16 * E) := by
6209      have h1 : t * a ≤ P * a := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right htP ha0
6210      have h2 : P * a ≤ P * (1 / (16 * E * P)) := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left haE.le hP0.le
6211      have h3 : P * (1 / (16 * E * P)) = 1 / (16 * E) := by field_simp
6212      linarith
6213    have hf5 : ∫ x, ((f x).toReal - 1) ^ 2 ∂μ ≤ (Cm' * deltaN d s N) ^ 2 := by
6214      refine hf4.trans (le_of_eq ?_)
6215      rw [hCm', mul_pow, mul_pow, sq_abs]
6216    exact WeakRef.variance_at hd hs0 hsd hK ha0 ha1.le hCa hCb hCm0 hP1 hE
6217      (fun x => (hrowsx x).1) (fun x => (hrowsx x).2) hN ht.1 htP hta ⟨f, hf1, hf2, hf5⟩
6218  · intro t ht
6219    have htP : t ≤ P := ht.2.trans hβP
6220    have hL : (1 : ℝ) ≤ 2 * N * P / t := by
6221      rw [le_div_iff₀ ht.1]
6222      have : (1 : ℝ) ≤ N := by exact_mod_cast hN1
6223      nlinarith
6224    have := WeakRef.psi2_ge_one hd hs0 hL
6225    exact add_nonneg (mul_nonneg hA₁0 (by linarith)) (mul_nonneg hA₂0 (by linarith))
6226  · intro t ht
6227    have h := ((hW₁d t ht).const_mul A₁).add ((hasDerivAt_id t).const_mul (A₂ * WeakRef.psi2 d s N))
6228    exact h.congr_deriv (by simp [hVdef])
6229  · exact (hW₁c.const_mul A₁).add ((continuous_const.mul continuous_id).continuousWithinAt)
6230  · simp [hWdef, hW₁0]

6233/-- Upper bound `K ≤ e^{C N δ_N}` of Lemma 5.7 (source lines 6281-6340), proved from `weak_pressure_upper_proof`. -/
6234theorem weak_pressure_upper_aux (hd : 1 ≤ d) (s : ℝ) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d)
6235    (cstar : ℝ) (hw : ∀ z : Torus d, z ≠ 0 → 1 ≤ periodicRiesz d s z + cstar)
6236    (βm βp : ℝ) (hβm : 0 < βm) (c₀ : ℝ) (hc₀ : 0 < c₀) (Λ : ℕ → ℝ) (A : ℝ) :
6237    ∃ a₀ C : ℝ, 0 < a₀ ∧
6238      ∀ ρ : Torus d → ℝ, IsProbDensity ρ → Torus.IsSmooth ρ → (∀ x, c₀ ≤ ρ x) →
6239        (∀ k : ℕ, Torus.cbNorm k ρ ≤ Λ k) →
6240      ∀ q : Torus d → Torus d → ℝ, Measurable (Function.uncurry q) → (∀ x y, q x y = q y x) →
6241        (∀ x y, 0 ≤ q x y) → (∀ x y, q x y ≤ A * (periodicRiesz d s (x - y) + cstar)) →
6242      ∀ a : ℝ, 0 ≤ a → a < a₀ →
6243        (∀ x, ∫⁻ y, ENNReal.ofReal (q x y) ∂(withDensityReal ρ) ≤ ENNReal.ofReal a) →
6244      ∀ N : ℕ, 2 ≤ N → ∀ β ∈ Set.Icc βm βp,
6245        pairPartition q N β (withDensityReal ρ) ≤
6246          ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (C * N * deltaN d s N)) :=
6247  weak_pressure_upper_proof hd s hs0 hsd cstar hw βm βp hβm c₀ hc₀ Λ A

6249/-- Lemma 5.7 `N:lem:weak-pressure` (source lines 6271-6278): `|log K^q_{N,β}(μ)| ≤ C N δ_N`, uniformly over
6250kernels with the stated envelope and small row bound. -/
6251theorem weak_reference_pressure (hd : 1 ≤ d) (s : ℝ) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d)
6252    (cstar : ℝ) (hw : ∀ z : Torus d, z ≠ 0 → 1 ≤ periodicRiesz d s z + cstar)
6253    (βm βp : ℝ) (hβm : 0 < βm) (c₀ : ℝ) (hc₀ : 0 < c₀) (Λ : ℕ → ℝ) (A : ℝ) :
6254    ∃ a₀ C : ℝ, 0 < a₀ ∧
6255      ∀ ρ : Torus d → ℝ, IsProbDensity ρ → Torus.IsSmooth ρ → (∀ x, c₀ ≤ ρ x) →
6256        (∀ k : ℕ, Torus.cbNorm k ρ ≤ Λ k) →
6257      ∀ q : Torus d → Torus d → ℝ, Measurable (Function.uncurry q) → (∀ x y, q x y = q y x) →
6258        (∀ x y, 0 ≤ q x y) → (∀ x y, q x y ≤ A * (periodicRiesz d s (x - y) + cstar)) →
6259      ∀ a : ℝ, 0 ≤ a → a < a₀ →
6260        (∀ x, ∫⁻ y, ENNReal.ofReal (q x y) ∂(withDensityReal ρ) ≤ ENNReal.ofReal a) →
6261      ∀ N : ℕ, 2 ≤ N → ∀ β ∈ Set.Icc βm βp,
6262        ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (-(C * N * deltaN d s N))) ≤
6263            pairPartition q N β (withDensityReal ρ) ∧
6264          pairPartition q N β (withDensityReal ρ) ≤
6265            ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (C * N * deltaN d s N)) := by
6266  obtain ⟨a₀, C₀, ha₀, hup⟩ := weak_pressure_upper_aux hd s hs0 hsd cstar hw βm βp hβm c₀ hc₀ Λ A
6267  refine ⟨a₀, max C₀ 0, ha₀, ?_⟩
6268  intro ρ hρ hsm hlow hcb q hqm hqs hq0 hqA a ha0 ha hrow N hN β hβ
6269  have : IsProbabilityMeasure (withDensityReal ρ) := isProb_withDensityReal hρ
6270  have hK : WeakRef.WeakKernel (withDensityReal ρ) a q := ⟨hqm, hqs, hq0, hrow⟩
6271  have hβ0 : 0 ≤ β := (hβm.trans_le hβ.1).le
6272  have hN0 : 0 < N := by omega
6273  have hδ : 0 ≤ (N : ℝ) * deltaN d s N :=
6274    mul_nonneg (Nat.cast_nonneg N) (deltaN_nonneg s (by omega))
6275  refine ⟨?_, ?_⟩
6276  · refine le_trans ?_ (hK.one_le_partition ha0 hβ0 hN0)
6277    refine ENNReal.ofReal_le_one.2 (Real.exp_le_one_iff.2 ?_)
6278    have : 0 ≤ max C₀ 0 * ((N : ℝ) * deltaN d s N) := mul_nonneg (le_max_right _ _) hδ
6279    linarith [show max C₀ 0 * N * deltaN d s N = max C₀ 0 * ((N : ℝ) * deltaN d s N) by ring]
6280  · refine (hup ρ hρ hsm hlow hcb q hqm hqs hq0 hqA a ha0 ha hrow N hN β hβ).trans ?_
6281    refine ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal (Real.exp_le_exp.2 ?_)
6282    have : C₀ * ((N : ℝ) * deltaN d s N) ≤ max C₀ 0 * ((N : ℝ) * deltaN d s N) :=
6283      mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right (le_max_left _ _) hδ
6284    linarith [show max C₀ 0 * N * deltaN d s N = max C₀ 0 * ((N : ℝ) * deltaN d s N) by ring,
6285      show C₀ * N * deltaN d s N = C₀ * ((N : ℝ) * deltaN d s N) by ring]
